### [1] [What's in a Tree ? Hierarchical $n$-gram models as controlled approximators of language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23093)
*Aritra Das,Francesco Cagnetta,Darshil Doshi,Victor V. Albert,Maissam Barkeshli,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 tree-grams——通过二阶与三阶张量的层级复合构建条件概率张量的自回归 n-gram 生成模型，其最大上下文窗口由 n 控制、模型容量由秩超参数 d 控制。该模型优于现有 n-gram 基线并与 Transformer 语言模型竞争力相当，且因其可控性可用于研究 μP 超参数迁移、微调性能差距及涌现稀疏性（可剪枝至多 95% 拓扑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级模型（hierarchical models）虽被提议作为研究语言的合成环境，但其与现实语言语料的相关性仍缺乏充分探索；需要一个可解释、系统可扩展的语言近似器族以开展受控研究。

Method: 引入 tree-grams，即自回归生成式 n-gram 模型，通过二阶与三阶张量的层级复合（hierarchical composition）构造条件概率张量 p(x_n | x_{1:n-1})；其中 n 控制最大上下文窗口，秩超参数 d 控制模型容量，形成可解释且系统可扩展的近似器族。

Result: tree-grams 优于现有 n-gram 基线，并与基于深度 Transformer 的语言模型保持竞争力；作为生成模型使用时，可使 μP 下的超参数迁移更稳健（在由中等 n 与 d 的 tree-grams 生成的更简单数据上调节更窄模型）；熵与相关性有效范围控制微调后的性能差距；允许在候选拓扑间路由时出现涌现稀疏性（emergent sparsity），可剪枝多达 95% 的拓扑而性能无显著变化。

Conclusion: tree-grams 因可显式控制视界（horizon）与容量，为语言建模的受控近似研究提供了有效工具，并揭示了超参数迁移鲁棒性、微调性能差距与拓扑稀疏性等可被系统研究的现象。

Abstract: Hierarchical models have been proposed as synthetic settings for studying language. However, their relevance to realistic language corpora remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce tree-grams, autoregressive generative $n$-gram models that build the conditional probability tensor $p(x_n \mid x_{1:n-1})$ through a hierarchical composition of order-2 and order-3 tensors. $n$ controls the maximal context window, whereas a rank hyperparameter $d$ controls model capacity, yielding an interpretable and systematically scalable family of approximators of language. We show how tree-grams outperform existing $n$-gram baselines, while remaining competitive with deep transformer-based language models. We demonstrate the utility of such controlled approximations by using tree-grams as generative models. In particular, we show that hyperparameter transfer under $μ$P can be made more robust when narrower models are tuned on simpler data, generated by tree-grams with moderate $n$ and $d$. In addition, we show that entropy and effective range of correlations control performance gap after fine-tuning. This is because tree-grams allow us to explicitly control horizon and capacity. When allowed to route across candidate topologies, we observe emergent sparsity in tree-grams. Such concentrated routing permits pruning upto 95\% of topologies without significant change in performance.

</details>

### [2] [Few Dipolar Bosons in a Ring Trap: Quantum States and Transitions between Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22562)
*Jieli Qin*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用严格对角化（exact diagonalization）方法研究环形势中少量偶极玻色子的量子态，揭示Wigner晶体、超流、量子液滴及其混合态，并证明可通过调节相互作用强度与粒子数诱导态间转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简并偶极玻色气体（degenerate dipolar Bose gas）中短程接触相互作用与长程偶极-偶极相互作用（dipole-dipole interactions）的竞争可产生新奇物质态；已有研究多依赖扩展平均场理论（extended mean-field theory）与Lee-Huang-Yang修正近似处理量子涨落（quantum fluctuation），对微观量子层面的完整量子特性刻画不足。

Method: 对囚禁于环形势（ring potential）中的少量偶极玻色子体系采用严格对角化（exact diagonalization）方法，从而获得体系的完整量子性质；通过考察体系的量子关联（quantum correlation）与相干性（coherence）性质来识别各量子态及其形成机制。

Result: 揭示了丰富的量子态类型，包括Wigner晶体（Wigner crystal）、超流（superfluid）、量子液滴（quantum droplet）及它们的混合态，并展示了通过调节相互作用强度和粒子数可实现这些量子态之间的转变。

Conclusion: 该工作为在微观量子层面理解偶极气体提供了有价值的洞见，并可能在量子传感（quantum sensing）与量子信息（quantum information）等领域获得应用。

Abstract: The degenerate dipolar Bose gas has attracted great research interest for studying novel quantum states of matter due to the interplay between short-range contact and long-range dipole-dipole interactions. The superfluid, quantum droplet, and supersolid states, along with the transition between them, have been widely studied using the extended mean-field theory, which relies on the Lee-Huang-Yang correction to approximately account for the effect of quantum fluctuation. In this work, we examine the system of a few dipolar bosons trapped in a ring potential employing the exact diagonalization method, which can yield the full quantum nature of the system. By examining the quantum correlation and coherence properties of the system, we reveal a rich variety of quantum states (including Wigner crystal, superfluid, quantum droplet, and the mixed states of them) and their formation mechanisms. Furthermore, we show that transitions between these quantum states can be induced by tuning the interaction strength and particle number. The findings of this work can provide valuable insights into the dipolar gas at the microscopic quantum level, and may also find applications in the fields such as quantum sensing and quantum information.

</details>


### [3] [Specific heat in the BCS-rashbon BEC crossover regime of an ultracold Fermi gas with a 3D spherical spin-orbit coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23121)
*Yusuke Okajima,Koki Takemoto,Shumpei Iwasaki,Yoji Ohashi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在Nozières–Schmitt-Rink强耦合理论框架下计入自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）引起的配对涨落，计算了可调各向同性SOC超冷费米气体的定容比热$C_V$，用于刻画从费米气体到rashbon气体的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该体系中即使配对相互作用本身不足以形成两体束缚态，SOC也可促成被称为rashbon的两体束缚态，因此需要一个灵敏的热力学量来区分费米气体、rashbon玻色气体及涨落区域。

Method: 采用Nozières与Schmitt-Rink发展的强耦合理论，在存在SOC的条件下纳入配对涨落，计算定容比热$C_V$；通过分析$T_{\rm c}$处及正常态中$C_V$的温度依赖性来划分不同物理区域。

Result: 在超流相变温度$T_{\rm c}$处，$C_V$在弱耦合费米气体区与强SOC rashbon气体区之间的中间SOC区间呈现峰结构；在$T_{\rm c}$以上正常态中，识别出由费米原子气体、稳定rashbon玻色气体以及涨落rashbon主导的三个区域。

Conclusion: 将上述结果与通过调节配对相互作用实现的常规BCS–BEC渡越（crossover）物理相结合，给出了BCS–BEC与BCS–rashbon BEC渡越现象的统一描述。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate strong-coupling properties of an ultracold Fermi gas with a tunable spherical (isotropic) spin-orbit interaction. In this spin-orbit coupled system, it is known that a two-body bound state, called rashbon, can be formed even in the coupling regime where a pairing interaction alone cannot form such a two-body bound molecule. In this paper, we evaluate the specific heat at constant volume $C_V$, including pairing fluctuations in the presence of SOC within the framework of the strong-coupling theory developed by Nozières and Schmitt-Rink, to show that this thermodynamic quantity is very useful to see how system properties vary from a Fermi gas to a rashbon gas with increasing spin-orbit coupling strength. At the superfluid phase transition temperature $T_{\rm c}$, $C_V$ is found to exhibit a peak structure in the intermediate spin-orbit coupling regime between the weak-coupling Fermi gas regime and a strong-SOC rashbon gas regime. In the normal state above $T_{\rm c}$, from detailed analyses of the temperature dependences of $C_V$, we also identify the regions where system properties are dominated by (1) a Fermi atomic gas, (2) a Bose gas of stable rashbons, and (3) fluctuating rashbons. Combining these results with the conventional Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS)--Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) crossover physics realized by tuning the strength of a pairing interaction, we present a unified description of the BCS--BEC and BCS--rashbon BEC crossover phenomena.

</details>


### [4] [Richtmyer-Meshkov instability induced by a Gaussian-kicked rotor-like potential in a three-component Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23171)
*Susovan Giri*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究在三组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）中通过“踢击”势场诱发Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性，数值上重现了界面失稳、涡旋生成直至量子湍流（quantum turbulence）的非线性演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究多组分超流体体系中的流体力学不稳定性（hydrodynamic instability）机制，并考察其涡旋产生与非平衡模式演化行为。

Method: 构建流体力学模型，以流体压强方程给出不稳定性出现的判据；采用分裂步Crank-Nicolson方法数值求解耦合Gross-Pitaevskii方程，先获得准二维简谐陷阱中的不相溶基态，再以kick rotor模型在两个界面引入类高斯扰动。

Result: 界面处演化出非线性不稳定性图样并最终形成量子湍流；不可压缩与可压缩分量的动能谱在低波数区分别呈-5/3与1的幂律标度，高波数区分别为k^{-4}与k^{-7/2}，与已知量子湍流谱特征一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为多组分相互作用超流体体系中的流体力学不稳定性、涡旋生成及量子湍流提供了新的认识视角。

Abstract: This research investigates the Richtmyer-Meshkov instability within a three-component Bose-Einstein condensate. We offer a kicking potential that replicates the impulsive shock across both interfaces of the three-component BEC to trigger the Richtmyer-Meshkov instability. We propose a hydrodynamic model that indicates the essential criteria for the onset of hydrodynamic instability in the BEC through the application of the hydrodynamic pressure equation. We utilise the split-step Crank-Nicolson method to numerically solve the coupled Gross-Pitaevskii equations, with the objective of obtaining the immiscible ground state of all components in a quasi-2D harmonic trap. Following this, we introduce a Gaussian-like disruption instigated by the kick rotor model at both interfaces of the confined three-component BEC to explore the time-dependent development of nonlinear hydrodynamic instability patterns, culminating in quantum turbulence and nonlinear configurations at the interfaces. The kinetic energy spectra of both the incompressible and compressible elements of the system exhibit scaling in the low-wavenumber domain with power-law exponents of $-5/3$ and $1$, respectively. Conversely, the high-wavenumber domain is defined by $k^{-4}$ and $k^{-7/2}$ scaling for the incompressible and compressible elements, respectively, aligning with the established spectral characteristics of quantum turbulence. This research offers fresh perspectives on the mechanisms of hydrodynamic instability, vortex generation, non-equilibrium pattern evolution, and quantum turbulence in superfluid systems comprising multiple interacting elements.

</details>

### [5] [Dynamics and transport in LiF using classical and machine learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21900)
*Harvey L. Devereux,Margaret-Ann Withington,Alin M. Elena,Cillian Cockrell,Kostya Trachenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟熔融氟化锂（molten Lithium Fluoride），比较经典势（Buckingham、Born-Huggins-Mayer）与机器学习原子间势（MACE-MP-0b3 基础模型及其微调版本），揭示机器学习势可再现实验剪切黏度（shear viscosity）并修正熔点（melting point）低估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往用经典势计算的黏度被系统性低估，曾被归因于熔点被抑制；作者据此检验机器学习势能否修正熔点低估并厘清黏度差异的微观来源。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟，对比经典 Buckingham 与 Born-Huggins-Mayer 势和机器学习势——MACE-MP-0b3（small）基础模型（foundation model）及其微调（fine-tuned）版本，并比较各势的熔点。

Result: MACE-MP-0b3 开箱即可在整个液态区间准确再现实验剪切黏度，可与可极化势（polarisable potentials）相比，尽管其本身不可极化；机器学习势修正了经典势对熔点的显著低估，但微调势与基础模型彼此差异明显。

Conclusion: 尽管经典势熔点更低，其固定电荷（fixed charges）导致更僵硬、更高频的单粒子与集体电荷输运动力学；而在长波极限（long-wavelength limit）下，多体机器学习势表现出更具弹性的集体运动，主导动量扩散（diffusion of momentum），从而解释剪切黏度增强。

Abstract: We perform molecular dynamics simulations of molten Lithium Fluoride, comparing the classical Buckingham and Born-Huggins-Mayer potentials with machine-learnt interatomic potentials - the MACE-MP-0b3 (small) foundation model potential, and a fine-tuned version thereof. We find that the MACE-MP-0b3, out-of-the-box, is able to accurately reproduce the experimental shear viscosity across the liquid state comparable with polarisable potentials despite not itself being polarisable. Previous viscosities computed using classical potentials are under-predicted, which has previously been attributed to a suppressed melting temperature. Prompted by this we compare the melting points of these potentials. We find that the machine-learnt potentials address the significant underestimation of the melting point of the classical potentials, though the refined potential and the foundation model differ significantly between themselves. We probe the microscopic structure and dynamics of each model to further elucidate the considerable change in viscosity between classical and machine-learnt potentials. We find that, despite their lower melting temperature, classical potentials produce considerably stiffer, higher frequency, single-particle and collective charge-transport dynamics on account of their fixed charges. However, in the long-wavelength limit, the many-body machine-learnt potentials feature more elastic collective motion that governs the diffusion of momentum and is therefore responsible for the enhanced shear viscosity.

</details>


### [6] [quantEM: Quantitative Electron Microscopy Analysis from Differentiable Physics to Neural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21903)
*Colin Ophus,Arthur RC McCray,Georgios Varnavides,Stephanie M Ribet*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出开源 Python 软件包 quantEM，以 PyTorch 张量与自动微分、Zarr 存储为基础，通过可微前向模型与可复用组件的组合方式统一支持多种定量电子显微学（quantitative electron microscopy）分析，并实现多模态联合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量电子显微学涵盖成像、衍射、谱学与层析等多种测量，其分析日益依赖带物理前向模型（forward model）的逆问题求解；然而现有开源软件大多围绕单一模态构建，导致多模态分析需在软件包间迁移数据，新方法也难以复用已有物理模型与优化器。

Method: quantEM 采用 PyTorch 张量进行计算并支持自动微分（automatic differentiation），以 Zarr 作为存储格式；每种分析方法通过编写可微前向模型（differentiable forward model），并从组件库中选择表示、损失函数、约束与优化器（optimizer）来实现，从而统一运行于 CPU 与 GPU、支持保存与恢复，并共享校准数据结构与可视化工具。

Result: 文中描述了当前包含的模块，包括取向与应变映射（orientation and strain mapping）、直接与迭代叠层衍射成像（ptychography）、基于神经表示的层析（tomography with neural representations）以及 X 射线与电子能量损失谱（energy-loss spectroscopy），并展示了如何由现有组件组装叠层衍射层析（ptychographic tomography）等联合方法。

Conclusion: quantEM 以 MIT 许可证开源发布，并提供文档、教程与精选示例数据集，为多模态定量电子显微学分析提供了组件化、可复用的统一框架。

Abstract: Quantitative electron microscopy includes imaging, diffraction, spectroscopy, and tomography measurements, and the analysis of these measurements increasingly requires solving inverse problems with physical forward models. Open-source software has made many of these analyses widely available, but most packages are organized around a single modality, so that multimodal analyses require moving data between packages, and new methods cannot easily reuse the physical models and optimizers of existing ones. Here we present quantEM, an open-source Python package for quantitative electron microscopy that uses PyTorch tensors for computation and automatic differentiation, and Zarr for storage. We implement each analysis method by writing a differentiable forward model and choosing a representation, loss function, constraints, and optimizer from a library of components, so that every method runs on CPUs and GPUs, can be saved and resumed, and uses the same calibrated data structures and visualization tools. We describe the current modules, including orientation and strain mapping, direct and iterative ptychography, tomography with neural representations, and X-ray and electron energy-loss spectroscopy, and show how we assemble combined methods such as ptychographic tomography from existing components. quantEM is available under the MIT license, together with documentation, tutorials, and curated example datasets.

</details>


### [7] [Weight-Space Programming for Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22013)
*Sk Md Ahnaf Akif Alvi,Raymundo Arróyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作表明，通用语言模型（language model）可依据任务文字描述，直接以文本形式写出一个 16 隐藏单元网络的权重，从而在零标签条件下为材料性能排序提供有效先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现（materials discovery）任务往往起始于目标性能标签极少的情形，而候选排序或生成器筛选模型又必须依赖这些最早的少数标签，因此缺乏一个可靠的冷启动起点。

Method: 沿用权重空间编程（weight-space programming）思路：向通用模型给出任务文字说明、输入名称及一个权重全为零、含 16 个隐藏单元的网络，由其根据所掌握的材料知识填充全部权重。

Result: 零标签下，最强模型网络对实验合金的排序 Spearman 系数（Spearman coefficient）达 0.64–0.79，优于五种简单描述符（descriptor）中最佳的 0.47–0.62；对生成回路中新晶体的体模量（bulk modulus）排序达 0.95，对 35 个相平衡（phase-equilibrium）目标平均 0.67。

Conclusion: 作为高斯过程（Gaussian process）先验均值，该网络在五个标签时将合金排序从 0.35 提升至 0.69、晶体从 0.15 提升至 0.95，并显著降低多数任务的贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization）遗憾值（regret）；增益仅限于最初数十个标签及尚无规则或子系统外推可排序的目标。

Abstract: A materials discovery campaign often begins with almost no measurements of the property it is searching for, and the models that rank candidates or screen the output of a generator then have to be built from those first few labels. Following recent work on weight-space programming, in which a language model writes the weights of a small network directly as text, we show that a general-purpose model, given the task in words, the names of the inputs and a network of 16 hidden units with every weight set to zero, can provide this starting point by filling in all of the weights from what it has learned about materials. With no labels, the networks of the strongest model rank experimental alloys at Spearman co-efficient = 0.64 to 0.79, where the best of five simple descriptors reaches 0.47 to 0.62. They rank the bulk modulus of crystals added during generative discovery loops at 0.95, where the inverse volume per atom reaches 0.89, and 35 phase-equilibrium targets computed with Thermo-Calc at a mean of 0.67, close to the 0.70 of an extrapolation from calculated binary and ternary data. As the prior mean of a Gaussian process they raise the ranking at five labels from 0.35 to 0.69 on alloys and from 0.15 to 0.95 on crystals, and they lower the regret of Bayesian optimization significantly on 4 of 5 experimental tasks and on 13 to 16 of 33 computed targets, depending on how multiple tests are corrected, without raising it significantly on any. The same networks also serve as the mean of a Bayesian neural network and as a screen for the samples of two crystal generators, where they do as well as standard models trained on 400 labeled structures. The gain is confined to the first tens of labels and to targets that no rule or sub-system extrapolation already ranks.

</details>


### [8] [Frame Based Inelasticity for Strength and Fracture in Anisotropic Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22020)
*Robert Lipton,Prakash K. C.*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 弹性软化（elastic softening）由应变动力学经本构律控制，从而实现应变与断裂的双向耦合；模拟结果在动态与准静态断裂分析与实验结果及其他模拟方法一致；模型可预测二氧化钛（titania）的材料强度，刻画钠钙玻璃（soda lime glass）与二氧化钛中强度与能量的竞争，并再现钠钙玻璃与混凝土（concrete）中的动态裂纹分叉（dynamic crack branching）。

Conclusion: 该场论框架可统一处理脆性材料的强度预测、能量竞争与裂纹分叉等断裂行为，具备动态与准静态断裂分析的适用性，但摘要未给出定量误差或适用范围的进一步说明。

Abstract: A field theory is presented for predicting damage and fracture in brittle materials. The model is capable of predicting material strength in titania and the competition between strength and energy in soda lime glass and titana and captures dynamic crack branching in soda lime glass and concrete.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识认为挠曲电极化（flexoelectric polarization）与晶体向上或向下弯曲无关，即对空间反演（space inversion）不敏感；作者旨在检验这一假设是否对所有材料成立。

Conclusion: 铁电体的挠曲电响应普遍具有空间反演敏感性破缺的二次项，可用于在极弱应变梯度下实现倍频电压输出，拓展了挠曲电效应（flexoelectric effect）的物理图像与器件潜力。

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快重离子辐照可在材料中产生纳米尺度损伤，为结构性质的可控调控提供途径，但改性区域中损伤形成的基本过程长期未获清晰理解，尤其是晶体结构如何影响损伤形成与演化尚待阐明。同时，TEM表征中的电子束效应可能干扰对辐照损伤的可靠解读。

Method: 以金红石与锐钛矿两种TiO2同质多形体（polymorph）为对象，结合透射电子显微镜表征与分子动力学模拟，对比分析原子排布差异对径迹（track）形貌及结构恢复（structural recovery）的调控作用，并考察TEM观察过程中延长电子束暴露所引起的结构变化。

Result: 两种多形体均形成非晶径迹（amorphous tracks），但形貌显著不同：金红石中径迹直径变化明显，呈沙漏状（hourglass-like）结构；锐钛矿中则保持相对均匀的圆柱形。模拟表明该差异源于两种结构在快速冷却过程中不同的再结晶行为，导致径迹演化与恢复路径不同。延长电子束暴露可促使初始非晶径迹与表面小丘（surface hillocks）再结晶，并伴随菲涅耳衬度（Fresnel contrast）的出现。

Conclusion: 晶体结构通过控制快速冷却中的再结晶路径决定SHI径迹形貌与恢复行为；电子束诱导转变会显著改变径迹的结构与表观形貌，因此在解释辐照损伤材料的TEM观测结果时必须加以考虑，以确保表征的可靠性。

Molecular dynamics simulations reveal that these differences arise from the distinct recrystallization behavior of the two crystal structures during rapid cooling, leading to different pathways of track evolution and recovery. Prolonged electron beam exposure promotes the recrystallization of initially amorphous tracks and surface hillocks, accompanied by the development of Fresnel contrast. These findings highlight the importance of understanding beam-induced transformations for the reliable interpretation of TEM observations of radiation-damaged materials.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇数周期超胞无法容纳全部标准倾转模式（Glazer倾转系统），从而约束八面体倾转；其对局部阳离子环境敏感的HEPOs影响程度尚不明确。

Conclusion: 超胞周期性会系统性偏置计算的八面体倾转与 energetics；据此提出等摩尔超胞的简单设计规则以解除倾转约束。

Abstract: Odd-period supercells such as $3 \times 3 \times 5$ are often used for equimolar five-component compositions in computational studies of high-entropy perovskite oxides (HEPOs). Using a machine-learning interatomic potential, we compared odd-period and tilt-commensurate cells for 13 HEPOs. *Sakshi Patel,Paras Patel,Prafulla K. Jha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压诱导的相变可用于探究III-V族半导体中晶体结构、电子行为与相稳定性之间的相互作用，而此前提出的相序（含Imm2结构）存在偏差，需重新厘定竞争相及其能量关系。

Result: 此前提出的Imm2结构位于计算得到的最低焓包络（minimum-enthalpy envelope）之上；PBEsol预测的相变序列为F4̄3m→Cmcm→I4̄m2→C2/m→Amm2，转变压力分别约为12、14、34和69 GPa。初始的闪锌矿（zinc blende）到Cmcm转变伴随约17.5%的体积收缩及金属化（metallization），五种相在代表性压力下的动力学稳定性得到声子计算支持。

Conclusion: 研究识别了GaAs的高压竞争结构，并将其相对能量学与压缩条件下的电子、振动和弹性响应联系起来，为理解该III-V族半导体的高压相图提供了依据。

Abstract: Pressure-induced transformations in GaAs offer a means to investigate the interplay between crystal structure, electronic behaviour, and phase stability in a technologically important III-V semiconductor. Here, we have used structure search algorithm combined with first-principles calculations and identified high-pressure competitive phases. The previously proposed $Imm2$ structure lies above the calculated minimum-enthalpy envelope. The static enthalpy calculations with the PBEsol functional predict the transition sequence $F\bar{4}3m \rightarrow Cmcm \rightarrow I\bar{4}m2 \rightarrow C2/m \rightarrow Amm2$, at approximately 12, 14, 34, and 69 GPa, respectively. The initial zinc blende to $Cmcm$ transition is accompanied by a volume reduction of approximately 17.5 % and metallization within the PBEsol description. Phonon calculations support the dynamical stability of all five phases at representative pressures, while elastic calculations characterize their mechanical response. Vibrational free energies are incorporated within the quasiharmonic approximation to examine phase competition over 0-80 GPa and 0-1000 K. These results identify competing high-pressure structures and connect their relative energetics with the electronic, vibrational, and elastic responses of compressed GaAs.

</details>

The approach is first validated by presenting calculated ion-clamped and unclamped dielectric and piezoelectric constants, quadratic dielectric susceptibilities, and Born effective charges for semiconductors AlX (X=P, As and Sb), showing good agreement with density functional perturbation theory calculations as well as with available experimental and theoretical data. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场诱导结构相变中的滞后（hysteresis）是亚磁形状记忆合金（metamagnetic shape memory alloys）应用的主要障碍，利用磁循环产生的亚稳马氏体态（metastable martensitic state）可降低滞后，但其作用机制尚不明确。

However, the mechanism by which the metastable state reduces hysteresis remains unclear. Magnetic-field cycling reduces the hysteresis width by approximately 50-60%, demonstrating that substantial suppression persists on a microsecond timescale. Comparison with millisecond pulsed-field measurements suggests that the reverse martensitic transformation field is more sensitive to the field sweep rate for the metastable martensite than for the initial martensite. Possible explanations include enhanced thermal activation associated with a lower effective activation barrier and differences in the athermal contribution arising from different metastable martensitic microstructures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs 能以极低代价接近量子力学参考计算的精度，效率本是其核心目的，但关于高效 MLIPs 的策略此前尚未得到系统性梳理，因此需要一篇以效率为纲的综述，并在共同基准上比较不同模型。

Method: 将 MLIPs 表示为完备基底展开，即 ACE 及图推广 GRACE；该展开由单粒子函数（single-particle functions）的乘积构造且具有规范性（canonical），可涵盖嵌入原子法（embedded-atom method）等经典势与等变 Transformer 等现代 MLIPs。在此基础上分析成本随体系尺寸与模型复杂度的标度行为，并讨论机器学习在提供非线性参数化与优化中的作用。

Result: 可证明 MLIP 的计算成本随体系尺寸线性增长，且在张量分解秩（rank of the tensor decomposition）固定时随模型复杂度线性增长。由于效率须在给定精度下才有意义，文中同时比较精度与成本：在 Matbench Discovery 排行榜上，三至五层模型已接近更深模型的精度，而沿误差—速度帕累托前沿（Pareto front）截断的单一基底可覆盖超过两个数量级的成本跨度。

Conclusion: 表面上多样的 MLIP 架构主要差异在于非线性、层耦合与优化的处理，而非可表示的相互作用本身；浅层模型在效率—精度权衡上具有竞争力。归根结底，训练数据决定任何 MLIP 的质量。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) reach an accuracy close to quantum mechanical reference calculations at a small fraction of the cost. Efficiency is the purpose of MLIPs, yet strategies for efficient MLIPs have not been reviewed systematically. This review takes efficiency as its guideline. To compare MLIPs on a common footing, we represent them in a complete basis expansion, the Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) and its graph generalization (GRACE). The expansion is canonical, built from products of single-particle functions. Classical interatomic potentials, such as the embedded-atom method, and many modern MLIPs, including equivariant transformers, can be represented within this expansion. Much of the apparent diversity of MLIP architectures therefore reflects how non-linearity, layer coupling and optimization are handled rather than which interactions the models can represent. We show how the cost of an MLIP becomes linear in system size and, at fixed rank of the tensor decomposition, in model complexity, and we discuss the role of machine learning, which provides non-linear parameterizations and optimization. Because efficiency is meaningful only at a given accuracy, we also compare accuracy and cost. On the Matbench Discovery leaderboard, models with three to five layers approach the accuracy of deeper models, and one basis, truncated along the Pareto front of error and speed, spans more than two orders of magnitude in cost. Ultimately, training data determine the quality of any MLIP.

</details>


### [17] [Burgers vectors from Voronoi topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22488)
*Emanuel A. Lazar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Voronoi拓扑、无可调参数（without adjustable parameters）的位错识别算法，可在多种晶体中精确给出Burgers矢量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动位错分析工具仅适用于少数晶体结构，缺乏通用且无需调参的方法。

Method: 以Voronoi拓扑为基础，将相邻且与理想晶体匹配的两个Voronoi胞元共享的面视为一步（step），沿闭合回路累加这些步骤得到精确Burgers矢量（Burgers vector），不使用几何模板。

Result: 在9,610个Voronoi胞元稳定且余量充足的Materials Project结构上测试插入的位错，全部给出正确Burgers矢量；在θ-Al₂Cu的11种位错类型及bcc铁的分子动力学（molecular dynamics）快照中（含至熔点），返回的Burgers矢量均正确，但近熔点处位错合并为单一团簇。

Conclusion: Voronoi拓扑提供了统一描述晶体、缺陷及其Burgers矢量的自然语言。

Abstract: Automated dislocation analysis is standard in atomistic simulations, yet current tools are limited to a handful of crystal structures. We introduce an algorithm without adjustable parameters, based on Voronoi topology, that identifies dislocations in a broad class of crystals. Each face shared by two cells that match the ideal crystal is taken as a step in it, and summing these steps around closed circuits of such cells yields exact Burgers vectors, without geometric templates. Tested on dislocations inserted into all 9,610 Materials Project structures whose Voronoi cells are stable with a wide margin, the algorithm finds every dislocation with its correct Burgers vector. In raw molecular-dynamics snapshots, including eleven dislocation types in $θ$-Al$_2$Cu and bcc iron up to its melting point, every Burgers vector it returns is correct, although near melting the dislocations merge into one cluster. Voronoi topology thus provides a natural language in which a crystal, its defects, and their Burgers vectors are all described.

</details>


### [18] [Combined magnetic and structural transition state in first-principles calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22489)
*Samare Rostami,Xavier Gonze*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在基于势能的约束密度泛函理论（constrained density-functional theory, cDFT）框架下推广的微动弹性带（nudged elastic band, NEB）方法，可同时优化原子、晶格与磁性自由度以计算过渡路径，并在Fe、FeRh与CrI3体系中完成基准验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准固定晶胞NEB计算仅限于原子位移，无法处理同时涉及晶格形变与磁矩转动的磁结构相变（magnetostructural transition），因此需要一个一致耦合原子力、应力与自旋力矩的过渡路径方法。

Method: 在cDFT框架内实现广义NEB，将磁力矩（magnetic torque）与广义晶胞坐标（generalized cell coordinates）纳入NEB优化，支持固定晶胞与可变晶胞两种模式，并适用于共线（collinear）与非共线（non-collinear）磁构型。

Result: 对代表性磁结构体系开展了基准计算：采用可变晶胞形式研究Fe的bcc-to-hcp相变与FeRh的反铁磁-铁磁（antiferromagnetic-to-ferromagnetic）转变；并考察二维CrI3中的自旋翻转（spin-reversal）过程，分析自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）对过渡路径及能垒的影响。

Conclusion: 该推广NEB方法能够一致地描述原子、晶格与磁性自由度耦合的过渡路径，为磁结构相变与自旋翻转过程的能垒计算提供了可行工具，并可用于评估自旋-轨道耦合等效应的影响。

Abstract: We present a generalized nudged elastic band (NEB) implementation within potential-based constrained density-functional theory (cDFT) for calculating transition pathways that simultaneously optimize atomic, lattice, and magnetic degrees of freedom. Unlike standard fixed-cell NEB calculations restricted to atomic displacements, our framework consistently couples atomic forces, stresses, and spin torques. The method supports fixed- and variable-cell approaches for collinear and non- collinear magnetic configurations, by incorporating magnetic torques and generalized cell coordinates within the NEB optimization. To validate the methodology, we present benchmark calculations for representative magnetostructural systems. Specifically, we investigate the bcc-to-hcp transformation in Fe and the antiferromagnetic-to-ferromagnetic transition in FeRh using the variable-cell formalism. Furthermore, we examine spin-reversal processes in two-dimensional CrI3 to analyze the influence of spin-orbit coupling on transition paths and their associated energy barriers.

</details>


### [19] [Koopmans spectral functionals for excitons in materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22524)
*Miki Bonacci,Edward Linscott,Julian Geiger,Nicola Marzari,Nicola Colonna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出以 Koopmans 相容谱泛函（Koopmans-compliant spectral functionals）的准粒子能量替代 GW 计算，构建 BSE@qKI 方法并结合 AiiDA 自动化工作流，在基准半导体与 CrI3 上验证了其与 BSE@G0W0 相当或更优的激子（exciton）描述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的第一性原理激子（exciton）表征通常依赖 Bethe-Salpeter 方程（Bethe-Salpeter equation, BSE）与 GW 准粒子（quasiparticle）能量的组合，但 GW 计算成本高昂，限制了系统性研究。

Method: 用 Koopmans 相容谱泛函（Koopmans-compliant spectral functionals）给出的准粒子能量替代 GW 步骤，形成 BSE@qKI 方法，并在自动化、可复现的 AiiDA 工作流中实现；在基准半导体及层状磁性半导体 CrI3 上进行测试。

Result: 在基准半导体上，BSE@qKI 对带隙（band gap）与第一激子能量的平均绝对相对误差分别为 13% 与 4%，带隙精度与 BSE@G0W0 相当，激发能（excitation energy）精度显著更优；对 CrI3 得到的吸收谱（absorption spectrum）再现了主要实验特征。

Conclusion: 结果表明 BSE@qKI 可作为免 GW（GW-free）的 BSE@G0W0 替代方案，用于复杂材料中激子性质（excitonic properties）的系统研究。

Abstract: Accurate first-principles characterization of excitons typically relies on the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) combined with quasiparticle energies from computationally demanding GW calculations. Here, we replace the GW step with quasiparticle energies from Koopmans-compliant spectral functionals, an approach we call BSE@qKI, and implement it in an automated, reproducible AiiDA workflow. For a set of benchmark semiconductors, BSE@qKI reproduces experimental band gaps and first exciton energies with mean absolute relative errors of 13% and 4%, respectively, comparable to BSE@G0W0 for the gaps and markedly better for the excitation energies. We also apply the method to the layered magnetic semiconductor CrI3, obtaining an absorption spectrum that reproduces the main experimental features. These results establish BSE@qKI as a GW-free alternative to BSE@G0W0 for systematic studies of excitonic properties in complex materials.

</details>


### [20] [Solvent/Antisolvent-Driven Etching of 2D Layered Halide Perovskite Microcrystals toward Endo-Epitaxial Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22529)
*Ajeet Singh,Sudhir Kumar Saini,Hriday Kumar Pal,Cara Elisabeth Bradsher,Sirous Khabbaz Abkenar,Roman Krahne,Liberato Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种溶剂/反溶剂驱动的化学刻蚀策略，在层状卤化物钙钛矿（layered halide perovskites, LHPs）单晶中制造规则矩形腔体，并以此为受限模板实现内部外延再生长，从而构建具有设计内部架构的异质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单晶内部工程化构建内部界面（internal interfaces）十分困难，尤其对层状卤化物钙钛矿等软离子材料而言，传统光刻（lithography）方法难以实施。

Result: 获得轮廓明确的矩形腔体，并将其作为受限模板再生长成分不同的LHP，形成具设计内部架构的内外延异质结构（endo-epitaxial heterostructures）；该方法适用于多种LHP组分与反溶剂体系，顺序添加前驱体可控制内部再生长程度。

Conclusion: 该策略通过受控化学刻蚀与受限生长（confined growth），为单晶钙钛矿内部异质界面（internal heterointerfaces）的工程化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Engineering internal interfaces within single crystals remains challenging, particularly in soft ionic materials such as layered halide perovskites (LHPs), for which conventional lithographic approaches are difficult to implement. Etching initiates at the top surface and proceeds preferentially along the crystallographic [100] and [010] directions. Cavity dimensions are tuned through etching time, temperature and etchant composition. The reaction can be interrupted and resumed, enabling stepwise cavity evolution through successive etching cycles. The resulting cavities serve as confined templates for regrowth of compositionally distinct LHPs, yielding endo-epitaxial heterostructures with designed internal architectures. The approach extends across diverse LHP compositions and antisolvent systems, while sequential precursor addition controls the extent of internal regrowth. This strategy establishes a route to engineering internal heterointerfaces in single-crystal perovskites through controlled chemical etching and confined growth.

</details>


### [21] [First-order correction to the self-consistent harmonic approximation free energy: Impact on the negative thermal expansion and phonon dynamics in ScF$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22538)
*Jincheng Yue,Manex Alkorta,Đorđe Dangić,Yue-Wen Fang,Ion Errea*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作从变分高斯参考态出发，推导了最低非零正规序残余顶点（normal-ordered residual vertex）所生成的一阶后变分修正，用于补充自洽谐振近似（self-consistent harmonic approximation）与自洽声子理论（self-consistent phonon theory）所遗漏的连通非高斯自由能关联。在ScF₃中的计算表明，该连通修正可显著影响自由能导数与结构失稳附近的软模动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强非谐晶体（strongly anharmonic crystals）常用变分优化高斯密度矩阵的自洽谐振近似或自洽声子理论处理，二者虽能刻画显著的声子重整化（phonon renormalization），却排除了连通的非高斯自由能关联（connected non-Gaussian free-energy correlations），因此需要一种系统的后变分修正方案。

Method: 将残余相互作用围绕优化态进行正规序化（normal ordering），发现线性与二次顶点在驻定变分解处消失，仅剩涨落修饰的立方相互作用（fluctuation-dressed cubic interaction）；其二级真空收缩与两点收缩分别给出自由能修正与动力学气泡自能（dynamical bubble self-energy）。随后在ScF₃的完整Born-Oppenheimer势上实施变分优化并与实验及自洽声子理论对比。

Result: 在ScF₃中，完整势上的变分优化相对于使用四阶势的自洽声子理论，已能解释热膨胀交叉温度（thermal-expansion crossover temperature）由负转正的大部分位移；连通修正对该交叉温度影响温和，但其体积导数明显移动压力重映射的三次局域稳定性极限（pressure-remapped cubic local-stability limit）。采用裸立方顶点（bare cubic vertex）可改善与热力学测量的一致性，却系统性低估高温下测量的区边界倾斜模能量（zone-boundary tilt-mode energies）；涨落修饰相互作用给出的热力学修正较温和，但声子能量与实验更吻合。

Conclusion: 连通非高斯关联可显著影响结构失稳附近的自由能导数与软模动力学（soft-mode dynamics），并确认优化高斯态是构建超越随机自洽谐振近似（stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation）的残余相互作用的自然参考态。

Abstract: Strongly anharmonic crystals are often treated using the self-consistent harmonic approximation with variationally optimized Gaussian density matrices or the related self-consistent phonon theory. Both approaches capture substantial phonon renormalization but exclude connected non-Gaussian free-energy correlations. Here, we derive the first-order post-variational correction generated by the lowest non-vanishing normal-ordered residual vertex. Normal ordering the residual interaction around the optimized state shows that the linear and quadratic vertices vanish at the stationary variational solution, leaving a fluctuation-dressed cubic interaction whose second-order vacuum and two-point contractions generate the free-energy correction and the dynamical bubble self-energy, respectively. In ScF$_3$, the variational optimization on the full Born-Oppenheimer potential accounts for most of the shift in the thermal-expansion crossover temperature between negative and positive values relative to self-consistent phonon theory with a fourth-order potential. The connected correction changes the crossover modestly, but its volume derivative appreciably shifts the pressure-remapped cubic local-stability limit. Using the bare cubic vertex appears to improve agreement with the thermodynamic measurements, yet it systematically underestimates the measured zone-boundary tilt-mode energies at high temperatures. The fluctuation-dressed interaction gives more moderate thermodynamic corrections but yields phonon energies in better agreement with experiments. These results show that connected non-Gaussian correlations can significantly affect free-energy derivatives and soft-mode dynamics near a structural instability, and identify the optimized Gaussian state as the natural reference for constructing residual interactions beyond the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation.

</details>


### [22] [Targeted material doping to enable the formation and coherent propagation of Laser Induced Periodic Surface Structures (LIPSS): the ZnO paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22573)
*Gonzalo Gomez-Munoz,Rocio Ariza,Juan Francisco Ramos-Justicia,Fatima Cabello Pardos,Antonio Pablo Pérez-Marín,Jose Gonzalo,Belén Sotillo,Binbing Xia,Paloma Fernandez,Javier Solis*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This work explores how the initial free carrier density governs the formation and coherent propagation of Low Spatial Frequency Laser-Induced Periodic Surface Structures (LSF-LIPSS). As a paradigm we have analyzed the behavior of ad-hoc-doped ZnO films. ZnO is a multifunctional Transparent Conducting Oxide (TCO) with many scientific and technologic uses including applications in biomedicine, photonics, sensing or photocatalysis. Many of them would benefit from the use of large area, electrically anisotropic surfaces. By introducing small amounts of Yb3+ as donor dopant in ZnO films grown by Pulse Laser Deposition (PLD), we have substantially improved its carrier density at room temperature (RT) and its electrical conductivity while preserving the functional properties of pure ZnO. For RT carrier densities ne > 1019 cm-3, coherent propagation of LSF-LIPSS has been successfully achieved upon irradiation with 350 fs laser pulses at 1030 nm at high repetition rate (500 kHz) and for laser scanning speeds well above 1 m/s. However, the ablation mechanism (ZnO sublimation at the LIPSS valleys) and the large melting temperature of ZnO, make the thermal conductivity of the substrate, the film thickness and the irradiation pulse duration to play a crucial role in determining the morphology of the LSF-LIPPS propagated over large areas, especially in avoiding film cracking. The results achieved upon optimization fully confirm the role of free carrier density in the large-scale propagation of high quality LSF-LIPSS in doped ZnO and enable using these structures to easily generate large electrical anisotropies over macroscopic regions with conductivities along the LIPSS several times larger than transversally.

</details>


### [23] [Prior-guided adaptive local refinement of foundation interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22626)
*Yunlong Wang,Jianzhen Ma,Yusu Zhang,Jiahang Li,Qinqiu Cheng,Junjie Yuan,Wenrui Yan,Yucheng Zhang,Chen Yu,Weibin Chu,Hongxia Hao,Jielan Li*

TL;DR: 提出先验引导的自适应精炼方案（PARS），通过比较分子动力学中局部环境的冻结表示与预训练环境原型来识别弱支持构型，将其重建为紧凑周期性晶胞并做第一性原理标注与迭代精炼，从而低成本地把基础原子间势适配到目标任务。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Foundation interatomic potentials enable simulations across diverse materials, but task-specific inaccuracies can require additional first-principles data. Here we introduce the Prior-guided Adaptive Refinement Scheme (PARS), which identifies weakly supported environments during molecular dynamics by comparing their frozen representations with prototypes of pretraining environments, without requiring initial task labels or model ensembles. ### [24] [Computational Modeling of Sublattice Magnetization in CoTb/Co/CoTb Magnetic Multilayer Nanojunctions Between Cobalt Leads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22644)

Abstract: VQkit is a Python-based computational toolkit developed to streamline the pre- and post-processing of density functional theory calculations performed using Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP) and Quantum ESPRESSO (QE). The framework employs a unified representation of lattice vectors, atomic coordinates, chemical species, and positional constraints, enabling cross-code structure conversion, geometry manipulation, crystallographic reconstruction, and code-specific input generation. Its pre-processing capabilities include symmetry-dependent reciprocal-space path generation, atomic constraints, and automated preparation of simulation inputs. The post-processing framework provides calculation convergence inspection, extraction of total energies and Fermi levels, electronic band-structure and density-of-states analysis, orbital-resolved electronic-structure processing, charge-density analysis, and evaluation of application-specific materials properties. The computational implementation is evaluated through elastic-property benchmarks for HfC, ZrC, ZrO2, Ti, and diamond, showing close agreement between VQkit and VASPKIT for VASP-based calculations, with differences observed in the corresponding QE results. Conclusion: 八极磁光效应与$f$求和规则（$f$-sum rule）相联系，可由光学测量定量估计自旋磁八极矩；该工作将磁光效应拓展至广泛反铁磁体系，并提供零场（field-free）光学途径探究磁八极序。

Abstract: Altermagnets break time-reversal symmetry despite their zero net magnetization, enabling time-reversal-odd responses while retaining the advantages of antiferromagnets. Furthermore, octupolar MO effects are linked to an $f$-sum rule, enabling a quantitative estimate of the spin magnetic octupole moment from optical measurements. These results extend the scope of MO effects to a broad class of antiferromagnets, including altermagnets, and open a field-free optical route to exploring magnetic octupole order.

Abstract: Polar discontinuities are typically described as arising from charged atomic planes, a spontaneous polarization, or a combination, in insulating films interfaced with vacuum, nonpolar insulators or metals.   <summary>Details</summary>

The asymmetric response of magnetic relaxation with respect to negative and positive temperature cycle favors the hierarchical model of memory effects over the droplet model.

Conclusion: NiMnCrAl 预测的自旋无带隙与亚铁磁态对化学无序高度敏感；反位无序引入短 Mn–Mn 间距及相互竞争的 Mn–Mn、Mn–Cr 反铁磁相互作用，为磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）提供微观基础。

Abstract: We combine first-principles calculations with structural, magnetic, and transport measurements to investigate the influence of chemical disorder on the quaternary Heusler alloy NiMnCrAl. </details>


### [30] [Mechanically induced self-propagating reactions within the realm of mechanochemistry: Fundamentals, overview, in situ monitoring and perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22840)
*Imelda Octa Tampubolon,Matej Baláž*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了近二十年机械诱导自蔓延反应（MSR）的反应体系、影响因素与原位监测进展，旨在推动这一机械化学（mechanochemistry）中被边缘化的基础研究方向复兴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSR 是机械化学中前景可观但研究不足的现象，相比逐步进行的机械化学反应，其在亚秒内呈燃烧式完成，具有产物形成快、时间效率高、能耗低的优势；然而尽管机械化学整体处于研究热点，MSR 研究仍处边缘，故需综述现状以促进基础研究复兴。

Method: 对过去二十年报道的 MSR 反应体系进行系统性综述，涵盖金属-类金属体系（碳化物、硼化物、硅化物、碳氮化物）、金属硫族化合物、金属氧化物及其他特定类型反应；每个体系简述若干案例并配以汇总表格，同时从球磨条件与试剂性质角度重审影响 MSR 进程的因素，并总结原位监测（in situ monitoring）带来的机理认识。

Result: 归纳出多类可经 MSR 进行的反应体系及对应案例与汇总表；识别出球磨条件与试剂性质等影响因素同 MSR 点火时间（ignition time）呈正相关或反相关关系；原位监测技术的发展为 MSR 过程提供了部分机理层面的认识。

Conclusion: MSR 是机械化学中快速、节能但研究不足的方向，综述通过整合反应体系、影响因素与机理线索，旨在为其基础研究的复兴提供推动；原位监测是深化 MSR 机理理解的重要途径。

Abstract: Mechanically induced self-propagating reaction (MSR) represents a promising but rather underexplored phenomenon within mechanochemistry, a solvent-free alternative to classical solution-based chemistry. In contrast to the gradual mechanochemical reactions, MSRs are sub-second combustive-like processes bringing about substantial benefits such as faster product formation, time efficiency, and lower energy consumption. Despite the fact that the mechanochemical field is in the research spotlight, MSR investigation remains on the periphery. This review aims to catalyze a renaissance in MSR fundamentals investigation by reviewing the current state-of-the-art. This is done via systematically reviewing the reaction systems that were reported to react via a MSR in the past twenty years, such as metal-metalloid ones (carbides, borides, silicides, and carbonitrides), metal chalcogenides, metal oxides, and other specific types of reactions. A couple of case studies are briefly reviewed for each system, and the comprehensive table follows afterward. The review also revisits the factors impacting the MSR course from milling conditions and reagent's properties point of view, showing either a direct or inverse relationship with MSR ignition time. The recent development in the in situ monitoring of mechanochemical reactions allowed to obtain some mechanistic insights into the MSR process, which are summarized in the last chapter.

</details>

Motivation: 研究聚焦于AlCoCuFeNi高熵合金纳米颗粒在熔化与凝固过程中的相变行为，关注冷却速率（cooling rate）对最终结构的影响。

The results show that the final structure changes from amorphous to crystalline as the cooling rate decreases. Abstract: In this study, we demonstrate the atomic-scale engineering of antiferromagnetic (LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n superlattices grown by MBE on (001)-oriented SrTiO3 substrates. Our work achieved a precise layer-by-layer control, revealing how interface-driven structural distortions can be systematically tuned. This behavior exists despite the distinct spin ordering temperatures observed in the constituent materials. Layer-resolved spectroscopic measurements reveal that the hybridization of La and Pr 5d orbitals with O 2p orbitals is also modulated and directly correlated to the oxygen octahedra rotations. By determining the orbital hybridization and mapping the cation displacements as proxies, we can visualize indirectly the trilinear coupling between oxygen octahedra rotations and antipolar rare earth displacements. Notably, the interfacial oxygen octahedra connectivity between cubic SrTiO3 substrate and orthorhombic vanadate layers significantly influences the structural distortions. These distortions, almost quenched near the interface, relax over few unit cells, demonstrating how strain and interfacial effects propagate through the superlattice. This study illustrates how artificial superlattices can be engineered to control structural distortions at the atomic scale, offering a pathway to tune the magnetic properties through strain-distortion relationships in perovskite oxides.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该四模腔磁子系统因吸收型奇异点规避本征基坍缩而具备高灵敏探测应用潜力，但摘要未给出具体实验数据或灵敏度指标。

Abstract: We consider a four mode cavity magnonics system consisting of two coupled resonators containing a pair of YIG spheres. The system considered here offers considerable flexibility and may be useful for high sensitivity detection as absorbing exceptional points can avoid the eigenbasis collapse associated with their resonant counterparts.

</details>


### [34] [Temperature Flips the Sign of the Hydrogen Response in Nanogranular Palladium Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22877)
*Niclas Alkazaz,Robert Zierold,Patrick Huber*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition）生长的纳米颗粒钯网络中，工作温度可在固定氢浓度下逆转电阻响应的符号；响应极性并非结构固有属性，而是可调的操作参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将纳米颗粒网络氢传感的净响应符号（正或负）视为架构的固定属性，但该假设可能导致检测盲区与误判，亟需厘清其温度依赖性。

Method: 在大孔硅（macroporous silicon）上以原子层沉积制备钯纳米颗粒网络，测量不同温度下的电阻响应，并建立热力学模型（thermodynamic model）解释粒子内散射（scattering）与粒子间间隙（gap width）变化的竞争。

Result: 升温使散射贡献减弱，亚埃级（sub-angstroem）间隙变化占主导；25–40 ℃间抵消点（cancellation point）预测升高逾十倍并跨越报警设定值；55 ℃时响应虽为负但出现极小值，可致高浓度被误读为低浓度；200 ℃退火（annealing）改变标定并使室温最低浓度响应转负。

Conclusion: 定量传感需明确的热预处理（thermal pretreatment）与能在目标浓度范围内给出可区分信号的工作温度；响应极性应视为操作参数，该温度依赖竞争机制可推广至其他吸附属形变的纳米颗粒网络，用于传感器与开关器件。

Abstract: In nanogranular palladium networks, hydrogen uptake has two opposing effects on electrical resistance. Absorbed hydrogen scatters electrons within the particles, raising their resistance, while particle expansion narrows the gaps between them and lowers the junction resistance. Such networks are widely studied as resistive hydrogen sensors, and the sign of their net response is usually treated as a fixed property of the architecture. In networks grown by atomic layer deposition on macroporous silicon, we show that changing the operating temperature can reverse this sign at a fixed hydrogen concentration. A thermodynamic model indicates that scattering contributes less as temperature rises, allowing sub-angstroem changes in gap width to dominate. Where the two effects cancel, the network absorbs hydrogen yet gives the same reading as in hydrogen-free gas, so a leak could escape detection. Between 25 and 40 degrees Celsius, this cancellation point is predicted to rise over tenfold, crossing reported leak-alarm setpoints. At 55 degrees Celsius, the measured response stays negative but passes through a minimum, so a concentration above a setpoint can mimic one below it. Annealing at 200 degrees Celsius alters the calibration and turns the room-temperature response negative at the lowest concentration tested. Quantitative sensing therefore requires a defined thermal pretreatment and an operating temperature giving distinct signals across the intended concentration range. Response polarity becomes an operating parameter rather than a fixed property. More broadly, any nanoparticle network whose particles deform on sorption may show the same temperature-dependent competition between transport within and between particles, with implications for sensors and switches beyond palladium-hydrogen.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：晶格的准周期着色（quasiperiodic colouring）能否被编码到单层碳片的键网络中，从而为设计准周期碳材料提供可检验的图案选择规则。

Method: 在三角晶格（triangular lattice）上构造金、银、铜平均（golden-, silver-, bronze-mean）三色图案，用一种颜色的位置通过最近邻规则（nearest-neighbour rule）确定另外两色；以局部能量模型（local-energy model）和密度泛函（density-functional）计算对比能量，并进行周期性胞与大规模有限碎片的几何弛豫与谱分析。

Result: 三种图案分别具有八、九、七种第一壳层环境（first-shell environments）；金、银图案中三个相同基元构成的三角形使三套分子字母表的交替键端口受阻，铜图案则容许四种直接或乙炔撑（ethynylene）连接的设计。对708原子薄片，独立匹配环境计数的预测能量比密度泛函结果低0.295 meV/碳；四个月期胞弛豫后键长均方根变化不超过0.00113 Å。13,950原子直接连接薄片中，π双重态（π doublet）79.05%的权重集中在仅占7.96%原子的边界壳层，而最低未占双重态仅占0.124%。

Conclusion: 该计算将可检验的基元选择规则（motif-selection rule）与候选准周期碳片的能量学及有限碎片的谱性质联系起来，为实验可验证的准周期碳结构设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Can the quasiperiodic colouring of a lattice be encoded in the bonds of a single carbon sheet? We construct golden-, silver- and bronze-mean three-colour patterns on a triangular lattice. The positions of one colour determine the other two through a nearest-neighbour rule. The three patterns have eight, nine and seven first-shell environments, respectively. A triangle of three identical motifs frustrates the alternating bond ports of each of the three molecular alphabets in the golden and silver patterns. The bronze pattern admits four designs with direct or ethynylene connections. Three periodic structures reproduce its infinite environment frequencies in a local-energy model. For a 708-atom sheet with independently matched environment counts, the predicted energy lies 0.295 meV per carbon below the density-functional result. Across four periodic cells initialized from two smaller donors, relaxation changes the bond lengths by at most 0.00113 angstroms in root-mean-square terms. Fitting one 60-degree sector to relaxed periodic geometries gives complete sixfold-symmetric fragments with up to 22,140 carbon atoms. In the 13,950-atom directly linked sheet, a $π$ doublet places 79.05% of its weight in a boundary shell comprising 7.96% of the atoms, whereas the lowest unoccupied doublet places only 0.124% there. These calculations connect a testable motif-selection rule to the energetics and finite-patch spectra of candidate quasiperiodic carbon sheets.

</details>


### [36] [Pair distribution function oscillations at electrostatically stabilized nanocrystal surfaces reflect nanocrystal lattice periodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22946)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性分子吸附能够修饰金属表面态的自旋与轨道特性，为解释自旋相关的界面现象提供电子结构基础，但界面处自旋与轨道角动量织构如何被吸附改变、其中哪些变化真正源于分子手性，尚需系统辨析。

Result: 吸附改变了自旋与OAM矢量的取向，并降低了费米能级附近Au衍生表面态的OAM幅值；两种七螺烯对映体（enantiomers）的织构通过镜面对称与时间反演对称（time-reversal symmetry）相互关联，而与晕苯体系的对比显示部分变化在无分子手性时同样出现。

Abstract: Chiral molecular adsorption can modify the spin and orbital character of metal surface states, providing an electronic-structure basis for interpreting spin-dependent interfacial phenomena. The two heptahelicene enantiomers exhibit textures related by mirror and time-reversal symmetries, whereas the comparison with coronene identifies changes that also occur without molecular chirality. These results clarify how molecular adsorption modifies interfacial spin and orbital character and establish symmetry constraints relevant to chirality-dependent photoemission.

### [38] [Charge-transfer excitation drives cooperative phonon-assisted exciton self-trapping in NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23131)
*Long Cheng,Alexander Holm Ohrt,Oliver Nagy,Yu Zhao,Chenyu Wang,Yaxian Wang,Sheng Meng,Guohui Li,Qinying Pan,Xingyu Huang,Sanshui Xiao,Sijie Zhang,Guoqian Liao,Yutong Li,Xuan Luo,Yuping Sun,Kaibo Zheng,Peter Uhd Jepsen,Binbin Zhou*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 载流子自陷（carrier self-trapping）是强关联量子材料（strongly correlated quantum materials）中的基本弛豫通道（relaxation pathway），但驱动该过程的微观晶格运动（microscopic lattice motions）此前基本无法被直接观测，本文旨在填补这一观测空白。

Method: 在NiPS₃中跨越配体-金属电荷转移带隙（ligand-to-metal charge-transfer gap）进行激发，采用超宽带光泵浦-太赫兹探测光谱同时追踪载流子局域化与相关晶格动力学（lattice dynamics），并结合变温测量、模式分辨介电动力学（mode-resolved dielectric dynamics）与对称性分析（symmetry analysis）。

Result: 瞬态导电响应（transient conductive response）以约109 fs的时间常数淬灭，并演化为局域激子态；同时伴随Ni-S光学声子（optical phonons）的显著重整化（renormalization）及瞬态2.7 THz振动模式的出现。分析表明2.7与8.0 THz响应可作为激子自陷的光谱指示，并指向一种协同双声子机制（cooperative two-phonon mechanism）。

Conclusion: 研究确立激发路径（excitation pathway）是控制强耦合量子材料中载流子局域化与多体弛豫（many-body relaxation）的关键参数，并揭示了一条先产生非束缚载流子、再演化为关联激子态的非平衡路径（nonequilibrium pathway）。

Abstract: Carrier self-trapping is a fundamental relaxation pathway in strongly correlated quantum materials, yet the microscopic lattice motions that drive it have remained largely inaccessible to direct observation. Here we investigate the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$ upon excitation across its ligand-to-metal charge-transfer gap, and reveal a nonequilibrium pathway that initially generates unbound carriers before they evolve into correlated excitonic states. Using ultrabroadband optical-pump terahertz-probe spectroscopy, we simultaneously track carrier localization and the associated lattice dynamics in real time. The transient conductive response quenches with a time constant of $\sim$109 fs and evolves into a localized exciton state, accompanied by pronounced renormalization of Ni-S optical phonons and the emergence of a transient 2.7 THz vibrational mode. Temperature-dependent measurements, together with mode-resolved dielectric dynamics and symmetry analysis, identify the 2.7 and 8.0 THz responses as spectroscopic indicators of exciton self-trapping and point to a cooperative two-phonon mechanism. These findings establish the excitation pathway as a key parameter controlling carrier localization and many-body relaxation in strongly coupled quantum materials.

</details>

Conclusion: 多位不变量的联合比较可有效筛选 class S 候选同构，且候选对呈现显著的族结构规律；IndexLab 已公开于 superconformal.com。

Abstract: We use IndexLab to scan untwisted and twisted class $\mathcal{S}$ constructions based on ADE Lie algebras of rank up to seven, as well as $A_8$ and $E_8$. We consider surfaces of genus at most three with up to five punctures, covering 167,244,647 constructions in total. To find candidate pairs of isomorphic theories, we compare Schur and Hall-Littlewood indices, Weyl anomalies, Coulomb branch spectra, and flavor data. We set aside pairs explained by known mechanisms, leaving a catalogue of 5,278 candidate pairs. Recurring changes in rank, puncture labels, and twists suggest families of candidate isomorphisms. We group 99.58% of the pairs into 25 families and 6 finite families. IndexLab is available at superconformal.com.

</details>

Method: 在刚性 AdS$_4$ 上取 Dirichlet 边界条件，以边界算符关联函数（correlation functions）承担振幅的角色；四点函数与基本超胶子（supergluon）态 $2\to2$ 振幅对应。树阶关联函数由微观理论直接给出，一圈关联函数由 AdS 幺正性（AdS unitarity）获得，并在 Mellin 空间（Mellin space）中表达。

Result: 弱耦合下一圈计算完成：树阶关联函数可由微观理论直接得到，一圈关联函数在 Mellin 空间中形式极为简洁；在平空间极限（flat-space limit）下可重现一圈散射振幅，红外发散以对数形式重新出现。

Abstract: Scattering amplitudes of massless gauge theories are not observables: they are infrared divergent, and the standard regulators are tied either to perturbation theory or the planar limit. We study $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory on rigid AdS$_4$ with Dirichlet boundary conditions, where the role of the amplitude is played by correlation functions of boundary operators. These are finite, and they are defined at any coupling and for any gauge group. Among them, the four-point function of the flavor-current supermultiplet is the counterpart of the $2\to2$ amplitude of the elementary supergluon states, and arguably the simplest non-trivial observable of the theory. In this paper, we compute it at weak coupling to one loop. The tree-level correlator follows directly from the microscopic theory, and the one-loop correlator, which we obtain from AdS unitarity, takes a remarkably simple form in Mellin space. In the flat-space limit, it reproduces the one-loop scattering amplitude, with the infrared divergences re-emerging as logarithms.

</details>

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出半经典引力中复度规积分循环（integration cycle）的非微扰构造：利用作用量的"前向-后向"梯度流，在鞍点邻域生成 Picard-Lefschetz 理论意义下的 Lefschetz 薄片（Lefschetz thimble），并证明其在小扰动极限下重现 GHP 处方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对实黎曼度规做欧氏半经典引力（Euclidean semiclassical gravity）路径积分时，存在共形因子问题（conformal factor problem）；GHP 仅给出微扰处理（将共形扰动积分围道旋转为虚轴），缺乏非微扰层面的严格构造。

Method: 构造 Einstein-Hilbert 作用量的"前向-后向"梯度流（forward-backward gradient flow）：自鞍点出发，作用量实部单调增、虚部恒定；共形因子（conformal factor）实部前向演化、共形类（conformal class）实部反向演化，虚部则相反。固定流末端的共形类实部与共形因子虚部即可得到单参数路径上的复度规，变化末端数据生成鞍点的 Lefschetz 薄片。

Result: 证明在实鞍点附近该构造重现 GHP 处方（GHP prescription）；刻画了前向-后向流存在的鞍点邻域，得到比 Kontsevich-Segal-Witten 条件更弱的限制；并在 4 维引力中数值展示了欧氏 de Sitter 与腔中 Schwarzschild（Schwarzschild in a cavity）两个鞍点的积分围道截面。

Conclusion: 该工作以梯度流给出了半经典引力复积分循环的非微扰构造，将 GHP 微扰处方纳入 Picard-Lefschetz 框架，并把可容许复度规的条件放宽至弱于 KSW 判据，为引力的复围道积分提供了可计算的方案。

Abstract: Integrating Euclidean semiclassical gravity over real Riemannian metrics is subtle due to the conformal factor problem. A perturbative prescription was given by Gibbons-Hawking-Perry (GHP) where the integration contour for the conformal perturbations is rotated to be imaginary. A potential non-perturbative remedy is that the integration cycles associated to semiclassical saddle points should be deformed into the space of complex metrics in the sense of Picard-Lefschetz theory. Here we propose a non-perturbative construction of such complex integration cycles. This employs a 'forward-backward' gradient flow of the Einstein-Hilbert action, starting at a saddle point, along which the action has monotonically increasing real part and constant imaginary part. The flow is not parabolic. In a neighbourhood of a saddle point, the real part of the conformal factor flows forward and that of the conformal class backwards, with the opposite true of their imaginary parts. Starting the flow at a saddle point, then roughly speaking, one specifies the real part of the conformal class and the imaginary part of the conformal factor at the end of the flow. Solving the flow one finds the complex metrics along a one parameter path within the integration cycle of that saddle, and varying this final data generates the integration cycle, the Lefschetz thimble of the saddle point. We find that near to a real saddle point this reproduces the GHP prescription. We characterise the neighbourhood of a saddle where such forward-backward flows may exist, finding a weaker restriction than that of Kontsevich-Segal-Witten. We numerically demonstrate flows explicitly for 4-dimensional gravity, constructing sections through the integration contours for the saddles given by Euclidean de Sitter, and for Schwarzschild in a cavity.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate signatures of weak cosmic censorship in non-supersymmetric vacua of both IIA and IIB string theory. In particular, we examine the Penrose Inequality, which can be proven holographically using Ryu-Takayanagi, and can be independently proven in SUSY AdS vacua for spherically symmetric initial data. We prove that any EFT of scalars coupled to gravity in AdS, including in particular those of string compactifications, satisfies both the Penrose Inequality and a positive mass theorem if a function that we call a "weak fake superpotential" exists. We demonstrate that this condition is satisfied globally for the DGKT skew-whiffed vacua in IIA. Since there is no known nonperturbative instability in this DGKT vacuum, this provides evidence that the IIA SUSY-broken AdS vacua may be absolutely stable. We also find the weak fake superpotential exists locally in field space in some SUSY-broken AdS vacua in a KKLT construction in IIB. Thus, within the framework considered here, some false SUSY-broken KKLT AdS vacua are stable against vacuum decay. Conversely, we find that some KKLT vacua do not have a weak fake superpotential, and correspondingly violate the Penrose Inequality and fail to have a positive mass theorem; the DGKT vacua only violate the Penrose Inequality in the presence of designer gravity boundary conditions. In all cases studied, this is due to false vacuum decay. We discuss the implications of these violations for holography and black hole thermodynamics in these SUSY-broken AdS vacua.

</details>

*Bercel Boldis,Nikolay Gromov,Gregory P. Korchemsky,Alessandro Testa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文为平面二维QCD与Ising场论（Ising field theory）中谱行列式（spectral determinants）的对数导数建立“局域化恒等式（localization identities）”，用特殊点处的Baxter Q-函数表达，并证明此前的猜想关系。

Conclusion: 局域化可由算子符号的有理闭合条件刻画，从而把结论从具体模型推广到一类谱问题，并明确了有限秩约化所依赖的条件。

Abstract: Spectral determinants encode the meson spectra of planar two-dimensional QCD and of Ising field theory in the two-particle Bethe-Salpeter approximation. We derive localization identities for these determinants which express their logarithmic derivatives with respect to the spectral parameter in terms of Baxter $Q-$functions evaluated at special points, proving previously conjectured relations. Our derivation uses finite-rank commutation relations and virial identities of the underlying integral spectral operators. We identify a sufficient rational closure condition on the symbol of these operators that extends localization to a broader class of spectral problems and determines the evaluation points. Applications include generalized two-dimensional QCD with a quartic gauge-field potential and symbols interpolating between the QCD and Ising cases. We also give a general construction of the spectral operators and establish conditions for the finite-rank reduction on which localization relies.

</details>

</details>

Abstract: We review the systematic construction of toric orientifold Calabi-Yau threefolds through divisor exchanges and coordinate reflections. We relate the homogeneous-coordinate and lattice descriptions and explain the computation of fixed loci and the geometric data entering Hodge splittings and tadpole conditions. Our triangulation-wise analysis identifies multiple compatible involutions on a single triangulation and tracks their realisations across different triangulations, revealing the orientifold structure within and across toric phases. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解 $\mathrm{SO}(2N)$ 规范理论 BMN 扇区中含单个 $ε$-张量算符的 BPS 算符结构。这类算符在 $\mathrm{O}(2N)$ 反射下为奇，携带包裹于 $\mathbb{RP}^3\subset\mathbb{RP}^5$ 的 D3-膜（D3-brane）的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 荷，构成区别于迹引力子扇区的 Pfaffian 扇区，其非引力子态的分类与保护性质尚待澄清。

Method: 在经典层面系统枚举 $\mathrm{SO}(8)$ 情形下能级至 $j=18$（能级为算符总荷的两倍）的 Pfaffian 扇区算符，并按 $\mathrm{SU}(3)_R$ 多重态分解。构造该扇区的覆盖空间（covering space），使含 $ε$-张量算符的四项族（four-term family）在各阶秩（rank）下封闭，并借助三重性（triality）配对单态与反射偶类，最后分析微扰修正对超荷（supercharge）的影响以判断保护性。

These operators are odd under the reflection in $\mathrm{O}(2N)$, carry the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ charge of a D3-brane wrapped on $\mathbb{RP}^3\subset\mathbb{RP}^5$, and form the Pfaffian sector. For $\mathrm{SO}(8)$ we determine this sector through level $j=18$, where the level is twice the total charge of an operator. The two classes restrict, along the spinor and the vector embedding of $\mathfrak{so}(7)$ respectively, to the known non-graviton singlet of $\mathrm{SO}(7)$, which is lifted at first order. We show that the two $\mathrm{SO}(8)$ classes, and the family for every $N\ge 4$, are protected to all orders of perturbation theory. </details>

Conclusion: 上述用途通过具体问题、检索段落与有限计算得到演示；教材合集与配套资源已在 GitHub 公开（https://github.com/xingyang-yu/StringQFT）。

Abstract: We present StringQFT as a textbook collection for string constructions of quantum field theory and as infrastructure for AI-assisted theoretical physics. The collection comprises 18 volumes, 190 chapters and 3,795 pages, covering brane probes, brane webs, geometric engineering, F-theory constructions, wrapped M5-branes and exact worldsheet constructions. Its open-book benchmark inventory contains 1,073 items, including 499 with automatic finite-answer checking. For retrieval-augmented generation, it provides 1,371 section records linked to textbook sources and PDF locations, together with a local lexical retriever. Training-data preparation is illustrated by 21 private, source-linked examples. For agents, it exposes 19 calculation tools with specified inputs and finite domains, connecting tool use to textbook derivations. We illustrate these uses through specific questions, retrieved passages and finite calculations. The textbook collection and accompanying resources are available at https://github.com/xingyang-yu/StringQFT.

</details>


### [71] [CFTs with Large Virasoro Primary Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23231)
*Ziming Ji,Jiahua Tian*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二维紧致幺正玻色型共形场论中，是否存在唯一真空且全自旋 Virasoro 原初能隙大于二的显式构造此前未知；该文旨在填补这一空白。

Conclusion: 该文首次显式实现了全自旋 Virasoro 原初能隙大于二的紧致幺正玻色型二维 CFT，并通过码与格构造得到能隙三和能隙四的无穷族，为相关能隙问题的进一步研究提供了具体范例。

Abstract: We construct the first explicit examples of compact unitary bosonic full conformal field theories (CFTs) in two dimensions with a unique vacuum and an all-spin Virasoro-primary gap greater than two. Our constructions use asymmetric orbifolds of lattice CFTs built from even unimodular lattices of sufficiently large minimum norm, ensuring that no charged state lies below the desired gap. The gauged symmetries combine a common orthogonal action on the left- and right-moving currents with independent sign reversals; the latter project out the dimension-two scalar primary $\sum_i J_L^iJ_R^i$ that survives when the symmetry acts identically on both chiral halves. We prove that the gauging is anomaly-free and determine the exact gaps by analyzing the invariant untwisted spectrum and all twisted sectors. From lattices constructed using ternary quadratic-residue codes, we obtain an infinite family with gap three at $(c_L,c_R)=(p+1,p+1)$ for primes $p\ge71$ with $p\equiv23\pmod{24}$. From Barnes-Wall lattices, we obtain an infinite family with gap four at $(c_L,c_R)=(2^m,2^m)$ for odd $m\ge7$. We also obtain two additional examples, with gap three at $(c_L,c_R)=(48,48)$ and gap four at $(c_L,c_R)=(512,512)$.

</details>

We show this beyond perturbation theory for the multi-kinks of the non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model. At one loop the vacuum at the centre of the multi-kink becomes the true vacuum at a coupling of order the classical lifting of the vacua. Tensor-network calculations of the lattice theory show that this inversion survives beyond one loop and has a continuum limit, and that the Gaussian effective potential captures it. On finite chains the multi-kink unbinds when the tension of the string of central vacuum between its two halves falls through zero

</details>

### [73] [On pseudospectra of the relativistic rotated harmonic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15180)
*David Krejcirik,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对量子谐振子经伸缩解析变换（dilation analytic transformation）所得非自伴算子（non-self-adjoint operator）的相对论性版本，建立了预解式范数（norm of the resolvent）的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在回答文献 [Proceedings of the Royal Society A 2025] 中遗留的开放问题，即相对论性非自伴算子预解式在大复能量下的衰减行为缺乏刻画。

Method: 通过对量子谐振子的伸缩解析变换所得非自伴算子构造相对论性对应版本，并推导其预解式范数的上界估计。

Result: 所得估计表明，沿角度大于伸缩角（dilation angle）的射线方向，预解式在大复能量（large complex energies）下呈衰减趋势。

Conclusion: 该上界估计正面回答了前述文献提出的开放问题，确认了相对论性非自伴算子预解式沿相应射线的衰减性质。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound to the norm of the resolvent of a relativistic version of the non-self-adjoint operator obtained by a dilation analytic transformation of the quantum harmonic oscillator. The estimate implies a decay for large complex energies along rays with angles larger than that of the dilation. In this way we positively answer a question left open in [Proceedings of the Royal Society A 2025].

</details>


### [74] [On Schroedinger operators with subordinated complex potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21141)
*David Krejcirik,Vladimir Lotoreichik*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文否证了一个存在约十年之久的关于薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operators）谱稳定性（spectral stability）的猜想；该猜想针对在从属意义（subordination sense）下势（potential）较小的情形。反例基于对临界吸引方势阱（critical attractive square well）的复扰动（complex perturbation）构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验一个已存在约十年的猜想：当势在从属意义下较小时，薛定谔算子的谱是否具有稳定性。该猜想长期未获定论，本文旨在通过构造反例予以否证。

Method: 方法为构造性反例：以一个临界吸引方势阱（critical attractive square well）为基础，对其施加复扰动（complex perturbation），从而得到违背该谱稳定性猜想的薛定谔算子实例。

Result: 结果表明该十年之久的猜想不成立，即存在势在从属意义下较小但谱不稳定的薛定谔算子反例。

Conclusion: 结论是原猜想被否证；临界吸引方势阱的复扰动可导致谱稳定性失效，说明从属意义下势的小性不足以保证薛定谔算子谱的稳定性。

Abstract: We disprove a decade-old conjecture about a spectral stability of Schroedinger operators with potentials which are small in a subordination sense. Our counterexample is based on a complex perturbation of a critical attractive square well.

</details>

### [75] [Contact-number hyperuniformity at glass close packing in infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22209)
*Harukuni Ikeda*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文用复本理论（replica theory）研究高维极限下谐波球（harmonic spheres）在玻璃密堆积（glass close packing, GCP）附近的接触数涨落，解析证明接触数超均匀性（contact-number hyperuniformity）及其在压缩下的破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃密堆积附近的接触数涨落及其空间关联缺乏微观层面的解析描述，尤其不清楚Gardner不稳定区（Gardner-unstable region）如何随空间维度变化而影响GCP的可达性。

Method: 采用复本理论，并引入与局域接触数耦合的外场（external field），从微观相互作用直接推导总接触数的方差与波数依赖的结构因子（structure factor）。

Result: 随空间维度增大，Gardner不稳定区向堵塞点收缩，使高维极限下GCP可沿稳定的一步复本对称破缺（one-step replica symmetry breaking）分支逼近；在GCP处结构因子在小标度波数下呈二次方消失，显示接触数超均匀性；GCP之上弱压缩时结构因子出现低波数平台，其高度正比于超额配位数（excess coordination）。

Conclusion: 结果给出接触数超均匀性及其在压缩下失效的解析描述，表明该性质与GCP处的稳定复本对称破缺结构密切相关。

Abstract: We investigate contact-number fluctuations of harmonic spheres near glass close packing (GCP), the amorphous jamming endpoint reached by compressing an ideal glass. Using replica theory, we show that the Gardner-unstable region shrinks toward jamming as the spatial dimension increases, allowing GCP to be approached along a stable one-step replica symmetry breaking branch in the high-dimensional limit. By coupling an external field to the local contact number, we derive the variance of the total contact number and the wave-number-dependent structure factor directly from the microscopic interactions. At GCP, the structure factor vanishes quadratically at small scaled wave numbers, demonstrating contact-number hyperuniformity. Upon weak compression above GCP, the structure factor develops a low-wave-number plateau whose height is proportional to the excess coordination. These results provide an analytical description of contact-number hyperuniformity and its breakdown upon compression.

</details>


### [76] [Stochastic Dynamics of Domain Walls on a Racetrack: Impact of Inhomogeneity of Magnetic Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22460)
*Anton V. Hlushchenko,Mykhailo I. Bratchenko,Oksana L. Andrieieva,Kostyantyn I. Polozhiy,Andriy M. Styervoyedov,Igor M. Sokolov,Samudrajit Thapa,Stuart S. P. Parkin,Aleksei V. Chechkin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作以二维分形Weierstrass–Mandelbrot函数模拟超薄磁性薄膜中各向异性不均匀性，通过微磁学模拟研究电流驱动畴壁（domain wall, DW）在无序赛道（racetrack）中的随机输运，并用透射率统计与唯象模型定量刻画无序诱导的可靠性极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赛道存储器（racetrack memory）依赖电流驱动磁畴壁实现非易失、高密度、高速存储，但超薄薄膜中原子尺度界面无序会导致空间不均匀磁各向异性，形成复杂空间变化能量景观并强烈影响畴壁运动，其定量描述尚不充分。

Method: 采用二维分形Weierstrass–Mandelbrot函数建模各向异性不均匀性，可独立调控无序幅度与特征空间尺度；通过微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）考察电流密度、无序幅度与空间尺度对畴壁输运的影响。

Result: 即使无热涨落，电流驱动畴壁输运仍表现出显著随机性；透射率（transmission fraction，成功到达探测器的畴壁比例）随电流密度、无序幅度与空间尺度的变化呈高斯误差函数（Gaussian error-function）型分布，并在按中位数归一化后表现出普适标度行为；畴壁位置的方差显著依赖无序幅度，可由所提唯象模型（phenomenological model）良好描述。

Conclusion: 研究为结构无序赛道中随机畴壁输运提供了定量框架，并确立了无序诱导的赛道存储器件可靠性极限。

Abstract: Racetrack memory is a spintronic technology that stores information in current-driven magnetic domain walls (DW), offering a promising route toward non-volatile, high-density, and high-speed memory devices. In ultrathin magnetic films, atomic-scale interface disorder can produce spatially inhomogeneous magnetic anisotropy, creating a complex spatially varying energy landscape that strongly influences DW motion. In this work, we model anisotropy inhomogeneities using a two-dimensional fractal Weierstrass--Mandelbrot function, which provides independent control over the disorder amplitude and characteristic spatial scale. Micromagnetic simulations reveal pronounced stochasticity of current-driven domain-wall transport even in the absence of thermal fluctuations. Transport is quantified by the transmission fraction, defined as the fraction of DWs that successfully reach a detector after propagating through a disordered racetrack. We investigate its dependence on current density, disorder amplitude, and the spatial scale of the anisotropy inhomogeneity. The resulting transmission statistics are described by Gaussian error-function profiles and exhibit a universal scaling behavior upon normalization by the corresponding median values. Statistical analysis of DW trajectories further characterizes the disorder-induced fluctuations of the propagation process. The variance of the random DW positions shows a pronounced dependence on disorder amplitude and is well described by the proposed phenomenological model. These results provide a quantitative framework for describing stochastic DW transport in structurally disordered racetracks and establish disorder-induced reliability limits in racetrack-memory devices.

</details>

### [77] [Observation of temporal routing of topological edge states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22199)
*Letian Xu,Muhammad Imran,Yongtao Jia,Ying Liu,Huibin Tao,Rujiang Li*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该研究在由两条耦合非线性 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链构成的电路中，实验观测到拓扑边缘态（topological edge states）在物理时间维度上的路由（routing）动力学，其行为由非线性强度调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑边缘态的切换与路由对免疫无序（disorder-immune）的态传输至关重要，但既有实验多依赖光波导阵列（optical waveguide arrays），将空间坐标等效为时间维度，因而在真实物理时间中直接观测拓扑边缘态的路由动力学仍具挑战性。

Method: 构建由两条耦合非线性 SSH 链组成的晶格（lattice）体系，并通过调节激励电压（excitation voltage）从线性区逐步增大，测量电压强度分布的时间演化以表征链间交换（interchain exchange）行为。

Result: 随激励电压增大，电压强度分布依次由周期性的链间交换转变为减速的链间交换，最终演化为链间交换被强烈抑制的局域化（localization），从而验证了时间路由现象；相关动力学在受电路耗散（circuit dissipation）限制的有限时间窗口内被观测到。

Conclusion: 研究提供了拓扑边缘态时间维度操控（temporal manipulation）的实验证据，表明非线性可作为调控拓扑边缘态路由的有效手段，并为无序免疫的态传输研究提供参考。

Abstract: The switching or routing of topological edge states is crucial for disorder-immune state transfer. Previous experimental observations have primarily relied on optical waveguide arrays that treat the spatial coordinate as an equivalent temporal dimension. However, direct experimental observation of the routing dynamics of topological edge states in physical time remains challenging. Here, we report the experimental observation of temporal routing of topological edge states. By constructing a lattice composed of two coupled nonlinear Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains, the topological edge states exhibit temporal routing behavior controlled by nonlinearity. As the excitation voltage increases from the linear regime, we observe that the voltage intensity distribution transitions from periodic interchain exchange to slowed interchain exchange and ultimately to localization with strongly suppressed interchain exchange, thereby validating the phenomenon of temporal routing. These dynamics are experimentally observed within a finite time window limited by circuit dissipation. Our findings provide valuable insights into the temporal manipulation of topological edge states.

</details>

### [78] [Raytracing Black Holes with AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26963)
*Thomas Fischbacher,Nicholas Kessler*

Main category: physics.ed-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个基于 JAX、不足 180+200 行 Python 代码加文档即可完全自包含的黑洞光线追踪器（black hole raytracer），说明主要为 AI 应用构建的软件基础设施可被有效用于其他科学计算目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机有三：(a) 向专业物理学家展示机器学习（Machine Learning, ML）加速器能以极少编码量与较低编程门槛简化面向 GPU 加速硬件的数值应用开发，即便问题本身不涉及 ML；(b) 让计算机科学从业者接触广义相对论（General Relativity, GR）的实质部分，其深度学习数学背景已使该理论易于掌握；(c) 启发学生与教育者探索以可微算法（differentiable algorithms）而非可微符号表达式（differentiable symbolic expressions）表达物理定律的路径。

Method: 方法为构建一个完全自包含（fully self-contained）的 JAX 实现黑洞光线追踪器，仅需不足 180+200 行 Python 代码及配套文档；核心思想是以可微算法表示物理规律，即局部微分量之间的关系。

Result: 以极少的代码量与编程专业知识即实现了黑洞光线追踪这一广义相对论数值应用，并可直接借助 ML 加速器在 GPU 硬件上运行；摘要未给出具体性能或精度指标。

Conclusion: AI 软件基础设施可复用于非 ML 的科学数值计算，降低 GPU 编程门槛；同时提示以可微算法表达物理定律对物理教学与跨学科研究具有潜在价值。

Abstract: We show how software infrastructure that was mainly built to power AI applications also can be used to great benefit for other purposes -- by demonstrating a fully self-contained JAX-based black hole raytracer in under 180+200 lines of Python code plus documentation.
  Major motivations for showcasing such a construction are (a) to show to professional physicists how Machine Learning (ML) accelerators can greatly simplify building numerical applications for GPU accelerator hardware with very modest coding effort and programming expertise -- even if the problems at hand do not involve ML per se, (b) to make a meaningful part of General Relativity (GR) accessible to computer science professionals who might not have realized that mathematical background they developed on Deep Learning problems may have brought this theory within easy reach for them, and (c) to inspire students and educators to explore the potential of an approach that represents physical law, i.e. relations between local differential quantities, not in terms of differentiable symbolic expressions, but in terms of differentiable algorithms.

</details>
