### [79] [Muon Collider: Towards the Energy Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22982)
*Mark A. Palmer,Daniel Schulte,Vladimir Shiltsev*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 该综述指出，μ子对撞机（muon collider）有望成为下一代能量前沿粒子加速器，兼具多TeV轻子对撞（lepton collisions）的发现潜力与更小的占地和功耗，并已从长期愿景转变为可信的技术路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有及拟议的强子对撞机（hadron collider）与正负电子对撞机（electron-positron collider）在规模与功耗上代价高昂，亟需一种兼具轻子碰撞洁净物理环境与可承受基础设施需求的旗舰装置。

Method: 以综述（review）形式，系统梳理物理机遇、加速器复合体（accelerator complex）设计与现状、独特实验环境、近年技术进展、国际研发（R&D）动态及后续实现步骤。

Result: 过去二十年在高功率靶（high-power targets）、μ子产生与俘获（muon production and capture）、电离冷却（ionization cooling）、快速加速、高场磁体（high-field magnets）、对撞环设计及机器-探测器集成（machine-detector integration）等方面的进展，支撑了该概念的可行性。

Conclusion: μ子对撞机已被视为通往未来旗舰设施的可信路径，目标是在本世纪中叶实现高能μ子对撞机，但仍存在需完成的剩余技术步骤。

Abstract: A muon collider has emerged as a compelling candidate for the next energy-frontier particle accelerator, combining the discovery potential of multi-TeV lepton collisions with a footprint and power consumption far smaller than existing or proposed hadron and electron-positron colliders. Advances over the past two decades in high-power targets, muon production and capture, ionization cooling, rapid acceleration, high-field magnets, collider ring design, and machine-detector integration have transformed the concept from a long-term vision into a credible path toward a future flagship facility. This review summarizes the physics opportunities, the accelerator complex's design and status, the unique experimental environment, recent technical achievements, ongoing international R&D, and remaining steps toward realizing a high-energy muon collider by the middle of the century.

</details>

### [80] [Hyperkähler Periods in Families of Parabolic Higgs Bundle Moduli Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21990)
*Panagiotis Dimakis,Laura Fredrickson,Arya Yae*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究变动抛物权重（parabolic weights）与希格斯场本征值（Higgs field eigenvalues）下抛物希格斯丛模空间族的周期映射，证明其关于参数仿射线性，并对 E6、E7、E8 型四维模空间给出显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有周期映射理论多针对固定权重情形，对权重与本征值同时变动的模空间族缺乏系统描述；本文旨在建立该族上的平行移动与周期映射结构。

Method: 将模空间组装为光滑族 π: 𝓜→Λ（Λ 编码 (α,σ)），在开稠密子集 Λ0 上沿路径定义同调（homology）的平行移动（parallel transport），并推广 Duistermaat–Heckman 经典定理以刻画周期映射。

Result: 周期映射在 (α,σ) 上仿射线性（affine linear），其斜率由 𝓜 上主环面丛（principal torus bundle）的曲率类（curvature class）决定；对 E6、E7、E8 型四维模空间计算该类并得到周期映射的显式公式。

Conclusion: 周期映射的可积结构由主环面丛曲率类完全控制，为变动权重下的希格斯丛模空间提供了显式计算框架。

Abstract: We consider the period map on families of parabolic Higgs bundle moduli spaces with varying parabolic weights $α$ and Higgs field eigenvalues $σ$. We assemble the moduli spaces into a smooth family $π:\mathscr M\toΛ$ where $Λ$ encodes $(α,σ)$, and define parallel transport for homology along paths in an open dense subset $Λ_0\subsetΛ$. By generalizing a classical theorem of Duistermaat and Heckman, we prove that the period map is affine linear in $(α,σ)$, with slope governed by the curvature class of a principal torus bundle over $\mathscr M$. For the four-dimensional moduli spaces of types $E_6,E_7,E_8$, we compute these classes to get explicit formulas for the period maps.

</details>

### [81] [Bandwidth-tunable thermal emitter via dynamic resonator coupling and loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21934)
*Jin-Woo Cho,Tanuj Kumar,Joseph A. Andrade,Shenwei Yin,Samir Rosas,Filiz Yesilköy,Dung T. Quach,Albert G. Wright,Colin M. Hessel,David Woolf,Mikhail A. Kats*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作展示了一种可由绝缘相/金属相切换实现窄带与宽带发射切换的全向热辐射器（omnidirectional thermal emitter），基于亚波长厚度的金属背板多层结构，并实现了电学快速调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在实现热辐射光谱的动态可调，即在窄带（narrowband）与宽带（broadband）发射态之间切换，并兼顾角度不敏感性与电学可控性，以拓展热辐射器在自由空间识别、标记与通信中的应用。

Method: 采用中红外亚波长厚度的金属背板多层结构，构成单输入端口、对入射角不敏感（最大至60°）的光学腔（optical cavity）；引入超薄VO2层动态调控该腔的耦合系数与损耗系数（coupling and loss coefficients）。结构围绕自支撑硅膜（free-standing silicon membrane）制备并集成于印制电路板（PCB）上，实现电学切换。

Result: 当VO2处于绝缘相时，多层结构中极性电介质层的振动模式（vibrational mode）产生窄带发射；当VO2切换至有损金属相时，近阻抗匹配腔（nearly impedance-matched cavity）产生宽带发射。电学切换时间尺度为十至数百毫秒（ten-to-hundred millisecond scale）。

Conclusion: 该结构实现了全向、角度不敏感且电学可切换的窄带/宽带热发射，可应用于自由空间识别、标记或通信（free-space identification, tagging, or communication）。

Abstract: We demonstrate a dynamically tunable omnidirectional thermal emitter that can be switched from a narrowband emission state to a broadband state. The structure is a metal-backed multilayer with subwavelength thickness across the mid-infrared, which forms an angle-insensitive (up to 60°) optical cavity with one input port. We used an ultrathin VO2 layer to dynamically switch the coupling and loss coefficients of this optical cavity, resulting in narrowband emission from the vibrational mode of a polar dielectric layer that is part of the multilayer stack when the VO2 is in its insulating phase, and broadband emission from a nearly impedance-matched cavity when the VO2 is switched to its lossy metallic phase. We fabricated the structure around a free-standing silicon membrane and integrated it onto a printed circuit board (PCB), which enabled electrical switching of the emitter at the ten-to-hundred millisecond scale, and can be used for free-space identification, tagging, or communication.

</details>


### [82] [Topological Timing of Bound States by Spacetime Mass Windings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22357)
*Hamdi Barkaoui*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文揭示拓扑除决定波被束缚的位置外，还可决定束缚态在何时变亮或变暗：空间与时间变化的两个狄拉克质量（Dirac mass）构成的质量场其孤立零点携带整数绕数（winding），绕数符号决定开关时刻束缚态取极大还是极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识中拓扑主要决定波的局域位置，而束缚态的时序（亮度随时间的变化）是否也受拓扑约束尚不明确，本文旨在探索拓扑对局域光学响应时间特性的控制能力。

Method: 构建二聚化光子晶格（dimerized photonic lattice）：空间变化的实狄拉克质量束缚 Jackiw-Rebbi 态，时间切换的交错增益与损耗（staggered gain and loss）作为作用于同一态的虚狄拉克质量；结合连续狄拉克描述（continuum Dirac description）与有限晶格模拟，并用传播距离代替时间的无源波导阵列（waveguide array）进行全波仿真。

Result: 绕数符号决定束缚态在切换时达到最大值还是最小值，且增益与损耗始终保持在体带隙（bulk gap）以下；绕数满足守恒律，成对产生与湮灭，沿边界的绕数之和决定哪些极值受到保护；仅用损耗的无源波导阵列全波仿真复现了上述预测。

Conclusion: 研究给出了一种调控局域光学响应时序的拓扑手段（topological handle），可应用于集成光子电路与拓扑激光器中受限模式的开关、门控与脉冲整形。

Abstract: Topology usually determines where a wave is trapped. Here we show that it can also determine when a trapped state brightens or dims. We consider a dimerized photonic lattice in which a real Dirac mass, varying in space, binds a Jackiw-Rebbi state, while staggered gain and loss, switched in time, act as an imaginary Dirac mass on that same state. Together, the two masses form a mass field in space and time whose isolated zeros carry an integer winding. Combining a continuum Dirac description with finite-lattice simulations, we show that the sign of this winding fixes whether the bound state reaches a maximum or a minimum at the switch, while the gain and loss remain below the bulk gap. The windings obey a conservation law: they are created and annihilated in pairs, and their sum along a boundary determines which extrema are protected. Full-wave simulations of a passive waveguide array, in which the propagation distance plays the role of time, reproduce these predictions with loss alone. These results provide a topological handle on the timing of localized optical responses, relevant to switching, gating, and pulse shaping of confined modes in integrated photonic circuits and topological lasers.

</details>


### [83] [Revisiting structured-light propagation in screw-dislocation geometries: Landau dynamics and gradient-induced polarization textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22789)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文从规范不变、最小耦合的 Maxwell 理论出发，推导有限电磁光束在连续分布螺旋位错的“有效度规（effective metric）”中的传播，给出几何光学、傍轴（paraxial）阶轨道运动与偏振演化的解析结果，并提出全斯托克斯（full-Stokes）测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续分布螺旋位错（screw dislocations）所诱导的有效度规如何影响有限电磁光束的传播，需要澄清真实密度梯度与坐标因子的区分，并建立可检验的偏振与轨道运动预言。

Method: 采用规范不变、最小耦合 Maxwell 理论（F=dA），由 Burgers 通量（Burgers flux）通过 σ(ρ)=f'(ρ)/(bρ) 确定度规剖面；在几何光学与傍轴近似下分析光锥、偏振演化与轨道运动，并用精确 Maxwell 本征模、饱和剖面的解析结果及数值传播进行检验。

Result: 两种偏振共享同一光锥；均匀密度使偏振相对几何适配标架均匀旋转；傍轴阶轨道运动呈 Landau 型并具闭式传播子。任意光滑轴对称密度下偏振织构初始增长正比于按光束强度加权的密度标准差，局域相位相干性为该分布的特征函数。相干横向琼斯场（Jones field）下，线性斯托克斯织构与局域圆偏振的精确关系对应空间—偏振不可分性（nonseparability）；线偏振实轴对称输入光束的衍射在传播距离二阶产生局域圆偏振，而无损领头傍轴理论中积分圆斯托克斯分量守恒。

Conclusion: 提出比较相同平均扭转的均匀与梯度样品的全斯托克斯测量，并给出束腰的闭式判据；这些预言适用于理想度规—Maxwell模型或实现该模型的变换光学（transformation optics）介质，而不适用于未经本构建模的天然位错晶体。

Abstract: We derive the propagation of finite electromagnetic beams in the effective metric of a continuous distribution of screw dislocations from gauge-invariant, minimally coupled Maxwell theory with $F=dA$. The Burgers flux fixes the metric profile through $σ(ρ)=f'(ρ)/(bρ)$, which separates true density gradients from coordinate factors. In geometric optics, both polarizations share one light cone, and a uniform density rotates the polarization uniformly relative to the geometry-adapted frame; laboratory polarimetry also requires matching at the interfaces. At leading paraxial order, the orbital motion is of Landau type, with a closed propagator for arbitrary inputs, and for a uniform density the polarization evolution factorizes exactly. For an arbitrary smooth axisymmetric density, the polarization texture initially grows in proportion to the standard deviation of the density weighted by the beam intensity; the local-phase coherence is the characteristic function of that distribution. For a coherent transverse Jones field, an exact relation links the linear-Stokes texture and the local circular polarization to spatial--polarization nonseparability. For linearly polarized, real axisymmetric input beams, diffraction produces local circular polarization at second order in the propagation distance, while the integrated circular Stokes component is conserved in the lossless leading-paraxial theory. Exact Maxwell eigenmodes, analytic results for a saturating profile, and numerical propagation test the approximation. We propose a full-Stokes measurement that compares uniform and graded samples with the same mean twist, with a closed-form criterion for the beam waist. These predictions apply to the ideal metric-Maxwell model or to a transformation-optics medium that realizes it, not to natural dislocated crystals without further constitutive modeling.

</details>

### [84] [Hill's Lunar Equation as a Leaf: Riccati Reduction and Frobenius Theory for Second-Order PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21818)
*Víctor León,Bruno Scárdua*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文将 Hill–Adams 月球近地点与交点方程、Mathieu 方程与 Lamé 方程统一视为一类"平方型叶状方程"（square-type foliated equations）沿叶的约束，建立其正则奇点与叶状结构解析线性化（analytic linearizability）的对应，并据此给出 Hill 常数与 Adams 常数的严格计算机辅助证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典月球运动理论中的 Hill 常数、Adams 常数以及 Mathieu、Lamé 方程长期以各自独立的方法处理，缺乏统一的理论框架；同时历史数值结果（如 Hill 自估误差范围内的常数）缺少严格验证手段。

Method: 研究形如 L²u + A Lu + B u = 0 的线性偏微分方程（L 为解析向量场，A、B 为解析函数）。在有理鞍点（rational saddles）处构造收敛解，其指数为首次积分（first integral）的全纯函数，并通过该函数的变差刻画单值性（monodromy）；再借助收敛级数计算特征指数，对 Hill 与 Adams 常数进行计算机辅助严格证明。

Result: 证明正则奇点当且仅当 L 定义的叶状结构解析可线性化；Hill 值在其自身误差估计内得到确认并确定第 15 位小数，近地点运动取值全部数字正确，Adams 值确认至 12 位小数；Mathieu 方程中控制离子囚禁的常特征指数曲线表现为经典 Frobenius 轨迹；对 Lamé 方程证明对数障碍（logarithmic obstructions）的乘积公式，其零点反映有限间隙（finite-gap）性质。

Conclusion: 平方型叶状方程为上述经典方程提供了统一框架，使特征指数可由全纯函数的收敛级数严格计算，从而把历史数值常数提升为可验证的严格结果，并揭示 Frobenius 解存在性与单值性、有限间隙性质之间的结构联系。

Abstract: The equations of Hill and Adams for the lunar perigee and node, Mathieu's equation and Lamé's equation arise as leafwise restrictions of one class of linear partial differential equations, the \emph{square-type foliated equations} $L^2u+A\,Lu+B\,u=0$, where $L$ is an analytic vector field and $A,B$ are analytic functions. Such an equation has a regular singularity exactly when the foliation defined by $L$ is analytically linearizable. At rational saddles, where every point of the indicial conic is resonant, we obtain convergent solutions whose exponents are holomorphic functions of a first integral; their variation describes the monodromy along the leaves and decides when classical Frobenius solutions exist.
  For a Hill equation, the characteristic exponent becomes the value of such a holomorphic function, given by a convergent series. This yields rigorous computer-assisted proofs of Hill's constant for the motion of the lunar perigee and of Adams' constant for the motion of the node: Hill's value is confirmed within his own error estimate and its fifteenth decimal is determined, his value of the motion of the perigee is shown to be correct in all its digits, and Adams' value is confirmed to twelve decimals. For Mathieu's equation, the curves of constant characteristic exponent that govern ion trapping appear as classical Frobenius loci. For Lamé's equation, we prove a product formula for the logarithmic obstructions, which coincide with the leading coefficients of the eigenvalue splittings computed by Volkmer and whose vanishing reflects the finite-gap property.

</details>


### [85] [Global phase-plane organization of a toxin-mediated plant-herbivore model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22451)
*Gabriele Grifò*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文对具有非单调功能反应（non-monotone functional response）的植物-食草动物模型进行定性与几何分析，刻画模型的动力学区域及其转迁，并给出相平面（phase plane）的全局结构分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物化学防御（chemical plant defence）与食草压力、资源可用性之间的非线性反馈可产生复杂种群动力学，需阐明其动力学组织方式。

Method: 针对含抑制放牧活动效应的非单调功能反应的齐次系统（homogeneous system），开展定性与几何分析，识别平衡态配置与全局相平面结构。

Result: 发现经Hopf分岔（Hopf bifurcation）产生振荡行为及稳定周期解（stable periodic solutions），并完整分类平衡态构型与异宿结构（heteroclinic structures），同时识别同宿连接（homoclinic connections）出现的条件。

Conclusion: 非线性防御反馈可导致灭绝、稳定共存、持续振荡及由异宿与同宿结构组织的全局分岔（global bifurcation）等性质不同的生态结果，分析给出相平面组织的统一描述。

Abstract: Plant-herbivore interactions with chemical plant defence mechanisms can generate complex population dynamics through the nonlinear interplay between resource availability, herbivore pressure, and defence-induced feedbacks. In this work, we investigate the dynamical organization of a plant-herbivore model characterized by a non-monotone functional response accounting for inhibitory effects on grazing activity. In particular, we perform a qualitative and geometric analysis of the associated homogeneous system, characterizing the dynamical regimes supported by the model and the transitions between them. The results reveal the emergence of oscillatory behaviour through a Hopf bifurcation and the occurrence of stable periodic solutions. We further characterize the global phase-plane organization, providing a complete classification of equilibrium configurations and heteroclinic structures supported by the model. In addition, we identify the conditions under which homoclinic connections may occur and discuss their role in shaping the global dynamics. The analysis provides a unified description of the phase-plane organization and highlights how nonlinear defence feedbacks generate qualitatively distinct ecological outcomes, including extinction, stable coexistence, persistent oscillations, and global bifurcation scenarios organized by heteroclinic and homoclinic structures.

</details>

### [86] [Nonlocal regularization of sharp-crack energetics: a variational bridge from Griffith geometry to emergent constitutive degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22284)
*Qing Cheng,Yuqi Sun,Xuejun Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于拉伸应变能非局部平均的变分弥散裂纹模型，退化由储能泛函自然决定，无需独立断裂场或人为退化律，并给出收敛性、Griffith恢复与结构保持数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相场断裂方法需引入独立断裂场并预设退化函数，退化规律带有较强人为性；本文旨在通过拉伸应变能的非局部平均使退化内生于储能泛函，从而减少对额外退化假设的依赖。

Method: 对拉伸应变能做非局部平均（nonlocal averaging），在缩放平均值上施加有界饱和势（bounded saturation potential），压缩能保持局部；由一阶变分得到本构应力，其拉伸项乘以饱和势导数的伴随平均（adjoint average）。数值上采用结构保持的标量辅助变量（scalar auxiliary variable, SAV）格式，并与速度-Verlet（velocity-Verlet）格式对比。

Result: 初始斜率在首阶意义下保持平均拉伸应变能密度；平均半径趋于零时，有界应变的光滑位移场能量密度收敛于经典弹性应变能密度；平面张开转变可恢复Griffith表面能系数；SAV格式满足修正能量增量与外力功之间的精确离散平衡。

Conclusion: 该模型将裂纹退化机制嵌入储能泛函，兼具渐近一致性与Griffith型表面能，并通过结构保持格式实现能量稳定的动力学模拟；动态裂纹分叉与Kalthoff–Winkler冲击对比验证了SAV与velocity-Verlet解的表现。

Abstract: We develop a variational diffuse-crack model based on nonlocal averaging of tensile strain energy. A bounded potential acts on the scaled average, while the compressive energy remains local. The initial slope preserves the averaged tensile strain-energy density to leading order. The first variation yields a constitutive stress in which the tensile contribution is multiplied by the adjoint average of the derivative of the saturation potential. The resulting degradation is therefore determined by the stored-energy functional, without introducing an independent fracture field or prescribing a separate degradation law. For fixed smooth displacement fields with bounded strain, the energy density converges to the classical elastic strain-energy density as the averaging radius tends to zero. The Griffith surface-energy coefficient is recovered for planar opening transitions. A structure-preserving scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) scheme satisfies an exact discrete balance between the modified-energy increment and external work. We compare SAV and velocity-Verlet solutions for dynamic crack branching and Kalthoff--Winkler impact.

</details>

### [87] [Molecular memory reduces timing noise in stochastic gene expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22554)
*Kuheli Biswas,Supravat Dey,Abhyudai Singh*

Main category: q-bio.MN

TL;DR: 多步mRNA生成与降解引入的分子记忆（molecular memory）会降低爆发式基因表达中首次通过时间（first-passage time, FPT）的噪声，但两者机制不同：多步爆发到达通过抑制拷贝数涨落降低定时噪声，多步降解通过重塑瞬态积累动态降低相对定时噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单步生灭模型假设反应等待时间服从指数分布（无记忆），但mRNA生成与降解常涉及多个连续步骤，呈亚指数分布（hypoexponential distribution），因此需明确分子记忆如何影响阈值事件的定时精度。

Method: 在爆发式基因表达框架下，于小噪声极限（small-noise limit）解析计算FPT统计量（均值与噪声），分别考察多步爆发到达与多步降解两类机制；并用随机模拟（stochastic simulation）验证解析预测及其适用范围。

Result: 两类多步机制均降低FPT噪声；多步爆发到达降低mRNA拷贝数涨落，多步降解在稳态mRNA噪声反而增大时仍降低相对定时噪声。使FPT噪声最小的阈值仍接近稳态均值的一半，仅在爆发规模恒定的多步爆发到达情形下移向更低值；模拟表明定性结论在小噪声近似之外依然成立。

Conclusion: 分子记忆对下游事件定时精度的影响取决于其来源：噪声抑制与瞬态动力学重塑是两条独立途径，且阈值最优位置对机制类型敏感。

Abstract: Fluctuations in gene expression affect the timing precision of downstream events triggered when a gene product reaches a critical threshold. The first-passage time (FPT) to this threshold characterizes the timing of such events. In conventional single-step birth--death models, reaction waiting times are exponentially distributed and hence memoryless. In this setting, FPT noise exhibits a U-shaped dependence on the threshold, attaining its minimum near one-half of the steady-state mean. However, mRNA production and degradation often involve multiple sequential steps, leading to hypoexponential distributions of mRNA production times and mRNA lifetimes. Here, we investigate how molecular memory arising from multistep mRNA production and degradation affects FPT statistics in bursty gene expression. We calculate FPT statistics in the small-noise limit. Both multistep mechanisms reduce FPT noise, but through distinct mechanisms. Multistep burst arrival reduces mRNA copy-number fluctuations, thereby suppressing timing noise. Multistep degradation instead reshapes transient accumulation dynamics, reducing relative timing noise even when steady-state mRNA noise increases. The threshold that minimizes FPT noise remains close to one-half of the steady-state mean, except for multistep burst arrival with constant burst sizes, for which it shifts to lower values. Stochastic simulations support the analytical predictions within their regimes of validity and show that these qualitative conclusions persist beyond the small-noise approximation.

</details>

### [88] [Magnetic tuning of odd elastic instabilities in chiral active crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21876)
*Umberto Marini Bettolo Marconi,Lorenzo Caprini*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究处于外磁场中的奇活动晶体（odd active crystal）的动力学性质，发现磁场与奇弹性横向力的耦合会在零波矢处诱发出独特的长期不稳定性，并可用于调控集体振荡、产生异常点（exceptional points）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨惯性动力学单元在外磁场洛伦兹力（Lorentz force）与活动手性（active chirality）驱动的奇弹性（odd elasticity）共同作用下，会产生何种非平庸的集体动力学行为，并寻找调控高密度活性体系集体现象的手段。

Method: 建立包含惯性动力学与洛伦兹力的奇活动晶体理论模型，解析分析磁场与横向力（transverse forces）耦合对长波不稳定性及有限波矢动力学不稳定性的影响，并给出位移谱、静态结构因子（static structure factor）与中间散射函数（intermediate scattering function）的解析预言。

Result: 磁场与奇弹性横向力的相互作用在零波矢处产生独特的长期不稳定性，该不稳定性在非磁弹性晶体与处于磁场的被动晶体中均不存在；磁场还能调节集体振荡，使异常点（exceptional points）出现并调控有限波矢处由奇弹性引起的动力学不稳定性。

Conclusion: 磁场可作为调控颗粒或胶体旋转体（spinners）等高密度体系集体现象的有力工具，并为位移谱与散射函数提供可检验的理论预测。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical properties of an odd active crystal whose elementary units follow an inertial dynamics and experience a Lorentz force induced by an external magnetic field. The continuous injection of energy associated with active chirality gives rise to odd elasticity, manifested through transverse forces between the crystal constituents. We show that the interplay between the magnetic field and these transverse forces produces a distinctive long-wavelength instability at vanishing wavevector, which is absent in both non-magnetic elastic crystals and passive crystals subjected to a magnetic field. Moreover, the magnetic field provides a means of tuning the collective oscillations of the crystal, enabling the emergence of exceptional points and tuning the dynamical instability induced by odd elasticity at finite wavevectors. Our analytical results yield explicit predictions for the displacement spectrum, static structure factor, and intermediate scattering function. These findings suggest that magnetic fields could provide a powerful tool for controlling collective phenomena in high-density systems of granular or colloidal spinners.

</details>


### [89] [Apparent contact angle equation for a dielectric droplet on a conductive hydrophilic surface in an electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22073)
*Ren-Qiang Huang,Gao-Xiao Jiang,Zhong-Yi Li,Rui Ma,Chen-Xu Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文提出一种闭式（closed form）理论，将介电液滴界面近似为单参数截断椭球体，并利用基底镜像对称构造静电前因子，从而以极低计算成本重现BEM平衡态及表观接触角规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介电液滴（dielectric droplet）置于导电基底上，在未接触上电极的间隙施加电压时会伸长；已有数值方法（如边界元法）计算代价高，且缺乏能统一描述形状与接触角的解析框架。

Method: 将液滴界面近似为仅含单一形状参数的截断椭球体（truncated spheroid），基于跨基底的镜像对称（mirror symmetry）修正自由空间去极化（depolarization），得到无量纲静电压前因子，并对总自由能在电压斜坡上做最小化。

Result: 该理论与边界元法（boundary-element-method, BEM）平衡解在液滴高度与接触线半径上一致，含重力与不含重力情形均适用，计算成本可忽略；并给出能坍缩全部数值数据的显式表观接触角（apparent contact angle）方程。

Conclusion: 该构型表现为接触角固定而表观角随施加电压增大，与Young–Lippmann方程（Young–Lippmann equation）的预测完全不同。

Abstract: A dielectric droplet resting on a conductive substrate elongates when a voltage is applied across the surrounding gap without touching the upper electrode. We construct a closed form theory of the equilibrium shapes by approximating the interface as a truncated spheroid with a single shape parameter and obtain the dimensionless electrostatic prefactor that corrects the free-space depolarization, based on the mirror symmetry across the substrate. Minimizing the resulting free energy along voltage ramps reproduces the boundary-element-method (BEM) equilibria, both the droplet height and the contact-line radius, with and without gravity, at negligible computational cost. An explicit apparent contact angle equation is found that collapses all the numerical calculation data. The configuration shows a behavior of fixed contact angle and an increasing apparent angle with the applied voltage, which is completely different from that of the Young--Lippmann equation.

</details>


### [90] [Accounts on Synthetic Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22371)
*The Frontiers of Life Research Network,Sébastien Lecommandoux,Hamid Kellay,C. Axel Innis,Carole Lartigue,Joelle Amédée,Rosanna Le Scouarnec,Léonie Beaupère,Kevin Peyraud-Vicré,Paramita Chaudhuri,Seamoon Deb,Jitra-Marie Jittasevi,Chloé Manseau,Eloise Equy,Colin Bonduelle,Emmanuel Ibarboure,Elisabeth Garanger,Thomas Salez,Vandana Sharma,Antoine Aubret,Ulysse Delabre,Valérie Desvergnes,Jeanne Leblond Chain,Mikayel Aznauryan,Stéphane Arbault,Antoine Loquet,Yann Fichou,Anne Devin,Patrick Paumard,Sven J. Saupe,Amine Maarouf,Damien Coudreuse,Vivien Willems,Zi Lin,Eleni Kopsini,Esra Baydar,Jose Fojo,Emmanuel Joseph,Cristian Villalobos-Concha,Laura Alvarez,Jacques Leng,Thomas Beneyton,Etienne Ducrot,Aurore Dupin,Nicolas Martin,Hugo De Oliveira,Harald Wodrich,Bastien Lambert,Laure Beven,Pierre Nassoy,Jean-Christophe Baret*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文介绍波尔多大学“生命前沿（Frontiers of Life）”研究网络以构建合成细胞（synthetic cell）为起点、对生命系统进行重新工程化（re-engineering）的动机与四年间所研究的基本构建单元（building blocks）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于从零构建细胞（build a cell from scratch），以此作为理解生命系统的实验路径，并可能孕育下一代生物技术（biotechnologies）。

Method: 以研究网络形式组织，围绕合成细胞的若干基本构建单元开展研究，将细胞重构同时作为方法论（methodology）与实验手段（experimental approaches）。

Result: 过去四年中已针对若干基本构建单元开展工作；合成细胞的最终目标仍属长期目标（long-term target），尚未实现。

Conclusion: 重建活细胞可加深对生命系统的理解，并具有发展成为下一代生物技术的潜力。

Abstract: Frontiers of Life is a research network of the University of Bordeaux focused on the re-engineering of living systems, starting from the construction of a synthetic cell. In this article, we present our motivation to build a cell from scratch and the elementary building blocks we worked on over the past four years. While the final objective regarding the construction of a synthetic cell remains a long-term target, the reconstruction of living cells provides both a methodology and experimental approaches to improve our understanding of living systems with potential to become the next generation of biotechnologies.

</details>


### [91] [A Variational Description of the Adsorption Phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22642)
*Giovanni Barbero,Anca Luiza Alexe Ionescu,Ioannis Lelidis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种变分方法（variational approach），将固-液界面吸附（adsorption）建模为吸附质粒子（adsorbate particles, APs）与宿主液体的二元混合物（binary mixture），在连续框架下解析获得密度分布并导出吸附等温行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统吸附模型依赖局域、离散的位点结合动力学（discrete site-binding kinetics），难以描述界面附近的非局域流体结构与粒子-粒子关联（particle-particle correlations）。作者希望建立热力学基础的连续描述。

Method: 从APs非均匀分布密度 n(z) 引起的混合熵（mixing entropy）变化出发，构造含 ∇n 平方项与体压缩率项（bulk compressibility）的自由能泛函（free energy functional），将欧拉-拉格朗日平衡方程（Euler-Lagrange equation）线性化，在粒子数守恒约束与两平行壁间/半无限平面壁边界条件下解析求解 n(z) 与表面密度 σ。

Result: 当基底相互作用为短程且指数衰减时，计算得 σ 在 n_0 较小时与 n_0 成正比（σ∼n_0），在 n_0 较大时饱和为常数，与经典 Langmuir 模型一致。在存在局域表面能时，进一步给出锚定强度（anchoring strength）趋于零与极大两种极限下的有效吸附能（effective adsorption energy）。

Conclusion: 该连续变分框架为唯象吸附等温线（phenomenological adsorption isotherms）提供了热力学基础，并可作为调控实验软物质体系表面修饰的预测工具。

Abstract: The adsorption of particles dispersed in a liquid is analyzed introducing a variational approach. While traditional adsorption models rely on localized, discrete site-binding kinetics, we treat the system as a binary mixture consisting of adsorbate particles (APs) and the host liquid within a continuous framework. An expression for the variation of the mixing entropy arising from an inhomogeneous distribution of the APs, of density $n(z)$ is derived. The interactions between APs are modeled by a bulk free energy functional containing a term proportional to the square of the density, $\nabla n$, and a bulk compressibility term which exhibits a minimum at the equilibrium bulk density of APs, $n_0$, in the absence of adsorption. This approach offers the distinct advantage of capturing non-local fluid structures and particle-particle correlations near interfaces that discrete models overlook. The profile $n(z)$ is obtained analytically by linearization of the Euler-Lagrange equilibrium equation for a sample confined between two parallel walls under the constraint of particles number conservation. The surface density of adsorbed particles, $σ$ is then calculated for the simplified case in which the interaction with the substrate is short-ranged and decays exponentially into the bulk. For a semi-infinite sample limited by a planar wall, the analysis predicts that $σ\sim n_0$ for small values of $n_0$, whereas it saturates at a constant value for large $n_0$, in agreement with the predictions of the classical Langmuir model, thereby providing a thermodynamic foundation for phenomenological adsorption isotherms. In case a localized surface energy is present, an effective adsorption energy is derived in the limiting cases of vanishing and very large anchoring strength, offering a predictive tool for tailoring surface modifications in experimental soft matter systems.

</details>


### [92] [Universal scaling of brine migration in ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22765)
*Natanael Braos-d'Urso,Nicolas Bain,Bruno Issenmann,Sylvain Deville*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种在受控温度梯度下捕获并监测冰内数百个移动卤水包裹体（brine inclusions）的实验方法，量化其迁移动力学，证实除冰前沿附近外迁移符合溶质扩散限制（solute diffusion-limited）机制，并给出普适标度（universal scaling）下的主曲线（master curve）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰中的移动卤水包裹体迁移影响盐分再分配、微观结构与输运，其运动通常被视为扩散限制过程，但实验证据稀少且不足，原因是迁移缓慢、可控的包裹体接种（controlled inclusion seeding）困难。

Method: 引入实验方法在受控温度梯度下捕获并监测冰中数百个包裹体；采用四种溶质、三种施加梯度以及初始浓度跨越两个数量级的条件，在广泛条件下量化迁移动力学。

Result: 除接近冰前沿（ice front）处外，包裹体迁移与溶质扩散限制区间一致；施加普适标度后，本研究数据与既有测量结果共同坍缩至一条主曲线。

Conclusion: 结果为描述冰内溶质输运（solute transport in ice）提供了通用框架，并支持扩散限制迁移的机制解释。

Abstract: Sea ice hosts mobile brine inclusions whose migration influences salt redistribution, microstructure and transport within the ice. Although their motion under temperature gradient is commonly described as a diffusion-limited process, experimental evidence has remained rare and insufficient because migration is slow and controlled inclusion seeding is challenging. Here we introduce an experimental method to trap and monitor hundreds of inclusions in ice under controlled temperature gradients. Using four solutes, three imposed gradients and two order of magnitude in initial concentration, we quantify migration dynamics over a broad range of conditions. We find that, except close to the ice front, inclusion migration is consistent with a regime limited by solute diffusion. After applying a universal scaling, both our data and previous measurements collapse onto a master curve, providing a general framework for describing solute transport in ice.

</details>


### [93] [Quasiconformal geometry and elastic anisotropy in liquid crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22978)
*Timothy J Atherton*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该摘要提出，向列相液晶（nematic liquid crystals）中splay与bend弹性常数不等所导致的指向矢依赖各向异性几何具有拟共形（quasiconformal）结构，其Beltrami系数的模由弹性常数固定、相位依赖指向矢相对于含SO(2)自旋联络的旋转标架的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面向列相液晶中splay与bend弹性常数不等，使几何呈现指向矢依赖的各向异性；既有共形（conformal）构造主要适用于各向同性缺陷，缺乏描述此类各向异性的更一般几何语言。

Method: 将各向异性几何表述为拟共形映射（quasiconformal map），引入其Beltrami系数（模由弹性常数确定、相位由指向矢相对兼容旋转标架决定，并涉及SO(2)自旋联络（spin connection））；针对混合排列薄膜（hybrid-aligned film）与孤立缺陷织构构造显式映射。

Result: 给出了混合排列薄膜及孤立缺陷织构的显式拟共形映射，将各向同性缺陷的共形构造推广至各向异性情形；表明经典Dzyaloshinskii缺陷解可理解为在辅助参考锥上作拟共形畸变、再共形投影到物理空间；受限+2缺陷的数值模拟得到的核形态与拟共形几何一致。

Conclusion: 拟共形映射为液晶及其他材料中的各向异性提供了更广泛的几何语言，并为经典Dzyaloshinskii缺陷解提供了新的几何解读。

Abstract: Unequal splay and bend elastic constants endow planar nematic liquid crystals with a director-dependent anisotropic geometry. Here, we show that this geometry is quasiconformal: the magnitude of the associated Beltrami coefficient is fixed by the elastic constants, while its phase depends on the director relative to a compatible rotating frame introducing an SO(2) spin connection. We construct explicit maps for a hybrid-aligned film and isolated defect textures, generalizing the conformal construction of isotropic defects. These results provide a new insight into the classical Dzyaloshinskii defect solutions, which are shown to define an auxiliary reference cone on which the defect texture is quasiconformally distorted before being projected conformally into physical space. Numerical simulations of constrained +2 defects reveal core morphologies consistent with the quasiconformal geometry. Our results suggest that quasiconformal maps provide a broader geometric language for anisotropy in liquid crystals and other materials.

</details>


### [94] [Indentation of pre-tensioned cortex-like fibre networks: tension, local stretching and the signature of defect architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23002)
*Shubham Agarwal,Olivier Noël,Martin H. Müser*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 单曲线压痕得到的“表观模量”具有歧义性：相同程度的网络弱化可使压痕力不变或降低十倍，取决于弱化区相对探针的位置。力反映的是探针与承力张力结构的耦合方式，而非弱化程度本身；检测局域弱化需空间采样与曲线形状分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压痕法（indentation）被广泛用于推断肌动球蛋白皮层（actomyosin cortex）等生物材料的力学状态，通常将单条力-压痕曲线简化为表观模量。但皮层是预张力化（pre-tensioned）、非均匀的纤维网络，该推断的可靠性未得到澄清，论文据此质疑单一模量标定能否反映真实弱化情况。

Method: 将受压力载荷、交联的纤维网络（fibre network）在刚性球压痕下的分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）与连续膜解（continuum membrane solution）相结合；并用两项拟合（two-term fit）F∝aδ+bδ^{3/2}表征力-深度响应，其中a对应全局张力（global tension），b对应随深度的硬化（stiffening）。

Result: 在固定弱化键比例下，弥散弱化（diffuse weakening）仅使力下降约1.5倍；探针下方的弱化团簇（weak cluster）使力下降8-12倍，因松弛团簇将探针与张力解耦。团簇偏离接触区时，力回到健康值的15%以内，源于两种相反效应相互抵消——整体层软化降低张力与力，而载荷经由探针下方完整网络传导导致局域硬化并抬升力。局部指数（local exponent）α=dlnF/dlnδ除团簇位于探针正下方外均随深度增大。

Conclusion: 局域弱化不能由单一拟合模量识别；需结合空间采样（spatial sampling）与曲线形状（曲线系数a、局部指数α）分析，因力水平所隐藏的信息仍保留在曲线形状中。

Abstract: Indentation is widely used to infer the mechanical state of biological materials like the actomyosin cortex. It reduces a single force-indentation curve to an apparent modulus. The cortex, however, is a pre-tensioned, heterogeneous fibre network. We show that this inference is ambiguous. The same amount of weakening can leave the indentation force unchanged or lower it tenfold, depending on where it lies relative to the probe. The force reflects how the probe couples to the tensioned, load-bearing structure, not the amount of weakening. We combine molecular dynamics simulations of a pressure-loaded, crosslinked fibre network indented by a rigid sphere with a continuum membrane solution. We represent the simulated force-depth response by a two-term fit, $F\propto aδ+bδ^{3/2}$, with $a$ reflecting the global tension, and $b$ the stiffening with depth. At a fixed fraction of weakened bonds, diffuse weakening lowers the force by a factor of about 1.5. A weak cluster beneath the probe lowers it 8-12-fold, because the slack cluster decouples the probe from the tension. When the clusters are displaced from the contact, the force returns to within 15% of the healthy value. Two opposite effects cancel. The clusters soften the whole layer, which lowers its tension and the force. But they also channel the load through the intact network beneath the probe, which stiffens it locally and raises the force. The shape of the curve retains what the force level hides. The coefficient $a$ reflects the softened layer when clusters beside the probe keep the force near the healthy value. The local exponent $α=\mathrm{d}\ln F/\mathrm{d}\lnδ$ increases with depth in every configuration except when a cluster lies beneath the probe, where the two-term form no longer applies. Detecting localised weakening requires spatial sampling and analysis of the curve shape, not a single fitted modulus.

</details>


### [95] [Spontaneous axis formation through defect fusion in active nematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23107)
*Mathieu Dedenon,Claire A. Dessalles,Aurélien Roux,Karsten Kruse*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作用全协变（fully covariant）活性向列流体动力学理论，证明可压缩球面活性向列体系中活性力可驱动四个分离的+1/2缺陷经两两融合转变为两个对跖的+1缺陷，从而定义体轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 起初为球形的胚胎需打破对称性以建立体轴，组织向列序中的拓扑缺陷（topological defects）被认为与轴形成有关，但活性材料中缺陷组织的物理机制尚不清楚。

Method: 在球面上构建全协变（fully covariant）的活性向列流体动力学理论，并保证其在被动极限下的热力学一致性（thermodynamic consistency）。

Result: 在可压缩（compressible）体系中，活性向列牵引（active nematic traction）驱动体系自发地从四个分离+1/2缺陷的平衡构型，经成对融合（pairwise fusion）转变为两个对跖的+1缺陷；所得状态定义了轴，极点处呈现星芒（aster）或涡旋（vortex）织构，取决于流向列耦合。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一种物理机制：活性力可重组拓扑缺陷，从而在球面上建立体轴。

Abstract: Initially spherical embryos break symmetry to establish a body axis. Topological defects in tissue nematic order have been implicated in axis formation, but the physical mechanisms underlying defect organization in active materials remain unclear. We study an active nematic fluid on a sphere using a fully covariant hydrodynamic theory that ensures thermodynamic consistency in the passive limit. We find that, in compressible systems, active nematic traction drives a spontaneous transition from the equilibrium configuration of four separated +1/2 defects to two antipodal +1 defects through pairwise fusion. The resulting state defines an axis, with aster or vortex textures at the poles depending on the flow-nematic coupling. These results identify a physical mechanism by which active forces reorganize topological defects to establish an axis on a spherical surface.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Micro-Macro Tensor Neural Surrogates for Uncertainty Quantification in Collisional Plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24205)
*Wei Chen,Giacomo Dimarco,Lorenzo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向 Vlasov–Poisson–Landau（VPL）系统不确定性量化（UQ）的降方差蒙特卡洛框架，用基于张量神经网络（tensor neural network）的代理模型替代昂贵的 Landau 碰撞项计算。该框架将高保真渐近保持（asymptotic-preserving）求解器与强相关的 VPFP/EP 代理模型耦合，并校准 VPFP 模型以提升相关性。数值实验显示相比标准蒙特卡洛显著降方差、以更少高保真样本获得准确统计量且墙钟时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体动理学方程（plasma kinetic equations）对模型参数与数据的微观扰动高度敏感，因此可靠高效的 UQ 对预测性模拟至关重要。但不确定性采样成本、高维相空间与多尺度刚性（multiscale stiffness）使传统数值方法在计算效率和误差控制上均面临严峻挑战；含碰撞情形下高维非局部碰撞积分与守恒性质进一步加剧了这些困难。

Method: 构建降方差蒙特卡洛（variance-reduced Monte Carlo）UQ 框架，将高保真渐近保持 VPL 求解器与低成本、强相关的代理模型（基于 VPFP 与 Euler–Poisson 方程）耦合，由神经网络代理替代多次 Landau 碰撞项评估。代理模型方面推广可分离物理信息神经网络（separable physics-informed neural network, SPINN），基于各向异性微观-宏观分解（anisotropic micro-macro decomposition）发展张量神经网络类，以降低速度矩成本、模型复杂度与维数灾难；并校准 VPFP 模型、设计小/大 Knudsen 数极限分别还原 EP 与 VP 系统的渐近保持 SPINN 以增强与 VPL 的相关性。

Result: 数值实验表明：相比标准蒙特卡洛实现显著方差缩减（variance reduction），在远少的高保真样本下获得准确统计量，墙钟时间（wall-clock time）更低，且对随机维数（stochastic dimension）保持稳健性。

Conclusion: 该工作为含碰撞等离子体动理学的 UQ 提供了一条兼顾效率与精度的路径，通过代理模型与多保真耦合有效缓解高维、多尺度与碰撞积分的计算瓶颈，并具备对随机维数的稳健性。

Abstract: Plasma kinetic equations exhibit pronounced sensitivity to microscopic perturbations in model parameters and data, making reliable and efficient uncertainty quantification (UQ) essential for predictive simulations. However, the cost of uncertainty sampling, the high-dimensional phase space, and multiscale stiffness pose severe challenges to both computational efficiency and error control in traditional numerical methods. These aspects are further emphasized in presence of collisions where the high-dimensional nonlocal collision integrations and conservation properties pose severe constraints. To overcome this, we present a variance-reduced Monte Carlo framework for UQ in the Vlasov--Poisson--Landau (VPL) system, in which neural network surrogates replace the multiple costly evaluations of the Landau collision term. The method couples a high-fidelity, asymptotic-preserving VPL solver with inexpensive, strongly correlated surrogates based on the Vlasov--Poisson--Fokker--Planck (VPFP) and Euler--Poisson (EP) equations. For the surrogate models, we introduce a generalization of the separable physics-informed neural network (SPINN), developing a class of tensor neural networks based on an anisotropic micro-macro decomposition, to reduce velocity-moment costs, model complexity, and the curse of dimensionality. To further increase correlation with VPL, we calibrate the VPFP model and design an asymptotic-preserving SPINN whose small- and large-Knudsen limits recover the EP and VP systems, respectively. Numerical experiments show substantial variance reduction over standard Monte Carlo, accurate statistics with far fewer high-fidelity samples, and lower wall-clock time, while maintaining robustness to stochastic dimension.

</details>


### [97] [DSADiff: Data Structure-Aware Diffusion for Crystal Structure Prediction via Mitigating Complexity- and Imbalance-Induced Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22050)
*Jiang Xiaobo,Dinghong Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出机制分析框架LAS与数据感知扩散模型DSADiff，用以识别并缓解晶体结构预测（CSP）中的生成偏差，在三个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散（diffusion）的晶体结构预测（Crystal Structure Prediction, CSP）方法在多样材料空间中存在生成偏差（generation bias），且缺乏系统性追踪性能差异来源的手段。

Method: 提出分层分析策略（Layered Analysis Strategy, LAS），识别晶体复杂度与元素组成不平衡两个影响生成难度的关键因素；进而提出数据结构感知扩散模型（Data Structure-Aware Diffusion, DSADiff），将关键因素转化为统一的生成难度表示，联合驱动特征建模与训练策略优化。

Result: 在包括 Carbon-24 在内的三个 CSP 基准数据集上，DSADiff 同时提升 Match Rate 与 RMSE，Match Rate 最高提升 9%，超越现有最先进（state-of-the-art）方法。

Conclusion: 该工作从生成偏差视角深化了对扩散式 CSP 模型的理解，并为设计面向复杂材料空间的低偏差生成模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Crystal Structure Prediction (CSP) is an important step in accelerating the generation and discovery of new materials. In recent years, diffusion-based CSP methods have made significant progress. However, existing models still suffer from generation bias when facing diverse materials spaces, while systematic approaches for tracing the sources of such performance variations remain limited. To address this issue, we propose LAS (Layered Analysis Strategy), a mechanism analysis framework that identifies crystal complexity and elemental composition imbalance as two key factors affecting generation difficulty and further reveals their relationships with model generation performance. Building on these findings, we further propose DSADiff (Data Structure-Aware Diffusion), a data structure-aware diffusion model that transforms the key factors identified by LAS into a unified generation difficulty representation and uses it to jointly drive feature modeling and training strategy optimization. This enables the model to adapt its representation and generation capability to samples with different data structures and difficulty levels, thereby mitigating learning bias across samples. Experiments on three CSP benchmark datasets, including Carbon-24, demonstrate that DSADiff consistently improves both Match Rate and RMSE, with a maximum Match Rate improvement of 9%, surpassing existing state-of-the-art methods and achieving new state-of-the-art performance. This work deepens the understanding of diffusion-based CSP models from the perspective of generation bias and provides a new direction for designing less biased generative models for complex materials spaces.

</details>


### [98] [Synthetic Crystal Data Generation for Diffusion Pretraining in Material Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22140)
*Aamod Varma,Shuyi Jia,Victor Fung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将基于扩散的预训练（diffusion-based pretraining）与合成结构生成相连接，以缓解材料性质预测中对昂贵 DFT 弛豫（DFT relaxation）近极小值结构的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散预训练虽无需为每个预训练结构提供能量、力、应力等量子力学标签，但仍依赖势能面（potential energy surface）局部极小值附近的结构，这些结构传统上来源于已知或已合成化合物的昂贵 DFT 弛豫，从而限制了预训练数据的规模与结构多样性。

Method: 构建合成数据生成流水线（synthetic data generation pipeline），融合扰动-弛豫（perturb-and-relax）、MatterGen 生成采样与原型结构枚举（protostructure enumeration）三种策略；弛豫环节以快速机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）替代逐结构 DFT 弛豫；并在随机初始化的 Orb-v2 骨干上比较坐标去噪控制与连续时间方差保持（VP）及 sub-VP 扩散目标。

Result: 在七个 MatBench 性质预测任务上，所有合成预训练条件均数值上优于随机初始化；固定 400 轮训练下将原型结构语料由 20k 扩至 165k，在多数任务上数值上匹配或超过 MP-20k 预训练，且第八个任务 mp_gap 纳入原型结构扩展研究。

Conclusion: 合成结构生成可有效缓解扩散预训练的 DFT 结构瓶颈；但需注意原型结构扩展协议同时增加数据多样性与优化更新次数，其效果反映数据与算力的联合扩展（joint data-and-compute scaling）。

Abstract: Diffusion-based pretraining has emerged as a powerful strategy for learning transferable representations in graph neural networks for materials property prediction without requiring quantum-mechanical labels such as energies, forces, and stresses for every pretraining structure. However, the method retains a critical dependency: it requires structures residing near local minima of a potential energy surface, which have traditionally been obtained through costly DFT relaxations of known or physically synthesized compounds. This reliance constrains both the scale and the structural diversity of available pretraining data, leaving large regions of crystal structure space unexplored. In this work, we connect diffusion-based pretraining with synthetic structure generation to reduce this bottleneck. We introduce a synthetic data generation pipeline combining three complementary strategies: perturb-and-relax, generative sampling via MatterGen, and protostructure enumeration. Where structural relaxation is required, we use fast machine-learned interatomic potentials instead of performing a new DFT relaxation for each generated structure. We further compare a coordinate-denoising control with continuous-time variance-preserving (VP) and sub-VP diffusion objectives using a randomly initialized Orb-v2 backbone. The source comparison covers seven MatBench property-prediction tasks, and an eighth task, \texttt{mp\_gap}, is included in the protostructure scaling study. Every synthetic-pretraining condition numerically outperforms random initialization on all seven source-comparison tasks. Scaling the protostructure corpus from 20k to 165k under a fixed 400-epoch schedule numerically matches or exceeds MP-20k pretraining on most tasks; because this protocol increases both data diversity and optimization updates, the result reflects joint data-and-compute scaling.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Search for the low-mass isotropic signature of QCD instantons in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV using the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22092)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS 在 LHC 上首次开展量子色动力学（QCD）瞬子（instanton）诱发事件的搜寻，利用 1.6 nb⁻¹ 极低瞬时亮度 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据，在二维分类变量上进行轮廓似然拟合，观测到低质量各向同性事件超出本底约 3.5σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬子是 QCD 杨-米尔斯（Yang-Mills）方程的半经典解，对应拓扑不同真空扇区之间的非微扰隧穿（non-perturbative tunneling），此前在 LHC 尚未被直接搜寻，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于重建带电粒子径迹构造角度变量，以区分低质量瞬子事件在静止系中预期的各向同性（isotropy）与最小偏差（minimum-bias）本底；本底由数据驱动技术结合重加权蒙特卡罗（reweighted Monte Carlo）模型预测，并在二维分类变量上做轮廓似然拟合（profile-likelihood fit）。

Result: 拟合给出低质量各向同性事件的超出，观测显著性（observed significance）为 3.5σ，尚未达到常规发现阈值。

Conclusion: 该分析首次展示了在 LHC 数据中搜寻 QCD 瞬子诱发事件的可行性，并提示存在约 3.5σ 的低质量各向同性事件超出，需更多数据进一步验证。

Abstract: This Letter presents the first search for QCD instanton-induced events at the LHC. Instantons are semi-classical solutions of the Yang-Mills equations of QCD and correspond to non-perturbative tunneling between topologically distinct vacuum sectors. The search is performed in 1.6 $\text{nb}^{-1}$ of very low instantaneous luminosity proton-proton collision data collected by the ATLAS detector at 13 TeV. Angular variables based on reconstructed charged-particle tracks are used to discriminate the expected rest-frame isotropy of low-mass instanton events from the minimum-bias background. The minimum-bias event background is predicted using a data-driven technique and reweighted Monte Carlo models. A profile-likelihood fit is performed in a two-dimensional classifier variable to extract a possible signal contribution, yielding an excess of low-mass isotropic events with an observed significance of $3.5σ$.

</details>


### [100] [AI-Assisted Analysis of CMS Open Data: Prompt J/psi Cross-Section Measurements in pp and Peripheral PbPb Collisions at sqrt(S)= 7 TeV and sqrt(sNN) = 2.76 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22723)
*Zhipeng Cui,Ziyu Wang,Gerry Bauer,Kai Yi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 研究评估了由人类物理学家监督的 AI 编码代理（AI coding agent）OpenAI Codex 在基于 CMS Open Data 的对撞机物理分析中的表现，完成了双缪子事例选择、拟合、蒙特卡罗模拟与系统误差评估，并在 pp 与 PbPb 数据上重测 prompt J/psi 截面，结果与已发表值量级一致但精度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨 AI 编码代理能否在人类监督下承担对撞机物理（collider physics）分析的完整流程，并检验其在真实公开数据（CMS Open Data）上的可靠性与失效模式。

Method: 让 Codex 从已发表研究与外部资料中获取物理与实验知识，在每一步人工审查下完成双缪子事例选择、二维不变质量与衰变长度拟合、蒙特卡罗探测器模拟及系统误差评估；使用定制全探测器模拟确定接收度与重建效率，并在两个 CMS 数据集上测试该流程。

Result: 在 sqrt(s)=7 TeV 的质子-质子碰撞中，测得 prompt J/psi 微分截面随横动量与快度的分布，39 个bin内重测与已发表 CMS 截面比值均值为 1.000、RMS 展宽 0.215；在 sqrt(s_NN)=2.76 TeV 外围（50–100% 中心度）PbPb 碰撞中，6 个区间内 fiducial 截面比值均值为 1.158、RMS 展宽 0.225。

Conclusion: AI 编码代理可在人类物理学家监督下完成端到端对撞机物理分析流程，但存在明确的失效模式（failure modes）；结构化人工审查对确保结果可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate the use of an artificial-intelligence (AI) coding agent, supervised by human physicists, for collider physics analyses using CMS Open Data. OpenAI Codex performs dimuon event selection, two-dimensional invariant-mass and decay-length fits, Monte Carlo detector simulations, and systematic uncertainty evaluation after acquiring physics and experimental knowledge from published studies and external references, with human review at each stage. The workflow is tested on two CMS datasets, with custom full-detector simulations used to determine acceptance and reconstruction efficiencies. In proton-proton collisions at sqrt(s) = 7 TeV, prompt J/psi differential cross sections are measured versus transverse momentum and rapidity. The ratios of remeasured to published CMS cross sections have a mean of 1.000 and an RMS spread of 0.215 across 39 bins. In peripheral (50-100% centrality) PbPb collisions at sqrt(s_NN) = 2.76 TeV, the corresponding prompt J/psi fiducial cross-section ratios have a mean of 1.158 and an RMS spread of 0.225 across six intervals. We identify agent failure modes and demonstrate the importance of structured human review in ensuring reliable results.

</details>


### [101] [Impact of errors-on-errors on the PDF determination from combined HERA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22891)
*Enzo Canonero,Glen Cowan,Georgia Brown,Hamed Abdolmaleki,Francesco Giuli,Alexander Glazov,Francesco Hautmann,Fredrick Olness,Pavel Starovoitov,Oleksandr Zenaiev*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文研究系统误差本身存在不确定度（称为 errors-on-errors）时，对基于 HERA 深度非弹性散射（deep-inelastic scattering, DIS）数据确定部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions, PDFs）的影响；采用 Gamma 方差模型（Gamma Variance Model）可将表观的数据-理论张力（data-theory tension）显著缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDF 拟合中系统误差（systematic errors）的标称大小通常被视为精确已知，但其本身可能具有不确定度，从而影响拟合结果与兼容性判断。作者希望量化这种 errors-on-errors 对 HERA 组合数据 PDF 确定的影响。

Method: 采用 Gamma 方差模型（Gamma Variance Model），将标称系统误差的平方视为对未知方差的 Gamma 分布估计，从而把 χ² 中常见的二次型冗余参数（nuisance parameter）约束替换为对数型约束。应用于 HERA 组合的包容性深度非弹性散射（inclusive DIS）数据，并复现 HERAPDF2.0 NNLO 拟合；给出两类应用：一是稳健性检验（robustness test），估计最大可能的 errors-on-errors 效应；二是先验给定 error-on-error 参数，模拟正式发表的 PDF 确定。

Result: 稳健性检验中，χ²/n_dof 由 1363/1131 降至 1306/1131，主要影响集中在低 Bjorken x（low Bjorken x）与低动量转移平方（low Q²）。在固定 NNLO 及 NNLO+NLL_x 重求和（resummation）下，配合更灵活的小 x 参数化（small-x parametrisation），也观察到类似幅度的 χ² 下降。

Conclusion: errors-on-errors 可解释 HERA 组合数据中相当一部分表观的数据-理论张力（data-theory tension），改善推断的兼容性（compatibility）并加宽拟合的 PDF 不确定度（PDF uncertainties），而基本不改变 PDF 本身或截面预言（cross-section predictions）。

Abstract: We study how the determination of parton distribution functions (PDFs) from the combined HERA inclusive deep-inelastic-scattering data is affected when the quoted sizes of the systematic errors are themselves treated as uncertain. We refer to these uncertainties informally as errors-on-errors and account for them using the Gamma Variance Model, which treats the squared quoted systematic errors as Gamma-distributed estimates of unknown variances. As a result, the usual quadratic nuisance parameter constraints in the $χ^2$ are replaced with logarithmic ones. We present two applications of the framework. In the first, a robustness test estimates the largest errors-on-errors effects that could be present in our reproduction of the HERAPDF2.0 NNLO fit, reducing $χ^2/n_{\rm dof}$ from 1363/1131 to 1306/1131, with the main impact concentrated at low Bjorken $x$ and low squared momentum transfer, $Q^2$. Comparable reductions are observed with a more flexible small-$x$ parametrisation, both at fixed NNLO and with NNLO+NLL$_x$ resummation. In the second, the error-on-error parameters are instead assigned a priori, as they would be in a PDF determination intended for publication. Taken together, the results indicate that errors-on-errors can account for a substantial part of the apparent data-theory tension in the combined HERA dataset, improving the inferred compatibility and widening the fitted PDF uncertainties without appreciably displacing the PDFs or cross-section predictions themselves.

</details>

Zangari,M. Zavertyaev,M. Zdybal,F. Zenesini,C. Zeng,M. Zeng,S. H Zeng,C. Zhang,C. Zhang,D. Zhang,J. Zhang,L. Zhang,Q. Z. Zhang,R. Zhang,S. Zhang,S. L. Zhang,Y. Zhang,Z. Zhang,J. Zhao,M. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo Gao,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验利用束-气成像方法对Run 2中√s=13 TeV质子-质子碰撞的亮度进行绝对刻度，测得顶点计数器（Vertex counter）刻度截面为57.61 ± 0.06 (stat) ± 0.52 (syst) mb，相对精度0.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于该数据集的截面测量所用的LHCb亮度刻度精度有限，需要通过绝对刻度（absolute calibration）提升精度，以支撑更精确的截面测量。

Method: 采用束-气成像方法（beam-gas imaging method），对基于重建主顶点（primary vertices）的统计量——顶点计数器（Vertex counter）——的刻度截面（calibration cross-section）进行测量。

Result: 测得σ_Vertex = 57.61 ± 0.06 (stat) ± 0.52 (syst) mb，相对精度为0.91%，相较此前该数据集所用的LHCb亮度刻度精度约提升一倍（约两倍改进）。

Conclusion: 该刻度显著提升了LHCb亮度精度；主要系统不确定度（systematic uncertainties）来自束-气顶点分辨率（beam-gas vertex resolution）认知不完善、截面的逐填充（fill-by-fill）离散以及横向束流轮廓拟合模型（transverse beam-profile fit model）的选择。

Abstract: An absolute calibration of the luminosity for proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV with the LHCb detector in Run 2 of the LHC is presented. The calibration cross-section of the Vertex counter, a statistic based on reconstructed primary vertices, is measured using the beam-gas imaging method to be $σ_\mathrm{Vertex} = 57.61 \pm 0.06\ (stat) \pm 0.52\ (syst)$ mb, corresponding to a relative precision of 0.91%. This result provides around a factor of two improvement in the precision of the LHCb luminosity calibration used in previous cross-section measurements with this dataset. The largest systematic uncertainties arise from the imperfect knowledge of the beam-gas vertex resolution, the observed fill-by-fill spread of the cross-section, and the choice of transverse beam-profile fit model.

</details>
