# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Parametric and Universal Thouless Invariants in Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21744)
*Leonardo Goller,Amartya H. Singh,Daniel D. Spiegel,Marco Venuti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无限链上平移不变单射矩阵乘积态（injective matrix product states）的连续族分类问题，在仿紧 Hausdorff 参数空间（paracompact Hausdorff space）$X$ 与紧 Hausdorff 对称群（compact Hausdorff symmetry）$G$ 下构造了取值于 $\check{H}^1(X;\widehat{G})$ 的相不变量（phase invariant）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于 $G$-对称 Thouless 泵（Thouless pumps）的 $\widehat{G}$ 相不变量仅适用于单一系统，缺少对由一般参数空间 $X$ 刻画的连续族的统一分类工具，故需推广该不变量以刻画族层面的拓扑相。

Method: 将相不变量构造为 $X$ 上某个万有 Čech 上同调类（universal Čech cohomology class）的拉回（pullback），该万有类定义在 $G$-对称平移不变单射矩阵乘积态的分类空间（classifying space）上。

Result: 所得不变量取值于 $\check{H}^1(X;\widehat{G})$，并在多种参数空间的具体例子中呈现非平凡（nontrivial）取值，说明该不变量能够区分不同的族相。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一类连续族矩阵乘积态相的分类框架，将 Thouless 泵的 $\widehat{G}$ 相不变量推广至参数化族情形，并通过实例验证了不变量的非平凡性。

Abstract: We investigate the classification of continuous families of translation-invariant injective matrix product states on an infinite chain, parametrized by a paracompact Hausdorff space $X$ and subject to a compact Hausdorff symmetry $G$. We construct an invariant for phases of such families, valued in $\check{H}^1(X;\widehat{G})$, that generalizes the known $\widehat{G}$ phase invariant for $G$-symmetric Thouless pumps. The phase invariant is constructed as the pullback to $X$ of a single universal Čech cohomology class on a classifying space of $G$-symmetric translation-invariant injective matrix product states. Several examples are given over various parameter spaces where this invariant is nontrivial.

</details>


### [104] [Existence of a third self-similar solution of the Yang--Mills equation in high even dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21767)
*Piotr Bizoń*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在任意偶数维 $d\geq 12$ 中，球对称 $SO(d)$ 杨–米尔斯方程除两个已知显式解外还存在第三个光滑自相似解；与奇数维情形不同，偶数维证明需处理非线性连接问题（nonlinear connection problem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作刻画了奇数维 $d\geq 11$ 的光滑自相似解存在性，而偶数维情形尚未解决；且偶数维中显式共振多项式的根计数方法不再适用，需要新的分析工具。

Method: 对球对称 $SO(d)$ 杨–米尔斯方程（Yang–Mills equation）的自相似解（self-similar profile）进行构造性存在性证明，核心是求解一个非线性连接问题（nonlinear connection problem）；在 $d=10$ 退化情形下给出形式论证（formal argument）。

Result: 在每一偶数维 $d\geq 12$ 中证明存在第三个光滑自相似解；$d=10$ 时（其中一个显式解退化为零解）形式地论证存在第二个非平凡光滑解。

Conclusion: 偶数维结果与奇数维存在性定理互为补充，共同给出 $d\geq 11$（奇）与 $d\geq 12$（偶）的光滑自相似解图景；偶数维的分类不能化归为共振多项式（resonance polynomial）的根计数。

Abstract: We prove that the spherically symmetric $SO(d)$ Yang--Mills equation admits a third smooth self-similar profile, in addition to the two known explicit profiles, in every even dimension $d\geq 12$. This complements the analogous existence theorem in odd dimensions $d\geq 11$ (arXiv:2610.36784). In contrast to the odd-dimensional case, where the classification of smooth self-similar profiles reduces to counting roots of an explicit resonance polynomial [Phys. Rev. D 114, 064021 (2026)], the even-dimensional proof requires solving a nonlinear connection problem. In $d=10$, where one explicit profile degenerates to the zero solution, we give a formal argument for the existence of a second nontrivial smooth profile in addition to the single nontrivial explicit one.

</details>


### [105] [A refined upper bound on the critical temperature for dilute Bose gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21916)
*Phan Thành Nam,Toan T. Nguyen,Izabella Stuhl,Yuri Suhov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文对具一般非负有限程相互作用（nonnegative finite-range interactions）的三维玻色气体（three-dimensional Bose gases），在稀薄区域（dilute regime）$a\rho^{1/3}\to0$ 下给出了临界温度移动的严格上界，其系数恰为 Huang（1999）预言的 $C_{\rm H}=3.523992\ldots$，改进了 Seiringer 与 Ueltschi（2009）的早期结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensation）临界温度随密度的移动是稀薄玻色气体的核心问题；Huang 于 1999 年预测了移动幅度 $\sqrt{a\rho^{1/3}}$ 前的普适系数，而既有严格结果（Seiringer–Ueltschi, 2009）给出的上界弱于此预言，需要收紧。

Method: 通过 Feynman–Kac 表示（Feynman–Kac representation）证明在所述温度之上关联函数（correlations）的指数衰减（exponential decay），并同时处理可积势（integrable interactions）与硬核势（hard-core interactions）两类相互作用。

Result: 证明了对一般非负有限程相互作用成立 $T_c(\rho)/T_c^{(0)}(\rho) \le 1+(C_{\rm H}+o(1))\sqrt{a\rho^{1/3}}$，其中 $C_{\rm H}=\frac{2^{7/2}\sqrt{\pi}}{3\zeta(3/2)^{2/3}}=3.523992\ldots$，即严格上界的系数与 Huang 的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将临界温度移动的严格上界改进至 Huang 预言系数，在稀薄极限下从上方确认了该系数的最优性图景；技术核心是借助 Feynman–Kac 表示在给定温度之上建立关联的指数衰减。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound on the critical temperature shift of three-dimensional Bose gases with general nonnegative finite-range interactions. In the dilute regime $aρ^{1/3}\to0$, we prove $T_c(ρ)/T_c^{(0)}(ρ) \le 1 + (C_{\rm H}+o(1))\sqrt{aρ^{1/3}}$, where \[
  C_{\rm H}=\frac{2^{7/2}\sqrtπ}{3ζ(3/2)^{2/3}}
  =3.523992\ldots \] is the coefficient predicted by Huang in 1999. This improves the earlier rigorous upper bound of Seiringer and Ueltschi in 2009. More precisely, we prove the exponential decay of the correlations above the stated temperature through the Feynman--Kac representation, for both integrable and hard-core interactions.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Born Geometries from Modular Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22041)
*Paolo Bertozzini,Roberto Conti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，量子化（quantization）与"热加倍"非交换Born几何（thermal-doubled non-commutative Born geometry）之间存在结构联系，其核心工具是Tomita-Takesaki模理论（modular theory）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为模代数量子引力纲领（modular algebraic quantum gravity program）中空间与几何概念的深层修正提供结构性线索，并探索"热量子时空假说"（thermal quantum space-time hypothesis）的可能性。

Method: 在复化与极化的正交辛空间（complexified and polarized ortho-symplectic spaces）上应用Tomita-Takesaki第一量子化模理论（first-quantized modular theory），揭示量子化与具有分裂四元数结构（split-quaternionic structure）的热加倍非交换Born几何之间的结构关联。

Result: 摘要未给出具体计算结果，仅指出该结构联系对模代数量子引力纲领具有启示意义，并暗示空间与几何概念可能需要进一步深刻修正。

Conclusion: 论文提出若干推测性方向，涉及Sakharov的引力诱导思想以及Boyle-Finn-Turok的CPT镜像宇宙学（CPT-mirror cosmologies）等；工作基于2026年4月底起在小范围共享的手稿"Modular Quantization and Non-commutative Space-time"。

Abstract: A structural link between quantization and "thermal-doubled" non-commutative Born geometry (with its split-quaternionic structure) is uncovered via Tomita-Takesaki first-quantized modular theory for complexified and polarized ortho-symplectic spaces.
  Implications for the modular algebraic quantum gravity program (arXiv:1007.4094) hint at further deep modifications of the notions of space and geometry (thermal quantum space-time hypothesis) waiting to be further explored.
  Speculative suggestions in the direction of A.Sakharov and L.Boyle-K.Finn-N.Turok CPT-mirror cosmologies will be mentioned, among several others.
  The present work is based on the manuscript "Modular Quantization and Non-commutative Space-time" (now on alphaXiv) that has been shared, with a selected number of researchers, since end of April 2026.

</details>


### [107] [Towards the componentwise three-soliton conjecture: cones, collisions, soft momenta and graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22194)
*Shi Zhenhua*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文（第二部分）研究Hirota三孤子条件（three-soliton condition）能否推出任意N孤子条件的分量式猜想，在无次数界的情形下证明了一系列限制性定理，并对若干分量族完成分类，从而在多项式图、Hietarinta列表中的三族及四阶方程等情形下验证猜想；猜想整体仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分提出的分量式三孤子猜想（componentwise three-soliton conjecture）断言：若偶多项式P（P(0)=0）在其色散簇（dispersion variety）某个重数为1的非超平面分量上满足Hirota三孤子条件，则在该分量上对任意N均满足N孤子条件。本文旨在为该猜想提供无次数界（no degree bound）的证据与结构性约束。

Method: 方法为对分量施加结构性限制并结合分类：考察分量方程最高齐次部分的非线性不可约因子、P在原点的阶与分量及二倍分量重数的关系、过原点分量的重数、缩放分量的极限传递性，以及平面分量Newton多边形（Newton polygon）的边方向；进而在Cubic图v=u^3、图v=q(u)（°q≥3）、Hietarinta列表中的三族分量上逐一分类满足条件的P；对Hietarinta四阶方程采用融合共振动量（resonant momenta）并在曲线上数零点的论证。

Result: 限制方面：若分量方程最高齐次部分含非线性不可约因子，则P必为二次型（quadratic form）的倍数；原点处阶与分量、二倍分量重数相关联；过原点分量重数为奇数；条件在缩放分量的一切极限下保持；平面分量的Newton多边形边仅取十种方向。分类方面：在v=u^3上四孤子条件恰在重数1时成立；在v=q(u)（°q≥3）上仅当q为奇三次或奇五次时P存在，故猜想对所有多项式图成立；Hietarinta列表三族中每个不可约成员在其色散簇上（至常数倍）唯一。三族均满足任意N的N孤子条件：两族属BKP hierarchy，对已知N≤4成立的Hietarinta四阶方程则给出完整证明。平移双曲线（translated hyperbolas）情形排除过原点者及次数≤8的全部P。

Conclusion: 图上重数为1的解均为已知的双线性方程（bilinear equations），新结论是不存在其他解；上述结果支持分量式三孤子猜想，但该猜想仍然开放（the conjecture remains open）。

Abstract: The componentwise three-soliton conjecture of Part~I states that if an even polynomial $P$ with $P(0)=0$ satisfies Hirota's three-soliton condition on a non-hyperplane component of multiplicity one of its dispersion variety, then it satisfies the $N$-soliton condition there for every $N$. We prove restrictions on components carrying this condition and classify the polynomials satisfying it on several families of components, with no degree bound. Restrictions: if the top homogeneous part of the equation of the component has a nonlinear irreducible factor, then $P$ is a multiple of a quadratic form; the order of $P$ at the origin is tied to the multiplicities of the component and of the doubled component; a component through the origin has odd multiplicity; the condition passes to all limits of the rescaled component, and the Newton polygon of a plane component has edges of only ten directions. Classifications: on the cubic graph $v=u^3$, where the four-soliton condition holds exactly in multiplicity one; on the graphs $v=q(u)$ with $°q\ge3$, where $P$ exists only if $q$ is an odd cubic or an odd quintic, so the conjecture holds for all graphs of polynomials; and for three families of Hietarinta's list, where each irreducible member is, up to a constant factor, the only such polynomial on its dispersion variety. All three satisfy the $N$-soliton condition for every $N$, so the conjecture holds for them: two belong to the BKP hierarchy, and for Hietarinta's quartic equation, known to satisfy it for $N\le4$, we prove it by fusing resonant momenta and counting zeros on its curve. For translated hyperbolas we exclude the one through the origin and all $P$ of degree at most eight. The solutions of multiplicity one on graphs are known bilinear equations; new is that there are no others. The conjecture remains open.

</details>


### [108] [Small-ball asymptotics of Gaussian matrix products: Open Fermi shells and the Grassmannian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22414)
*Jiahua Tian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文精确刻画了独立标准高斯矩阵乘积（实或复）的小球概率（small-ball probability）的领头项，形式为 κ ε^C (log 1/ε)^q，其中 C 为已知的多样性阶（diversity order），对数幂 q 由费米子填充规则给出，并用于级联多天线中断概率与深度线性网络贝叶斯证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作已知多样性阶 C，但对其领头系数与对数幂的精确结构缺乏完整刻画；实数情形的对数典范阈值（log canonical threshold）及其重数此前依赖显式奇点消解（resolution of singularities），希望给出无需该技术的证明。

Method: 对复情形，按矩阵尺寸构造阶梯上的费米子填充，最后占据能级的简并度 μ 与粒子数 h 给出 q=h(μ-h)，并将最后壳层（shell）的行列式在 Grassmannian Gr(h,μ) 的 Schubert 胞腔分次的 Plücker 坐标下展开，领头系数与 Schubert 簇的归一化次数（normalized degrees）成比例；对实情形用类似分析确定 Frobenius 与算子范数概率的阶。

Result: 复情形得到算子范数与 Frobenius 小球概率领头项 κ ε^C (log 1/ε)^q 的精确形式及 ‖P²‖_F 拉普拉斯变换的渐近；实情形证明 Frobenius 小球概率的领头项为 ε^{C/2}(log 1/ε)^q 且系数为正，算子范数概率同阶，从而给出实对数典范阈值及重数的新证明；并对至多含一个对数的壳层（含所有二因子乘积）给出实领头系数的闭式表达。

Conclusion: 结果统一了小概率渐近的组合（费米子填充、Schubert 胞腔）与几何（Schubert 簇次数）刻画，并给出一般实领头系数形式的猜想，与向量链（vector chains）的经典常数一致；应用于级联多天线信道高信噪比下的中断概率（outage probability）与深度线性网络（deep linear networks）的贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）。

Abstract: Let $P=A_N\cdots A_1$ be a product of independent standard real or complex Gaussian matrices of arbitrary fixed sizes. For complex entries we determine the exact leading terms, of the form $κ\,\varepsilon^C(\log1/\varepsilon)^q$, of the operator-norm and Frobenius small-ball probabilities of $P$, together with the corresponding asymptotics of the Laplace transform of $\|P^2\|_{F}$. The exponent $C$ is the known diversity order, while the logarithmic power follows a filling rule: when fermions are placed on ladders of states determined by the sizes and the last occupied level has degeneracy $μ$ and contains $h$ particles, $q=h(μ-h)$. The determinant of this last shell expands in Plücker coordinates graded by the Schubert cells of the Grassmannian $\mathrm{Gr}(h,μ)$, with leading coefficients proportional to normalized degrees of Schubert varieties. For real entries we prove that the Frobenius small-ball probability has an exact positive leading term of order $\varepsilon^{C/2}(\log1/\varepsilon)^q$ and that the operator-norm probability has the same order, which gives a proof of the known real log canonical threshold and its multiplicity that needs no explicit resolution of singularities. We evaluate the real leading coefficient in closed form for every shell with at most one logarithm, a class that contains all products of two factors, and we conjecture its general form, which agrees with the classical constant of vector chains. Applications include the outage probability of cascaded multi-antenna channels at high signal-to-noise ratio and the Bayesian evidence of deep linear networks.

</details>


### [109] [Extended Hamiltonian formalism for multicontact field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22783)
*Manuel de León,Rubén Izquierdo-López,Xavier Rivas,Narciso Román-Roy,Annamaria Villanova*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在多接触几何框架下为作用量依赖（Herglotz型）经典场论建立扩展哈密顿形式，将理论从限制多动量丛提升至带能量型坐标与典范多接触结构的扩展多动量丛，并证明其与限制形式在正则与几乎正则情形下的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作用量依赖（action-dependent）的Herglotz型经典场论的标准哈密顿描述局限于限制多动量丛（restricted multimomentum bundle），缺少显式的能量型坐标与统一几何结构。作者希望借助扩展多动量丛的多接触几何（multicontact geometry）给出更完整、且能自然容纳奇异情形的哈密顿表述。

Method: 在扩展多动量丛（extended multimomentum bundle）上引入哈密顿密度与其关联的哈密顿一形式（Hamiltonian one-form），定义扩展哈密顿多接触系统，并刻画可容许一形式；证明对截面（sections）、多向量场（multivector fields）与Ehresmann联络三种表述给出的Hamilton–de Donder–Weyl场方程相互等价；随后建立扩展形式与限制形式在正则（多接触）与几乎正则（前多接触）情形下的关系，后者需借助约束算法（constraint algorithm）。

Result: 所确定的一形式子流形可还原出限制形式；三种等价表述的场方程得到证明；扩展与限制形式在正则与几乎正则情形下的精确关系得以建立。示例中，作用量依赖Klein–Gordon方程（含telegrapher方程作为特例）与热方程（其接触化给出Burgers方程）验证了该形式。

Conclusion: 扩展多接触哈密顿形式为Herglotz型场论提供了统一几何框架，兼容正则与奇异（几乎正则）情形，并能覆盖Klein–Gordon、telegrapher、热方程与Burgers方程等具体模型。

Abstract: We develop an extended Hamiltonian formalism for action-dependent (Herglotz-type) classical field theories within the framework of multicontact geometry. While the standard Hamiltonian description of these theories is formulated on the restricted multimomentum bundle, we work instead on the extended multimomentum bundle, which carries an additional energy-type coordinate together with a canonical multicontact structure. In this setting, an extended Hamiltonian multicontact system is governed by a Hamiltonian density and its associated Hamiltonian one-form, and the submanifold they determine recovers the restricted formalism. We characterise the admissible one-forms and prove that the associated Hamilton-de Donder-Weyl field equations, stated equivalently for sections, multivector fields, and Ehresmann connections, are mutually equivalent. We then establish the precise relation between the extended and restricted formalisms in both the regular (multicontact) and the almost-regular (premulticontact) settings, the latter requiring a constraint algorithm. The formalism is illustrated with two examples: the action-dependent Klein-Gordon equation, which includes the telegrapher equation as a particular case, and the heat equation, whose contactification yields Burgers' equation.

</details>


### [110] [Exact Inversion of Relaxation from Rheometer Measurement Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22917)
*Yu Jiang,Takayuki Kurokawa,Ching-Lung Lin,Gen Nakamura*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅凭两组不同转动惯量下的输入-输出关系，精确反演流变材料松弛函数（relaxation function）的格式。方法通过Prony级数表示松弛函数，借助Padé近似（Padé approximation）恢复传递函数极点，并用数值实验验证了噪声对反演结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流变仪（rheometer）常用于测量流变材料的输入-输出关系以分析应力松弛。基于时间谐波力矩（time-harmonic torque）输入与角位移输出的关系反演松弛函数是核心需求，但仅靠单一输入-输出关系难以严格恢复材料参数，因此需要设计可行的精确反演方案。

Method: 将松弛函数表示为项数已知的Prony级数（含正常数项与具有不同松弛时间的项），构造增广系统（augmented system）把材料参数与特征根联系起来。传递函数（transfer function）为输出与输入Laplace变换之比，是Laplace变量的有理函数，其特征根即其极点；通过仅转动惯量（moment of inertia）不同的两组输入-输出关系，用Padé近似（Padé approximation）恢复传递函数极点，从而实现松弛函数的严格反演。

Result: 数值实验基于两组给定转动惯量对应的合成角位移（synthetic angular displacements）重建了松弛函数，结果表明测量噪声（measurement noise）会影响所恢复的特征根与松弛函数。

Conclusion: 仅通过两组转动惯量不同的输入-输出关系即可严格恢复松弛函数，并揭示了特征根与传递函数极点之间的明确联系；但反演精度受测量噪声制约。

Abstract: A rheometer is commonly used to measure the input-output relation for analyzing the relaxation of stress for rheological materials. Here, the input is a time-harmonic torque and the output is the angular displacement. This paper aims to propose an exact inversion scheme of the relaxation function from two input-output relations only having different moments of inertia. The relaxation function is represented by a Prony's series of known number of terms, with a positive constant term and terms with distinct relaxation times. The augmented system relates the material parameters to its characteristic roots. These roots are the poles of the transfer function. Here the transfer function is the Laplace transform of the output divided by that of the input, and it is a rational function in the Laplace variable. The poles of the transfer function are recovered using Pade's approximation. By measuring two input-output relations only having different moments of inertia, we can rigorously recover the relaxation function. Thus, we note a clear link between the characteristic roots and the poles. Numerical experiments reconstruct the relaxation function from synthetic angular displacements corresponding to two given moments of inertia based on our inversion method. The results show the effect of measurement noise on the recovered characteristic roots and relaxation function.

</details>


### [111] [Sobolev theory for the inverse problem of time-dependent density functional theory on the torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22942)
*Asbjørn Bækgaard Lauritsen,Mathieu Lewin,Jakob Oldenburg*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：给定足够正则的时间依赖密度，存在唯一外势使含该外势的薛定谔方程具有指定的一粒子密度；相较前作（要求空间解析性），本文放宽至有限 Sobolev 正则性，并借助"配方法"（completion-of-the-square）处理导数损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的相关工作（Lauritsen, Lewin, Oldenburg, arXiv:2609.08984）依赖 Cauchy-Kovalevskaya 定理处理导数损失（loss of derivatives），因而只能处理空间解析（analytic-in-space）的密度与势；本文旨在将正则性假设放宽到更一般的有限 Sobolev 正则性情形。

Method: 将问题改写为一个带导数损失的非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation with a loss of derivatives），并用配方法（completion-of-the-square）加以处理，而非使用 Cauchy-Kovalevskaya 定理。密度假设为时间上二阶可微（twice differentiable in time）、空间上具有限 Sobolev 正则性。

Result: 在给定正则性假设下，存在唯一的外势，使相应薛定谔方程具有预先指定的单粒子密度（one-particle density）；所得势在时间上连续，并在空间上具有限 Sobolev 正则性。

Conclusion: 本文是对作者近期工作的改进与精细化：以配方法替代 Cauchy-Kovalevskaya 定理来处理导数损失，从而将适用对象从空间解析的密度与势推广至有限 Sobolev 正则性的密度与势。

Abstract: We show that, given a sufficiently regular time-dependent density, there exists a unique potential such that the Schrödinger equation with this external potential has the prescribed one-particle density. We treat densities that are twice differentiable in time and of finite Sobolev regularity in space; the resulting potentials are continuous in time and of finite Sobolev regularity in space. Our method of proof is to rewrite the problem as a nonlinear Schrödinger equation with a loss of derivatives, which we in turn handle with a completion-of-the-square argument. This is a refinement of our recent work [Lauritsen, Lewin, and Oldenburg, arXiv:2609.08984], which handled this loss of derivatives using the Cauchy-Kovalevskaya theorem and treated analytic-in-space densities and potentials.

</details>


### [112] [Comment on "The eigenvalues and eigenfunctions of the toroidal dipole operator'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23006)
*G. Vaman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该评论（Comment）指出，Anghel 与 Dolineanu（Phys. Scr. 98 (2023) 015223）所研究算子 $\hat{T}_3^{(\theta)}$ 的谱（spectrum）是连续的（continuous）而非离散的（discrete），因此不存在可实验观测的能级劈裂（splitting of energy levels）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文声称该算子的谱为离散谱，并据此预言可被实验观测的能级劈裂；本评论的动机在于纠正这一关于谱性质的判断错误。

Method: 以评论（comment）形式对原论文关于算子 $\hat{T}_3^{(\theta)}$ 谱结构的论证进行重新审视与反驳，论证其谱应为连续谱。

Result: 算子 $\hat{T}_3^{(\theta)}$ 的谱是连续的，而非原论文所主张的离散谱；随之，该算子对应的能级劈裂并不存在。

Conclusion: 由于谱为连续谱，原论文所预言的可实验观测的能级劈裂（energy-level splitting）不成立，相关实验预期应予否定。

Abstract: We show here that the spectrum of the operator $\hat{T}_3^{(θ)}$ studied by Anghel and Dolineanu (Phys. Scr. {\bf 98} (2023) 015223) is continuous, not discrete, contrary to what is claimed by the authors of the paper to which this comment refers. Consequently, there is no splitting of the energy levels corresponding to this operator that could be observed experimentally.

</details>


### [113] [On the $φ^4_2$ theory on $\mathbb R\times S$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23032)
*Azam Jahandideh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文借助抛物型随机量子化（parabolic stochastic quantization），在 $\mathbb R\times S$ 上构造欧氏二维 $\varphi^4$ 量子场论模型，并讨论其 Osterwalder–Schrader 公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机来自单圈弦散射振幅（one-loop string scattering amplitudes），需要在几何变化（含退化极限）的一族环面上建立可控的测度构造。

Method: 先在不同几何的环面（tori）上构造有限体积测度；随后在复结构变化时建立一致的重整化（renormalization）与随机估计（stochastic estimates），覆盖环面趋于无限圆柱的退化情形，从而在圆柱上得到无限体积测度。

Result: 建立了随复结构一致成立的重整化与随机估计，并据此构造出圆柱上的无限体积测度，同时讨论其 Osterwalder–Schrader 公理满足情况。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了 $\mathbb R\times S$ 上二维 $\varphi^4$ 模型的欧氏量子场论实现，其一致性依赖跨几何（含退化极限）的均匀估计。

Abstract: We present a construction of the Euclidean two-dimensional $φ^4$ quantum field theory model on $\mathbb R\times S$ using parabolic stochastic quantization and discuss the Osterwalder-Schrader axioms. Motivated by one-loop string scattering amplitudes, we first construct finite-volume measures on a family of tori with varying geometry. To construct an infinite-volume measure on the cylinder, we establish uniform renormalization and stochastic estimates as the complex structure varies, including the degenerating regime in which the torus approaches an infinite cylinder.

</details>


### [114] [Unique continuation for the Pauli-Fierz Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23062)
*Mihály A. Csirik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Pauli-Fierz哈密顿量本征态（视为以自旋-光子空间为值的电子位形函数）的唯一延拓性质，并在正耦合强度下给出了Hohenberg-Kohn定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将唯一延拓（unique continuation）技术推广至耦合量子电动力学的多体系统，从而为含光子场的密度泛函理论奠定严格基础。

Method: 在较弱的正则性假设下（矢量势属于 $L^6_\mathrm{loc}$，其散度、旋度及标量势属于某 $p>2$ 的 $L^p_\mathrm{loc}$）证明Pauli-Fierz本征态的唯一延拓性质，并据此推导Hohenberg-Kohn型结论。

Result: 自旋求和后的基态密度（spin-summed ground-state density）与合并电流密度（combined current density）在相差一个规范变换（gauge transformation）与一个加性常数的意义下唯一确定外部势。

Conclusion: 结果表明唯一延拓方法可有效处理含量子化光子场与自旋的情形，并将Hohenberg-Kohn定理扩展至正耦合强度。

Abstract: We prove a unique continuation property for eigenstates of the Pauli-Fierz Hamiltonian, viewed as functions of the electron configuration with values in the spin-photon space. The vector potential may lie in $L^6_\mathrm{loc}$, with its divergence, curl, and the scalar potential in $L^p_\mathrm{loc}$ for some $p>2$. As an application, we prove a Hohenberg-Kohn theorem at positive coupling strength: the spin-summed ground-state density and combined current density determine the external potentials up to a gauge transformation and an additive constant.

</details>


### [115] [The Infinitesimal Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23086)
*Leonard Huang,Lara Ismert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在实基本 $C^{\ast}$-动力系统框架下建立协变 Stone-von Neumann 定理的无穷小版本，证明每个无穷小 Heisenberg 表示均为无穷小 Schrödinger 表示的直和，并给出位置与动量算子的显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作已建立协变 Stone-von Neumann 定理（Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem），对交换基本 $C^{\ast}$-动力系统的酉 Heisenberg 表示进行分类；本文旨在将其推广到无穷小（生成元）层面，并处理算子的定义域与可积性问题。

Method: 首先将 Stone 定理推广至 Hilbert $C^{\ast}$-模（Hilbert $C^{\ast}$-module），建立强连续单参数酉群与正则自伴稠定算子的一一对应；随后定义实 $C^{\ast}$-动力系统的无穷小 Heisenberg 表示与无穷小 Schrödinger 表示，构造二者之间的双向对应（称为“Heisenberg 可微-可积性”）。

Result: 证明无穷小协变 Stone-von Neumann 定理：实基本 $C^{\ast}$-动力系统的每个无穷小 Heisenberg 表示均为无穷小 Schrödinger 表示的直和；并给出位置算子与动量算子的显式描述及一个自然的公共不变解析向量核。

Conclusion: 该工作在全域酉形式与由正则自伴稠定算子表述的无穷小形式之间建立了严格的双向通道，完整处理了技术性的定义域与可积性问题，从而把协变 Stone-von Neumann 分类推进到无穷小层面。

Abstract: In a previous paper, the authors established the Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem, which generalizes the Stone-von Neumann Theorem in quantum mechanics and yields a classification of all unitary Heisenberg representations of an abelian elementary $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system up to unitary equivalence. In this sequel to that paper, we establish an infinitesimal version of the Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem in the setting of real elementary $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical systems. Firstly, we generalize Stone's Theorem to secure a one-to-one correspondence between the strongly continuous one-parameter unitary groups and the regular self-adjoint densely defined operators on a Hilbert $ C^{\ast} $-module. Secondly, we define the infinitesimal Heisenberg representations and the infinitesimal Schrödinger representation of a real $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system. Thirdly, we construct a one-to-one correspondence between the unitary Heisenberg representation and the infinitesimal Heisenberg representations of a real $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system, providing a rigorous two-way passage--which we call ``Heisenberg differentiability-integrability''--between the global unitary formulation of Heisenberg covariance and its infinitesimal formulation in terms of regular self-adjoint densely defined operators. This allows us to prove the Infinitesimal Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem: For a real elementary $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system, every infinitesimal Heisenberg representation is a direct sum of copies of the infinitesimal Schrödinger representation. Lastly, we obtain explicit descriptions of the position and momentum operators for a real $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system and construct a natural common invariant core of analytic vectors for them. All requisite technical domain and integrability issues are fully accounted for.

</details>


### [116] [Zero mode surface of Hermitian two-by-two matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23087)
*Andreas Sykora*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用欧几里得群协变性、酉共轭不变性与奇异值分解，将任意厄米 2×2 矩阵组的零模曲面（Clifford 谱）化归为正规形，并按 (b₁,b₂,b₃) 的三角不等式给出其拓扑分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零模曲面（zero mode surface，又称 Clifford 谱）的几何与拓扑结构此前缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在对厄米 2×2 矩阵情形给出完整分类。

Method: 借助 Dirac 算子在欧几里得群（Euclidean group）下的协变性、在酉共轭（unitary conjugation）下的不变性以及奇异值分解（singular value decomposition），在 m<3 时通过补零矩阵，将任意 m 元组约化为正规形 (b₁σ₁,b₂σ₂,b₃σ₃,0,…,0)，其中 b₁≥b₂≥b₃≥0；并给出 Dirac 算子行列式的闭式表达及临界点的显式求解。

Result: 零模曲面在 b₃=0 时为一个或两个点；否则当 b₁>b₂+b₃ 时为两个不相交的拓扑球面，当 b₁=b₂+b₃ 时为两个在一点相切的拓扑球面，当 b₁<b₂+b₃ 时为单个拓扑球面。拓扑由 (b₁,b₂,b₃) 的三角不等式（triangle inequality）决定，并可用海伦公式（Heron's formula）解释。

Conclusion: 任意厄米 2×2 矩阵组的 Clifford 谱均非空，其结构与拓扑完全由正规形三个参数的三角不等式关系刻画。

Abstract: We classify the zero mode surfaces, also called Clifford spectra, of Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices. Using the covariance of the Dirac operator under the Euclidean group and its invariance under unitary conjugation, together with a singular value decomposition, every $m$-tuple of Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices, after adjoining zero matrices if $m<3$, is reduced to the normal form $(b_1σ_1,b_2σ_2,b_3σ_3,0,\dots,0)$ with $b_1\geq b_2\geq b_3\geq 0$. Since adding a vanishing matrix only embeds the Clifford spectrum into a higher-dimensional space, it suffices to consider three matrices. In this normal form the determinant of the Dirac operator is given in closed form and its critical points are determined explicitly. The zero mode surface consists of one or two points if $b_3=0$. Otherwise it consists of two disjoint topological spheres if $b_1>b_2+b_3$, of two topological spheres touching at one point if $b_1=b_2+b_3$, and of a single topological sphere if $b_1<b_2+b_3$. The topology is thus governed by the triangle inequality for $(b_1,b_2,b_3)$, which can be interpreted with Heron's formula. In particular, the Clifford spectrum of any tuple of Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices is never empty.

</details>

### [117] [Combining Pulsar Timing data with $\texttt{MetaPulsar}$. The linearized timing model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23114)
*Wang-Wei Yu,Rutger van Haasteren*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MetaPulsar 是一个软件包，可将各脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）合作发布的 .par 与 .tim 文件直接合并为一致且确定性的联合数据集模型，兼容 PINT 与 Tempo2，并在两小时内完成此前需数年的数据组合流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合多个 PTA 的数据可提升对纳赫兹引力波（nanohertz gravitational waves）及脉冲星间相关信号的灵敏度，但国际脉冲星计时阵列（International PTA, IPTA）的历史数据组合项目往往耗时数年，制约了联合分析的时效性。

Method: MetaPulsar 读取各数据发布版本的 .par 与 .tim 文件，生成单一、一致、确定性的联合数据集模型，兼容 PINT 与 Tempo2 两套计时软件；并以四种方式验证：检验联合数据集的线性化计时解对 .par 参数值的依赖性、对 PSR J0613−0200 的残差建模、用双样本张力统计量（two-sample tension statistic）量化各 PTA 计时参数一致性，以及在 NANOGrav 9-yr、EPTA DR1（v2.2）与 PPTA DR2 直接合并数据上开展引力波背景（gravitational-wave background）搜索。

Result: 两颗脉冲星的联合数据集线性化解不依赖 .par 参数值（Shapiro 延迟参数为轻微例外），且无需人工重新拟合；PSR J0613−0200 的残差在计入内禀红噪声（red noise）与色散量（dispersion measure）过程后可被计时模型解释，各单 PTA 计时后验一致（视差除外）；对三个数据发布的直接合并在两小时内完成，恢复的共同过程振幅与谱指数与已发表的 IPTA DR2 结果一致。

Conclusion: MetaPulsar 使联合 PTA 分析可在各合作方发布数据后即行原型验证，而无需等待长达数年的重处理流程，显著缩短了多阵列数据联合分析的周期。

Abstract: Combining the data of multiple pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations increases the sensitivity to nanohertz gravitational waves (GWs) and other signals correlated between pulsars. The International PTA (IPTA) has combined such data, but historically these projects have taken many years. We introduce $\texttt{MetaPulsar}$, a software package that ingests the $\texttt{.par}$ and $\texttt{.tim}$ files of individual data releases, and turns them into single consistent deterministic models for the combined datasets, compatible with both timing packages $\texttt{PINT}$ and $\texttt{Tempo2}$. We validate the method in four ways. First, we show for two pulsars that the linearized timing solution of a combined dataset does not depend on the parameter values in the $\texttt{.par}$. A mild exception is formed by the Shapiro-delay parameters. No combination considered requires a manual refit. Second, for PSR J0613$-$0200 we show that the residuals are explained by the timing model if we take intrinsic red-noise and dispersion-measure processes into account, and that the single-PTA timing posteriors agree with one another and with the combination, except for the parallax. Third, we quantify the consistency of the timing parameters measured by different PTAs with a two-sample tension statistic, for every pulsar that is observed by multiple PTAs. Finally, we carry out a gravitational-wave background search on the direct combination of the NANOGrav 9-yr, EPTA DR1 (v2.2), and PPTA DR2 data releases, the constituents of IPTA Data Release 2 (DR2). The combined dataset is assembled in under two hours, and the recovered amplitude and spectral index of the common process are consistent with the published IPTA DR2 result. With $\texttt{MetaPulsar}$, a joint PTA analysis can be prototyped as soon as the individual collaborations publish their data, instead of after a years-long reprocessing effort.

</details>

### [118] [Ultrafast imprinting of fused ferromagnetic/antiferromagnetic skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21915)
*Coline Thevenard,Carenza E. Cronshaw,Romain Tahay,Daria Gusakova,Vincent Baltz,Richard F. L. Evans*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作利用热激光脉冲在铁磁/反铁磁双层（ferromagnetic/antiferromagnetic bilayer）中实现皮秒量级的反铁磁斯格明子（antiferromagnetic skyrmion）压印，并确定其阈值能量可由磁场调制从而实现确定性成核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁斯格明子是具有拓扑保护（topologically protected）特性的信息存储与太赫兹（THz）输运载体，但既有生成手段依赖缓慢技术或特定磁结构，限制其与自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）的集成。

Method: 在耦合的铁磁/反铁磁双层中施加热激光脉冲（thermal laser pulse），实现数皮秒内的反铁磁斯格明子压印；并分析启动融合斯格明子相（fused skyrmion phase）所需的阈值能量及其磁场调控。

Result: 识别出启动融合斯格明子相所需的阈值能量，并证明该阈值可被磁场调制，从而实现反铁磁斯格明子的确定性成核（deterministic nucleation）。

Conclusion: 与常规慢速场冷却（field-cooling）过程相比，激光诱导斯格明子压印可快数个数量级，为反铁磁斯格明子的超快（ultrafast）产生与操控提供了实验可行的途径。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic skyrmions are promising topologically protected entities for robust storage and THz transport of information. Their generation has so far relied on slow techniques or on specific magnetic structures, which limit their integration into spintronic devices. Here, we demonstrate the imprinting of antiferromagnetic skyrmions within a few picoseconds in a coupled ferromagnetic/antiferromagnetic bilayer with a thermal laser pulse. We identify a threshold energy required to initiate the fused skyrmion phase and show that this can be modulated by a magnetic field, enabling deterministic nucleation of antiferromagnetic skyrmions. Compared to conventional slow field-cooling processes to control antiferromagnets we show that laser-induced skyrmion imprinting can be orders of magnitude faster and provides an experimentally feasible route towards ultrafast creation and manipulation of antiferromagnetic skyrmions.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Interband-Coherence-Driven Inverse Faraday Effect in Intrinsic Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22320)
*Xingdi Wang,Haiming Dong,Kai Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文建立了本征石墨烯中逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect）的解析量子理论，指出螺旋度依赖的轨道磁化源于二阶密度矩阵的非对角零谐波分量，而非带内载流子环流或对角布局失衡，并给出时域表面磁化及杂散磁场的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆法拉第效应可将圆偏振光（circularly polarized light）的角动量转化为准静态磁化，是实现超快光控磁场的基本途径；但本征石墨烯中无质量狄拉克费米子（massless Dirac fermions）、电子-空穴对称性（electron-hole symmetry）与弱自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）使传统基于布居（population）的解释失效，亟需新的理论框架。

Method: 发展本征石墨烯中逆法拉第效应的解析量子理论（analytical quantum theory），基于二阶密度矩阵（second-order density matrix）分析螺旋度依赖的轨道磁化（orbital magnetization）来源，并推导有限脉冲（finite-pulse）动力学及二维源产生非局域杂散磁场（nonlocal stray magnetic field）的闭式表达式。

Result: 螺旋度依赖的轨道磁化来自二阶密度矩阵的非对角零谐波分量（off-diagonal zero-harmonic component），而非带内载流子环流（intraband carrier circulation）或对角布局失衡（diagonal population imbalance）；光螺旋度（optical helicity）、频率、脉冲包络（pulse envelope）与光束轮廓（beam profile）可各自独立调控该响应。

Conclusion: 本征石墨烯可作为二维狄拉克物质（2D Dirac matter）中相干轨道光磁（coherent orbital optomagnetism）的理想平台。

Abstract: The inverse Faraday effect converts the angular momentum of circularly polarized light into a quasistatic magnetization, providing a fundamental route to ultrafast optical control of magnetic fields. We develop an analytical quantum theory of this effect in intrinsic graphene, where massless Dirac fermions, electron-hole symmetry, and weak spin-orbit coupling suppress conventional population-based interpretations. We show that in intrinsic graphene, the helicity-dependent orbital magnetization arises from the off-diagonal zero-harmonic component of the second-order density matrix, rather than from intraband carrier circulation or diagonal population imbalance. We obtain closed expressions for the induced temporal surface magnetization, its finite-pulse dynamics, and the nonlocal stray magnetic field generated by the two-dimensional (2D) source. Optical helicity, frequency, pulse envelope, and beam profile independently control the response. Our results identify intrinsic graphene as an ideal platform for coherent orbital optomagnetism in 2D Dirac matter.

</details>


### [120] [Thermal radiation of nanowires and near-field interaction with substrates: exact computation and proximity approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22419)
*Jose M. Sojo-Gordillo,Ahmed Alwakil,Olivier Merchiers,Pierre-Olivier Chapuis*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作计算了单根纳米线的热辐射及悬浮硅纳米线与平面基底间的辐射相互作用，发现金属纳米线发射率特别高，硅纳米线与基底的近场辐射交换可比Stefan-Boltzmann预测高一个数量级，并对多种近似方法进行了基准检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂纳米物体的辐射传热难以评估：亚波长尺寸使其偏离宏观Planck不透明表面间理论框架，而小间距又引入近场辐射（光子隧穿）效应，导致纳米线热输运分析中热辐射长期被忽略。

Method: 通过计算单根纳米线的热发射以及悬浮硅纳米线（silicon nanowire）与平面基底之间的辐射相互作用，并在此构型下对多种常用或原创近似进行基准评估，包括近场中的邻近近似（proximity approximation）、厚度依赖的PA、静电近似（electrostatic approach）以及远场中的有效发射率（effective emissivity）。

Result: 金属纳米线相比其他材料纳米线可表现出特别强的热发射；孤立硅纳米线的热发射低于Stefan-Boltzmann预测，而与基底的辐射交换可增大一个数量级。

Conclusion: 纳米尺度辐射传热显著偏离宏观理论，近场效应可大幅增强辐射交换；所考察构型为不同层级的近似方法提供了可基准化的平台。

Abstract: Radiative heat transfer involving complex nano-objects is difficult to evaluate due to their sub-wavelength size, which induces deviation to macroscopic Planck's opaque surface-to-surface formalism, and due to the small distances involved, which lead to near-field radiative (photon tunneling) effects. As a result, thermal radiation has mostly been neglected in analyses of thermal transport in nanowires. Here, we report on calculations of thermal emission by single nanowires and on the radiative interaction between suspended silicon nanowires and flat substrates. We observe that metallic nanowires can be particularly emissive in comparison to wires made of other materials. While thermal emission from an isolated silicon nanowire is lower than Stefan-Boltzmann's prediction, the radiative exchange with a substrate can be larger by one order of magnitude. The considered configurations also allow benchmarking various levels of common or original approximations, including, in near field, the proximity approximation, the thickness-dependent PA, the electrostatic approach, and the effective emissivity in the far field.

</details>

### [121] [Quantum-classical dynamical density functional theory for solvation dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22786)
*Paul Bergold,Irene Burghardt,Francesco Di Maiolo,Cesare Tronci*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出过阻尼极性环境（overdamped polar environments）中热化与非平衡光谱的统一建模框架，将动力学密度泛函理论扩展至量子-经典耦合演化，并验证了非线性经典扩散对维持细致平衡的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为过阻尼极性溶剂中的热化（thermalization）与非平衡光谱（non-equilibrium spectroscopy）提供统一描述，弥补量子-经典混合动力学在保热平衡方面的不足。

Method: 将动力学密度泛函理论（dynamical density functional theory）扩展为量子-经典 Ehrenfest--Smoluchowski 方程，用算符值分布描述分子电子子系统与反应场坐标（reaction-field coordinate）表征的过阻尼极性溶剂的耦合演化；并与采用量子-经典线性扩散的 Liouville--Smoluchowski 模型对比。

Result: 线性扩散模型长期行为不稳定且无法达到稳态热平衡，而非线性扩散模型保持非增自由能与细致平衡（detailed balance）；应用于氯仿、二氯甲烷、二甲基亚砜中的双能级极性染料模型，由偶极-偶极关联函数得到吸收与时间分辨发射光谱，复现光谱展宽、动态 Stokes 位移（dynamic Stokes shift）及发射带宽演化，与三种溶剂的实验弛豫趋势吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该框架可统一处理过阻尼极性环境的热化与非平衡光谱，且非线性经典扩散是维持量子-经典动力学长期稳定性与热平衡的关键。

Abstract: We provide a unified modeling framework for thermalization and non-equilibrium spectroscopy in overdamped polar environments. This framework extends dynamical density functional theory to describe the coupled quantum-classical evolution of a molecular electronic subsystem and an overdamped polar solvent represented by a reaction-field coordinate. The nonadiabatic dynamics is formulated in terms of an operator-valued distribution obeying a quantum-classical Ehrenfest--Smoluchowski equation which exhibits nonlinear classical diffusion and non-increasing free energy, thereby retaining detailed balance. In contrast, a quantum-classical Liouville--Smoluchowski model with quantum-classical linear diffusion develops unstable long-time behavior and fails to reach a stationary thermal state. The method is applied to a two-level model of a polar dye in three different solvents: chloroform, dichloromethane, and dimethyl sulfoxide, each differing in polarity and dielectric relaxation time. Absorption and time-resolved emission spectra are obtained from dipole-dipole correlation functions, capturing the solvent-induced spectral broadening, the dynamic Stokes shift, and the time evolution of the emission bandwidth. The calculated solvent spectral response agrees well with the experimental relaxation trends of the three solvents.

</details>


### [122] [Symmetry-reduced k-point sampling and Wannier analysis with localized Gaussian orbitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21801)
*Johann V. Pototschnig,Jan Kloppenburg,Shaojie He,Chengze Zhou,Shiyang Wang,Sam Azadi,Hossein Mirhosseini,Jürg Hutter,Thomas D. Kühne*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出非正交高斯基（nonorthogonal Gaussian bases）下实空间稀疏矩阵与布洛赫态（Bloch states）变换的统一表述并在 CP2K 中实现，使对称性约化的布里渊区积分（Brillouin-zone sampling）能够同时重建矩阵与波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域轨道下的布里渊区取样需要在稀疏实空间矩阵与复布洛赫态之间做一致的变换；对称性虽可减少倒空间问题数，但仅凭标量积分权重无法重建密度与轨道分析所需的矩阵和波函数。

Method: 建立非正交高斯基下这些变换的统一表述并在 CP2K 中实现：以原子置换（atom permutations）、局域轨道旋转（local orbital rotations）与晶格相位（lattice phases）将不可约点解映射到完整对称轨道并保持重叠度规（overlap metric），配合直接与快速晶格变换接入自洽计算（含复轨道优化与有限温度占据）。

Result: 在硅与铝测试中，对称性将 64 点网格约化为 4 个代表点，同时晶胞能量或电子自由能与未约化计算的偏差在 1.5×10⁻¹² hartree 以内；规范一致的全网格轨道与点间有向重叠支持 Wannier90 导出、Wannier 构造与哈密顿量插值（Hamiltonian interpolation）。

Conclusion: 该框架将对称性约化的积分与局域轨道表象下规范一致的周期电子态分析统一起来，并在杂化交换（hybrid exchange）与多体应用中体现辅助基（auxiliary bases）与转移动量（transfer momenta）的额外作用。

Abstract: Brillouin-zone sampling with localized orbitals requires consistent transformations between sparse real-space matrices and complex Bloch states. Symmetry reduces the number of reciprocal-space problems, but scalar integration weights alone cannot reconstruct the matrices and wavefunctions needed for densities and orbital analysis. We develop a unified formulation of these transformations for nonorthogonal Gaussian bases and describe its implementation in CP2K. Atom permutations, local orbital rotations, and lattice phases connect irreducible-point solutions to full symmetry orbits while preserving the overlap metric. Direct and fast lattice transforms couple this construction to self-consistent calculations, including complex orbital optimization and finite-temperature occupations. Gauge-consistent full-mesh orbitals and directed inter-point overlaps support Wannier90 export, Wannier construction, and Hamiltonian interpolation. In silicon and aluminium tests, symmetry reduces a 64-point mesh to four representatives while retaining cell energies or electronic free energies within $1.5\times10^{-12}$ hartree of the unreduced calculation. Hybrid exchange and many-body applications illustrate the additional role of auxiliary bases and transfer momenta. The resulting framework connects symmetry-reduced integration to gauge-consistent analysis of periodic electronic states within a localized-orbital representation.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Using the 0i Einstein equation to break dark-sector degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22498)
*Camille Bonvin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文指出，现有宇宙学检验依赖不可观测量，导致引力修改、暗能量与暗物质三者的偏离相互退化、无法区分；作者据此构建一组仅依赖可观测量的三项检验（含通常未被检验的 $0i$ 爱因斯坦方程），联合使用时能够区分这三个物理来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近十年宇宙学观测开始对 $Λ$CDM 模型构成挑战；若这些张力（tensions）持续存在，则需回答偏离究竟源自引力修改、演化或成团的暗能量，还是超出冷暗物质（cold dark matter）范式的暗物质性质。

Method: 作者首先论证现有方法无法实现上述区分：当前检验依赖不可观测量，并通过假定特定方程成立或与特定理论类别的预言比较来建立联系，从而破坏模型无关性（model independence）并造成退化（degeneracies）。为此，作者构建了一组仅依赖可观测量的三项检验，并将通常不用于标准分析的 $0i$ 爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equation）与引力滑移方程（gravitational slip equation）和欧拉方程（Euler equation）结合。

Result: 在现有方法下，任何观测到的偏离都可归因于不止一个扇区（sector），因而无法判定张力来自引力、暗能量还是暗物质；而作者提出的三项检验在联合使用时可以区分这三个扇区。

Conclusion: $0i$ 爱因斯坦方程虽在标准分析中从未被检验，却为引力滑移方程与欧拉方程提供的信息增添了关键补充，从而使仅基于可观测量的联合检验具备区分引力、暗能量与暗物质的能力。

Abstract: Over the past decade, cosmological observations have started to challenge the $Λ$CDM model. If these tensions persist, they immediately raise one fundamental question: what new physics could be responsible for them? More specifically, do these deviations arise from a modification of gravity, from an evolving or clustering dark energy component, or from dark matter properties beyond the cold dark matter paradigm? In this paper we show that, with current methods, any observed deviation can always be attributed to more than one sector, preventing us from determining if the tension is due to gravity, dark energy or dark matter. The main reason is that current tests rely on unobservable quantities. These quantities are then either related to observables by assuming the validity of specific equations, or compared with theoretical predictions computed within a given class of theories. Both approaches inevitably break the model independence of the tests, leading to degeneracies between gravity, dark energy, and dark matter. To overcome this limitation, we construct a set of three tests that rely only on observable quantities and that, when combined, can distinguish between the three sectors. We show that the $0i$ Einstein equation -- which is never tested in standard analyses -- adds crucial information to that provided by the gravitational slip equation and the Euler equation.

</details>


### [124] [Probing Lorentz-violating electrodynamics with CMB polarization. II. Updated constraints and forecasts for the Simons Observatory and LiteBIRD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23082)
*Margherita Lembo,Luca Caloni,Martina Gerbino,Serena Giardiello,Giulia Gubitosi,Massimiliano Lattanzi,Luca Pagano,Nicolò Raffuzzi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该研究利用宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）偏振数据更新洛伦兹破缺电动力学（Lorentz-violating electrodynamics）的约束，在最小标准模型扩展（minimal Standard Model Extension）框架下将CPT-奇（CPT-odd）系数限制到约10^{-45} GeV量级、CPT-偶（CPT-even）系数限制到约10^{-32}量级，并给出SO与LiteBIRD的预测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子扇区的可重整CPT-奇与CPT-偶算符分别诱导宇宙双折射（cosmic birefringence）与圆偏振，因此CMB偏振可作为一种检验洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz violation）的探针；本文旨在利用最新多实验偏振数据更新并加强此类约束。

Method: 联合Planck、Atacama Cosmology Telescope（ACT）与South Pole Telescope（SPT）的温度与E模数据，以及BICEP/Keck、SPT-3G D1与SPTpol的B模测量进行约束；首次在该类分析中加入Planck PR4的EB谱，并纳入Cosmology Large Angular Scale Surveyor（CLASS）的最新圆偏振测量；最后对Simons Observatory（SO）与LiteBIRD的灵敏度进行预测。

Result: 在95%可信水平下得到|k^{(3)}_{(V)00}|<7.75×10^{-45} GeV与|k_AF|<3.82×10^{-45} GeV，为迄今所有探针中最强限制，主要由SPT-3G D1的B模数据驱动；Planck PR4 EB谱给出对非零|k^{(3)}_{(V)00}|的轻微偏好；CPT-偶系数为k_{F,E+B}<5.99×10^{-32}，纳入CLASS圆偏振数据后收紧约7%，这是CMB圆偏振数据首次参与该系数的约束。

Conclusion: 仅利用线偏振时，SO与LiteBIRD具有互补性：SO将CPT-偶界限收紧约30%，LiteBIRD将CPT-奇界限收紧40%–45%；若利用其半波片（half-wave plate）非理想性带来的圆偏振灵敏度，LiteBIRD可将CPT-偶界限改善一个数量级以上。

Abstract: We update the constraints on Lorentz-violating electrodynamics from the polarization of the cosmic microwave background (CMB). Within the minimal Standard Model Extension, we consider the renormalizable CPT-odd and CPT-even operators of the photon sector, which source cosmic birefringence and circular polarization, respectively. Combining Planck, Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT) and South Pole Telescope (SPT) temperature and $E$-mode data with $B$-mode measurements from BICEP/Keck, SPT-3G D1 and SPTpol, we constrain the CPT-odd coefficients to $|k^{(3)}_{(V)00}|<7.75\times10^{-45}$ GeV and $|\mathbf{k_{AF}}|<3.82\times10^{-45}$ GeV at 95% credible level. These are the strongest bounds to date from any probe, and are driven by the SPT-3G D1 $B$-mode data. For the first time in this type of analysis, we also include the Planck PR4 $EB$ spectrum, which yields a mild preference for a non-zero $|k^{(3)}_{(V)00}|$. For the CPT-even coefficient, we find $k_{F,E+B}<5.99\times10^{-32}$. Including the latest circular polarization measurements from the Cosmology Large Angular Scale Surveyor (CLASS) tightens this bound by about 7%. This is the first time that CMB circular polarization data contribute to constraining this coefficient. Finally, we forecast the sensitivity of the Simons Observatory (SO) and LiteBIRD. With linear polarization only, the two experiments are complementary, with SO tightening the CPT-even bound by about 30% and LiteBIRD the CPT-odd ones by 40 to 45%. Exploiting the sensitivity to circular polarization provided by the non-idealities of its half-wave plate, LiteBIRD could also improve the CPT-even bound by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [125] [A Universal Ratio Linking Cosmic Expansion and the Growth of Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23200)
*Anjan A Sen,Purba Mukherjee*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文指出在空间平坦的ΛCDM宇宙中，宇宙加速起始红移（$z_t$）与线性增长观测量$f\sigma_8$的转折红移（$z_{\rm pk}$）之间存在一个普适比值$R\equiv(1+z_t)/(1+z_{\rm pk})=1.1596$，不依赖于当前物质占比与整体归一化，可作为ΛCDM的新型一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ΛCDM检验多分别针对背景膨胀历史或结构增长，缺少将二者联系起来的、对参数与归一化不敏感的一致性关系；该文旨在寻找此类可检验关系。

Method: 在空间平坦ΛCDM框架下解析推导加速起始红移（onset of cosmic acceleration）与$f\sigma_8$转折红移（turnover）之间的关系，并进一步分析该关系向更广泛暗能量（dark energy）与修改引力（modified gravity）理论推广所需的条件。

Result: 得到普适常数比值$R=(1+z_t)/(1+z_{\rm pk})=1.1596$，其独立于当前物质分数（matter fraction）以及膨胀与增长的整体归一化（normalisation）。

Conclusion: 该比值构成联系背景动力学与增长动力学的ΛCDM一致性检验（consistency test），并给出了其在更广泛暗能量与修改引力模型中成立的条件。

Abstract: We identify a universal relation between the onset of cosmic acceleration ($z_t$) and the turnover ($z_{\rm pk}$) of the linear growth observable $fσ_{8}$. In a spatially flat $Λ$CDM Universe, these two redshifts obey $R \equiv (1+z_t)/(1+z_{\rm pk}) = 1.1596$, independent of the present matter fraction and the overall expansion and growth normalisations. This provides a new consistency test for $Λ$CDM, linking both background and growth dynamics. We further establish the conditions under which this extends to broader classes of dark energy and modified gravity theories.

</details>

### [126] [Why do continual physical learners need sleep?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22070)
*Nada Elmeligy,Nidhi Pashine,J. M. Schwarz*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究以无序弹簧网络（disordered spring network）为物理学习模型，发现持续学习会导致键刚度专一化与学习能力下降，并证明离线修复阶段（sleep-like phase）的必要性可源于物理学习力学本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠在无中枢神经系统的生物中亦普遍存在，作者据此提出核心问题：学习是否会在非生物系统中同样产生对睡眠的需求。

Method: 训练无序弹簧网络通过键刚度的局部调整完成一系列机械变构任务（mechanical allosteric tasks），并引入随机内部扰动使各键估计自身力学支配度（mechanical dominance）以局部调节刚度。

Result: 持续学习使单键日益专一化、逐步削弱获取新任务的能力；作者找到键支配极化（polarization of bond dominance）与剩余学习容量之间的精确代数关系，并发现主动学习所伴生的力会偏置局部测量、干扰修复。

Conclusion: 将学习与修复分离为类清醒（wake-like）与类睡眠（sleep-like）阶段可消除该干扰并提升持续学习表现，表明离线修复阶段的需求可从物理学习机制中涌现，无需生物组分。

Abstract: Sleep is widespread across the animal kingdom, including in organisms without a centralized nervous system. Does learning create a need for sleep, even without biology? To explore this question, we study a disordered spring network trained to perform a sequence of mechanical allosteric tasks through local changes in bond stiffness. We find that continual learning progressively reduces the network's ability to acquire new tasks as individual bonds become increasingly specialized. We identify an exact algebraic relationship between the polarization of bond dominance and the network's remaining learning capacity, providing a quantitative measure of this loss of adaptability. We then demonstrate that random internal perturbations allow individual bonds to estimate their mechanical dominance and locally adjust their stiffnesses to restore learning capacity. However, the forces associated with active learning bias these local measurements and interfere with restoration. Separating learning and repair into wake-like and sleep-like phases removes this interference and improves continual-learning performance. Our results suggest that the need for offline restorative phases can emerge from the mechanics of physical learning, even in systems without biological components.

</details>

### [127] [Exact solution of the two-dimensional one-component plasma on the truncated Beltrami pseudosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21652)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在截断的Beltrami伪球面（常负曲率、由两条平行线围成的曲面）上精确求解了耦合常数Γ=2的二维单组分等离子体（2D one-component plasma）。通过共形映射到平直圆柱面，配分函数化为不完全伽马函数之积，密度显式给出，且全部关联函数均为行列式形式；在固定形状下取大N极限得到的自由能不含ln N项，与欧拉示性数为零时的共形反常预言一致，并逐项对应Shen–Forrester的圆柱面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验常负曲率曲面上二维单组分等离子体的可解性，以及共形反常（conformal anomaly）对自由能常数项的预言。特别地，该曲面使高斯曲率积分与边界曲率积分分别非零却相互抵消，从而为欧拉示性数为零的共形反常预言提供一个非平凡检验平台。

Method: 利用共形映射（conformal map）将截断Beltrami伪球面映到平直圆柱面，粒子间相互作用退化为圆柱面势，曲率仅通过面积元进入。由此配分函数写为不完全伽马函数（incomplete gamma functions）的乘积，密度与关联函数可精确求出；在面积有界条件下以固定形状取大N极限，并将渐近展开与N≤4096的精确解及Γ=1、4、6的蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟进行对照。

Result: 自由能展开包含体相项(1/2)N ln N、与N成正比的项、平直硬壁表面张力（hard-wall surface tension）乘以总边界长度，以及一个形状相关常数，且不含ln N项，符合欧拉示性数为零的共形反常预言，并与Shen与Forrester的圆柱面结果逐项一致，其普适常数由两层边界层的求积给出。两壁处密度从相反方向趋于平直接触值nb ln 2，尽管周长相差因子cosh umax；蒙特卡罗模拟经精确Γ=2密度验证后，展示了边界层随耦合常数的演化。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了截断Beltrami伪球面上Γ=2等离子体的精确解，证实了欧拉示性数为零情形下共形反常对自由能常数项（无ln N项）的预言，并揭示了边界层结构与耦合常数的依赖关系。

Abstract: We solve exactly the two-dimensional one-component plasma at coupling $Γ=2$ on the truncated Beltrami pseudosphere, a surface of constant negative curvature bounded by two parallels. A conformal map onto a flat cylinder leaves the particles interacting through the flat-cylinder potential, with the curvature entering only through the area element. The partition function becomes a product of incomplete gamma functions, the density is explicit, and all correlations are determinantal. Since the area is bounded, the large-N limit is taken at fixed shape. The free energy, obtained through the constant term with every coefficient in closed form, contains a bulk term (1/2)N ln N, a term linear in N, the flat hard-wall surface tension times the total boundary length, and a shape-dependent constant, with no ln N term. This is the conformal-anomaly prediction for Euler characteristic zero, tested here on a surface where the integrated Gaussian and boundary curvatures are separately nonzero and cancel. The expansion coincides term by term with the cylinder result of Shen and Forrester, whose universal constants are quadratures of the two boundary layers. At both walls the density tends to the flat contact value $n_b$ ln 2, from opposite sides, although the circumferences differ by a factor cosh u_max. We compare the asymptotics with the exact solution up to N=4096, and Monte Carlo simulations at $Γ= $1, 4 and 6, validated against the exact $Γ= $ 2 density, show how the boundary layers evolve with coupling.

</details>


### [128] [Renormalization-Group Foundation of Critical Modes in Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21676)
*Yanhua Zhang,Mingmei Xu,Jinghua Fu,Yuanfang Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于重整化群理论的标度算符，构建了临界敏感涨落可观测量及其连通关联矩阵的完整族构造框架，并在二维Ising模型的自旋与能量扇区中验证了多尺度临界关联模式与有限尺寸标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界涨落的关联结构具有多尺度特征，需要一个系统化的理论框架来构造完整的临界敏感涨落可观测量族及其关联矩阵，以刻画这些关联模式。

Method: 以重整化群（renormalization-group）理论的相关标度算符（scaling operators）为基础，构造临界敏感涨落可观测量及其连通关联矩阵（connected correlation matrices），并分析其本征态与本征值；随后应用于二维Ising模型，分别构建自旋与能量算符对应的关联矩阵。

Result: 关联矩阵的本征态揭示出多尺度临界关联模式（multiscale critical correlation patterns）的层级结构，主导归一化本征值及其比值呈现有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）行为；二维Ising模型中能量扇区的本征态模式与主导本征值标度行为进一步支持该框架。

Conclusion: 该框架可系统性刻画临界涨落的关联结构并获得有限尺寸标度行为，且论文讨论了其向未知哈密顿量（unknown Hamiltonians）系统推广的潜在可能。

Abstract: Based on the relevant scaling operators of renormalization-group theory, we establish a theoretical framework for constructing complete families of critical-sensitive fluctuation observables and their connected correlation matrices. The corresponding eigenstates of the correlation matrices reveal a hierarchy of multiscale critical correlation patterns and the leading normalized eigenvalues and their ratios show the finite-size scaling behavior. Applying this framework to the two-dimensional Ising model, the correlation matrices associated with both spin and energy operators are constructed. The resulting energy-sector eigenstate patterns, together with the scaling behavior of the leading eigenvalues, provide further support for the proposed framework. Finally, we discuss its potential extension to systems with unknown Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [129] [Exact Dynamic Scaling of Quantum Jump Statistics in Monitored Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22029)
*Kazuki Yamamoto,Taiki Ishiyama,Toranosuke Matsubara,Kazuya Fujimoto,Tomohiro Sasamoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文发现监测多体系统中子系统内量子跳跃数目的涨落呈现新型动力学标度行为，具有扩散时间尺度及依赖子系统尺寸的反常超泊松系统尺度标度，并通过精确求解刘维尔自相关函数确定普适标度指数与标度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量诱导的纠缠转变等现象通常需要昂贵的后选择（postselection）才能实验观测，而量子跳跃统计（quantum jump statistics）提供了无需后选择研究此类测量诱导多体现象的另一途径，因而需要寻找测量结果本身的普适性。

Method: 针对连续监测下的自由费米子（free fermions），通过推导刘维尔自相关函数（Liouvillian autocorrelation function）的精确有限尺寸解，确定标度指数与标度函数；并在大测量强度极限下对相互作用费米子（interacting fermions）进行解析论证。

Result: 子系统量子跳跃数目的涨落表现出由扩散时间尺度（diffusive time scale）与反常超泊松系统尺度标度（super-Poissonian system-size scaling）刻画的新动力学标度，且标度行为依赖子系统尺寸；自由费米子情形可精确确定标度指数与标度函数，相互作用费米子在大测量强度极限下具有相同标度指数与标度函数。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个通过实验友好、无需后选择的观测量来分类测量诱导非平衡多体普适性（measurement-induced nonequilibrium many-body universality）的新框架。

Abstract: Quantum measurement has opened a new frontier in nonequilibrium many-body physics, revealing striking phenomena such as measurement-induced entanglement transitions, whose experimental observation typically requires costly postselection. Quantum jump statistics offer another route to studying such measurement-induced many-body phenomena without postselection, motivating the search for universality in measurement outcomes themselves. In this Letter, we discover that fluctuation of the number of quantum jumps within a subsystem exhibits novel dynamic scaling, characterized by diffusive time scale and anomalous super-Poissonian system-size scaling that depend on the subsystem size in monitored many-body systems. For free fermions under continuous monitoring, we exactly determine the scaling exponent and the scaling function by deriving an exact finite-size solution of the Liouvillian autocorrelation function. We also analytically show that the dynamic scaling with the same scaling exponent and the scaling function even holds for interacting fermions in the limit of large measurement strength. Our work provides a novel framework to classify measurement-induced nonequilibrium many-body universality through experimentally friendly, postselection-free quantities.

</details>


### [130] [Confinement Alters Energy Relaxation in Subrecoil Laser Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22436)
*Daiki Kanbe,Eli Barkai,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在亚反冲激光冷却（subrecoil laser cooling）中，简谐约束（harmonic confinement）通过改变相空间几何，定性改变长时间能量弛豫律，并给出最优散射率指数 α=4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光冷却既需降低动能，也需将原子限制在有限空间区域，但约束如何影响亚反冲冷却的长时间能量弛豫尚不清楚。

Method: 采用随机散射率模型（stochastic scattering-rate model）、数值模拟，以及可解析处理的重整近似（renewal approximation），考察散射率在低动量下按 R(p)∝|p|^α 消失时的渐近行为。

Result: 自由空间中稀有长俘获事件仅需小动量；在简谐约束下位置与动量沿相空间轨道耦合，使长寿低散射轨迹被限制在相空间原点附近，从而改变俘获时间统计并产生两次动力学交叉（dynamical crossovers），平均能量最快渐近衰减对应 α=4。

Conclusion: 即使局域散射律不变，空间约束本身也会改变渐近能量弛豫动力学，说明约束是亚反冲冷却中长期弛豫行为的关键因素。

Abstract: Controlling atomic motion through laser cooling requires both reducing kinetic energy and confining atoms to a finite spatial region. Here, we investigate how harmonic confinement modifies the long-time energy relaxation in subrecoil laser cooling. Using a stochastic scattering-rate model, numerical simulations, and an analytically tractable renewal approximation, we show that confinement qualitatively changes the asymptotic relaxation law. For a scattering rate that vanishes at low momentum as $R(p)\propto |p|^α$, the change arises because confinement reshapes the phase-space geometry of rare, long trapping events. In free space, such events require only small momentum. Under harmonic confinement, however, position and momentum are coupled along phase-space orbits, restricting long-lived low-scattering trajectories to the vicinity of the phase-space origin. This geometric constraint modifies the trapping-time statistics, produces two dynamical crossovers, and leads to an optimal scattering-rate exponent of $α=4$ for the fastest asymptotic decay of the mean energy. Our results demonstrate that confinement changes the asymptotic energy-relaxation dynamics even when the local scattering law remains unchanged.

</details>


### [131] [Bypassing the Entropy Production Speed Limit of State-to-State Transitions with Adaptive Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22712)
*Daniel B. K. Chu,Rémi Goerlich,Itamar Steinitz,Yael Roichman,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过引入基于无复位轨迹学习速率的自适应复位（adaptive resetting）机制，使胶体与冷原子中的态间跃迁以非局域跳跃方式实现，从而突破传统驱动速度—熵产（entropy production）基本界限，并在三种变换中验证了混合协议（hybrid protocol）超越扩散界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体（colloids）与冷原子（cold atoms）中的态间跃迁（如陷阱压缩、比特擦除）通常依赖驱动实现，其速度与熵产受基本界限约束，本文旨在寻找突破该界限的途径。

Method: 改变输运机制（transport mechanism）：采用自适应复位，通过非局域跳跃（nonlocal jumps）完成跃迁，其速率从无复位轨迹（reset-free trajectories）中学习；并提出将复位与切换至目标势相结合的混合协议。

Result: 混合协议在三种变换中均超越了扩散界限（diffusive bound）；同时推导出支配耦合扩散—复位动力学（coupled diffusion-reset dynamics）的速度极限（speed limit）。

Conclusion: 改变输运机制而非单纯优化驱动，可超越传统速度—熵产界限；自适应复位与混合协议为胶体和冷原子中的快速低耗态间跃迁提供了可行方案。

Abstract: State-to-state transitions in colloids and cold atoms (trap compression, bit erasure) are often achieved through driving, whose speed and entropy production are constrained by a fundamental bound. We show that this bound can be surpassed by changing the transport mechanism. Adaptive resetting enables transitions through nonlocal jumps, with rates learned from reset-free trajectories. A hybrid protocol, combining resetting with a switch to the target potential, surpasses the diffusive bound in three transformations. Finally, we derive a speed limit governing coupled diffusion-reset dynamics.

</details>


### [132] [Effects of probe size, shape, and probe-surface interactions in imaging multilayer island growth, coalescence, and formation of a smooth film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22758)
*Ismael S. S. Carrasco,Tiago J. Oliveira,Fábio D. A. Aarão Reis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过动力学蒙特卡罗（kinetic Monte Carlo, KMC）模拟与探针-表面相互作用模型，解释C60薄膜中AFM测得粗糙度峰值显著低于XRR结果的现象，指出针尖卷积效应可使SPM图像低估薄膜厚度不均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱相互作用衬底上沉积研究显示多层岛状生长及岛合并后平滑成膜，但C60薄膜中AFM粗糙度峰值显著低于X射线反射（X-ray reflectometry, XRR）结果，暗示存在针尖-样品卷积（tip-sample convolution）效应，需澄清其成因。

Method: 采用KMC模拟该类薄膜生长，并结合AFM各成像模式的探针-表面相互作用模型：非接触模式以探针宽度L对高度取平均；接触模式分别建模圆柱探针硬核相互作用及尖锐金字塔/圆锥探针；另用长方体岛生长与合并的几何模型进行验证。

Result: 非接触模式下，当L小于平均岛间距时粗糙度峰仍存在，但峰高随L增大而降低且位置略偏移；圆柱探针接触模式中峰高下降并向较小厚度显著移动，表现为岛合并加快；尖锐金字塔或圆锥探针仅在L较大时产生偏差，且此类针尖实验上难以实现。

Conclusion: SPM图像在岛合并沉积体系中可能低估薄膜厚度不均匀性；几何模型定量证实了接触模式与探针形状的影响；结果提示近期研究的C60薄膜在XRR与AFM测量之间可能发生了岛粗化（coarsening）与去润湿（dewetting）等生长后过程。

Abstract: Recent studies of deposition onto weakly interacting substrates have revealed multilayer island growth, followed by smooth-film formation upon island coalescence. However, for C60 films, the roughness peak measured by atomic force microscopy (AFM) was significantly lower than that obtained by X-ray reflectometry (XRR), suggesting tip-sample convolution effects. We investigate this problem using kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) simulations of such films and models of probe-surface interactions for AFM imaging modes. To model the non-contact mode, surface profiles are transformed by averaging heights over regions matching the probe-tip width. A roughness peak remains if the probe width L is smaller than the average island distance, but its height decreases with L and its position is slightly displaced. In the contact-mode model with cylindrical probes and hard-core interactions, the peak also decreases and shifts significantly to smaller thicknesses, indicating apparently faster island coalescence. Thus, SPM images under these conditions may underestimate film-thickness nonuniformity in deposits with coalescing islands. Instead, models of sharp pyramidal or conical probes with hard-core interactions produce deviations in the roughness peaks only for large L, although such sharp tips are difficult to realize experimentally. A geometric model of cuboid island growth and coalescence quantitatively confirms the effects of contact modes and probe shapes observed in the simulations. The results suggest that post-growth processes, such as island coarsening and dewetting, may have occurred in the recently studied C60 film between the XRR and AFM measurements.

</details>


### [133] [Polymer adsorption on vicinal surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22966)
*Nathann T. Rodrigues,Tiago J. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文通过大规模模拟研究接枝于邻晶面（vicinal surfaces）台阶边缘的柔性高分子链，发现宽平台下存在台阶吸附（SA）、脱附与平台吸附（TA）三相及一个新奇的多元临界点（multicritical point），窄平台下则仅存SA-TA交叉（crossover）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分子在平坦表面吸附已有较完善理论，粗糙表面亦有部分认识，但几乎平坦的台阶表面（stepped surfaces）这一常见且具实际意义的构型研究不足，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 对接枝于邻晶面台阶边缘的柔性高分子链进行大规模模拟（simulations），考察其相行为随平台宽度（terrace width）的变化，并检验台阶处扭结（kinks）的影响。

Result: 当平台宽度 𝒲 ≳ 3R_g^(2D)（R_g^(2D) 为TA高分子的回转半径）时，出现稳定的台阶吸附（SA）相，与脱附相、平台吸附（TA）相由连续相变线分隔，三线交于此前未预期的多元临界点；当 𝒲 ≲ 3R_g^(2D) 时仅存在SA与TA间的简单交叉，相图更简单；台阶扭结不改变上述行为。

Conclusion: 结果具有理论意义，并揭示了通过设计台阶表面上的吸附-脱附调控高分子构象、制备高分子纳米线（polymer nanowires）的潜在途径。

Abstract: While polymer adsorption is theoretically well established on flat surfaces --- and somewhat understood on rough surfaces --- the common and practically relevant case of nearly flat stepped surfaces remains poorly studied. Here, we address this gap via extensive simulations of flexible polymer chains grafted at the step edge of vicinal surfaces. For wide terraces, the polymer-step interaction yields a stable step-attached (SA) phase, alongside the desorbed and terrace-adsorbed (TA) phases, all of them separated by continuous transition lines that meet at a multicritical point previously unanticipated in polymer thermodynamics. This scenario is observed for terrace widths $\mathcal{W} \gtrsim 3 R_g^{(2D)}$, being $R_g^{(2D)}$ the radius of gyration of TA polymers. Simpler phase diagrams, absent this multicriticality, are found for $\mathcal{W} \lesssim 3 R_g^{(2D)}$, where a simple crossover exists between the SA and TA phases. The presence of kinks at the steps does not alter these behaviors. Besides their theoretical relevance, these findings unveil an interesting route to produce polymer nanowires and control polymer conformation via step attachment-detachment on designed stepped surfaces.

</details>


### [134] [Dynamical theory of the East model. II. A path-probability formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23008)
*Chong Zha,Yuliang Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于路径概率密度（path-probability density）发展 East 模型的动力学理论，采用逐级因子化近似得到持续函数的封闭方程组，数值结果在较宽温度范围内与蒙特卡罗模拟及 Zwanzig–Mori 双域基近似一致，但均未复现低温超阿伦尼乌斯标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: East 模型是研究玻璃态动力学中层级约束（hierarchically constrained）行为的关键模型，亟需一个系统且可严格化的动力学理论框架来完整描述其动力学。

Method: 以单自旋路径概率密度为变量，经拉普拉斯变换（Laplace transformation）得到代数差分方程；引入系统性因子化近似（factorization approximation），将相邻翻转区间（flip interval）的联合概率密度因子化为低阶联合概率密度，并具体实施一阶单区间因子化，得到四个持续函数（persistence function）方程的封闭方程组并数值求解。

Result: 计算所得时间关联函数（time correlation function）与弛豫时间（relaxation time）在较宽温度范围内与蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）及 Zwanzig–Mori 形式论的双域基近似（two-domain-basis approximation）吻合良好；但两种近似均未能复现低温下弛豫时间的精确超阿伦尼乌斯标度（super-Arrhenius scaling）。

Conclusion: 该形式论为 East 模型动力学的完整严格理论描述提供了合理基础，并为推广至其他系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical theory of the East model---a one-dimensional, hierarchically constrained kinetic Ising model---based on path-probability densities. This theory yields an infinite but closed set of equations for single-spin path-probability densities, which, after Laplace transformation, become algebraic difference equations. To render the theory tractable, we introduce a systematic factorization approximation in which the joint probability densities of consecutive flip intervals are factorized into lower-order joint probability densities. We implement the first-order single-interval factorization approximation, obtain a closed set of four equations for the persistence functions, and solve them numerically. The resulting time correlation functions and relaxation times agree closely with Monte Carlo simulations and with the two-domain-basis approximation of the Zwanzig--Mori formalism over a wide range of temperatures, although both approximations fail to capture the exact low-temperature asymptotic super-Arrhenius scaling of the relaxation time. The present formalism provides a plausible foundation for a complete and rigorous theoretical description of the dynamics of the East model and offers a promising route toward generalization to other systems.

</details>


### [135] [Memory kernel and closure for systems driven by intermittent forcing with power-law waiting times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23092)
*Marco Bianucci,Riccardo Mannella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文针对由间歇性控制变量（intermittent control variables）驱动的系统，推导了主方程记忆核（memory kernel）中G-累积量（G-cumulants）系数的精确分解，并给出幂律等待时间下的长时滞核闭合形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇噪声在更新步上使受控变量的主方程带有以噪声G-累积量为系数的记忆核，但该核如何由跳跃统计与更新（renewal）统计构成、在重尾等待时间下如何简化，尚缺乏精确刻画。

Method: 对幅值与持续时间独立的任意等待时间分布，推导这些系数的精确分解，将跳跃统计与更新统计分离；对均值有限的幂律等待时间分析长时滞极限，并用含加性与关联乘性噪声的精确可解模型进行验证。

Result: 长时滞下各阶系数（从布尔累积量（Boolean cumulants）到跳跃律的通常矩）出现交汇，长时滞核成为冻结确定性通量核（frozen deterministic flux kernels）以 ξ²p(ξ) 加权的叠加，并由此得到闭合形式的主方程。对 2<μ<3（噪声关联时间无限），二阶闭合误差在慢动力学下仍有限，而冻结流主方程给出精确的平稳均值；电报过程（telegraphic process）是二阶结论精确成立的唯一更新噪声情形。

Conclusion: 结论表明重尾等待时间使长时滞记忆核结构显著简化并可由冻结流通量核叠加精确描述，同时界定了二阶闭合的适用边界，仅电报过程情形下二阶结论严格成立。

Abstract: Many systems are controlled by intermittent control variables that take on a random value for a random time interval. In the presence of such noise at update steps, the main equation for the controlled variables has a memory kernel whose coefficients are the cumulants, also known as $G$-cumulants, of the noise, which are fully ordered in time. In the case of Poisson switching, these correspond to the Boolean cumulants of the jump distribution, and the kernel can be summed exactly. We derive an exact decomposition of these coefficients for any waiting-time distribution with independent amplitudes and durations, which separates the jump statistics from the renewal statistics. In the case of waiting times that follow a power-law distribution with a finite mean, it is highly probable that a long time interval will be covered by a single period, and we have shown that, as a result, the coefficients for long delays intersect in all orders, from Boolean cumulants to the usual moments of the jump law. The long-lag kernel thus becomes a superposition of frozen deterministic flux kernels, weighted by the expression $ξ^2p(ξ)$, and from this the corresponding main equation in closed form follows. For $2<μ<3$, where the noise correlation time is infinite, the second-order closure retains a finite error even in the presence of slow dynamics, whereas the main equation of the frozen flows in this limit yields an exact stationary mean value. The telegraphic process is the only case of noise with renewal steps for which second-order conclusions are exact. An exactly solvable model with additive and correlated multiplicative noise confirms these predictions.

</details>


### [136] [Color Through Time: Distributions, Sparsity and Dominance in Western Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23139)
*Ortensia Forni,Alexandre Darmon,Michael Benzaquen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究以大规模数字化画作为语料，在感知均匀色彩空间（perceptually uniform color space）内用固定调色板上的颜色分布刻画画作，借助 Jensen-Shannon 距离（Jensen-Shannon distance）与稀疏度（sparsity）、主导度（dominance）两个可解释描述符，揭示西方绘画色彩从暗沉土色向更广、更饱和色域的转变，并呈现"先拓宽用色范围、后增强色彩主导"的两阶段轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在以可量化的方式刻画西方绘画色彩的长期演变，并区分按时代划分的时期与艺术流派（artistic movements）在色彩特征上的差异，同时考察艺术调色板相对自然图像（natural images）的偏离方式。

Method: 将每幅画作表示为感知均匀空间中固定调色板上的颜色分布，用 Jensen-Shannon 距离比较不同历史时期与艺术流派的颜色分布差异，并引入稀疏度（反映用色数量的多少）与主导度（反映颜色分布的不均匀程度）两个可解释描述符；另外开展与自然图像的对照以及对生成图像的两项问卷调查。

Result: 结果显示，西方绘画色彩经历了从暗沉土色调向更广泛、更饱和色域的历史性转变，且自十九世纪起出现若干明显跃迁；按时期划分的演变较平滑，而艺术流派日益呈现超越时间顺序的独特色彩特征。在稀疏度—主导度平面上，绘画呈现两阶段历史轨迹：先拓宽用色范围，后提高色彩主导度。与自然图像比较表明，艺术调色板的偏离体现在所用颜色或颜色分布的广度与均匀度两方面；关于生成图像的两项调查则显示，和谐色彩平衡的判断集中于两种互补模式：多色呈强层级，或少色均匀分布。

Conclusion: 西方绘画色彩演变可由少量可解释描述符定量刻画，并呈现出与自然图像及时间顺序相区别的规律；关于和谐色彩平衡的判断集中于"多色强层级"与"少色均匀"两种互补模式。

Abstract: We investigate the evolution of color in Western painting using a large corpus of digitized artworks. Each painting is represented by its distribution over a fixed palette of colors in a perceptually uniform space. We compare the color distributions of historical periods and artistic movements through their Jensen-Shannon distances, and introduce two interpretable descriptors, sparsity and dominance, that quantify how many colors are used and how unevenly they are represented. We find a historical transition from dark, earthy palettes toward a broader and more saturated chromatic range, unfolding through distinct shifts from the nineteenth century onward. Periods evolve smoothly over time, whereas artistic movements display increasingly distinct chromatic signatures beyond chronology. In the sparsity-dominance plane, painting follows a two-stage historical trajectory, first broadening the range of colors used and later increasing color dominance. Comparison with natural images reveals distinct ways in which artistic palettes depart from nature, either through the colors they use or through how broadly and evenly those colors are distributed. Finally, two surveys on generated images show that judgments of harmonious color balance concentrate around two complementary regimes: many colors arranged in a strong hierarchy, or few colors used evenly.

</details>


### [137] [From nonlocal correlations to local symmetry breaking: Asymmetry dynamics of crosscap states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23224)
*Konstantinos Chalas,Pasquale Calabrese,Colin Rylands*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究表明，当对称破缺相干性（symmetry-breaking coherence）初始编码于长程纠缠（long-range entanglement）中时，幺正演化下“纠缠增长、局域对称恢复”的常规图像可被反转：淬火后纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）下降而纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）上升，初始对称子系统动态地产生局域对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规认知中，保持全局对称性（global symmetry）的幺正动力学（unitary dynamics）会使双分纠缠（bipartite entanglement）增长、初始破缺的对称性在局域恢复。本文旨在检验当对称破缺相干性初始存储于长程纠缠时，这一传统场景是否仍然成立。

Method: 研究对象为一类电荷形变交叉帽态（charge deformed crosscap states），其特征是对跖（antipodal）纠缠对，且纠缠强度与对称破缺程度可调。先分析其静态性质（纠缠剖面、纠缠不对称性、对称分辨纠缠（symmetry-resolved entanglement）及对称划分连续形变下的演化），再通过淬火（quench）至自由费米子紧束缚链（free-fermion tight-binding chain）研究动力学，并用描述对跖关联传播的准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture）进行定量刻画。

Result: 静态上发现类 Page 的纠缠剖面；纠缠不对称性在子系统小于系统尺寸一半时为零，而在更大子系统中呈对数增长。动力学上呈现非常规行为：纠缠熵减小而纠缠不对称性增大，初始对称的子系统在演化中发展出局域对称破缺，该行为可由准粒子图像定量解释。

Conclusion: 保持对称性的动力学可将全局编码的对称破缺相干性重新分配，使其在局域可及；初始纠缠结构是决定非平衡态下局域对称性命运的关键因素。

Abstract: Under unitary dynamics preserving a global symmetry, bipartite entanglement typically grows while an initially broken symmetry is locally restored. Here, we show that this conventional scenario can be reversed when symmetry-breaking coherence is initially encoded in long-range entanglement. We investigate this phenomenon in a family of charge deformed crosscap states, characterized by antipodal entangled pairs with tunable entanglement and symmetry breaking. We first characterize their static properties, finding a Page-like entanglement profile and an entanglement asymmetry that vanishes for subsystems smaller than half the system size, but grows logarithmically for larger subsystems. For symmetric subsystems, we also analyze the symmetry-resolved entanglement and investigate how it evolves as the subsystem is continuously deformed away from a symmetric partition. Following a quench to a free-fermion tight-binding chain, we uncover an unconventional dynamics in which the entanglement entropy decreases whereas the entanglement asymmetry increases, so that initially symmetric subsystems dynamically develop local symmetry breaking. This behavior is quantitatively captured by a quasiparticle picture describing the propagation of antipodal correlations. Our results reveal how symmetry-preserving dynamics can redistribute globally encoded symmetry-breaking coherence, making it locally accessible, and establish the initial entanglement structure as a key ingredient in determining the fate of local symmetries out of equilibrium.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Statistical Properties of the $q$-Deformed Monolayer and Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21675)
*Omar Pedraza,Erik Díaz-Bautista,L. A. López,Daniel O-Campa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究恒定外磁场下单层与双层石墨烯中 $q$-形变狄拉克振子（$q$-deformed Dirac oscillator）的统计性质，给出能谱、本征函数与概率流密度，并借助 zeta 函数技术与 Euler–Maclaurin 公式推导配分函数及热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察 $q$-形变（$q$-deformation）如何影响石墨烯中狄拉克振子的能谱结构与统计/热力学响应，并比较单层与双层体系的差异。

Method: 采用 $q$-形变产生与湮灭算符（$q$-deformed creation and annihilation operators）求解能谱与本征函数及概率流密度；双层情形下显式构造最低朗道能级（lowest Landau levels）；再利用 zeta 函数技术（zeta-function techniques）与 Euler–Maclaurin 公式推导配分函数（partition function），并在形变参数较小时计算主要热力学量。

Result: 在未形变极限下可回到常规朗道能级（Landau-level）结构；$q$-形变使能谱、概率流密度分量行为以及体系的热力学响应发生改变；单层与双层石墨烯表现出不同的热行为，反映其朗道能级结构差异。

Conclusion: $q$-形变对石墨烯狄拉克振子的能谱与统计性质具有可观测的修正作用，且其影响依赖单层/双层能级结构，凸显形变参数对统计性质的调控意义。

Abstract: We investigate the statistical properties of the $q$-deformed Dirac oscillator for monolayer and bilayer graphene in the presence of a constant external magnetic field. Using $q$-deformed creation and annihilation operators, we obtain the corresponding energy spectra and eigenfunctions, as well as their probability current densities, recovering the conventional Landau-level structures in the undeformed limit. For bilayer graphene, the lowest Landau levels are explicitly constructed within the $q$-deformed framework. The partition functions for both systems are derived using zeta-function techniques and the Euler-Maclaurin formula, allowing us to determine their main thermodynamic quantities for small values of the deformation parameter. Our results show that the $q$-deformation modifies the energy spectra, the behavior of the probability current density components, and the thermodynamic response of the systems. A comparison between monolayer and bilayer graphene reveals distinct thermal behaviors, reflecting their different Landau-level structures and highlighting the influence of the $q$-deformation on their statistical properties.

</details>


### [139] [The Kitaev model on finite hyperbolic PBC clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21697)
*Alberto Zorzato,Jiri Minář*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在有限双曲格点上构造并求解三色 Kitaev 模型，利用三角群（triangle groups）的有限商生成紧致亏格-g 曲面上的正则映射（regular maps）以施加一致的边界条件，并在各向同性点分析基态超选择扇区、Wilson 环、有限尺寸双 vison 能隙与物质费米子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在有限双曲格点上对三色 Kitaev 模型开展可控的有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling），需要一套自洽的格点生成与边界条件实现方案，并检验自旋液体（spin liquid）相特征在有限体系中的表现。

Method: 采用保向三角群（orientation-preserving triangle groups）的有限商构造紧致亏格-g 曲面上的正则映射，从中提取格点连通性、plaquette 结构、同调环（homology cycles）与 Wilson 扇区（Wilson sectors），从而实现一致的周期边界条件；在 {8,3} 与 {12,3} 格点上枚举至多 72 个顶点的商作为有限尺寸序列。

Result: 在各向同性点确定了基态超选择扇区（superselection sectors），量化其相对均匀 π 通量扇区的偏离，并表明所选通量背景依赖于商的几何；基态扇区简并完全由作用于 plaquette 通量与不可收缩 Wilson 环的物理格点对称性产生。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸双 vison 能隙（two-vison gap）不随体系增大单调减小，但在所考虑的适中尺寸下，各向同性点的物质费米子关联（matter fermion correlations）仍呈现预期的代数衰减（algebraic decay）。

Abstract: We construct and solve the three-colored Kitaev model on finite hyperbolic lattices with periodic boundary conditions. We describe a self-contained procedure which uses finite quotients of orientation- preserving triangle groups to generate regular maps on compact genus-g surfaces. From these, one can extract their lattice connectivity, plaquette structure, homology cycles and Wilson sectors while implementing consistent boundary conditions. On the example of {8, 3} and {12, 3} lattices, we enumerate a selection of quotients with up to 72 vertices, which constitute the discrete steps in a finite-size scaling analysis. For the isotropic point, corresponding to the spin-liquid phase in the thermodynamic limit, we identify the ground state superselection sectors, quantify their deviation from the homogeneous π flux sector and show how the selected flux background depends on the quotient geometry. We then discuss how the resulting ground state sector degeneracies are entirely generated by physical lattice symmetries acting on both the plaquette fluxes and non-contractible Wilson loops. Finally, we study the behaviour of the finite-size two-vison gap and matter fermion correlations.
