We find that the gap does not decrease monotonically with increasing system size, while one still recovers the expected algebraic decay of the correlations at the isotropic point, even for the considered modest system sizes.

</details>


### [140] [Magnon-Phonon-Polariton Optomechanics in a van der Waals Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21869)
*Jens-Christian Drawer,Vladislav Yu. Shishkov,Bo Han,Vita Solovyeva,Iva Plutnarová,Zdeněk Sofer,Christian Schneider,Anton V. Zasedatelev,Martin Esmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过共振泵浦-探测光谱研究悬浮CrSBr薄膜中激子-极化激元、磁振子与声子间的三体光力学耦合，测得约20 GHz频段内吸引型磁-声相互作用强度|V|/2π=43 MHz，主导致声子放大与磁振子向声子的单向能量转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状反铁磁体CrSBr中的自杂化激子-极化激元可参数化耦合相干磁振子与声子，但三者在同一体系中如何相互作用（尤其是磁-声耦合的强度与方向性）尚待阐明；该问题的回答关系到千兆赫兹频段（GHz range）量子相干换能（quantum-coherent transduction）的可行性。

Method: 采用支撑于自由悬浮的CrSBr薄膜、支持高Q值呼吸型声学声子模（breathing-type acoustic phonon modes）的结构，对极化激元态进行共振泵浦-探测光谱（resonant pump-probe spectroscopy）测量，解析约20 GHz范围内的耦合动力学；并通过外加磁场扫描磁振子模使其穿过声子共振。

Result: 观测到吸引型磁-声相互作用（attractive magnon-phonon interaction），强度V/2π = −43 MHz，其主要效应为声子放大；在磁-声共振处能量流以磁振子向声子单向转移为主，反向作用（back-action）受相干磁振子的动力学损耗（kinetic losses）限制。

Conclusion: 研究实现了层状范德华（van der Waals）材料中集体磁激发与声、光的直接接口，为GHz频段兼具光学读出的量子相干换能提供了途径。

Abstract: The layered anti-ferromagnet CrSBr hosts self-hybridized exciton-polaritons that couple parametrically to coherent magnons and phonons. In this work, we explore the tripartite optomechanical interaction between these quasiparticles in suspended CrSBr membranes supporting high-$Q$ breathing-type acoustic phonon modes. Using resonant pump-probe spectroscopy on the polariton state, we resolve the coupled dynamics in the $\simeq 20$ GHz range while sweeping the magnon mode across the phonon resonance with an external magnetic field. We observe an attractive magnon-phonon interaction of strength $V/2π= -43$ MHz, which primarily leads to phonon amplification. At the magnon-phonon resonance, we find predominantly unidirectional energy flow from magnons to phonons while the possible back-action is limited by kinetic losses of coherent magnons. Our study enables direct interfacing of collective magnetic excitations with sound and light in layered van der Waals materials, paving the way for quantum-coherent transduction in the GHz range with optical readout.

</details>


### [141] [Optical characterization of Fermi polarons in monolayer MoSe$_2$ encapsulated in amorphous silicon nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21931)
*Christos Paspalides,Brendan C. Mulkerin,Rafael Luque Merino,Alvaro Castilla Lamas,Samuel Palacios Valdepeñas,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jesper Levinsen,Meera M. Parish,Luis Viña,Maria Dolores Martín,Francesca Maria Marchetti,Andres Castellanos-Gomez,Carlos Antón-Solanas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文比较了非晶氮化硅（amorphous silicon nitride）封装与六方氮化硼（hBN）封装的单层 MoSe$_2$，发现氮化硅引入约 $(0.8$–$0.9)\times10^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$ 的电子掺杂，使激子响应由中性激子主导转为费米极化子（Fermi polaron）主导，并由有限温费米极化子理论定量复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶氮化硅（amorphous silicon nitride）是晶圆级集成光子学（integrated photonics）的低损耗、CMOS 兼容平台，将过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenide, TMD）单层封装其中有望实现可扩展的 TMD-光子电路；但该非晶介质对单层光学响应的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用微分反射率（differential reflectivity）与光致发光（photoluminescence）对比非晶氮化硅封装与六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride）封装的单层 MoSe$_2$，并将温度依赖反射率与微观有限温费米极化子理论（finite-temperature Fermi-polaron theory）进行定量比较。

Result: hBN 参考样品以中性激子（neutral exciton）为主导；氮化硅封装样品出现间隔约 31 meV 的吸引型与排斥型极化子共振（attractive and repulsive polaron resonances），发光以吸引支为主。理论拟合给出氮化硅诱导的电子密度为 $(0.8$–$0.9)\times10^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$，并仅以该密度与三子结合能（trion binding energy）为输入参数，在约 80 K 以下定量复现光致发光谱。

Conclusion: 非晶氮化硅封装会显著引入电子掺杂并改变单层 MoSe$_2$ 的激子响应，其行为可由费米极化子图像定量描述，这为在集成光子平台上设计 TMD 光电器件提供了依据。

Abstract: Amorphous silicon nitride is the leading low-loss, CMOS-compatible platform for wafer-scale integrated photonics. Encapsulating transition-metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers within this platform offers a promising route towards scalable TMD-photonic circuits. Yet the effect of this amorphous dielectric on the optical response of the monolayer remains poorly understood. Here, we compare monolayer MoSe$_2$ encapsulated in amorphous silicon nitride with a hexagonal boron nitride-encapsulated reference, using differential reflectivity and photoluminescence. Whereas the reference is dominated by the neutral exciton, the nitride-encapsulated monolayer shows attractive and repulsive polaron resonances separated by ${\sim}31$~meV, with emission dominated by the attractive branch. A quantitative comparison of temperature-dependent reflectance with a microscopic finite-temperature Fermi-polaron theory shows that the nitride induces substantial electron doping in the monolayer, with an electron density of $(0.8$--$0.9)\times10^{12}$~cm$^{-2}$. Using the extracted density and trion binding energy as the only input parameters, the theory reproduces the photoluminescence spectra up to ${\sim}80$ K in excellent quantitative agreement with experiments, thus corroborating the Fermi-polaron description of the observed excitonic response.

</details>


### [142] [Reconfigurable 2D Lateral Heterostructure Platform for Unified Detection, Emission, and Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22170)
*Purbasha Ray,Rupali Verma,Biswajeet Nayak,Suman Kumar Chakraborty,Shailendra K Varshney,Mayank Shrivastava,Prasana Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作演示了双层 MoSe2-WSe2 横向异质结（lateral heterojunction）单片器件，在统一平台上集成光电探测、可重构电致发光（electroluminescence, EL）与 CMOS 逻辑运算，三者共享同一静电调控框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感、发光、通信与计算的异构集成（heterogeneous integration）通常依赖专用器件，带来复杂互连、额外制备开销、寄生损耗与能耗代价；实现可单片集成、可重构地控制载流子产生、输运与复合的器件是长期挑战，二维横向异质结凭借空间分立的不同电荷极性畴与可动态调节的静电势场提供了可行路径。

Method: 制备双层 MoSe2-WSe2 横向异质结（bilayer lateral heterojunction），利用不同极性畴构成的栅极可调耗尽区（gate-tunable depletion region），在同一器件中实现光电探测、电注入下的空间可编程电致发光以及 CMOS 反相器逻辑运算。

Result: 光电探测器（photodetector）响应度为 35 A W-1，比探测率（specific detectivity）为 5×10^13 Jones，噪声等效功率（noise-equivalent power, NEP）为 6.3×10^-18 W Hz^-1/2；电注入下静电栅控可在 MoSe2 畴、横向异质界面与 WSe2 畴之间动态切换辐射复合区（radiative recombination zone），实现空间可编程电致发光、光学复用（optical multiplexing）与三值光学逻辑（ternary optical logic）；两畴互补输运特性支持 0.25 kHz 的 CMOS 反相器（inverter）工作。

Conclusion: 上述功能均源自同一静电框架：栅极可调耗尽区同时支配载流子提取、复合与输运。该研究确立了二维横向异质结作为多功能构建单元（building block）的地位，可用于集光传感、光产生与信息处理于一体的单片电子与光电子系统。

Abstract: Heterogeneous integration of sensing, light generation, communication, and computation typically requires specialized devices, resulting in complex interconnects, fabrication overhead, parasitic losses, and energy penalties. Developing a monolithic, reconfigurable device capable of controlling carrier generation, transport, and recombination remains a longstanding challenge. Two-dimensional (2D) lateral heterostructures offer a promising approach by combining spatially distinct domains of different charge polarity with a dynamically tunable electrostatic landscape. Here, multifunctional bilayer MoSe2-WSe2 lateral heterojunctions are demonstrated, integrating photodetection, reconfigurable electroluminescence (EL), and CMOS logic operation within a unified platform. The photodetector exhibits a responsivity of 35 A W-1, a specific detectivity of 5 x 10^13 Jones, and a noise-equivalent power of 6.3 x 10^-18 W Hz^-1/2, enabled by efficient charge separation and photoconductive gain. Under electrical injection, electrostatic gating dynamically switches the radiative recombination zone among the MoSe2 domain, lateral heterointerface, and WSe2 domain, enabling spatially programmable EL, optical multiplexing, and ternary optical logic. Furthermore, the complementary transport characteristics of the two domains enable CMOS inverter operation at 0.25 kHz. These functionalities originate from a common electrostatic framework in which the gate-tunable depletion region governs carrier extraction, recombination, and transport. This work establishes 2D lateral heterostructures as multifunctional building blocks for monolithically integrated electronic and optoelectronic systems combining light sensing, generation, and information processing.

</details>


### [143] [Intrinsic second-order nonlinear Hall effect from perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22229)
*Zhi-Hao Wei,Xiao-Bin Qiang,Renjie Tong,Hai-Zhou Lu,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种教科书式（textbook-style）的微扰理论，用于计算本征非线性电导率（intrinsic nonlinear conductivity），并与基于波包（wavepacket）的理论进行比较和一致性讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性输运（nonlinear transport）是探测量子材料电子结构与对称性的有效手段，其中本征非线性霍尔效应（intrinsic nonlinear Hall effect）因不依赖杂质散射（impurity scattering）且直接反映布洛赫电子（Bloch electrons）的量子几何（quantum geometry）而备受关注，需要简洁统一的理论框架。

Method: 基于定义良好的布洛赫态速度（Bloch-state velocity），通过微扰方法确定场修正后的速度（field-corrected velocity）与分布函数（distribution function），由此直接导出电导率。

Result: 该形式化方法给出了本征非线性电导率的直接推导，并与基于波包的理论进行了对比，讨论了二者的一致性（consistency）。

Conclusion: 所提出的教科书式微扰理论为理解本征非线性输运提供了简洁途径，且与既有波包理论相互一致。

Abstract: Nonlinear transport provides an efficient probe of the electronic structure and symmetry of quantum materials. As a prominent example, the intrinsic nonlinear Hall effect has attracted considerable attention in recent years because it is independent of impurity scattering and directly reflects the quantum geometry of Bloch electrons. Here we establish a simple textbook-style perturbation theory for the intrinsic nonlinear conductivity. Based on the well-defined Bloch-state velocity, our formulation perturbatively determines the field-corrected velocity and distribution function, from which the conductivity follows directly. We further compare our approach with wavepacket-based theories and discuss their consistency.

</details>


### [144] [Complex mobility edges from competing localization channels in non-Hermitian quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22336)
*Bing Shao,Weiwei Zhu,Longwen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文将 Ganeshan-Pixley-Das Sarma 模型推广至同时含非对称跃迁（asymmetric hopping）与复在位势（complex onsite potential）的情形，揭示两类能量依赖的局域化贡献及其竞争如何决定迁移率边（mobility edges）结构，并给出区分物理迁移率边与被抑制解析候选的筛选规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米局域化转变（non-Hermitian localization transitions）常伴随多重迁移率边，但其物理起源存在歧义；需要一个能涵盖广泛非厄米准周期链（non-Hermitian quasiperiodic chains）的框架来厘清其来源。

Method: 构建带非对称跃迁与复在位势的推广模型；借助 Avila 全局理论（Avila's global theory）推导精确 Lyapunov 指数（Lyapunov exponents），在复能量平面上求出两个通道的阈值，并建立筛选规则（selection rule）；辅以数值能谱（numerical spectra）与有限尺寸局域化诊断（finite-size localization diagnostics）验证。

Result: 发现两个表现为不同 Lyapunov 增长标度的能量依赖局域化贡献，其竞争决定迁移率边结构；唯一控制局域化的通道可随能量切换，从而产生非厄米起源的复杂迁移率边；非互易跃迁（nonreciprocal hopping）进一步移动阈值并改变体态能量图样（bulk energy patterns）；数值结果与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为刻画非厄米准晶体（non-Hermitian quasicrystals）中竞争局域化机制下的相变与迁移率边提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian localization transitions often accompany multiple mobility edges, but their physical origin can be ambiguous. To address the issue, we introduce an extension of the Ganeshan-Pixley-Das Sarma model with both asymmetric hopping and complex onsite potential, thereby incorporating a broad range of non-Hermitian quasiperiodic chains. Its solution reveals two energy-dependent localization contributions manifested as distinct Lyapunov growth scales, whose competition determines the structure of mobility edges. We derive exact Lyapunov exponents via Avila's global theory, obtain thresholds of the two channels on complex-energy plane, and formulate a selection rule that distinguishes physical mobility edges from suppressed analytical candidates. The channel that uniquely controls localization can switch with energy, producing complex mobility edges of non-Hermitian origins. Nonreciprocal hopping further shifts the thresholds and alters the bulk energy patterns. Numerical spectra and finite-size localization diagnostics confirm the theory. Our work thus provides a unified framework for characterizing phase transitions and mobility edges under competing localization mechanisms in non-Hermitian quasicrystals.

</details>


### [145] [Landscape index and its boundary correspondence in inhomogeneous chiral systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22500)
*Guo-Jian Qiao,Zhi-Lei Zhang,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于手征系统（chiral systems）零能本征方程对应空间动力学的二次型能量景观（quadratic landscape）几何构造的实空间拓扑指数（real-space topological index），并证明其与连续统绕数（continuum winding number）一致、界面两侧指数差等于受保护的手征零模数（net chiral zero-mode count），且可推广至高维并提供解析参数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑指数通常依赖渐近均匀的体区域（asymptotically homogeneous bulk regions），难以处理非均匀界面与准周期调制等情形；因此需要一个不依赖详细空间形式、仅由景观几何确定的实空间拓扑刻画。

Method: 从手征系统零能本征方程所支配的空间动力学出发，构造与二次型景观几何相关的实空间指数；该指数在保持衰减与增长空间解之间均匀指数分离（uniform exponential separation）的连续形变下不变，并在均匀极限下等于连续统绕数。进一步通过维度约化（dimensional reduction）将指数及其边界对应关系推广到高维系统。

Result: 界面两侧指数差等于受保护净手征零模数，无需渐近均匀体区；显式景观构造给出对哈密顿量参数及其空间梯度（spatial gradients）的解析界，对满足这些界的所有空间分布均确定其指数。所得界保证任意横向余维（transverse codimension）的有效 Kitaev 型系统中的受保护径向零模，并识别周期调制量子反常霍尔（quantum anomalous Hall）系统中支持手征边缘态的参量与动量区域；对考虑的准周期界面，指数差保证单一受保护手征分支，数值计算予以确认。

Conclusion: 景观几何将实空间拓扑与边界态的受保护净手征性（protected net chirality）联系起来，为无需假定均匀体的拓扑判定与边界态预测提供统一框架。

Abstract: We define a real-space topological index from the geometry of a quadratic landscape associated with the spatial dynamics, which is governed by the zero-energy eigenvalue equation of chiral systems. The landscape index remains invariant under continuous deformations preserving the uniform exponential separation between decaying and growing spatial solutions and equals the continuum winding number in the homogeneous limit. Across an inhomogeneous interface, the difference between the indices on the two sides equals the protected net chiral zero-mode count, without requiring asymptotically homogeneous bulk regions. Explicit landscape constructions yield analytical bounds on Hamiltonian parameters and their spatial gradients that determine the indices for all profiles satisfying these bounds, without specifying their detailed spatial form. Dimensional reduction extends the landscape index and its boundary correspondence to higher-dimensional systems. The resulting bounds guarantee protected radial zero modes in effective Kitaev-type systems of arbitrary transverse codimension and identify parameter and momentum regions supporting chiral edge states in periodically modulated quantum anomalous Hall systems. For the quasiperiodic interface considered, the index difference guarantees a single protected chiral branch despite persistent modulation on both sides, as confirmed by numerical calculations. Landscape geometry thus connects real-space topology to the protected net chirality of boundary states.

</details>


### [146] [Axion electrodynamics and the advection-diffusion structure of TEM modes in hollow-conductor waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22839)
*André H. Gomes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文系统研究充填轴子电动力学（axion electrodynamics）介质的空心导体波导中的横电磁（TEM）模式，发现轴子场与外加磁场的耦合使本不能传播的TEM模式得以传播，且其横向静电势满足平流-扩散型方程，并给出圆柱波导的显式解与场分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规单导体波导中TEM模式无法传播，而轴子电动力学背景可能改变这一限制；论文旨在系统刻画轴子场与外磁场耦合对空心导体波导中TEM模式的影响及其数学结构。

Method: 以轴子电动力学描述填充介质的空心导体波导，推导TEM模式的横向静电势方程，并将其与平流-扩散（advection-diffusion）方程建立数学对应；针对圆柱波导，分别考虑均匀与刚性旋转漂移场（单独及组合）以及不同佩克莱数（Péclet number）进行求解。

Result: 结果表明轴子场与外部磁场的耦合可使TEM模式在单导体波导中传播；所得TEM模式由横向静电势的平流-扩散型方程支配，并给出圆柱波导在多种漂移场配置与不同佩克莱数下的显式解与场分布。

Conclusion: 研究建立了轴子电动力学与平流-扩散现象之间的直接数学联系，并提供以输运（transport）为基础的框架，用以理解轴子电动力学背景的空间变化如何塑造空心导体波导中的横向电磁场。

Abstract: We present a systematic investigation of transverse electromagnetic (TEM) modes in hollow-conductor waveguides filled with a dielectric medium described by axion electrodynamics. While such modes do not propagate in conventional single-conductor waveguides, the coupling of the axion field to an external magnetic field enables their propagation. We show that the resulting TEM modes are governed by an advection-diffusion-type equation for the transverse electrostatic potential, establishing a direct mathematical connection between axion electrodynamics and advection-diffusion phenomena. We illustrate this connection through explicit solutions and field profiles for cylindrical waveguides, considering uniform and rigid rotational drift fields, both individually and in combination, as well as different values of the parameter controlling the advection-to-diffusion ratio (Péclet number). These results establish a transport-based framework for understanding how spatial variations of the axion-electrodynamics background shape the transverse electromagnetic fields in hollow-conductor waveguides.

</details>


### [147] [Performance limits and benchmarking of ideal 2D topological insulator field-effect transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22779)
*Yosep Park,Jaeyoung Eom,Yeonghun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究建立了理想拓扑绝缘体场效应晶体管（topological insulator field-effect transistors, TIFETs）的性能极限，并在等效与真实条件下将其开关能量-延迟性能与硅基器件及二维材料基器件进行系统基准对比，表明TIFETs具有显著更低的开关能量与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅基场效应晶体管（Si-based FETs）面临缩放极限（scaling limits），TIFETs被视为有前景的替代方案，但其性能极限及与其他器件技术的对比尚需系统评估。

Method: 建立理想TIFETs的性能极限，并在等效与真实条件下对TIFETs、硅基FETs（FinFET与LGAA）及二维材料基FETs（MoS₂与InSe）的开关能量-延迟性能开展综合基准测试（benchmarking），同时分析接触电阻（R_C）、寄生电容（C_par）、沟道长度（L）与沟道宽度（W）等关键影响因素。

Result: TIFETs实现显著更低的开关能量与延迟时间，优于硅基FETs与二维材料基FETs；从1-stack到3-stack的转变可带来约六倍的降低。

Conclusion: 基准研究表明TIFETs具备有竞争力的开关性能，在未来拓扑电子器件（topological electronic devices）中具有较强应用潜力。

Abstract: Topological insulator field-effect transistors (TIFETs) have emerged as a promising alternative to overcome the scaling limits of silicon-based field-effect transistors (Si-based FETs). In this study, we establish the performance limits of ideal TIFETs and present a comprehensive benchmarking of the switching energy-delay performance of TIFETs against Si-based FETs (FinFET and LGAA) and 2-D material-based FETs ($\mathrm{MoS}_2$ and InSe) under equivalent and realistic conditions. Furthermore, we analyze the key factors influencing the switching energy-delay performance of TIFETs, including contact resistance ($R_{\mathrm{C}}$), parasitic capacitance ($C_{\mathrm{par}}$), channel length ($L$), and channel width ($W$). Notably, TIFETs achieve substantially lower switching energy and delay time, outperforming both Si-based FETs and 2-D material-based FETs, where a transition from 1-stack to 3-stack yields approximately a sixfold reduction. This benchmarking study shows that TIFETs exhibit competitive switching performance and hold strong potential for future topological electronic devices.

</details>


### [148] [Electrical addressing of compensated magnetic order in artificial spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22912)
*Stéphane Nilsson,Lauren J. Riddiford,Emily Darwin,Hans J. Hug,Aleš Hrabec,Laura J. Heyderman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究以三角形人工自旋冰（artificial spin ice）为介观平台，利用自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torque）电流的方向依赖性与平面霍尔效应（planar Hall effect），实现对补偿态（compensated state）的电学写入与读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补偿磁性系统（如反铁磁体）因杂散场（stray field）为零、抗扰动且动力学超快，在自旋电子学器件中潜力巨大，但其有序态的电学写入与读取仍是主要难题。

Method: 采用三角形人工自旋冰作为可工程化、可成像、可电控的介观平台；利用自旋轨道力矩翻转电流（switching current）的方向依赖性，经单一电流通道独立切换三个纳米磁体子晶格（sublattice），并以平面霍尔效应实现子晶格分辨读出。

Result: 可实现三角形 plaquette 全部八种磁化状态（含完全补偿态）的电学访问与控制，并获得非线性、依赖历史（history-dependent）的电学响应。

Conclusion: 研究建立了电学可编程补偿磁系统的方法，为可重构磁性器件（reconfigurable magnetic devices）与非传统计算（unconventional computing）奠定基础。

Abstract: Compensated magnetic systems, such as antiferromagnets, hold immense potential for spintronic devices thanks to vanishing stray fields, robustness to perturbations, and ultrafast dynamics. Yet, electrical writing and reading of their order remains a major challenge. Here we use artificial spin ice as a mesoscale platform in which compensated order can be engineered, directly imaged, and electrically controlled. Specifically, we demonstrate combined electrical writing and reading of a compensated state in a triangular artificial spin ice. Exploiting the orientation dependence of the spin-orbit torque switching current, we independently switch three nanomagnet sublattices through a single current channel, with the planar Hall effect providing sublattice-resolved readout. This grants access to all eight magnetization states of the triangular plaquette, including its fully compensated state, yielding a nonlinear, history-dependent electrical response. Our results establish an approach to electrically programmable compensated magnetic systems and provide an important foundation for reconfigurable magnetic devices and unconventional computing.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamics of a Single-Domain Dissipative Magnetic Gyroscope in Electric and Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23167)
*Y. B. Band,Igor Kuzmenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将单畴对称陀螺形铁磁针视为"磁陀螺（magnetic gyroscope）"，建立静态磁场与电场中自旋-转动耦合动力学模型，含各向异性、塞曼能、诱导电偶极静电能与磁电耦合，并引入吉尔伯特阻尼与德拜弛豫耗散，采用四元数无奇异运动学求解。对120 nm钴针在0.5 T场中的计算给出翻转奇偶性决定易轴取向、约20 ns的章动-进动摆动及电场极化转矩主导等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是刻画单畴铁磁针（磁陀螺）在静态磁场与电场联合作用下的自旋-转动耦合动力学，厘清各向异性场、磁电耦合、运动（自旋-轨道）耦合与电场诱导极化的相对重要性，并评估此类导电针作为磁强计（magnetometer）的可行性条件。

Method: 构建保守哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），包含转动动能、磁晶与形状各向异性、塞曼能（Zeeman energy）、导电针诱导电偶极的静电能、线性磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling）及相对论运动（自旋-轨道）耦合；引入吉尔伯特阻尼（Gilbert damping）与电场下诱导电偶极的德拜弛豫（Debye relaxation）耗散，并以无奇异四元数运动学（quaternion kinematics）在实验室系与体固系给出运动方程。

Result: 对120 nm单畴钴针在0.5 T场中：(i) 各向异性场约1.3 T超过外加场，初始吉尔伯特阻尼瞬态中自旋多次反向，反向次数奇偶性决定易轴最终平行或反平行于自旋；(ii) 瞬态后针绕场摆动（进动伴随二倍进动频率的章动），周期约20 ns，与摆动周期 2π(I₁/μB)^{1/2} 一致，塞曼能与转动动能反相振荡而总能量守恒；(iii) 静电场通过形状相关极化转矩（polarization torque）使长轴趋向与E对齐，磁电相互作用与运动磁场（≲1 μT）的力矩可忽略。

Conclusion: 导电针的电场极化转矩远大于磁电与运动磁场力矩，故仅在 E ≪ E_c 时该导电针才可作为磁强计使用；各向异性场与外加场的相对大小及阻尼瞬态中的自旋翻转奇偶性对最终磁化取向具有决定性作用。

Abstract: We formulate the coupled spin--rotation dynamics of a single-domain symmetric top ferromagnetic needle (a magnetic gyroscope) in static magnetic and electric fields. The conservative Hamiltonian contains the rotational kinetic energy, the magnetocrystalline and shape anisotropy, the Zeeman energy, the electrostatic energy of the dipole induced in the conducting needle, the linear magnetoelectric coupling, and the relativistic motional (spin-orbit) coupling. Gilbert damping is introduced, and in an electric field, Debye relaxation of the induced electric dipole of the conducting needle also results in dissipation. We give the equations of motion in the laboratory and body-fixed frames, with singularity-free quaternion kinematics. For a 120-nm-long single-domain cobalt needle in a 0.5~T field we find: (i) because the anisotropy field ($\approx 1.3$~T) exceeds the applied magnetic field, the spin reverses direction several times during the initial Gilbert-damping transient, and the parity of the number of reversals determines whether the easy axis ends up parallel or antiparallel to the spin; (ii) after the transient the needle librates about the field---precession accompanied by nutation at twice the precession frequency---with a period of about 20~ns, consistent with the libration period $2π(I_1/μB)^{1/2}$, while the Zeeman and rotational kinetic energies oscillate out of phase and the total energy is conserved; (iii) a static electric field $\mathbf E$ exerts on the conducting needle a shape-dependent polarization torque that tends to align the long axis of the needle with $\mathbf E$. The torques due to the magnetoelectric interaction and motional magnetic fields ($\lesssim1~μ$T) are negligible compared with the polarization torque. A conducting needle can therefore serve as a magnetometer only if $E\ll E_c$.

</details>


### [150] [Photon condensation driven by scalar spin chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23178)
*Dimitrije Stepanenko,Daniel Loss,Mircea Trif*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 三角分子磁体经环交换将腔磁通耦合到标量自旋手性，从而在平衡态实现光子凝聚（photon condensation），突破规范不变性（gauge invariance）对偶极耦合的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范不变性（gauge invariance）禁止空间均匀电偶极耦合下的平衡态光子凝聚，因此需要寻找基于轨道磁响应（orbital magnetic response）的替代路径。

Method: 从Mott区半填充Hubbard模型出发，推导出使虚环交换（ring exchange）将腔磁通直接耦合至标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality）的有效哈密顿量，并采用平均场理论、集体涨落计算与有限尺寸精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行分析。

Result: 发现自发手性序与宏观腔位移共存的相，以及手性-光子杂化激发（hybrid chirality-photon excitations）；凝聚阈值可通过分子手性劈裂进行电学调控。

Conclusion: 阻挫磁体中的涌现轨道自由度（emergent orbital degrees of freedom）可作为超越常规偶极腔量子电动力学（cavity QED）实现平衡态光-物质序的途径。

Abstract: Gauge invariance forbids equilibrium photon condensation for spatially uniform electric-dipole coupling, motivating alternative routes based on orbital magnetic response. We show that triangular molecular magnets coupled to a cavity support photon condensation driven by emergent scalar spin chirality. Starting from the half-filled Hubbard model in the Mott regime, we derive an effective Hamiltonian in which virtual ring exchange couples the cavity flux directly to scalar spin chirality. Mean-field theory, collective-fluctuation calculations, and finite-size exact diagonalization reveal a phase with spontaneous chirality ordering and a macroscopic cavity displacement, together with hybrid chirality-photon excitations. The condensation threshold can be tuned electrically through the molecular chirality splitting. Our results establish emergent orbital degrees of freedom in frustrated magnets as a route to equilibrium light-matter ordering beyond conventional dipolar cavity quantum electrodynamics.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Astrophysical Constraints for Bosonic Dark Matter in and Around Quark Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21368)
*Júlia Tartera Barberà,Sarah Louisa Pitz,Jürgen Schaffner-Bielich,Laura Tolos*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在非理想 MIT 袋模型（MIT Bag Model）中引入带 $\\phi^n$ 自相互作用势的自相互作用玻色暗物质，通过求解双流体 Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff 方程，系统研究混合星的结构、稳定性与质量—半径关系，并指出 XTE J1814$-$338 可作为暗部门探测的关键候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密星可作为探测暗物质（dark matter）的天然实验室，但夸克物质与自相互作用玻色暗物质混合体系的结构与稳定性尚缺乏系统性刻画，尤其是自相互作用强度与玻色子质量对超致密解和暗物质分布形态的影响有待厘清。

Method: 采用非理想 MIT 袋模型描述夸克物质，并耦合具有 $\\phi^n$ 自相互作用势的玻色暗物质，求解双流体（two-fluid）TOV 方程；参数空间覆盖玻色子质量 200 MeV–1 GeV 及 $n=4$ 和 $n=40$ 的自相互作用强度。

Result: 强自相互作用与较高玻色子质量可提升高中心压强下构型的稳定性，但降低最大总质量；紧凑度 $C \\ge 1/3$ 的超致密（ultra-compact）解较强子模型显著受限，仅在强自相互作用且暗物质占比超过 90% 时出现；参数变化使星体呈现延展暗物质晕（halo）或致密暗物质核心（core）两种形态。与观测比对显示 PSR J1614$-$2230 和 XTE J1814$-$338 是暗物质探测的潜在窗口。

Conclusion: XTE J1814$-$338 因要求较轻玻色子质量（$m_b \\approx 200$–$300$ MeV）及强暗物质主导构型，有望成为确认暗部门（dark sector）存在的关键观测目标。

Abstract: We investigate the structure, stability, and mass-radius relations of compact stars composed of quark matter within the non-ideal MIT Bag Model mixed with self-interacting bosonic dark matter with a $φ^n$ self-interaction potential. By solving the coupled two-fluid Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations for boson masses between 200 MeV and 1 GeV and self-interaction strengths with $n=4$ and $n=40$, we show that strong self-interactions and higher boson masses enhance the stability of compact star configurations at high central pressures, though they reduce maximum overall masses. Ultra-compact solutions with $C \ge 1/3$ capable of mimicking black holes are significantly restricted compared to hadronic models, emerging only for strong self-interactions and dark matter fractions exceeding 90\%. Depending on the parameters, the compact stars form either extended dark matter halos or compact cores: stronger self-interactions and higher boson masses suppress the radial extension to favor compact cores of dark matter. Matching our models against observational constraints highlights PSR J1614$-$2230 and XTE J1814$-$338 as potential window of opportunity for dark matter detection. Notably, XTE J1814$-$338 could confirm a dark sector, given its requirement for light boson masses ($m_b \approx 200\text{--}300\text{ MeV}$) and strongly DM-dominated configurations.

</details>


### [152] [Probing the Macroscopic Imprints of Singularity Resolution: Polarization Diagnostics of Rotating ABG Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22936)
*Zi-Yang Li,Jia-Hui Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于逆向光线追踪（backward ray tracing）研究旋转 Ayón-Beato-García 正则时空的光学与偏振观测特征，发现正则化参数 $Q$ 显著收缩内阴影（inner shadow），并使退偏区域（depolarization regions）形态发生显著改变，为区分正则时空与 Kerr 黑洞提供可能的光学观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视界望远镜（EHT）对 M87* 与 Sgr A* 的观测为事件视界附近物理提供了实验支持；在缺乏完整量子引力理论的情况下，研究正则化几何（regularized geometries）中吸积流的观测外观与偏振图像、寻找其与 Kerr 黑洞的观测差异，对理解奇点问题具有价值。

Method: 采用逆向光线追踪方法，计算含正则化参数 $Q$ 的旋转 Ayón-Beato-García 时空的光学图像与偏振图像，并考察两类偏振观测量：电场矢量位置角（electric vector position angles, EVPAs）与观测屏上的退偏区域。

Result: 相较于 Kerr 情形，参数 $Q$ 使内阴影尺寸显著收缩，且在低倾角（如 $17^\circ$）图像经示意性高斯滤波（Gaussian filter）后仍可见；在高倾角（如 $83^\circ$）下，退偏区域不一定位于亮光子环（photon ring）附近，并呈多分支结构；EVPAs 分布对 $Q$ 不敏感，而退偏区域形态受 $Q$ 影响显著。

Conclusion: 内阴影收缩与退偏区域形态变化可作为旋转正则时空（rotating regular spacetime）可能的光学观测特征，为区分其与 Kerr 黑洞提供依据。

Abstract: Recent observations by the Event Horizon Telescope have revealed important electromagnetic information around the supermassive black holes $\text{M87}^*$ and $\text{Sgr A}^*$, providing experimental supports for investigating physics near the event horizon. In the absence of a complete quantum gravity theory, exploring the observational appearances and polarized images of accretion flows in regularized geometries, and seeking specific observational differences from Kerr black holes, is of value for understanding the singularity problem. Based on backward ray tracing method, this work investigates both the optical and polarimetric observational features of rotating Ayón-Beato-García spacetime with regularization parameter $Q$. It is found that, compared with the Kerr case, the parameter $Q$ leads to a significant contraction of the size of the inner shadow in the observed optical images. This contraction remains visible even after an illustrative Gaussian filter for images observed at a low inclination angle (e.g. $17^\circ$). In the polarized images, we focus on two observables, the electric vector position angles (EVPAs) and the depolarization regions, on the observer's screen. It is found that depolarization regions do not necessarily reside in the vicinity of the bright photon ring in images at high inclination angles (e.g. $83^\circ$), and a multi-branch structure can be observed. Furthermore, the distribution of EVPAs is insensitive to the regularization parameter $Q$ while the morphology of the depolarization regions can be impacted remarkably by $Q$. The results provide possible optical observable characteristics for the rotating regular spacetime.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [The SymTFT and Quantum Phases of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21715)
*Salvatore D. Pace,Sakura Schafer-Nameki,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该综述系统介绍对称性拓扑场论（symmetry topological field theory, SymTFT）框架，即以高一维拓扑理论编码量子多体系统对称性，并将其应用于量子物质的相分类与反常分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通对称性与广义对称性缺乏统一描述框架，而既有方法将对称性数据与具体量子系统细节混杂，需要一种能分离二者、便于统一处理反常与相分类的理论工具。

Method: 采用教学式（pedagogical）综述方式，把对称性编码为高一维的拓扑理论；在一维加一维（1+1）d 的融合范畴（fusion category）对称性情形下，说明 SymTFT 如何刻画反常（anomalies）、对能隙相（gapped phases）分类并组织无能隙相（gapless phases）的对称性结构。

Result: SymTFT 可统一刻画 1+1 维融合范畴对称性的反常、能隙相分类与无能隙相中的对称性组织方式，并已推广至更高维对称性、连续对称性、费米子系统（fermionic systems）、混合态（mixed states）及时空对称性（spacetime symmetries）。

Conclusion: SymTFT 为普通与广义对称性提供了统一且可推广的框架，其应用范围覆盖量子物质相的多方面，并在多个方向上持续拓展。

Abstract: The symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT) provides a unified framework for ordinary and generalized symmetries of quantum many-body systems. By encoding symmetry in a topological theory in one higher dimension, it separates symmetry data from the details of a specific quantum system. In this review, we give a pedagogical introduction to this framework and survey its applications to phases of quantum matter. For fusion category symmetries in ${(1+1)}$d, we explain how the SymTFT characterizes anomalies, classifies gapped phases, and organizes the symmetry structure of gapless phases. We also give a broad overview of further developments of this framework, including extensions to symmetries in higher dimensions, continuous symmetries, fermionic systems, mixed states, and spacetime symmetries.

</details>


### [154] [Constraints on the volume enclosed by Fermi surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21720)
*Subir Sachdev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该综述将费米液体（Fermi liquid, FL）的Luttinger定理证明推广至量子自旋液体与分馏费米液体（fractionalized Fermi liquid, FL*）的部分子规范理论（parton gauge theory），确立FL*的费米面体积由ρ减去量子化偏移量（通常为ρ−1）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL*中费米面所包围体积对应的电子密度存在量子化偏移，这一偏移与量子自旋液体的对称性分数化（symmetry fractionalization）数据相关，但缺乏类似Luttinger定理的系统性证明，故需从理论上予以确立。

Method: 方法是把针对FL的原始Luttinger证明推广到描述量子自旋液体与FL*的部分子规范理论框架中；并讨论Oshikawa的通量插入（flux insertion）论证及其向FL*的扩展，其中包括对无能隙自旋液体（gapless spin liquid）的讨论。

Result: 在部分子规范理论框架下确立了FL*的费米体积规则：费米面体积由ρ经量子化平移（最常见为ρ−1，并假设自旋旋转不变性）后的表达式决定，该平移量对应量子自旋液体的量子化对称性分数化数据。

Conclusion: FL*的费米体积规则可通过Luttinger与Oshikawa两类论证一致地推广得到，费米面准粒子与分数化自旋子（spinon）激发共存时仍遵循量子化的体积约束。

Abstract: A fundamental property of a Fermi liquid (FL) is that the volume enclosed by the Fermi surface is determined by $ρ$, the total density of electrons. In a fractionalized Fermi liquid (FL*), the volume enclosed by the Fermi surface is determined by the same expression after $ρ$ has been shifted by a quantized amount, most commonly to $ρ-1$ (we assume spin rotation invariance, for simplicity). The FL* shift is related to the quantized symmetry fractionalization data of a quantum spin liquid, whose fractionalized spinon excitations are present alongside the Fermi surface quasiparticles. In this review article, we establish the FL* Fermi volume by generalizing the original Luttinger proof for FL to parton gauge theories of the quantum spin liquid and FL*. We also discuss Oshikawa's flux insertion arguments for FL, and their extension to FL*, including a discussion of gapless spin liquids.

</details>


### [155] [Tunable Topological Charge Order in Magnetic Moiré Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21745)
*Conor Smith,Yubo Yang,Shiwei Zhang,Miguel A. Morales*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用神经网络量子态（neural quantum states, NQS）方法研究莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）中强磁场下二维电子的基态，揭示量子霍尔液体（quantum Hall liquid）与电子晶体（electron crystal）之间存在分子电荷序（molecular charge order）与量子化霍尔响应共存的扩展区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场下二维电子可呈现互不相容的两种基态：具有量子化霍尔电导（quantized Hall conductance）的不可压缩量子霍尔液体，以及相互作用驱动的有序电子晶体（electron crystal）。过渡金属硫族化合物双层中的莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）为研究这一竞争提供了可调平台，但其基态相图尚需系统刻画。

Method: 采用神经网络量子态（neural quantum states, NQS）并结合若干方法学改进，计算朗道能级填充（Landau-level filling）ν_B=1、每个莫尔原胞含三个电子时的基态相图，考察莫尔超晶格势场深度与几何构型（深三角势极限与蜂窝点）的影响。

Result: 在深三角势极限下，电子形成拓扑平庸的"维格纳分子晶体"（Wigner molecular crystal），即三电子人造原子；随势场深度降低，分子电荷序保留而量子化霍尔响应出现。蜂窝点处每个原胞内势阱呈单占据或双占据，双占据子（doublon）提供演生的伊辛自由度（Ising degree of freedom）；在陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）内出现短程双占据子关联，并随相互作用增强最终破坏子晶格对称性，形成具有长程有序的奈尔电荷密度波（Néel CDW）。

Conclusion: 拓扑分子区间所处扭转角与磁场范围可直接由现有实验触及；所提出的方法学进展为在磁场下探索关联量子物质（correlated quantum matter）的广泛问题提供了可扩展框架。

Abstract: Two-dimensional electrons in a strong magnetic field exhibit two seemingly incompatible ground states: incompressible quantum Hall liquids with a quantized Hall conductance, and electron crystals in which interactions drive the charge into ordered patterns. Moiré superlattices in transition-metal-dichalcogenide bilayers offer a tunable arena for this competition. Using neural quantum states (NQS) together with several methodological advances, we show how this competition unfolds. In particular, we demonstrate that the two end points are separated by an extended regime in which molecular charge order coexists with a quantized Hall response. We map the ground-state phase diagram of electrons at Landau-level filling $ν_B=1$ in the presence of a moiré superlattice potential, with three electrons per moiré cell. In the deep triangular potential limit, the electrons form the topologically trivial "Wigner molecular crystal'' of three-electron artificial atoms, a state recently imaged at zero magnetic field in moiré bilayers. As the potential depth is lowered, molecular charge order remains while quantized Hall response emerges. At the honeycomb point, where each cell contains three electrons and two degenerate moiré minima, wells are either singly or doubly occupied and the doublons provide an emergent Ising degree of freedom. Starting from modest interaction strength, we find short-range doublon correlations within the Chern insulator which grow with interaction strength and eventually break sublattice symmetry, forming a Néel CDW with long-range order between doublons. The topological molecular regime lies at twist angles and magnetic fields directly accessible to current experiments. The methodological advances presented provide a scalable framework for exploring a broad range of questions in correlated quantum matter in the presence of magnetic fields.

</details>


### [156] [Coexisting orders and dipolar Luttinger liquids in $\mathbb{Z}_N$ quantum torus chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22046)
*Seunghun Lee,Seungho Lee,Hyun-Yong Lee,Jung Hoon Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文为一维 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 量子环面链（quantum torus chain）建立了统一的基态相图，并借助不可逆对偶（noninvertible duality）将其与偶极团簇链（dipolar cluster chain）和偶极伊辛链（dipolar Ising chain）相联系。相图由局域希尔伯特空间维度 $N$ 及其奇偶性决定，包含 $X$ 与 $Z$ 长程序共存的 $C$ 相及两种偶极 Luttinger 液体（dipolar Luttinger liquid, dLL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为一维 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 量子环面链及经不可逆对偶关联的偶极团簇链、偶极伊辛链提供一个统一相图框架，厘清局域希尔伯特空间维度 $N$ 及其奇偶性如何控制基态性质与相变。

Method: 方法上结合张量网络计算（tensor-network calculations）、玻色化（bosonization）与微扰论（perturbation theory），并以涌现的 Luttinger 液体理论（emergent Luttinger liquid theory）分析 dLL 相的稳定性及由其引出的 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 转变。

Result: 结果得到由 $N$ 及其奇偶性支配的丰富相图：识别出 $X$、$Z$ 长程序共存的 $C$ 相，以及分隔共存相与单一序相的 dLL$_{\rm I}$、dLL$_{\rm II}$，其中无能隙偶极激发与长程序共存；$C$ 与 dLL 相均在 $N\ge 5$ 出现，但仅 $C$ 相在 $N\to\infty$ 时存留。dLL 相稳定性由 umklapp 与 clock 各向异性微扰竞争驱动的 BKT 转变所控制；奇数 $N\ge 5$ 时在 $\theta=\pi$ 附近因指数简并基态流形内的低能动力学（映射为有效 XXZ 链）出现第三种 dLL$_{\rm III}$ 相。

Conclusion: 结论表明 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 量子环面链的相结构可由 $N$ 与奇偶性统一刻画，dLL 相可借涌现 Luttinger 液体理论理解。基于对偶性考虑，作者进一步提示偶极团簇链中可能存在无能隙偶极对称保护拓扑相（symmetry-protected topological phase）以及自发偶极对称破缺，但这些属于对偶性推测。

Abstract: We establish a unified ground-state phase diagram for the one-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ quantum torus chain, which is connected to dipolar cluster and dipolar Ising chains via noninvertible dualities. Combining tensor-network calculations with bosonization and perturbation theory, we establish a rich phase diagram governed by the local Hilbert-space dimension $N$ and its parity. We identify phases with coexisting $X$ and $Z$ long-range orders ($C$ phase), and dipolar Luttinger liquids (dLL$_{\rm I}$ and dLL$_{\rm II}$) separating coexisting phases from single-order phases. Gapless dipolar excitations coexist with long-range order in dLLs. Both the $C$ and dLL phases appear at $N \geq 5$, but only the $C$ phases persist as $N\to \infty$. The stability of the dLL phase is understood by an emergent Luttinger liquid theory, with Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions out of it driven by competing umklapp and clock anisotropy perturbations. For odd $N \geq 5$, a third dLL phase (dLL$_{\rm III}$) emerges near $θ= π$ due to the low-energy dynamics inside the exponentially degenerate ground-state manifold that maps onto an effective XXZ chain. Duality considerations suggest a possible gapless dipolar symmetry-protected topological phase and spontaneous dipole-symmetry breaking in dipolar cluster chains.

</details>


### [157] [Orbital differentiation in a rare earth ion near half filling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22462)
*D. W. Tam,Ø. S. Fjellvåg,B. Gonano,E. X. M. Trewick,M. Månsson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用Quanty软件对立方岩盐结构SmN进行全多重态（full multiplet）计算，所得磁化率、非弹性中子散射及自旋密度与实验和DFT一致，并揭示基态J=5/2流形存在轨道分化（orbital differentiation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土离子电子结构的细节对理解关联态（correlated states）起源至关重要，但现有基于波函数的方法（如CASSCF）计算昂贵且无法用于金属体系，亟需快速廉价的替代计算手段。

Method: 使用晶体场软件Quanty对立方岩盐结构的SmN开展全多重态计算，并调节晶体场参数（crystal field parameters）以获得波函数。

Result: 模型计算的可观测量与磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）及非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）实验精确吻合，空间分辨自旋密度与DFT预测一致；波函数显示基态J=5/2流形分化为两个具有不同轨道刚度（orbital stiffness）的子空间，构成对Hund规则（Hund's rule）的违背。

Conclusion: 该快速低成本计算工具揭示的轨道分化推进了稀土材料微观模型的发展，并可能为SmN中非常规三重态超导（unconventional triplet superconductivity）的主张提供支持。

Abstract: In the electronic structure of rare earth ions, details can be crucial for understanding the origin of correlated states. Currently, state-of-the-art wavefunction-based methods (e.g. CASSCF) can capture single-ion properties of molecular magnets and some band insulating systems, but are expensive to perform, and cannot be applied to metallic systems. Here, we carry out full multiplet calculations for SmN with the cubic rock-salt structure using the crystal field software Quanty. The calculated experimental observables from our model agree precisely with magnetic susceptibility and inelastic neutron scattering experiments, and the spatially resolved spin density also agrees with DFT predictions. By tuning the crystal field parameters, our full multiplet-derived wavefunctions reveal the presence of orbital differentiation of the ground $J=\frac{5}{2}$ manifold into two groups, which are distinct subspaces with different orbital stiffness. This violation of Hund's rule, observed with a fast and inexpensive calculation tool, represents a step forward for microscopic models of rare earth materials, and may provide support for the claim of unconventional triplet superconductivity in SmN.

</details>


### [158] [Non-Invertible Quantum Cellular Automata I: Microscopic Definition of Non-Invertible Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22928)
*Sahand Seifnashri,Shu-Heng Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出“不可逆量子胞自动机（non-invertible quantum cellular automata, QCAs）”的算子代数定义，即兼具可对偶性（dualizability）与纯性（purity）的保局域性量子信道，并猜想其均可由可对偶拓扑缺陷或可对偶顺序量子电路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为量子格点系统的不可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）提供算子代数层面的严格定义，以局域算子上的作用为出发点刻画这类对称性。

Method: 以保局域性量子信道（locality-preserving quantum channels）为基础，施加可对偶性与纯性两条定义性条件；针对量子自旋链论证这些条件的合理性，并构造包括反常（anomalous）在位不可逆对称性在内的广泛例子及高维推广。

Result: 给出不可逆 QCA 的定义框架与猜想，并构造出涵盖反常在位不可逆对称性与高维情形的广泛实例；文中预告的第二部分将重构拓扑缺陷，其边界代数给出按混合等价（blend equivalence）分类不可逆 QCA 的指标理论（index theory）。

Conclusion: 不可逆 QCA 可作为量子格点系统不可逆对称性的统一算子代数刻画，其完整分类有赖于基于拓扑缺陷边界代数的指标理论，该工作为后续分类研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce non-invertible quantum cellular automata (QCAs), defined as locality-preserving quantum channels that are dualizable and pure. This provides an operator-algebraic definition of non-invertible symmetries of quantum lattice systems, formulated in terms of their action on local operators. Focusing on quantum spin chains, we motivate these defining conditions and conjecture that every such quantum channel admits a realization by dualizable topological defects or, equivalently, by dualizable sequential quantum circuits. We construct a broad class of examples, including on-site non-invertible symmetries that are anomalous, as well as higher-dimensional examples. In Part II of this work, we reconstruct topological defects whose boundary algebras yield an index theory that classifies non-invertible QCAs up to blend equivalence.

</details>


### [159] [Preformed excitons in the excitonic density-wave state of monolayer 1$T'$-MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22536)
*Fei Yang,Keyu Wei,Dekun Yang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yong Zhang,Xuefeng Wang,Fucong Fei,Fengqi Song,Binghai Yan,Xiaoxiang Xi,Junwei Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在单层1T'-MoTe2中利用激子密度波作为相干探针，揭示了T_c≈80 K至T_g≈100 K之间存在"预形成激子"赝能隙区间：密度波与集体激发在T_c以上消失，而单粒子能隙延续至T_g，理论上分别归因于玻色型相位退相干与费米型对破坏两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子电中性使凝聚体相位相干性难以直接探测，导致预形成激子（preformed excitons）的特征信号与物理机制长期不明；作者寻求一种实验可及的相位相干性探针。

Method: 在具有动量分离电子/空穴费米口袋的体系（如单层1T'-MoTe2）中，以相位相干配对所驱动的激子密度波（excitonic density wave）作为探针，结合拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与输运、隧穿测量，并辅以自洽微观理论计算。

Result: 密度波序及其涌现集体激发在T_c≈80 K以上消失，而单粒子能隙表征的激子绝缘态持续至T_g≈100 K；理论识别出被晶格钉扎的相子（phason，相位模），其热致能隙软化增强相位涨落并在T_c以上驱动相位退相干，费米准粒子热激发则在T_g处破坏配对振幅，自洽计算定量再现观测。

Conclusion: 该研究给出预形成配对物理（preformed-pairing physics）的激子实现，并表明玻色型相位退相干与费米型对破坏是导致激子序丧失的两个可分离过程。

Abstract: Phase-incoherent preformed excitons have long been anticipated in excitonic insulators, yet their characteristic signatures and underlying physics remain elusive, as the electrical neutrality of excitons hinders direct probes of condensate phase coherence. Here we exploit the emergent excitonic density wave driven by phase-coherent pairings in systems hosting momentum-separated electron and hole Fermi pockets, such as monolayer 1$T'$-MoTe$_2$, as an experimentally accessible probe of excitonic phase coherence. We observe in 1$T'$-MoTe$_2$ that the density-wave order and its associated emergent collective excitations, as revealed by Raman spectroscopy, vanish above $T_c\approx80~$K, whereas the excitonic-insulator state, characterized by a single-particle gap in transport and tunneling measurements, persists up to $T_g\approx100~$K, revealing an intermediate pseudogap-like regime of preformed excitons without long-range phase coherence. Theoretically, we attribute this intriguing separation to two distinct processes governing the loss of excitonic order by its own emergent excitations: boson-driven phase decoherence and fermion-driven pair breaking. Specifically, we identify an emergent low-lying collective excitation as a lattice-pinned phason (phase mode), whose heating-induced gap softening enhances phase fluctuations and drives condensate phase decoherence above $T_c$, while thermal excitations of fermionic quasiparticles gradually suppress and ultimately destroy pairing amplitude at the higher $T_g$. The self-consistent microscopic calculation quantitatively captures our key observations, establishing an excitonic realization of preformed-pairing physics.

</details>


### [160] [Domain walls of a spin-chirality ferroelectric as tunable quantum particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22662)
*Aleksandr Buzdakov,Atharva Hingane,Sergey Artyukhin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在阻挫自旋-1/2 XXZ链中，键交替δ打开自旋能隙后，手性畴壁（chirality domain wall）表现为一个量子粒子：有限链中呈盒中粒子的能级与波函数，并可被电场加速；无限链中其质量随δ减小剧增并在δ*≈0.012处发散，能带底部变平，δ*以下出现两个偏离中心的简并极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫自旋链中矢量自旋手性（vector spin chirality）决定电极化，因而铁电畴壁即手性畴壁；半经典图像提示拓扑约束会导致非局域翻转动力学，但量子自旋链中此类畴壁的性质尚不清楚。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）对存在竞争近邻与次近邻交换的阻挫自旋-1/2 XXZ链进行计算，其中键交替（bond alternation）δ打开自旋能隙。

Result: 有限链中畴壁具有盒中粒子的能级与波函数，并在电场下如受恒力粒子般运动；无限链中质量随δ减小增长逾两个数量级，于δ*≈0.012处发散（能带底部变平），δ*以下能带出现两个偏离中心的简并极小；大质量源于面内与面外交换能的近抵消（near cancellation）。

Conclusion: 阻挫量子自旋链中的手性畴壁可被有效描述为具有可调质量的量子粒子，其质量在临界键交替δ*处发散并伴随能带结构转变，为畴壁的量子输运与拓扑约束提供定量图像。

Abstract: In frustrated spin chains with competing nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor exchange, the vector spin chirality sets the electric polarization, so a ferroelectric domain wall is a domain wall of the chirality. The semiclassical picture suggests nonlocal switching dynamics due to topological constraints on the spins, but little is known about such domain walls in a quantum spin chain. Using density-matrix renormalization group calculations for a frustrated spin-1/2 XXZ chain in which a bond alternation $δ$ opens a spin gap, we show that the wall then becomes a quantum particle. In a finite chain, it has the energy levels and wave functions of a particle in a box, and it moves under an electric field as a particle under a constant force does. In the infinite chain, its mass grows by more than two orders of magnitude as $δ$ decreases and diverges at $δ^*\approx0.012$, where the bottom of its band becomes flat; below $δ^*$ the band has two degenerate minima away from its center. The large mass results from a near cancellation of in-plane and out-of-plane exchange energies.

</details>


### [161] [Structural Response to the Hidden Charge Order in Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22864)
*Darren C. Peets,Nikita D. Andriushin,Tobias Ritschel,Morgan C. Allison,Nikolai S. Pavlovskii,Anton A. Kulbakov,Alexandre Ivanov,Jose R. Linares Mardegan,Pablo J. Bereciartua,Sonia Francoual,Jochen Geck,Dmytro S. Inosov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 中子散射（neutron scattering）数据表明，中心对称斯格明子（skyrmion）宿主Sr₃Fe₂O₇中约330 K以下的"隐藏"电荷有序（charge order）伴随明确的结构调制，而非单纯的电子重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前通过共振X射线散射（resonant x-ray scattering）确认了电荷歧化（charge disproportionation）与局域化转变，但该手段仅间接敏感于晶格，无法判定该转变是否具有真实结构成分。

Method: 采用对晶格直接敏感的中子散射（neutron scattering）手段，对Sr₃Fe₂O₇的电荷有序转变进行结构探测。

Result: 中子数据显示，该电荷有序态伴随清晰的结构修改（structural modification），即转变具有结构成分。

Conclusion: 电荷有序转变并非仅为电子重新组织，而是与晶格结构变化相耦合，补充并完善了此前共振X射线散射的结论。

Abstract: We previously reported `hidden' charge order below $\sim$330 K in the centrosymmetric skyrmion host Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_7$ [J.-H. Kim et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 097203 (2021)], finally identifying the charge disproportionation and localization transition that had been first seen in the 1960s. We detected this through resonant x-ray scattering, which directly probes electron density, but is only indirectly sensitive to the lattice structure. This left open the question of whether the charge-ordering transition has a true structural component or consists merely of the reorganization of electrons. Neutrons probe the lattice directly, and we now show, based on neutron data, that the charge order is accompanied by a clear structural modification.

</details>


### [162] [Competing Magnetic Orders and Continuously Varying Exponents in $U(1)$ Spin Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23170)
*Vira Shyta,Flavio S. Nogueira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在2+1维QED（quantum electrodynamics）中引入与共同涌现规范场耦合不等强度的双味四分量狄拉克费米子，研究通过耦合比κ连续调控竞争磁不稳定性。结果表明单凝聚相之间可由共存区或有限无能隙区间连接，并给出磁关联的连续变化临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U(1)自旋液体混合物（spin-liquid mixtures）中，对共同涌现规范场（emergent gauge field）的不等耦合可能提供连续调节竞争磁不稳定性（magnetic instabilities）的机制，但该机制的具体相图与临界性质尚需在可控场论模型中检验。

Method: 采用2+1维QED，含两族四分量狄拉克费米子（four-component Dirac fermions）；结合大N领头阶处理（leading large-N treatment）与Schwinger–Dyson方程，求解两sector中的手征凝聚（chiral condensate），并分析重整化群（renormalization group）流与规范不变交错磁化（staggered magnetization）关联。

Result: 在固定费米子数下，耦合比κ=e_2^2/e_1^2控制哪一sector形成凝聚；依N不同，单凝聚相之间通过共存（coexistence）或两族费米子均保持无能隙的有限区间相连。质量产生阈值互为倒数，费米子能隙以Miransky本质奇点（Miransky essential singularities）关闭；RG流存在由不变量κ参数化的圆形不动点线（line of fixed points）。无能隙区内规范不变交错磁化关联的指数随κ连续变化，而两sector间关联的指数与κ无关。

Conclusion: 磁序竞争可映射为连续族临界理论（continuous family of critical theories），并给出中间无能隙区（gapless regime）的磁关联特征，为在自旋液体混合物中识别该区间提供可检验的关联信号。

Abstract: Unequal couplings to a common emergent gauge field provide a continuous way to tune competing magnetic instabilities in an effective description of $U(1)$ spin-liquid mixtures. We study this mechanism in quantum electrodynamics in 2+1 dimensions with two families of four-component Dirac fermions. Combining a leading large-$N$ treatment with Schwinger--Dyson equations, we obtain chiral condensates representing Néel order in the two sectors. At fixed fermion number, the coupling ratio $κ=e_2^2/e_1^2$ controls which condensates develop. Depending on $N$, the phases with a single condensate are connected through either coexistence or a finite interval in which both fermion families remain gapless. The mass-generation thresholds are reciprocal, and the fermion gaps close with Miransky essential singularities. The renormalization group flow has a circular line of fixed points parametrized by the invariant ratio $κ$. At the calculated order, gauge-invariant staggered magnetization correlations have continuously varying exponents in the gapless regime, while the exponent for correlations between the two sectors is independent of $κ$. These results connect the competition between magnetic orders to a continuous family of critical theories and identify magnetic correlation signatures of the intervening gapless regime.

</details>


### [163] [Multiple Field-Induced Transitions, Magnetocaloric Response and Anisotropic Magnetotransport in Orthorhombic RAlGe (R = Dy, Ho)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23181)
*Ram Kumar,D. S. Sokratov,P. Sobel,C. Mueller,D. E. Graf,J. Paglione*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究系统表征了Weyl半金属家族成员DyAlGe与HoAlGe单晶的磁学、热力学及磁热性质，揭示多重磁相变与各向异性磁熵变，并指出其可作为研究局域4f磁性与晶体对称性相互作用的模型体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAlGe家族属于Weyl半金属（Weyl semimetal）体系，但其局域4f磁性（4f magnetism）与拓扑非平庸电子结构、晶体对称性之间的关联尚缺系统实验刻画，磁热效应（magnetocaloric effect, MCE）在该类材料中的表现也有待澄清。

Method: 对DyAlGe与HoAlGe单晶开展磁化（magnetization）、热容（heat capacity）与输运（transport）测量，并由等温磁化曲线提取磁熵变（ΔS_M），在B ∥ a方向0–7 T场变化下评估磁热效应。

Result: DyAlGe主磁有序温度为T_N1 = 30.5 K，HoAlGe为11.5 K，并分别在6.6 K与8 K出现额外一级磁相变；B ∥ a方向磁化等温线呈显著各向异性，ΔS_M最大值约5–23 J/kg-K，且在两体系中均出现符号反转。

Conclusion: ΔS_M的符号反转反映场诱导的自旋构型转变；中等磁热效应与磁各向异性表明该类材料可作为研究拓扑非平庸金属间化合物（topologically nontrivial intermetallics）中磁热行为的模型体系。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of the magnetic, thermodynamic, and magnetocaloric properties of single-crystalline DyAlGe and HoAlGe, both members of the RAlGe Weyl semimetal family. Magnetization, heat capacity and transport measurements reveal multiple magnetic phase transitions associated with the evolution of complex magnetic structures. For DyAlGe, the primary magnetic ordering occurs at \textit{$T_{N1}$}= 30.5 K , while HoAlGe orders at a much lower temperature of \textit{$T_{N1}$}= 11.5 K, followed by additional first-order magnetic transitions at 6.6 K and 8 K, respectively. The magnetization isotherms display pronounced anisotropy for the B $\parallel$ a, consistent with competing exchange and crystal-field interactions. The magnetic entropy change ($ΔS_M$) derived from isothermal magnetization demonstrates a significant magnetocaloric effect (MCE) with sign change in both systems, with a maximum $ΔS_M$ of $\approx$ 5$-$23 J/kg-K along B $\parallel$ a for a field change of 0$-$7 T. The sign reversal of $ΔS_M$ across field-induced transitions highlights the complex field-tuned spin configurations. The moderate MCE and magnetic anisotropy suggest that these materials may serve as model systems for studying magnetocaloric effects in topologically nontrivial intermetallics with interplay between localized 4\textit{f} magnetism and crystal symmetry.

</details>

### [164] [Shortest-path percolation and structural robustness of quantum internet networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21855)
*Victor H. F. Francheto,Thomas Peron*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于光纤的空间网络（量子互联网模型）上的最短路径渗流（shortest-path percolation），以路径移除模拟纠缠链路（entangled links）消耗。结果表明网络鲁棒性随平均度增加而提升，并提出"等效路径代价"概念，其随节点数约按平方根增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子互联网依托光纤空间网络实现通信，通信过程会消耗纠缠链路，需要理解路径受限条件下的网络退化（degradation）与鲁棒性（robustness）行为。

Method: 在平均度随系统规模增长或近似保持恒定的空间网络上，比较有限与无限制路径长度界（path-length bounds）下的最短路径渗流；通过蒙特卡洛采样（Monte Carlo sampling）最短路径长度分布，估计可近似复现无限制情形的等效路径代价（equivalent path cost）。

Result: 平均度增加使网络更鲁棒；近似恒定平均度的网络在固定有限界下呈现数据塌缩（data collapse）；等效路径代价约随节点数按平方根增长。对 Erdős-Rényi 随机网络，发现初始平均度与不同路径长度界渗流曲线近似共同交叉点（crossing point）之间的经验关系。

Conclusion: 超出等效路径代价的更大界仅增加计算时间而不显著改变渗流动力学（percolation dynamics），故该代价可作为有限界的实用选取依据。

Abstract: We study shortest-path percolation on spatial networks that model the quantum internet based on optical fibers. In this process, the removal of paths describes the consumption of the entangled links used for communication. We compare finite and unrestricted path-length bounds for networks whose average degree either increases with system size or remains approximately constant. We show that networks become more robust as the average degree increases, whereas networks with approximately constant average degree exhibit a data collapse for the fixed finite bounds considered. Using Monte Carlo sampling of the shortest-path-length distribution, we estimate an equivalent path cost, a finite bound that approximately reproduces the degradation of the network in the unrestricted case. For networks with fixed average degree, this cost grows approximately as the square root of the number of nodes. Increasing the bound beyond this value requires additional computation time without appreciably changing the percolation dynamics. As a complementary result, for Erdős-Rényi random networks, we find an empirical relation between the initial average degree and the approximately common crossing point of the percolation curves for different path-length bounds.

</details>


### [165] [Structural balance in complex networks: from measurement to dynamics, emergence and implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21963)
*Giacomo Vaccario,Piotr J. Górski,Georges Andres,Manuel S. Mariani,Janusz A. Hołyst*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该综述将结构平衡理论（structural balance theory）从社会心理学的三元年表述重新诠释为统计物理（statistical physics）与复杂网络（complex networks）框架下的统计系综视角：平衡由内禀三体相互作用（three-body interactions）的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）编码，平衡构型对应低能态，平衡度量充当序参量（order parameter）。文中按测量（Measurement）、动力学与涌现（Dynamics and Emergence）、启示（Implications）三部分组织该领域，并指出证据对局部三元稳定性最强、对系统整体平衡最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构平衡理论原用于社会心理学，描述友好与敌对关系如何组织成一致模式；本文旨在综述其经由统计物理与网络科学重新诠释后的进展，统合分散的测量方法、动力学模型与应用后果，厘清该领域的共识与未决问题。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，将三元表述扩展为统计系综（statistical-ensemble）视角，以哈密顿量描述三体相互作用、以低能态对应平衡构型、以平衡度量作为共识、极化与无序之间转变的序参量；并分三节展开：测量部分比较结构平衡度量与零模型（null models），动力学与涌现部分梳理确定性（deterministic）与随机（stochastic）系统，包括梯度模型（gradient model）、哈密顿模型（Hamiltonian model）与局部三元模型（local triad model），考察噪声、拓扑与竞争机制的作用，启示部分综述对信念形成、观点传染、极化、国际关系与市场的影响。

Result: 结论对所选度量与零模型具有依赖性，即同一系统的判断可随二者不同而改变；噪声、网络拓扑与竞争机制共同决定平衡能否涌现；跨领域证据显示局部三元稳定性支持最强、系统整体平衡支持最弱，且平衡并不等同于和谐，平衡结构可对应凝聚、极化或持续冲突；其经验相关性在部分互惠性社会系统中获得较好支持，在生物及其他复杂网络中仍不确定。

Conclusion: 结构平衡应被视为统计物理意义下的能量景观与序参量问题，而非单纯的和谐指标；未来研究需明确度量与零模型选择、区分局部与全局层次的平衡，并在生物等非社会网络中进一步检验其适用性。

Abstract: Structural balance theory, introduced in social psychology to describe how friendly and hostile ties organize into consistent patterns, has been reinterpreted through statistical physics and complex networks. In the classical formulation, a triad with positive or negative links, representing friendship and hostility, is balanced when the product of its signs is positive; a balanced network splits into at most two internally friendly, mutually hostile groups. This review synthesizes how the triadic formulation has been extended to a statistical-ensemble perspective in which balance is encoded by immanent three-body interactions described by a proper Hamiltonian, balanced configurations correspond to low-energy states, and balance measures serve as order parameters for transitions between consensus, polarization, and disorder. We organize the field into three sections: Measurement, Dynamics and Emergence, and Implications. In Measurement, we compare measures of structural balance and null models, and show that conclusions for a given system depend on the chosen measure and null model. In Dynamics and Emergence, we review deterministic and stochastic systems, including the gradient, Hamiltonian, and local triad models, and examine how noise, topology, and competing mechanisms determine whether balance emerges. In Implications, we survey consequences for belief formation, opinion contagion dynamics, polarization, international relations, and markets. Across domains, the evidence is strongest for local triadic stability and weakest for system-wide balance. Balance is not synonymous with harmony: balanced structures can reflect cohesion, polarization, or persistent conflict. Its empirical relevance is well supported in some reciprocal social systems, but remains less settled in biological and other complex networks.

</details>


### [166] [Reversing the text is not reversing the path: a fixed-text measure without a zero, and an assembly-order comparison that vanishes identically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23049)
*Y. -H. Taguchi*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出固定文本上的 Δ=[log P(x)−log P(x̃)]/L 缺乏定理保证的零点，转而利用句子全排列下的精确反对称性构造出一个无量纲、有界、零居中的零点，并量化因果顺序对中位秩的贡献仅占 8%–15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涨落定理（fluctuation theorems）只在轨迹系综上赋予熵产生（entropy production）以零点，而单条文本既非分布也非动力学，且相关文本由其他模型生成，因此固定文本的 Δ 表达式的参照零点无定理支撑。

Method: 对文本的 n 个句子在全部 n! 种顺序下打分，利用反转操作 π→π∘rev 为保测对合（measure-preserving involution）导致 Δ(π)=−Δ(π∘rev) 严格成立，使取值为零中心；另以掩码模型（masked model）的任意顺序链式法则给出真实对数概率，比较左到右与右到左装配顺序得到 σ。

Result: 在五个自回归评估器（124M 至 4B 参数）上，中位秩（midrank）与零假设距离中仅 8%–15% 可归因于因果顺序，其余来自文本被生成时的原有排列。

Conclusion: 固定文本量不构成熵产生：Δ 的零点不随表达式一同迁移；σ 在评估器条件概率来自联合分布时恒为零，非零读数反映评估器内部不自洽（incompatibility）而非文本性质。

Abstract: For an ensemble of trajectories the fluctuation theorems give the entropy production its meaning and zero. The same expression has recently been evaluated on one fixed text, $Δ= [\log P(x) - \log P(\tilde x)]/L$ with $\tilde x$ the text in reversed order, read as an entropy production separating causally from arbitrarily ordered prose. A single text is neither a distribution nor a dynamics, and the texts at issue were produced by other models. No theorem fixes where the zero of a fixed-text quantity lies: the expression carries over from an ensemble, its zero does not.
  We supply a zero from an exact antisymmetry. Scoring a text's $n$ sentences in all $n!$ orders, reversal carries $π$ to $π\circ \mathrm{rev}$, a measure-preserving involution, so $Δ(π) = -Δ(π\circ \mathrm{rev})$ identically and the values are centred at zero for any score. The authored arrangement's midrank is then unitless, bounded, and 0.50 for a score blind to order. Across five autoregressive evaluators from 124M to 4B parameters, and taking the prompt-defined labels at face value, 8% to 15% of the median midrank's distance from the null is attributable to causal order and the rest to the arrangement being the order the passage was produced in.
  The thermodynamic reading needs a comparison whose zero does hold on the text read, and a masked model supplies one: the any-order chain rule gives a genuine log-probability, and its left-to-right and right-to-left orders for one text give $σ$, a comparison of two assembly orders. Both run from the empty sequence to the same text, so it is not a path against its time reverse. It vanishes identically when the evaluator's conditionals come from a joint, and a non-zero reading reports the evaluator's incompatibility, not the text. Neither is a fixed-text entropy production, for different reasons.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

### [167] [Construction and regularity of lump solutions of the modified KP-I equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22745)
*Tianwei Qiu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该文将修正Kadomtsev–Petviashvili-I（mKP-I）方程的标准与高阶lump解（lump solutions）的τ函数表示为积分形式的Gram行列式，并在τ函数为复值、无法借助Hermite Gram矩阵正定性保证非零的情形下，给出解正则性的显式谱条件与代数衰减估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mKP-I方程的τ函数为复值，其非零性不能像KP-I方程那样由Hermite Gram矩阵（Hermitian Gram matrix）的正定性直接推出，因此标准的正则性论证失效，需要新的分析工具来刻画lump解的存在性与光滑性。

Method: 把标准与高阶lump解的τ函数写成积分形式的Gram行列式（Gram determinants），据此推导解正则性的显式谱条件（spectral conditions）；对高阶lump解，证明sharp条件仅依赖谱参数与指标向量（index vector）的最大分量。

Result: 得到了保证lump解正则性的显式谱条件；高阶lump解情形下的sharp条件只取决于谱参数和指标向量的最大分量；并证明lump解及其所有空间导数在每个空间方向上代数衰减（algebraically decay），且在紧时间区间上一致成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为复值τ函数情形下的mKP-I lump解提供了基于Gram行列式的统一构造与正则性判据，并给出其空间衰减的定量刻画，补充了KP-I方程既有结果所不能覆盖的情形。

Abstract: We express the $τ$-functions of standard and higher-order lump solutions of the modified Kadomtsev--Petviashvili-I (mKP-I) equation as Gram determinants in integral form. Unlike the KP-I equation, the mKP-I $τ$-function is complex-valued, and its nonvanishing does not follow from the positive definiteness of a Hermitian Gram matrix. We derive explicit spectral conditions for the regularity of these solutions; for higher-order lumps, the sharp condition depends only on the spectral parameter and the largest entry of the index vector. We also prove that the lump solutions and all their spatial derivatives decay algebraically in every spatial direction, uniformly on compact time intervals.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [What Do LLMs Use to Judge Synthesis Recipes? An Experimental Analysis on 240 Solid-State Syntheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22623)
*Derek van Tilborg,Laura-Bianca Pasca,Harry Sevi,Kyle Reeves,Thomas Loux,Aikaterini Flessa Savvidou,Varincka Jean-Gilles,Marilou Carouge,Joonatan E. M. Laulainen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该文以240个实验的无机氧化物固相合成基准，评估大语言模型（large language model, LLM）`GPT-5.6-Sol`在固定合成配方库上的零样本排序（zero-shot ranking）能力，发现其排序主要受热学特征驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无机材料发现（inorganic materials discovery）的瓶颈已从材料设计转向合成（synthesis），而LLM被用于推荐合成条件时缺乏其实验证据与偏差评估。

Method: 对八种已知与未知无机氧化物开展240次合成实验构建基准；对同一目标氧化物的配方两两配对，由`GPT-5.6-Sol`选择预期目标相纯度（target-phase purity）更高者，再用正则化Bradley-Terry模型（regularized Bradley-Terry model）聚合为排序；并以X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）实测纯度作参照，通过选择性屏蔽输入信息进行消融。

Result: 零样本排序的两两准确率（pairwise accuracy）为0.72；排序准确率与各目标纯度—温度相关性同步（Spearman ρ=0.91，p=0.002）；在六个热驱动目标上，隐藏全部化学身份影响甚微（0.68），而隐藏温度则降至0.56；已知目标表现高于未知目标，但组间差异统计上不具结论性。

Conclusion: 排序主要由热学特征（thermal features）主导，反应化学（reaction chemistry）起调节作用；LLM可定位为热学信息先验（thermally informed prior），并凸显建立更多经实验验证的合成基准的必要性。

Abstract: Inorganic materials discovery is increasingly limited by synthesis rather than by material design. Large language models (LLMs) are being adopted to propose synthesis conditions with little experimental evidence of their capabilities and biases. Here, we carry out an experimental synthesis benchmark of 240 experiments across eight known and unknown inorganic oxides and subsequently evaluate an LLM as a zero-shot ranker over fixed banks of solid-state synthesis recipes. For each pair of recipes for the same oxide target, `GPT-5.6-Sol` selects the recipe expected to yield higher target-phase purity. We aggregate these pairwise preferences using a regularized Bradley-Terry model to obtain a ranked list of synthesis recipes for a given target material. By comparison with the actual purity, obtained through X-ray diffraction, we measure `GPT-5.6-Sol`'s performance as a zero-shot ranker (0.72 pairwise accuracy). By selective removal of the information exposed to the model during this ranking, we conclude that ranking is driven predominantly by thermal features, with reaction chemistry modulating the effect. Ranking accuracy tracks the purity-temperature correlation by target (Spearman ρ= 0.91\), \(p = 0.002\), and on the six thermally-driven targets withholding all chemical identity hardly impacts performance (0.68) compared to withholding temperature (0.56). Ranking performance was higher on known than unknown targets, but the group-level difference was not statistically conclusive. The methodological choices highlight the need for more experimentally validated synthesis benchmarks and position LLMs as a thermally informed prior.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>
