### [169] [Signatures of Leptophilic Baryogenesis at Muon Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20774)
*Hooman Davoudiasl,Ian M. Lewis,Miguel A. Soto Alcaraz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种由3个希格斯场构成的“希格斯三驾马车（Higgs Troika）”模型，可在无需强一阶电弱相变（strong first order electroweak phase transition）的条件下产生宇宙重子不对称（baryon asymmetry of the Universe），并探讨其在未来缪子对撞机（muon collider）上的可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电弱重子生成通常依赖强一阶电弱相变，该文旨在寻求规避这一要求的重子生成机制，同时避免与味物理（flavor physics）约束产生冲突，并为新标量寻找可探测的对撞机场所。

Method: 采用3个希格斯场的希格斯三驾马车模型，新希格斯场与标准模型（Standard Model）费米子的耦合遵循“自发味破坏（Spontaneous Flavor Violation, SFV）”框架以规避味物理限制；文中考虑SFV的“亲轻子（leptophilic）”变体，并评估未来多TeV质心能量缪子对撞机上的潜在信号。

Result: 多TeV缪子对撞机被视为搜寻亲轻子希格斯三驾马车重子生成机制的最优场所；对新标量信号（signal）的预期探测范围（projected reach）可覆盖该重子生成模型的关键组成部分，且对应尺度满足宇宙学（cosmology）要求。

Conclusion: 亲轻子SFV希格斯三驾马车为宇宙重子不对称提供了一条不依赖强一阶电弱相变的可检验路径，其关键成分可由未来缪子对撞机在符合宇宙学约束的参数区间内加以探测。

Abstract: The baryon asymmetry of the Universe may be produced in a model with 3 Higgs fields, a Higgs Troika, without the need for a strong first order electroweak phase transition. The couplings of the new Higgs fields to Standard Model fermions are assumed in accord with the "Spontaneous Flavor Violation (SFV)" framework, in order to avoid large deviations from flavor physics constraints. We consider a leptophilic version of SFV and outline its potential signatures at future muon colliders, envisioned to be multi-TeV partonic center of momentum machines. Hence, they provide optimal venues to search for the leptophilic Higgs Troika baryogenesis mechanism. The projected reach for signals of the new scalars allows for probing the key components of the baryogenesis model at scales that accommodate cosmological requirements.

</details>


### [170] [Feebly-Interacting Particle Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21719)
*Jonathan L. Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 弱相互作用粒子（feebly-interacting particles, FIPs）已从粒子物理的边缘议题转变为主流研究方向，本文综述其三重动机并简介若干前沿进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FIPs 地位提升由三点驱动：其一是新粒子的两大可能类别之一；其二，作为暗物质候选者，其动机强度不亚于弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMPs）；其三，FIPs 与粒子物理中的重要问题相关联。

Method: 采用综述性（review）方式，先系统梳理上述三类动机，再对若干具体研究方向作简要进展评述。

Result: 综述覆盖的研究领域包括类轴子粒子（axion-like particles）、暗光子（dark photons）及其他规范玻色子（gauge bosons）、X17 反常（X17 anomaly）、quirks，以及前向物理实验设施（Forward Physics Facility）。

Conclusion: FIPs 已成为粒子物理主流方向，其动机多元且与暗物质及粒子物理核心问题紧密相连，相关理论与实验方向近期均有显著进展。

Abstract: In the last several years, feebly-interacting particles (FIPs) have moved from "exotica" to the mainstream of particle physics. This development is fueled by three motivations: FIPs are one of two broad possibilities for new particles, FIPs are as well motivated by dark matter as WIMPs, and FIPs are connected to important problems in particle physics. I review these motivations and then briefly highlight recent progress in a few areas, including axion-like particles, dark photons and other gauge bosons, the X17 anomaly, quirks, and the Forward Physics Facility.

</details>


### [171] [Plugging the Pion Chimney](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21728)
*Nikita Blinov,Andrew M. Evans,Shirley Weishi Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，质量处于中性π介子质量附近的胶子耦合轴子样粒子（ALP）窗口“π介子烟囱（pion chimney）”此前未被任何搜寻覆盖，而利用现有LHC数据（ATLAS单喷注（monojet）搜寻与ATLAS包含光子测量）可将其闭合至ALP有效理论截断能标，截断以上则由最简紫外完备（ultraviolet completion）中色态搜寻排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeV至GeV量级的重QCD轴子（heavy QCD axion）与胶子耦合ALP是理论上受青睐且已被广泛搜寻的目标，但中性π介子质量附近的“π介子烟囱”因ALP与π⁰强烈混合、实验特征重叠而逃避了所有搜寻，形成探测空白。

Method: 关键在于混合会改变两个质量本征态的寿命但不使其相等；在LHC能量下粒子被充分助推（boosted），寿命足以决定其是逃逸探测器还是在探测器内部衰变。据此，ATLAS单喷注搜寻约束逃逸情形，ATLAS包含光子测量约束内部衰变情形，二者联合覆盖该窗口。文中另论证K⁺宽度限制无法触及该窗口（因ALP与π⁰通过相同选择条件），并从实测π±–π⁰质量劈裂导出独立的纯运动学约束。

Result: ATLAS单喷注搜寻与包含光子测量联合可将π介子烟囱排除至ALP有效理论的截断能标；在截断能标以上，针对最简紫外完备模型中色态的LHC搜寻排除了剩余参数空间。同时表明K⁺宽度给出的限制无法到达该烟囱区域。

Conclusion: 中性π介子质量附近的ALP窗口可由现有LHC数据完全闭合，无需新数据；混合导致的寿命差异与LHC高能助推是区分逃逸与衰变两类信号的核心机制，并补充了来自π±–π⁰质量劈裂的独立运动学约束。

Abstract: Heavy QCD axions and other gluon-coupled axion-like particles (ALPs) with masses from MeV to GeV are well motivated and have been searched for extensively. However, a window around the neutral pion mass, the so-called pion chimney, has escaped every search. There the ALP mixes strongly with the $π^0$ and therefore shares its experimental signatures. We show that the chimney can be closed with existing LHC data. The key point is that mixing changes the lifetimes of the two mass eigenstates but does not make them equal, and at LHC energies the states are boosted enough for the lifetime to decide whether they escape the detector or decay inside it. The ATLAS monojet search constrains the first case and the ATLAS inclusive photon measurement the second, and together they exclude the chimney up to the cutoff of the ALP effective theory. Above the cutoff, LHC searches for the colored states of the simplest ultraviolet completions exclude the rest. We also show that the bound from the $K^+$ width does not reach the chimney, because an ALP passes the same selection as a $π^0$, and we derive an independent, purely kinematic constraint from the measured $π^\pm$-$π^0$ mass splitting.

</details>


### [172] [Probing $τ$ lepton dipole moments at multi-TeV muon colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21813)
*Dario Buttazzo,Gabriele Levati,Yang Ma,Fabio Maltoni,Paride Paradisi,ZeQiang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文评估未来多TeV缪子对撞机（muon collider）对τ轻子电磁与弱偶极矩的灵敏度，基于六维标准模型有效场论（SMEFT），发现伴随希格斯产生道灵敏度最强，10 TeV、10 ab⁻¹下可给出10⁻⁵量级的磁偶极矩与10⁻²⁰ e·cm量级的电偶极矩约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ轻子偶极矩（dipole moments）是检验标准模型（Standard Model）与探寻新物理的重要探针，现有实验约束较弱，需评估未来高能对撞机的探测潜力。

Method: 在六维标准模型有效场论框架下，考察μ⁺μ⁻湮灭产生τ⁺τ⁻、矢量玻色子散射（vector-boson scattering）、电弱玻色子伴随τ产生及辐射希格斯衰变H→τ⁺τ⁻γ等多个通道，并计入偶极算符Wilson系数（Wilson coefficients）的二次项贡献。

Result: 高能下偶极诱导的二次贡献显著，μ⁺μ⁻→τ⁺τ⁻H道因能量增强的接触相互作用（contact interactions）灵敏度最高；10 TeV、10 ab⁻¹时95%置信度下联合灵敏度为|Δa_τ|<1.2×10⁻⁵、|d_τ|<6.6×10⁻²⁰ e·cm、|Δa_τ^Z|<1.8×10⁻⁵、|d_τ^Z|<1.0×10⁻¹⁹ e·cm。

Conclusion: 相较FCC-ee预期，缪子对撞机对电偶极矩（electric dipole moments）灵敏度显著更强，两者对磁偶极矩（magnetic dipole moments）互为补充；后续需纳入真实探测器效应与系统误差研究。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of future multi-TeV muon colliders to the electromagnetic and weak dipole moments of the $τ$ lepton within the dimension-six Standard Model Effective Field Theory. We consider $τ^+τ^-$ production through $μ^+μ^-$ annihilation and vector-boson scattering, electroweak-boson-associated $τ$ production, and the radiative Higgs decay $H\toτ^+τ^-γ$. At high energies, the dipole-induced contributions quadratic in the Wilson coefficients become particularly important, with associated Higgs production, $μ^+μ^-\toτ^+τ^-H$, providing the strongest sensitivity through energy-enhanced contact interactions. The remaining channels offer complementary probes of the underlying dipole operators. For a $10$~TeV muon collider with an integrated luminosity of $10\,\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, we obtain combined sensitivities at the nominal $95\%$ confidence level of $|Δa_τ|<1.2\times10^{-5}$, $|d_τ|<6.6\times10^{-20}\,e\,\mathrm{cm}$, $|Δa_τ^Z|<1.8\times10^{-5}$, and $|d_τ^Z|<1.0\times10^{-19}\,e\,\mathrm{cm}$. Compared with FCC-ee projections, the muon collider offers substantially stronger sensitivity to the electric dipole moments, while both facilities offer complementary probes of magnetic dipole moments. These results motivate further studies incorporating realistic detector effects and systematic uncertainties.

</details>


### [173] [Triangle singularity with different dynamical couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22424)
*Jun Wang,Xiao-Hai Liu,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究顶点动力学耦合（dynamical couplings of the vertices）对圈振幅中三角形奇点（triangle singularity, TS）表现形式的影响，通过12种自旋-宇称量子数组合并结合 η(1405)→π⁺π⁻π⁰ 基准运动学，发现同一圈拓扑可产生窄峰、被压低的峰与尖点状（cusp-like）三种不同线型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识通常以Landau条件判断TS增强是否可见，本文旨在检验顶点动力学结构是否会重塑TS的可观测表现。

Method: 枚举覆盖 PPS、VPP、VVP、VPA、VVV、VVA、VVS 有效顶点（effective vertices）的12种内外部自旋-宇称组合，用 Passarino–Veltman 分解将圈振幅约化为标量积分 A₀、B₀、C₀，其 Lorentz 标量系数编码顶点动力学；以 η(1405)→π⁺π⁻π⁰ 经 K*K̄K 圈并含 f₀(980) 中间共振的质量与运动学作为统一数值基准。

Result: 同一圈拓扑在十二种自旋-宇称指派下呈现三种由线型（lineshape）区分的现象：由 C₀ 贡献主导的窄峰；B₀ 与 C₀ 贡献相当的被压低的峰；以及领头 Landau 对数系数在物理TS点消失所形成的尖点状轮廓。

Conclusion: TS 增强的可见性并非仅由 Landau 条件决定，而是被圈跃迁中涉及的详细动力学所重塑。

Abstract: We carry out a systematic study of the impact of dynamical couplings of the vertices on the manifestations of the triangle singularity (TS) in the loop amplitude. Twelve combinations of external and internal spin-parity quantum numbers, covering the $PPS$, $VPP$, $VVP$, $VPA$, $VVV$, $VVA$, and $VVS$ effective vertices, are enumerated. Each loop amplitude is reduced via the Passarino--Veltman decomposition into scalar integrals $A_{0}$, $B_{0}$, and $C_{0}$, whose Lorentz-scalar coefficients encode the dynamical content of the vertices. Using the masses and kinematics of the $η(1405)\toπ^{+}π^{-}π^{0}$ transition via the $K^{*}\bar{K}K$ loop with the intermediate $f_{0}(980)$ resonance in the $π^+π^-$ invariant spectrum as a common numerical benchmark for the twelve spin-parity assignments, we find that the same loop topology produces three distinct phenomena which manifest through the lineshapes, i.e. a narrow peak dominated by the $C_{0}$ contribution; a suppressed peak in which the $B_{0}$ and $C_{0}$ function contributions are comparable; and a cusp-like profile in which the coefficient of the leading Landau logarithm vanishes at the physical TS point. These results indicate that the visibility of a TS enhancement is not fixed by the Landau condition alone but reshaped by the detailed dynamics involved in the loop transitions.

</details>


### [174] [An exact separability criterion for the $J/ψΛ$ spin state in $Λ_b^0\to J/ψ\,Λ$ decays at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22701)
*Yujie Li,Xiaofan Hu,Liming Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出含LHC极化效应的J/ψΛ自旋态精确可分离判据，并提出剖面似然比检验（profiled likelihood ratio test）用于探测纠缠，其在理想赝实验（pseudoexperiments）中达到相同拒绝功效所需信号事例数约为负度检验（negativity test）与角矩检验的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Λ_b^0→J/ψΛ衰变产物J/ψΛ自旋态是否纠缠是量子纠缠研究在高能物理中的关键问题，而LHC环境下Λ_b^0极化会影响纠缠判据的适用性，需要更精确的判据与更高效的检验方法。

Method: 在四振幅族{a_±,b_±}框架下，计入LHC环境中由Λ产生角θ与极化度P_b描述的条件，推导出系统可分离的充要条件；并构造剖面似然比检验，在完整五维角分析中探测纠缠。

Result: 可分离的充要条件为 (1+P_b cosθ)a_+b_+ = (1−P_b cosθ)a_−b_− = P_b sinθ b_+b_− = 0。理想赝实验显示，在3σ与5σ显著性下，所提检验以约一半的信号事例数即达到90%拒绝功效（相对于负度检验及基于角矩M_7、M_9的检验）。

Conclusion: 结果支持在现有样本规模上获得纠缠证据、并在更大样本下实现纠缠观测的前景。

Abstract: We derive an exact separability criterion for the spin state of the $J/ψΛ$ pair produced in $Λ_b^0\to J/ψ(\toμ^+μ^-)\,Λ(\to pπ^-)$ decays, taking into account the $Λ_b^0$ polarization at the LHC. Within the four-amplitude family $\{a_\pm,b_\pm\}$, the system is separable if and only if $(1+P_b\cosθ)a_+b_+=(1-P_b\cosθ)a_-b_-=P_b\sinθb_+b_-=0$, where $θ$ is the $Λ$ production angle defined in the LHC environment. We propose a profiled likelihood ratio test to probe entanglement in the full five-dimensional angular analysis. Idealized pseudoexperiments compare this test with a negativity test and a test based on the angular moments $M_7$ and $M_9$ for rejecting the $a_+=b_-=0$ hypothesis. At both $3σ$ and $5σ$ significance, the proposed test achieves a $90\%$ rejection power with about half as many signal events as the other two methods. These results support the prospects for entanglement evidence with samples on the existing scale and for observation with larger samples.

</details>


### [175] [Scalar inelastic scattering of pseudo-Majorana dark matter through a radion portal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22890)
*Ruifeng Leng,Kirtimaan A. Mohan,Keping Xie,Hong-Hao Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文以 radion portal 框架解释 LZ 实验高反冲事件 LZ230616，提出 pseudo-Majorana 暗物质可通过主导的标量跃迁而非弹性散射产生该信号，并给出可行基准点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规 pseudo-Dirac 情形下弹性散射受限，难以解释 LZ 高反冲事件；pseudo-Majorana 结构可压制弹性散射、保留主导标量跃迁，为 LZ230616 提供新的粒子物理诠释路径。

Method: 采用 radion portal 下的 pseudo-Majorana 暗物质模型，结合吸热运动学（endothermic kinematics）去除低能反冲、核响应塑造高反冲谱，并将 LZ 事件计数与 Born 级热遗迹丰度（thermal relic abundance）联合求解。

Result: 得到基准解：m_χ=1 TeV、δ=340 keV、m_r=30 GeV，落入 LZ 已发表的自旋无关 O_1^s 区间，并与 OPAL、CMS 限制相容；简化 HL-LHC 双光子投影可对其检验，太阳中微子搜寻提供互补检验，其高能子体代理率与 IceCube 直接 b b̄、τ⁺τ⁻ 模板重建的速率量级相当。

Conclusion: radion portal 的 pseudo-Majorana 暗物质可自洽解释 LZ230616；IceCube-Gen2 的示意性重标度预示未来灵敏度，但模型特定评估仍需 radion 级联中微子谱与探测器响应。

Abstract: We investigate a radion portal for pseudo-Majorana dark matter as an interpretation of the high-recoil event LZ230616. In contrast to the familiar pseudo-Dirac regime, the pseudo-Majorana structure allows a dominant scalar transition while suppressing elastic scattering. Endothermic kinematics remove low-energy recoils, while the nuclear response shapes the surviving high-recoil spectrum. Combining the accepted LZ event count with the Born-level thermal relic abundance yields a reference solution with $m_χ=1$ TeV, $δ=340$ keV, and $m_r=30$ GeV. This solution lies within the published LZ spin-independent $\mathcal{O}_1^s$ interval and remains compatible with existing OPAL and CMS constraints, while a simplified HL-LHC diphoton projection could probe it. Solar neutrino searches offer a complementary test. The benchmark's energetic-daughter proxy rates are comparable in magnitude to the rate scales reconstructed from IceCube's direct $b\bar{b}$ and $τ^+τ^-$ templates. An illustrative IceCube-Gen2 rescaling suggests future sensitivity, while a model-specific assessment requires the radion-cascade neutrino spectra and detector response.

</details>


### [176] [Universal Mass Equation for Equal-Quantum Excited-States Sets~III](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22945)
*L. David Roper,Igor Strakovsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于简单的对数普适质量方程（logarithmic Universal Mass Equation），评估PDG2026所列量子数不符合常规 \(q\bar{q}\) 结构的重介子态，并利用格点量子色动力学（LQCD）预测检验标量 \(0^{++}\) 与张量 \(2^{++}\) 胶球（glueball）系列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDG2026 报告了一系列量子数不允许标准 \(q\bar{q}\) 组态的介子，需解释其非 \(q\bar{q}\) 本质；同时胶球（glueball）候选态的鉴别缺乏系统质量预言，因此有必要对重非 \(q\bar{q}\) 介子态进行统一描述。

Method: 采用对数普适质量方程，对重非 \(q\bar{q}\) 介子态进行计算；并结合已有格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD, LQCD）预言，评估标量 \(0^{++}\) 与张量 \(2^{++}\) 胶球系列。此外，对部分介子态的可能误认（misidentification）情形进行讨论。

Result: 该方法给出了重非 \(q\bar{q}\) 介子态的质量预言，并可对胶球系列与常规介子态进行对照；同时指出某些已报道介子态存在被误识别的可能性。

Conclusion: 对数普适质量方程可用于预言需未来实验检验的重非 \(q\bar{q}\) 态，并为胶球鉴别及介子态误认问题提供判据。

Abstract: Using our simple logarithmic Universal Mass Equation, we considered heavy non-$q\bar{q}$ meson states. PDG2026 reported a series of mesons with quantum numbers not allowed for standard $q\bar{q}$ states. Using LQCD predictions, we evaluated a scalar $0^{++}$ and tensor $2^{++}$ glueball series for which predictions are available. This approach allows us to predict heavy states that require future experimental studies. Additionally, we consider the possible misidentification of some meson states.

</details>


### [177] [Loop-induced $gg\to ZH$ at NLO+PS for LHC simulations: exact, fast scale and PDF event weights, theory uncertainties, and the limits of rescaled LO+PS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23020)
*Licheng Zhang,Christopher Palmer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在 QCD 次领头阶（NLO）与部分子簇射（parton showers）匹配框架下研究环致 $gg\to ZH$ 产生过程，提出环振幅缓存与 Born 抑制等加速与统计改进手段，并构建基于梯度提升分类器（gradient-boosted classifiers）的带电轻子与中微子通道修正模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环致 $gg\to ZH$ 过程在增强区（boosted regime）的 NLO+PS 预测对理论不确定性敏感，且现有 LO+PS 样本难以通过单一 inclusive $K$ 因子复现其谱形，需要系统评估并复用已有样本。

Method: 使用公开软件在 $\sqrt{s}=13$ 与 13.6 TeV 下计算 NLO QCD 匹配部分子簇射的 $gg\to ZH$ 产生，采用精确环振幅缓存（loop-amplitude cache）与 Born 抑制；并以梯度提升分类器集合构建 CMS/ATLAS 风格 vetoe 源的分通道修正，含校准、目标理论变化与迁移不确定性。

Result: 环振幅缓存在 450 组标度与 PDF 权重下使事例产生时间缩短 26 倍；13.6 TeV 下顶夸克质量方案变化在 $p_\mathrm{T}^V\ge150$ GeV 的中心希格斯 STXS 区间达 32–50%，主导统计精确的区间。修正后在多数分布上显著优于 inclusive 重标度，但未实现一致闭合；带电轻子校准比较中 STXS 中位不确定度为 17%（CMS 与 ATLAS 风格），低于固定 $K$ 下 LO 标度的 28% 与 26%。

Conclusion: 该设置、参考结果及分源分通道模型可支持新的 NLO+PS 产生与既有样本的受限复用；修正无法恢复被耗尽的硬反冲（hard-recoil）事例群，最高 ATLAS 风格 $p_\mathrm{T}^H$ 区间即使在上边界仍低于 NLO+PS，且比较未涵盖完整目标理论预算。

Abstract: We study loop-induced $gg\to ZH$ production with charged-lepton and neutrino $Z$ decays at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD matched to parton showers, using public software at $\sqrt{s}=13$ and 13.6 TeV. An exact loop-amplitude cache reduces event-generation time by a factor of 26 with 450 scale and PDF weights, while Born suppression improves boosted-event statistics without changing the weighted prediction. We present inclusive results for both channels and charged-lepton differential and STXS Stage 1.3 predictions with theory uncertainties. At 13.6 TeV, fixed-order top-quark mass-scheme variations transferred to NLO+PS dominate the statistically precise central-Higgs STXS bins for $p_\mathrm{T}^V\ge150$ GeV, reaching 32-50%. An inclusive $K$ factor does not reproduce the boosted and jet-dependent NLO+PS spectra of the tested LO+PS samples, and the differences depend strongly on the shower prescription. For specified CMS- and ATLAS-style vetoed sources at 13.6 TeV, we construct separate charged-lepton and neutrino corrections using ensembles of gradient-boosted classifiers, with calibration, target-theory variations and transfer uncertainties. Independent tests show substantial improvement over inclusive rescaling in most tested distributions, but not uniform closure. In charged-lepton calibration comparisons, the median STXS uncertainty from transported NLO scales and transfer terms is 17% for both the CMS- and ATLAS-style corrections, compared with LO scale uncertainties of 28% and 26% at fixed $K$. These comparisons do not include the complete target-theory budget. The corrections do not restore the depleted hard-recoil population; the highest ATLAS-style $p_\mathrm{T}^H$ bin stays below NLO+PS even at the upper edge of its band. The setup, reference results, and source- and channel-specific models support new NLO+PS generation and reuse of existing samples within stated limits.

</details>


### [178] [Holographic Approaches to Hadron Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35818)
*Luigi Cappiello,Anton Rebhan,Shigeki Sugimoto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文是关于全息方法（holographic approaches）在量子色动力学（QCD）与强子结构（hadron structure）中应用的导论性综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为全息QCD（holographic QCD）领域提供入门介绍与总体概览，连接弦理论（string theory）基础与其强子现象学预测。

Method: 综述性方法：先介绍全息QCD的弦理论（string-theoretic）基础，再比较自上而下（top-down）与自下而上（bottom-up）两类模型对介子（mesons）、重子（baryons）和胶球（glueballs）的预测。

Result: 重点评述基于Witten低能Yang-Mills模型中探针味膜（probe flavor branes）的自上而下模型，以及基于五维反德西特空间（five-dimensional anti-de Sitter space）的自下而上最小模型；后者能一致地实现整体对称性（global symmetries）及其反常（anomalies），并使高能行为（high-energy behavior）更好地匹配真实QCD。

Conclusion: 全息方法为QCD与强子结构研究提供了自上而下与自下而上互补的建模途径，后者在保持对称性与反常一致性的同时更贴合真实QCD的高能行为。

Abstract: We give an introduction to and an overview of holographic approaches to QCD and hadron structure. After an introduction to the string-theoretic foundations of holographic QCD, we review the predictions of top-down and bottom-up models for the phenomenology of mesons, baryons, and glueballs. We focus on the top-down model following from probe flavor branes in the Witten model of low-energy Yang-Mills theory and, in the more phenomenological approach of bottom-up models, mostly on minimal models based on five-dimensional anti-de Sitter space, which also implement global symmetries and their anomalies consistently, but allow the high-energy behavior to better match real QCD.

</details>


### [179] [A new angle on coherence-violating logarithms in color-singlet processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21763)
*Mrinal Dasgupta,Alexander Fraley,Pier Francesco Monni,Saad Nabeebaccus*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种新的重求和形式，用于处理Drell-Yan过程中快度间隔内横向能量流的相干破坏对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有相干破坏对数（coherence-violating logarithms）重求和仅在形式上的小间隔极限成立，而现象学关心的是间隔宽度与横向能量对数相当的区间，故需新形式。

Method: 构建可在间隔宽度与横向能量对数可比区域进行重求和的新表述，并在小间隔极限下验证其退化为已知最先进结果。

Result: 在小间隔极限下与已知重求和一致；但在中等间隔宽度下识别出可观的新修正，显著改变LHC应用的现象学图像。

Conclusion: 该工作将作为未来推广至一系列全局与非全局可观测量（global and non-global observables）的重要基础。

Abstract: We introduce a new formulation for the resummation of coherence-violating logarithms for the transverse energy flow in rapidity gaps in the Drell-Yan process. Our formalism allows us to resum coherence-violating logarithms in the gap cross section in the phenomenologically important regime where the gap size is comparable to the logarithm of the transverse energy. In the formal small gap limit, our result reduces to the known state-of-the-art resummation of coherence-violating logarithms for the Drell-Yan process. However, we identify sizable new corrections even for moderate gap widths that significantly modify the phenomenological picture for LHC applications. This work will form an important element of future extensions to a range of global and non-global observables.

</details>


### [180] [A Universal Axion in a Twin-QCD Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23015)
*Stuart Raby,Scott Watson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究轴子（axion）同时耦合可见与孪生QCD、由孪生重子（twin baryons）主导暗物质的多组分方案。借助格点QCD结果与两温度宇宙学描述，给出轴子质量增强及相对丰度的定量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质可能由粒子物理起源不同的多个组分构成；第二个强相互作用扇区既可增强轴子质量、又能保留其解决强CP问题（strong-CP problem）的作用，值得系统考察。

Method: 采用格点信息（lattice-informed）的QCD结果与早期宇宙的两温度（two-temperature）描述，考察暗扇区组分的宇宙学演化与相对丰度，并检验额外相对论自由度、强CP对齐与稳定孪生物质相互作用等约束。

Result: 对禁闭标度（confinement scale）为普通QCD五倍的均匀重标度孪生谱，轴子质量约增强25倍；代表性基准下轴子仅占观测暗物质的小部分，其余可由孪生重子贡献（需合适的原初重子不对称）。

Conclusion: 结果为混合轴子—孪生重子暗物质提供定量框架，并厘清其可行实现所需的粒子物理与宇宙学条件，包括热历史与宇宙结构形成约束。

Abstract: Dark matter may consist of multiple components with distinct particle-physics origins. We investigate a scenario in which a universal axion couples to both visible and twin QCD, with twin baryons providing a possible dominant dark-matter component. The presence of a second strongly interacting sector can substantially enhance the axion mass while preserving its role in resolving the strong-CP problem, provided the physical vacuum angles are appropriately aligned. For a representative uniformly rescaled twin spectrum with a confinement scale five times that of ordinary QCD, the axion mass is enhanced by approximately a factor of 25.
  Using lattice-informed QCD results and a two-temperature description of the early universe, we investigate the cosmological evolution and relative abundances of the dark-sector components. In a representative conditional numerical benchmark, axions account for a small percentage of the observed dark matter, while twin baryons could constitute the remainder for a suitable primordial baryon asymmetry. We also examine the implications for additional relativistic species, strong-CP alignment, and the interactions of stable twin matter.
  Our results provide a quantitative framework for exploring mixed axion--twin-baryon dark matter and identify the particle-physics and cosmological conditions necessary for a viable realization, including constraints from the thermal history and formation of cosmic structure.

</details>


### [181] [Complete Cosmological Collider Signatures from Right-handed Neutrino Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23220)
*Linghao Song,Jingtao You,Ming Cheng,Hong-Jian He,Xingang Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用完整模式函数与完整圈积分，首次精确计算了化学势增强下右手中微子圈在共线挤压构型中的软部分，并给出有限、闭合的复时钟系数（clock coefficient）解析公式及大化学势展开下的领头宇宙对撞机（cosmological collider, CC）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 右手中微子圈可借化学势增强产生可观测的宇宙对撞机信号，但此前缺乏对软部分费米子圈的精确计算，尤其是完整模式函数与完整圈积分下的有限振荡贡献。

Method: 在共线挤压构型（collinear squeezed configuration）下将右手中微子三角圈因子化为硬、软两部分，硬部分按树图子图处理；用完整模式函数与完整圈积分精确计算软部分，减去软圈被积函数的大动量渐近项并保留其对振荡的有限贡献，进而完成自旋-螺旋度（spinor-helicity）与 Schwinger-Keldysh 求和；并通过 Mellin 极点留数求和分析软部分结构。

Result: 得到复时钟系数的有限、闭合解析公式，以及大化学势展开下领头 CC 信号的紧凑公式；并表明每条软线上第一个局域极贡献某条 Schwinger-Keldysh 路径上渐近系数的一半，第二条软线上其余局域极提供另一半。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了化学势增强右手中微子圈 CC 信号的完整解析刻画，明确了软部分极点结构与时钟系数的来源，为相关宇宙对撞机观测检验提供理论基础。

Abstract: We study the complete cosmological collider (CC) signatures from right-handed neutrino loops with chemical-potential enhancement. These CC signals are induced by the inflaton-right-handed-neutrino interaction and can be extracted in the collinear squeezed configuration, which allows the right-handed-neutrino triangle loop to be factorized into the hard and soft parts. We precisely compute the soft part of the fermion loop for the first time by using the complete mode functions and the full loop integral, and treat the hard part as a tree-level subdiagram. We subtract the large-momentum asymptotic terms of the soft loop integrand and retain their finite contributions to the oscillation. We then evaluate the full spinor-helicity and Schwinger-Keldysh sums to obtain a finite, closed analytic formula for the complex clock coefficient. We further derive a compact formula for the leading CC signals in the large-chemical-potential expansion. We analyze the structure of the soft-part contribution by examining the sum of residues of the Mellin poles of the fermion propagators. For this, we demonstrate that the first local pole on each soft line contributes half of the asymptotic coefficient on one Schwinger-Keldysh path and the remaining local poles on the second soft line supply the other half.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Robust Passive Quantum Memory in Three Dimensions, Part I: Thermal and Zero-Temperature Perturbative Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21749)
*Patrick Hayden*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明 BDL 码族的一个轻微变体，可在“扰动—温度”相图的两条边界轴上（零温扰动轴与有温无扰动轴）随系统尺寸增长而保持稳定，从而将三维被动自纠正从无扰动情形推进到对抗弱量子扰动稳定的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维量子多体系统能否被动保护量子信息（passive protection）自 Kitaev 提出拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computation）以来一直是核心开放问题。BDL 虽已构造递归稳定子哈密顿量（stabilizer Hamiltonian）实现无扰动三维热自纠正（thermal self-correction），但其构造对量子扰动是否稳定（即便零温）仍属未决。

Method: 对 BDL 码族作轻微变体；零温稳定性通过在多尺度层级（multiscale hierarchy）上建立局部拓扑量子序（local topological quantum order），并采用常规 Bravyi-Hastings-Michalakis 判据的线性膨胀松弛（linearly dilated relaxation）来证明。

Result: 无扰动时系统在临界温度以下热稳定：与满足细致平衡（detailed balance）、耦合速率有界的单格点系统—热浴相互作用所产生的一般局部马尔可夫热浴（Markovian thermal bath）耦合时，记忆寿命呈拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）增长。零温下面对任意弱、超多项式衰减（superpolynomially decaying）的量子扰动，保持一致的体能隙（bulk spectral gap）、超多项式小的基态空间劈裂（ground-space splitting），以及对准局域缀饰逻辑可观测量（quasi-locally dressed logical observables）的超多项式长闭环幺正记忆寿命。

Conclusion: 该码族在相图两条边界轴上具备随尺寸增长的稳定性；作者在姊妹篇中结合本文工具与有限温世界线团簇展开（worldline cluster expansion），进一步建立正温扰动内部区域的被动自纠正。

Abstract: Whether a three-dimensional quantum many-body system with geometrically local, bounded-degree interactions can passively protect encoded quantum information has remained a central open question since Kitaev introduced topological quantum computation. Passive protection requires stability against both thermal noise at nonzero temperature and generic static Hamiltonian perturbations Balasubramanian, Davydova, and Lin (BDL) recently established unperturbed three-dimensional thermal self-correction using a recursively defined family of stabilizer Hamiltonians. However, they left open whether their construction remains stable against quantum perturbations, even at zero temperature. In this paper, we show that a slight variant of the BDL code family achieves stability along both boundary axes of the perturbation-temperature phase diagram as the system size grows. Following BDL, in the absence of perturbation it is thermally stable below a critical temperature, achieving a stretched-exponentially long memory lifetime when coupled to any local Markovian thermal bath generated by single-site system-bath interactions satisfying detailed balance with bounded coupling rates. At zero temperature under arbitrary weak, superpolynomially decaying quantum perturbations, the family maintains a uniform bulk spectral gap, superpolynomially small ground-space splitting, and a superpolynomially long closed-system unitary memory lifetime for quasi-locally dressed logical observables. To prove this zero-temperature stability, we establish local topological quantum order across the multiscale hierarchy using a linearly dilated relaxation of the usual Bravyi-Hastings-Michalakis criteria. In the companion paper, we combine the tools developed here with finite-temperature worldline cluster expansions to establish passive self-correction throughout the phase diagram's positive-temperature perturbed interior.

</details>


### [183] [Quantum Reference Frames, Locality, and Microcausality in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21768)
*Christopher J. Fewster,Leon Loveridge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 受相对论谱条件（relativistic spectrum condition）限制，闵可夫斯基空间平移的量子参考系（quantum reference frame）无法用投影值测度描述，且常不满足范数-1条件（norm-1 condition）；这使相对于该参考系描述的量子场论中严格局域性退化为几乎局域性，微观因果性（microcausality）变为随距离快速衰减的对易子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在考察当量子场论（quantum field theory）相对于量子参考系而非经典参考系表述时，局域性与因果性等基本结构会受到何种影响，从而检验传统公理体系在相对化情境下的适用性。

Method: 分析基于相对论谱条件对平移量子参考系的可描述性限制，考察范数-1条件的失效，并研究由有限多个几乎局域 Kraus 算子（almost local Kraus operators）构成的操作，例如相对化局域可观测量的非选择性测量（nonselective measurements）。

Result: 对一大类参考系态，严格局域性（strict locality）被几乎局域性（almost locality）取代，微观因果性被随间距快速衰减的对易子取代，等效于光锥（light cone）被参考系态的局域化密度抹平；此类操作满足近似的无信号界（no-signalling bound），其残差归因于参考系的局域化不确定性。

Conclusion: 最强操作形式的因果性——在所有物理可实现操作下的非扰动性（non-disturbance）——在相对化后是否仍成立，论文未作定论；但结论表明，相对于量子参考系表述的量子场论不能依赖传统公理，至少部分公理须由考虑参考系物理实际性质的关系性公理化（relational axiomatisation）替代。

Abstract: As a consequence of the relativistic spectrum condition, quantum reference frames for translations on Minkowski space are never described by projection-valued measures, and in many cases fail a weaker spacetime localisation property known as the norm-$1$ condition. This has several implications for the local and observable structure of quantum field theories described relative to even the best available frame: for a large class of frame states, strict locality is replaced by almost locality, and microcausality by commutators that decay rapidly with increasing separation, corresponding to an effective smearing of the light cone by the localisation density of the frame state. Operations built from finitely many almost local Kraus operators, for example nonselective measurements of relativised local observables, satisfy an approximate no-signalling bound, with a residue attributable to the localisation uncertainty of the frame. Whether causality in its strongest operational form -- non-disturbance under all physically realisable operations -- survives relativisation is left open. An upshot of our finding is that quantum field theories formulated relative to a quantum reference frame cannot rest on the traditional axioms, and some general relational axiomatisation must replace at least some of them by taking into account physically realistic properties of the frame.

</details>


### [184] [A Proposal for Quantum Reference Frames in Quantum Field Theory and their Experimental Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21740)
*Ivan Agullo,Fabio M. Mele*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在量子场论（quantum field theory）中构造以标量场局域模式为参考的量子参考系（quantum reference frames, QRFs），用于其平移对称性（shift symmetry），并借助相对论量子信息（relativistic quantum information）工具计算关系型可观测量的熵与关联度量，揭示其参考系依赖性与场论特征带来的新性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子参考系研究多针对有限自由度系统，缺乏对场论情形下局域参考系性质的系统分析；本文旨在探讨场论中局域参考系与有限自由度情形的本质差异及其对关系型可观测量（relational observables）的影响。

Method: 以标量场（scalar field）的局域模式（localized modes）作为平移对称性的参考系构造量子参考系，并引入相对论量子信息工具，显式计算关系型可观测量的熵（entropies）与关联度量（correlation measures），分析其参考系依赖性。

Result: 与有限自由度情形不同，场论中的局域量子参考系在物理相关态中必然为混合态（mixed），且通常与被描述的子系统存在关联（correlated）；这些特性以微妙方式影响关系型可观测量的性质。文中还提出可利用基于超冷原子（ultracold atoms）与量子流体（quantum fluids）的现有量子模拟器（quantum simulators）检验相关预言。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子参考系物理与模拟引力（analogue gravity）平台相联系，为在场论与量子模拟实验中检验参考系依赖性预言提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We construct quantum reference frames (QRFs) in quantum field theory, using localized modes of a scalar field as frames for its shift symmetry. We import tools from relativistic quantum information to explicitly compute entropies and correlation measures of relational observables, investigating their frame dependence and the new features arising from the field-theoretic character of the system. Unlike QRFs for systems with finitely many degrees of freedom, localized field-theoretic QRFs are necessarily mixed in physically relevant states and generically correlated with the subsystems they describe. These aspects of QRFs for fields affect the properties of relational observables in a subtle way. Furthermore, we put forward a proposal for testing these predictions with existing quantum simulators based on ultracold atoms and quantum fluids, thus connecting QRF physics with analogue gravity platforms.

</details>


### [185] [Fundamental physics with quantum sensors in space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21997)
*Jason Arakawa,Daniel J. Blumenthal,Oliver Buchmueller,Dmitry Budker,Luigi Cacciapuoti,Emily D. Caldwell,Andrei Derevianko,Benjamin P. Dix-Matthews,Michael A. Fedderke,Naceur Gaaloul,Susan Gardner,Jonah Glick,Paul Hamilton,Elena Jordan,Hidetoshi Katori,Peter Kurczynski,David R. Leibrandt,Anna L. Marchant,Indra Periwal,Igor Pikovski,Marianna S. Safronova,Christian Sanner,Sascha W. Schediwy,Volodymyr Takhistov,Yu-Cheng Tien,Yu-Dai Tsai,Jason R. Williams*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该会议论文集总结利用空间量子传感器（quantum sensors）探测超越标准模型物理的科学依据、现有能力与空间任务构想，涵盖暗物质、广义相对论检验及地基不可及频段引力波探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子钟、原子干涉仪、原子磁力计等量子传感器可作为超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）物理的灵敏探针；空间环境提供微重力、长基线、低环境噪声及变化引力势等地面难以获得的优势。

Method: 本文为 Aspen Center for Physics 于2026年2月22–27日举办的“Fundamental physics in space with quantum sensors”研讨会的会议论文集（proceedings），以综述形式梳理科学论证、现有技术能力与空间任务概念。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据或定量结果，主要汇总了暗物质探测、广义相对论（general relativity）检验以及地面无法覆盖频段引力波（gravitational waves）探测的研究方向与任务设想。

Conclusion: 空间量子传感器为超越标准模型物理研究提供了独特平台，可借助太阳、月球、行星及小行星等空间资源拓展探测手段；具体结论性判断需参考论文集正文。

Abstract: Quantum sensors such as atomic clocks, atom interferometers, and atomic magnetometers can be used as sensitive probes of physics beyond the Standard Model. Space offers microgravity, long baselines, low environmental noise, access to varying gravitational potentials, and unique opportunities involving the Sun, Moon, planets, and asteroids. These proceedings of the Aspen Center for Physics workshop on "Fundamental physics in space with quantum sensors," held February 22-27, 2026, summarize the scientific case, current capabilities, and space mission concepts for investigating dark matter, testing general relativity, and detecting gravitational waves in frequency bands inaccessible from the ground.

</details>


### [186] [A Geometric Framework for Local-to-Global Symmetry: Global Constraints on Local Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21898)
*Connor Terry Weatherly,Roel Tempelaar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个协变的“局部到全局（local-to-global）”表示框架，将局部对称性（local symmetry）数据重构为全局对称性（global symmetry）表示，并借此把全局对称性强制的动力学禁戒（dynamical exclusions）分解为按位点解析（site-resolved）的跃迁路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚集体分子体系（aggregate molecular systems）常被分解为单体、化学基团等功能结构单元，这隐含假设局部结构在聚集体中仍有意义；本文追问局部结构对称性与聚集体整体对称性的关系，以及全局对称性对局部对称性扇区（symmetry sectors）间跃迁施加的约束。

Method: 基于带标架（framed）的局部对称性数据构造协变局部到全局表示，证明其重构映射与一个全局量子表示（quantum representation）交织（intertwine）；通过确定哪些全局对称性扇区与各局部扇区重叠，并借助Frobenius互反律（Frobenius reciprocity），由局部与全局对称群的常规特征标表（character tables）计算这些禁戒，其中内积的特征标值按局部结构在聚集体中的相对位置与取向配对。

Result: 全局对称性强制（symmetry-enforced）的动力学禁戒可被解析为位点分辨的跃迁路径，且可由局部与全局对称群的标准化特征标表直接确定；尽管动机来自分子聚集体，该构造适用于由环境变换群（ambient transformation group）的齐性空间（homogeneous space）建模的任意几何。

Conclusion: 局部结构的对称性与聚集体全局对称性通过协变重构映射相关联，全局对称性约束可在局部扇区层面被精确分解，为分析聚集体中的对称性禁戒跃迁提供了基于特征标理论的通用工具。

Abstract: Aggregate molecular systems are routinely understood by decomposing them into local structural units, such as monomers in J/H-aggregates, chemical moieties, motifs, and functional groups. Implicit in such decompositions is the assumption that these local structures remain meaningful within the aggregate. Here, we ask how the symmetries of constituent structures relate to aggregate symmetry, and how this relation determines the constraints that global symmetry imposes on transitions between local symmetry sectors. We construct a covariant local-to-global representation from framed local symmetry data and show that its reconstruction map intertwines with a global quantum representation. By identifying which global symmetry sectors overlap each local symmetry sector, we resolve symmetry-enforced dynamical exclusions into site-resolved pathways. Through Frobenius reciprocity, these exclusions can be determined from standard character tables of the local and global symmetry groups, with the character values in the inner product paired according to the relative positions and orientations of local structures within the aggregate. Although motivated by molecular aggregates, the construction is formulated for arbitrary geometries modeled by a homogeneous space of an ambient transformation group.

</details>


### [187] [Time-Travel Paradox in a Bohmian Extension of Deutsch's Approach: Mandatory Tunneling as the "Banana Peel Mechanism"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22794)
*Vladimir Nikitin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 de Broglie-Bohm 理论框架下扩展 Deutsch 的量子自洽方案，为粒子的本体构型（ontic configuration）附加自洽条件，从而在一维双通道模型中给出个体粒子的自洽时间旅行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自洽性在"粒子回到过去阻挡年轻时的自己"这一自预防悖论（self-prevention paradox）中失效；Deutsch 的量子方案虽以密度矩阵（density matrix）自洽条件化解矛盾，但其物理解释存在争议，如 Dunlap 认为该模型预设了相互作用的平行世界。

Method: 在 de Broglie-Bohm 理论中扩展 Deutsch 方法，除对量子态施加自洽条件外，还对粒子的本体构型施加自洽性要求，以刻画单个粒子的自洽演化；并在双通道、窄带高斯近似（two-channel, narrow-band Gaussian approximation）下求解。

Result: 得到一类自洽解，其中量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）成为必要条件，尽管该过程在普通散射中概率可任意小，由此实现了"香蕉皮机制"（banana peel mechanism）。

Conclusion: 通过引入本体构型的自洽性，可在不诉诸平行世界解释的前提下讨论单个粒子的自洽历史；返回过去的旅程中，通常极不可能发生的事件会被强制发生以规避悖论。

Abstract: We consider a self-prevention paradox in which a particle travels through a wormhole into its own past, encounters its younger version, and blocks its movement. In a specially constructed one-dimensional model, this collision rules out a classically self-consistent history. Deutsch's quantum approach can resolve such contradictions by imposing a self-consistency condition on the density matrix. The physical interpretation of this approach, however, remains debated. In particular, Dunlap argues that Deutsch's model presupposes the existence of interacting parallel worlds. Here we extend Deutsch's approach within de Broglie-Bohm theory by supplementing the self-consistency condition on the quantum state with a self-consistency requirement for the particle's ontic configuration. This allows us to consider the self-consistent evolution of an individual particle. In a two-channel, narrow-band Gaussian approximation, we find a self-consistent solution in which quantum tunneling is mandatory, despite its arbitrarily small probability in an ordinary scattering process. This realizes the "banana peel mechanism": to avoid a contradiction during a journey into the past, an event that would ordinarily be extremely unlikely occurs nonetheless.

</details>


### [188] [Generalized Schrieffer-Wolff transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22014)
*Tie-Cheng Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出广义 Schrieffer-Wolff 变换（generalized Schrieffer-Wolff transformation, gSWT）与对偶 SWT（dual SWT），旨在将原本多用于弱耦合的 SWT 拓展至强耦合区。核心贡献是给出规范固定的测地线原理（geodesic principle）与任意阶生成元的精确闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Schrieffer-Wolff 变换（SWT）主要适用于弱耦合问题，而强耦合情形缺乏系统对应方法；同时，通用闭式生成元与规范固定（gauge fixing）的物理原则尚不明确，使变换的内在结构被掩盖。

Method: 将 gSWT 形式化到任意厄米算符（Hermitian operator）与任意块划分（block partition）上，为规范选取建立测地线原理，并推导每一阶生成元的精确闭式解——一个仅以活跃能隙集合（active gap set）为输入的有限嵌套对易子求和（nested-commutator sum）。

Result: 获得 gSWT 生成元各阶的精确闭式表达，并同时揭示 SWT 物理的几何与代数两个层面；框架在集体自旋模型（collective spin model）与横场 Ising 模型（transverse-field Ising model）两个量子多体模型中作了示例说明。

Conclusion: gSWT 提供了处理强耦合问题的系统途径，并以测地线规范原则与闭式生成元统一刻画了 SWT 的结构；其在量子多体模型中的示例表明该框架具有可操作性。

Abstract: The Schrieffer-Wolff transformation (SWT) is a cornerstone of effective-Hamiltonian theory. However, the SWT is predominantly used for weak-coupling problems, while a systematic counterpart for strongly coupled regimes remains largely unexplored. A general closed-form SWT generator and a clear physical principle for gauge fixing remain elusive, obscuring the transformation's underlying structure. We introduce the dual SWT to deal with the strong-coupling problems. The generalized SWT (gSWT) is formulated for any Hermitian operator and block partition. We establish the geodesic principle for its canonical gauge. Furthermore, we derive an exact closed-form solution of the gSWT generator at every order as a finite nested-commutator sum whose only input is the active gap set. Both the geometric and algebraic aspects of the SWT physics are revealed. The gSWT framework is illustrated in two quantum many-body models, the collective spin model and the transverse-field Ising model.

</details>


### [189] [Black holes as black boxes: a query lower bound on python's lunch decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21723)
*Chris Waddell,Michelle Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Haar随机预言机模型（Haar random oracle model）下为“python's lunch”解码问题建立紧致的查询下界，证明以恒定平均优势恢复单个逻辑比特需Ω(2^{m/2})次查询，结合振幅放大（amplitude amplification）上界得到Θ(2^{m/2})的查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: python's lunch猜想将全息张量网络（holographic tensor networks）中体（bulk）重构的复杂度与后选择（post-selection）带来的指数代价相联系，但该猜想此前缺乏严格的查询复杂度下界证明。

Method: 构造python's lunch电路：对逻辑输入与固定辅助比特（ancillas）施加Haar随机酉算符U，再对m个输出量子比特做后选择；在给定一份归一化编码态及对U、U†（含受控查询）的相干预言机访问下，证明解码的查询下界，并与近似等距编码（approximately isometric encodings）的状态无关振幅放大上界相结合。

Result: 证明即使仅以恒定平均优势恢复单个逻辑比特，也需Ω(2^{m/2})次查询；与上界合并后在恒定解码精度下得到紧致查询复杂度Θ(2^{m/2})，并对其他可容许（admissible）酉系综的解码代价作了讨论。

Conclusion: 结果在全息张量网络解码问题上严格验证了python's lunch猜想的指数查询代价，且该复杂度在常数精度下是紧的，并可推广至产生近似等距全息映射的其他酉系综。

Abstract: The python's lunch conjecture relates the complexity of bulk reconstruction in holographic tensor networks to an exponential cost associated with post-selection. We establish a tight query lower bound for this decoding problem in a Haar random oracle model, with a python's lunch circuit that applies Haar random unitary $U$ to a logical input and fixed ancillas, followed by post-selection on $m$ output qubits. Given one copy of the normalized encoded state and coherent oracle access to $U$ and $U^\dagger$, including controlled queries, we prove that recovering even a single logical bit with constant average advantage requires $Ω(2^{m/2})$ queries. Together with the state-independent amplitude amplification upper bound for approximately isometric encodings, this establishes query complexity $Θ(2^{m/2})$ at constant decoding accuracy. We also comment on the decoding cost for other "admissible'" ensembles of unitaries $U$ which give rise to families of approximately isometric holographic maps.

</details>


### [190] [Entanglement cost and classical capacity of multimode Gaussian states and channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22915)
*Yuan Gao,Gerardo Adesso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明任意有限模、有限能量双分高斯态（Gaussian state）的生成纠缠（entanglement of formation）、高斯生成纠缠与纠缠成本（entanglement cost）三者相等，并将该结果应用于多模高斯信道（Gaussian channel）的纠缠成本与经典容量（classical capacity）的确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯态与高斯操作是连续变量量子信息与通信（continuous-variable quantum information and communication）的核心，但在有限模高斯态下，生成纠缠、高斯生成纠缠与纠缠成本是否一致此前缺乏一般性结论；同时多模高斯信道的纠缠成本与经典容量也亟待刻画。

Method: 对任意有限模数的双分高斯态证明三者相等，其共同值具强超可加性（strong superadditivity）；证明融合纯高斯态纠缠的测地凸性（geodesic convexity）与任意态的relative-entropy不等式（相对熵不等式）。

Result: 三者共同值对具有相同协方差矩阵（covariance matrix）的任意非高斯态给出生成纠缠与纠缠成本的下界；确定每个多模高斯信道的纠缠成本及均值能量约束下的经典容量，二者化为高斯协方差优化，容量由纯高斯信号配高斯位移调制（Gaussian displacement modulation）且跨信道使用时独立可达；并给出四模束缚纠缠态（bound entangled state）族的纠缠成本闭式公式。

Conclusion: 结果表明高斯态下的三种纠缠度量在有限能量情形下完全一致，且该一致性可下界非高斯情形并支撑高斯信道容量与纠缠成本的精确求解，为连续变量量子通信的资源理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Gaussian states and operations are central to continuous-variable quantum information and communication. For systems with any finite number of modes, we prove that entanglement of formation, Gaussian entanglement of formation, and entanglement cost coincide for every finite-energy bipartite Gaussian state. Their common value is strongly superadditive and lower-bounds entanglement of formation and entanglement cost for arbitrary non-Gaussian states with the same covariance matrix. We then determine the entanglement cost of every multimode Gaussian channel as well as its classical capacity under a mean energy constraint. These quantities reduce to Gaussian covariance optimizations, and the capacity is attained by pure Gaussian signals with Gaussian displacement modulation, independently across channel uses. The proof combines geodesic convexity of entanglement for pure Gaussian states with a relative-entropy inequality for arbitrary states. We also obtain a closed formula for the entanglement cost of a family of four-mode bound entangled states.

</details>


### [191] [Infrared fluctuations and collective entanglement modes in harmonic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21830)
*Mahsa Lezgi,Ali Mollabashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究相对论性谐振子链及其 Lifshitz 推广中，两个有限区域间真空纠缠如何受红外涨落与空间分离影响，并区分红外增强的总关联与可蒸馏纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前观点可能将红外增强的总关联视为无界纠缠资源，但二者关系尚需澄清；本文旨在辨明红外涨落对真空纠缠（vacuum entanglement）与可蒸馏纠缠（distillable entanglement）的不同作用。

Method: 在固定晶格间距与有限几何下，计算对数负性（logarithmic negativity）与互信息（mutual information），并借助局域辛整合（local symplectic consolidation）提取承载负性的集体正则模式，用相邻态正则基（adjacent-state canonical basis）展开追踪其随分离的演化。

Result: 质量趋于零时对数负性一致有界而互信息发散；负性集中于单一正则对，其空间参与度光滑且由红外主导，参与模式由面向边界局域化过渡为区域内集体分布；以负性半径标度分离后归一化参与度趋近普适长程轮廓，但细节形式与纠缠范围仍对紫外形变敏感。

Conclusion: 红外增强的关联不等同于可蒸馏纠缠；长程负性的持续源于参与振子的长波集体空间组织，从而细化了紫外模式图像。

Abstract: We investigate how infrared fluctuations and spatial separation shape vacuum entanglement between two finite regions in the relativistic harmonic chain and its Lifshitz generalizations. At fixed lattice spacing and finite geometry, we establish that logarithmic negativity remains uniformly bounded as the mass approaches zero, while mutual information diverges. Infrared-enhanced total correlations therefore do not provide an unbounded distillable-entanglement resource. Using local symplectic consolidation, we identify the collective canonical modes carrying the negativity and follow their evolution with separation. Their participation crosses over from localization near the facing boundaries to a collective distribution within each region. Expansion in the adjacent-state canonical basis connects this evolution to progressively weaker, more extended reference modes, including adjacent-halo directions.
  Building on the established concentration of long-distance logarithmic negativity into a single canonical pair, we show that its spatial participation is smooth and infrared-dominated despite its ultraviolet canonical structure, a distinction also present in pure-state entanglement modes. These results refine the ultraviolet mode picture by resolving the long-wavelength organization of the oscillators participating in the surviving entanglement. Across the massless dispersions studied, the normalized participation approaches a universal long-distance profile when separation is scaled by the negativity radius. Its detailed form and the entanglement range nevertheless remain sensitive to ultraviolet deformations. Together, these findings distinguish infrared-enhanced correlations from distillable entanglement and reveal the collective spatial organization underlying its persistence at large separation.

</details>


### [192] [Remark on internal relaxation in large number qubit systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21681)
*Martin Janßen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要讨论在长相干时间假设之外，量子计算机内部耦合导致的"本征弛豫（intrinsic relaxation）"问题：当计算时间超过弛豫时间尺度时相干性会指数衰减，并由此给出一台N量子比特量子计算机存在临界规模N_c的量级估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为量子计算可依赖指数长的海森堡时间（Heisenberg time）t_H来避免退相干，但作者指出，在t_c远短于t_H的阶段系统动力学已"有效不可逆"，量子比特间的耦合本身会产生弛豫，因此需要评估计算过程中可能出现的本征弛豫风险。

Method: 以含N个量子比特的量子计算机为对象，将系统一部分视为另一部分的环境，把弛豫时间τ_r估计为两部分之间典型跃迁速率（transition rate）的倒数；通过比较计算时间t_c、弛豫时间τ_r与海森堡时间t_H的量级关系进行论证与量级估计。

Result: 当t_H≫t_c≳τ_r时，量子相干性（quantum coherence）会指数快速丧失；由于t_H随N指数增长，存在阈值N_c，使得N≫N_c时必须考虑计算期间的本征弛豫。量级估计给出N_c≈ln(τ_r/T)，其中T为单个量子比特的典型系统特征振荡时间。

Conclusion: 量子比特之间的耦合本身即构成不可忽视的退相干来源，可扩展性（scalability）存在由本征弛豫决定的临界比特数N_c；该结果仅为量级估计，τ_r需由系统具体耦合与跃迁速率确定。

Abstract: For a time $t_{c}$ of calculation in an $N$ qubit quantum computer that is much shorter than the intrinsic Heisenberg time $t_H$, $t_c\ll t_H$, the dynamics of the system is effectively irreversible and the couplings between the qubits give rise to a relaxation time scale $τ_r$. If $t_H\gg t_c >\sim τ_r$ one looses quantum coherence exponentially fast. Since $t_H$ scales exponentially with $N$ there is a threshold $N_c$ such that for $N\gg N_c$ one must be aware of possible intrinsic relaxation during calculation. As an order of magnitude estimation we find $N_c\approx \ln(τ_r/T)$ where $T$ is a typical system specific oscillation time of a single qubit. $τ_r$ can be estimated as the typical inverse transition rate between two parts of the system, one of which is treated as an environment of the other.

</details>


### [193] [Strong eigenstate thermalization in Singer-cycle Clifford circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22391)
*Naoto Tsuji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作构造了由 Singer 循环（Singer cycle）诱导的砖墙型 Clifford 电路，并证明其全部 Floquet 本征态均严格满足无穷温度下的本征态热化假说（ETH），给出了对角与非对角矩阵元的统一界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征态热化假说（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, ETH）是理解孤立量子系统热化的核心框架。近期仅在线性反馈移位寄存器量子电路中确立了几乎所有 Floquet 本征态的无穷温度 ETH，尚缺乏对所有本征态无例外成立的强形式 ETH 的实例，因此需要构造新的电路模型加以研究。

Method: 引入含 L 个量子比特的砖墙型 Clifford 电路（brickwork Clifford circuits），其 Pauli 算符（Pauli operators）的诱导变换由有限域 𝔽₂ 上阶为 2^L+1 的不可约辛矩阵（irreducible symplectic matrix）表示，即 Singer 循环（Singer cycle）。基于该结构解析证明强形式 ETH，并推导固定数量格点上任意可观测量（observable）的矩阵元界。

Result: 证明所有 Floquet 本征态在无穷温度下均为热态且无例外；得到对对角项偏离热期望值（thermal expectation values）与非对角矩阵元的统一界（uniform bounds）。据此，在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下任意初态的可观测量的长时间平均趋于无穷温度值。

Conclusion: 可观测量的期望值在时间上的涨落（temporal fluctuation）随热力学极限指数压低，表明在几乎所有时刻每个可观测量都保持接近无穷温度值，从而给出强形式 ETH 的严格实例。

Abstract: Eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) provides a central framework for understanding thermalization in isolated quantum systems. Recently, the infinite-temperature ETH has been established for almost all Floquet eigenstates in linear feedback shift register quantum circuits. Here we introduce brickwork Clifford circuits with $L$ qubits where the induced transformation of Pauli operators is represented by an irreducible symplectic matrix over the finite field $\mathbb F_2$ of the maximal order $2^L+1$, known as a Singer cycle. For these Singer-cycle Clifford circuits, we prove a strong version of ETH: all Floquet eigenstates are thermal at infinite temperature without exceptions. We derive uniform bounds on both diagonal deviations from thermal expectation values and off-diagonal matrix elements of arbitrary observables supported on a fixed number of sites. The results indicate that long-time averages of observables approach the infinite-temperature values in the thermodynamic limit for arbitrary initial states. We also show that the temporal fluctuation of expectation values of observables is exponentially suppressed in the thermodynamic limit, implying that each observable remains close to the infinite-temperature value for almost all times.

</details>


### [194] [Quantum Codes for Distributed Quantum Fault Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22530)
*Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对模块化量子处理器（modular quantum processors）中的分布式故障定位（fault sensing）问题，建立了最优量子码判据。结论表明：在无保护存储（protected storage）条件下，成对纠缠无增益，仅靠受暴露探针逼近最优需大深度纠缠且参与探针数随阵列规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化量子处理器需要定向恢复（targeted recovery），前提是定位出发生故障的部件。独立量子比特可通过反复制备与测量监测故障；贝尔对探针（Bell-pair probes）能提升未知局域信道下保证正确站点概率，但需要将伙伴量子比特置于远离故障处。本文因而追问：暴露探针之间的纠缠能否在不依赖保护存储的情况下提供同等优势。

Method: 针对强度落在给定正区间内的单个未知局域量子比特信道（local qubit channel），建立最优分布式故障感知的量子码判据（quantum-code criterion）。分析场景包括：单次询问、零误报、无背景噪声；以及固定强度无记忆退极化背景（memoryless depolarizing background）0<eta<1 下持续故障的重复询问。以最坏情况正确站点概率（worst-case correct-site probability）为优化指标，并考察纠缠深度（entanglement depth）与探针参与规模。

Result: 单次询问、零误报、无背景噪声时，合适码态可达最优最坏情况正确站点概率，在所有量子比特均暴露的条件下较独立探针提升50%。精确达到最优等价于输入子空间与单量子比特泡利误差子空间（single-qubit Pauli error subspaces）相互正交。成对纠缠（pairwise entanglement）不带来改进；无保护辅助（protected ancillas）时纠缠深度至多4，无法超过最优值的95.54%。逼近最优还要求与阵列规模成正比的探针数参与更大的纠缠因子。持续故障加固定强度退极化背景下，独立探针已达到最优的对数询问标度（logarithmic interrogation scaling）。三个含1000站点的对比中，编码探针协议（encoded-probe protocol）所需询问次数少于独立探针，仅比最优解码的理想贝尔对多一次，且不存储任何保护量子比特。

Conclusion: 纠缠对分布式故障感知的优势受量子码结构严格约束：最优性对应正交子空间条件，成对纠缠无效，浅纠缠（深度≤4）远不能逼近最优。实际方案中，编码探针可在无保护量子比特存储的条件下接近理想贝尔对性能，显示出分布式传感中量子码设计的实用价值。

Abstract: Targeted recovery in modular quantum processors requires identifying the component affected by a fault. Independent qubits can monitor faults through repeated preparation and measurement. Bell-pair probes improve the guaranteed correct-site probability for unknown local channels, but require partners kept away from the fault. Can entanglement among exposed probes supply this advantage without protected storage? We establish a quantum-code criterion for optimal distributed fault sensing of one unknown local qubit channel with strength in a prescribed positive range. For one interrogation with zero false alarms and no background noise, suitable code states attain the optimal worst-case correct-site probability, improving on independent probes by 50% with every qubit exposed. Exact attainment is equivalent to mutually orthogonal input and single-qubit Pauli error subspaces. Pairwise entanglement gives no improvement, and entanglement depth at most four cannot exceed 95.54% of the optimum without protected ancillas. Approaching the optimum without protected ancillas also requires a number of probes proportional to the array size to participate in larger entangled factors. For a persistent fault under memoryless depolarizing background of fixed strength 0 < eta < 1, independent probes already attain the optimal logarithmic interrogation scaling at fixed success between zero and one and a positive false-alarm budget. In three 1000-site comparisons with specified readouts, an encoded-probe protocol certifies fewer interrogations than independent probes and only one more than optimally decoded ideal Bell pairs, while storing no protected qubits.

</details>


### [195] [Predicting Quantum Mpemba Effects from Quantum Speed Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22547)
*Andrea Nava,Rosario Fazio,Reinhold Egger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出以量子速度极限（Quantum Speed Limits）作为量子姆潘巴效应（Quantum Mpemba Effects）的几何指示指标，可在系统接近稳态之前实现低成本预测，并在耗散量子比特与开放自旋链上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应通常需在系统接近稳态的较晚阶段才能识别，缺乏早期预判手段；论文旨在检验该效应是否在系统尚未接近稳态时即已"印刻"于速度极限之中。

Method: 将量子速度极限用作量子姆潘巴效应的几何指示器（geometric indicators），并以耗散量子比特（dissipative qubits）与开放自旋链（open spin chains）作为基准测试体系进行验证。

Result: 短时速度极限可在广泛参数区间内以较低成本和合理精度预测量子姆潘巴效应的出现，表明姆潘巴物理已体现在速度极限之中。

Conclusion: 速度极限对开放多体量子系统（open many-body quantum systems）具有实用价值，可作为姆潘巴效应的低成本早期预测工具。

Abstract: Can anomalous Quantum Mpemba Effects be anticipated before a system comes close to its stationary state? We employ Quantum Speed Limits as geometric indicators of Quantum Mpemba Effects, arguing that Mpemba physics is already imprinted in speed limits by benchmarking dissipative qubits and open spin chains. Our results show that short-time speed limits can predict Quantum Mpemba Effects at low cost and with reasonable precision over broad parameter regimes. This predictive power implies a useful application of speed limits for open many-body quantum systems.

</details>


### [196] [Non-equilibrium topological order from Floquet anyon proliferation in the toric code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23132)
*Francesco Petiziol*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 Floquet 驱动下 Kitaev toric code 的动力学，发现其长时间态偏离纯态拓扑序但仍保留长程纠缠，从而实现本征混合态拓扑序的 Floquet 类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动系统通常认为加热会破坏有序，作者希望考察当驱动相干地产生并传播费米型 f 任意子（f anyons）时，toric code 的拓扑序在长时间极限下如何演化。

Method: 推导纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）与对数负性（logarithmic negativity）的分解，将基态贡献（含拓扑项）与受驱准粒子贡献分离；对诱导无相互作用 f 动力学的驱动，借助自由费米子数值方法，并联系 f 任意子向周期吉布斯系综（periodic Gibbs ensemble）的平衡化。

Result: 长时间态不再是纯态拓扑序，但保持长程纠缠（long-range entanglement），表现为本征混合态拓扑序（intrinsic mixed-state topological order）的 Floquet 版本；该有序态的出现可归因于 f 任意子的平衡化，并在 f 满足 Floquet 本征态热化（Floquet eigenstate thermalization）时给出加热稳定非平衡拓扑相的情形。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Floquet 加热可稳定真正的非平衡拓扑相，与“加热是周期驱动系统有序的障碍”这一常规观点形成对比。

Abstract: We analyze the dynamics of Kitaev's toric code in Floquet quenches where the drive coherently creates and propagates fermionic $f$ anyons. We show that the long-time state departs from pure-state topological order but retains long-range entanglement, thereby realizing a Floquet analog of intrinsic mixed-state topological order. As a key result enabling this analysis, we derive decompositions of the entanglement entropy and logarithmic negativity that separate the ground-state value, including topological terms, from a contribution given by the driven quasiparticles. For a drive inducing non-interacting $f$ dynamics, these decompositions allow us to leverage numerical methods for free fermions and to link the emergence of the proposed long-time ordered state to the equilibration of $f$ anyons to a periodic Gibbs ensemble. For drives inducing Floquet eigenstate thermalization of $f$, our results suggest a scenario where Floquet heating of the $f$ sector stabilizes a genuine non-equilibrium topological phase, in contrast with the usual view of heating as an obstacle to order in periodically driven systems.

</details>


### [197] [Local interference algorithms for observables in large random circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23207)
*Philippe Suchsland,Brayden Ware,Vladislav D. Kurilovich,Vadim Smelyanskiy,Kostyantyn Kechedzhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在随机电路动力学（random circuit dynamics）的观测值模拟任务中，干涉（interference）引起的符号问题（sign problem）通常并不构成障碍：一阶乱序关联函数（OTOCs）与电荷-电荷时序关联函数（TOCs）可由经典随机过程近似，并结合蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）与张量网络（tensor network）方法实现多项式复杂度的采样算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学中普遍认为干涉效应会导致经典蒙特卡洛采样出现符号问题，从而使采样失效；论文旨在检验该论断在随机电路动力学观测值演化模拟中是否普遍成立。

Method: 选取两类具大信号的观测量：无守恒律的无结构随机电路中的一阶OTOC，以及粒子数守恒随机电路中的时序电荷-电荷关联函数（TOC）；将其近似为经典随机过程，并用包含门簇（clusters of gates）相干修正的级数展开系统性改进近似，进而设计结合蒙特卡洛采样与张量网络方法的算法来逼近该级数。

Result: 数值上观察到TOC与OTOC的逼近复杂度在电路规模与目标精度上均呈多项式标度；通过将系综平均误差直接映射为复制统计模型（replica statistical models）的配分函数（partition function），在一维砖墙（brickwork）几何下完成微扰分析与数值验证，理论分析解释了所观察到的多项式复杂度。

Conclusion: 理论分析支持多项式复杂度可延伸至任意小精度及热力学极限（thermodynamic limit），说明符号问题在此类随机电路观测值模拟任务中并非普遍性障碍。

Abstract: In quantum dynamics, it is generally believed that interference effects lead to a sign problem for classical Monte Carlo sampling methods, rendering sampling ineffective. In this paper we show that this is generically not the case for the task of simulating the evolution of observables in random instances of circuit dynamics. Specifically, we consider two examples of observables which give large signals despite the randomness of the dynamics: first order out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) in unstructured random circuits without conservation laws and time-ordered charge-charge correlators (TOCs) in particle number-preserving random circuits. We show that these observables can be approximated by classical stochastic processes and that these approximations can be systematically improved with a series expansion that incorporates coherent corrections associated with clusters of gates. We design and implement algorithms that combine Monte Carlo sampling and tensor network methods to approximate the series. We observe numerically that the computational complexity of approximating TOCs and OTOCs scales polynomially in the circuit size and in the target precision. We study the accuracy of these algorithms by directly mapping their ensemble-averaged errors to partition functions of replica statistical models, allowing for perturbative analysis and numerics in $1$d brickwork geometries. Our theoretical analysis explains the empirical observation of polynomial complexity of these algorithms and is consistent with polynomial complexity extending to arbitrarily small precision and to the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [198] [Expressivity of two-dimensional Gaussian fermion tensor network states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23227)
*Pok Man Tam,William Musk,Zhehao Dai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对二维（2D）高斯费米子态（Gaussian fermionic states）的两类体系——单带BCS超导体与半满双带绝缘体（含半金属），证明Renyi-0熵的面积律（area law）与存在有限键维度（bond dimension）的精确高斯费米子张量网络态（TNS）表示等价，并给出基于紧支撑Wannier型轨道与动量空间波函数的等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络态（TNS）的键维度刻画其计算资源需求；一维情形下Renyi-0熵的面积律保证键维度与系统尺寸无关的精确矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）表示，而二维一般态无类似结论。因此二维TNS的表达能力与其所表示态纠缠性质之间的关系尚待澄清。

Method: 聚焦两类二维平移不变（translation-invariant）高斯费米子态：单带BCS超导体与半满双带绝缘体（包括半金属），对每一类分别证明Renyi-0熵的面积律与有限键维度的精确高斯费米子TNS表示之间的等价性，并以紧支撑Wannier型轨道（compactly supported Wannier-type orbitals）及动量空间波函数（momentum-space wavefunction）给出等价刻画。

Result: 对两类体系均证明了等价性：Renyi-0熵满足面积律当且仅当存在精确的高斯费米子TNS表示，且键维度由Renyi-0熵决定（有限）。同时建立了以Wannier型轨道的空间延展范围与波函数结构来刻画所需键维度的等价表述。

Conclusion: 在所述二维高斯费米子态范围内，纠缠的面积律与TNS表示的可压缩性（有限键维度）严格对应，从而在二维情形下部分回答了TNS表达能力与纠缠性质关系的问题，并提供了轨道局域性与波函数结构层面的可操作判据。

Abstract: Tensor network states (TNS) provide a family of efficiently compressed approximations to physically relevant many-body quantum states. An important quantity that characterizes the computational resources required by a TNS is its bond dimension. While in one dimension an area law for the Renyi-0 entropy guarantees an exact matrix product state representation with a bond dimension independent of the system size, an analogous statement does not hold for general states in two dimensions (2D). This leaves open the question of how the expressivity of 2D TNS is related to the entanglement properties of the states they represent. In this work, we focus on two families of 2D translation-invariant Gaussian fermionic states: single-band BCS superconductors and half-filled two-band insulators (including semimetals). For each family, we prove that an area law for the Renyi-0 entropy is equivalent to having an exact Gaussian fermionic TNS representation with a finite bond dimension determined by the Renyi-0 entropy. We also establish equivalent characterizations in terms of compactly supported Wannier-type orbitals and the momentum-space wavefunction, relating the spatial extent of these orbitals and the structure of the wavefunction to the required bond dimension.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>
