### [199] [Linearization of nonlocal elasticity based on nonlocal gradients via Γ-convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21872)
*José Carlos Bellido,Guillermo García-Sáez*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文基于非局部梯度（nonlocal gradients）的一般非线性弹性模型，严格地变分推导出线性非局部弹性（linear nonlocal elasticity）：证明在小应变极限下，重整化后的非线性能量在 Γ-收敛（Γ-convergence，即 Gamma 收敛）意义下收敛到相应的二次线性化能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为线性非局部弹性提供严格的变分基础，说明它可由一般非线性弹性模型在小应变极限下导出，而非作为独立假设引入。

Method: 在非线性弹性（nonlinear elasticity）的标准假设下，采用变分方法研究重整化非线性能量的 Γ-收敛；证明依赖非局部几何刚性估计（nonlocal geometric rigidity estimate），且不要求存储能量密度（stored-energy density）满足任何全局上增长条件。

Result: 证明了重整化后的非线性能量在小应变极限下 Γ-收敛到对应的二次线性化能量，从而在无全局上增长条件的情形下建立了非局部弹性的线性化结果。

Conclusion: 线性非局部弹性可由非局部梯度型非线性模型经小应变极限严格导出；非局部几何刚性估计是核心工具，且结论不依赖存储能量密度的全局上增长假设。

Abstract: We provide a rigorous variational derivation of linear nonlocal elasticity from a general nonlinear elastic model based on nonlocal gradients. Under standard assumptions of nonlinear elasticity, we prove that the rescaled nonlinear energies Γ-converge, in the small-strain limit, to the corresponding quadratic linearized energy. The proof relies on a nonlocal geometric rigidity estimate and does not require any global upper growth condition on the stored-energy density.

</details>


### [200] [Orbital Stability and Instability of Slow and Fast Solitary Waves of gILW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21989)
*Diego Yupanqui*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究广义中介长波方程（gILW）在幂型非线性下的孤立波，证明其存在性、两类极限收敛性，并给出慢波与快波的轨道稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Albert–Bona–Henry 关于中介长波方程（ILW，p=1）的经典稳定性结果推广至 p>1，并揭示慢波（对应 gKdV 极限）与快波（对应 gBO 极限）在稳定性阈值上的差异。

Method: 结合集中紧性（concentration-compactness）与对称重排（symmetric rearrangement）证明孤立波存在性；采用微扰理论（perturbation theory），通过对应孤立波算子的收敛性将 gKdV 与 gBO 的谱信息传递给 gILW，且不依赖孤立波剖面的显式公式。

Result: 对任意 p≥1 得到偶、严格正、径向递减的孤立波；深度参数趋于无穷时在能量空间与 L^q（q∈[2,∞]）中收敛到 gBO 孤立波，趋于零时经适当重标度在 L^q（q∈[2,∞]）中收敛到 gKdV 孤立波。慢波在 p=1,2,3 时轨道稳定、p≥5 时不稳定；快波在 p=1 时稳定、p≥3 时不稳定。

Conclusion: 稳定性阈值分别与 gKdV（慢波）和 gBO（快波）一致，且 p=3 时稳定性随深度参数区间发生转变，说明 gILW 桥接了两类极限方程并在中间区域呈现非平凡行为。

Abstract: We study the solitary waves of the generalized Intermediate Long Wave equation (gILW), for a power-type nonlinearity of order $p\geq1$. Using concentration-compactness and symmetric rearrangement, we prove existence of suitable (even, strictly positive and radially decreasing) solitary waves of gILW for every $p\geq1$, and show that, as the depth parameter tends to infinity, they converge in the energy space and in $L^q$, $q\in[2,\infty]$, to the solitary waves of the generalized Benjamin-Ono equation (gBO), while, as the depth parameter tends to zero, after a suitable rescaling, they converge in $L^q$, $q\in[2,\infty]$, to the solitary waves of the generalized Korteweg-de Vries equation (gKdV). Our main results concern the orbital stability of slow and fast solitary waves of gILW, that is, of those for which the product $δc$ of the depth parameter and the velocity is small or large, respectively. For slow waves we obtain stability for $p=1,2,3$ and instability for $p\geq5$ (the same threshold as for gKdV), while for fast waves we obtain stability for $p=1$ and instability for $p\geq3$ (the same threshold as for gBO). In particular, for $p=3$ the stability of the solitary waves changes from one regime to the other. These results extend to $p>1$ the classical result of Albert, Bona and Henry for the Intermediate Long Wave equation ($p=1$). The proof proceeds by perturbation theory, transferring spectral information from gKdV and gBO to gILW through the convergence of the corresponding solitary-wave operators, and in particular avoids any use of an explicit formula for the solitary wave profile, none being available for $p>1$.

</details>


### [201] [Global well-posedness and regularization for a nonlinear relativistic Fokker--Planck equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22381)
*Simone Calogero*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究动量空间上的非线性相对论 Fokker–Planck 方程，证明保能量-动量 $(E,P)$ 的弱概率密度解的整体存在唯一性、瞬时光滑性，以及偶数初值时扩散系数的全局一致正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方程扩散系数与漂移向量场是解的非线性积分泛函，其一致正性是证明整体适定性的根本障碍；论文旨在解决这一问题并建立弱解的整体存在唯一性。

Method: 通过证明标量扩散系数在每个有限时间区间上一致为正来完成证明；关键机制是严格相对论不等式 $E^2-|P|^2>1$ 与能量尾部在有限时间区间上的一致可积性（uniform integrability）。

Result: 得到弱概率密度解的整体存在唯一性并保持相对论能量-动量 $(E,P)$；进一步证明整体弱解在正时刻瞬时光滑（instantaneously smooth）且严格为正；对偶数初值，扩散系数在所有时间上一致为正。

Conclusion: 严格相对论不等式与能量尾部一致可积性是非线性扩散系数一致正性的核心机制，由此建立的适定性结果为该相对论 Fokker–Planck 方程的进一步分析（正则性、长时间行为）提供基础。

Abstract: We consider a nonlinear relativistic Fokker--Planck equation on momentum space, whose diffusion coefficient and drift vector field are nonlinear integral functionals of the solution. We prove global existence and uniqueness of weak probability density solutions preserving the relativistic energy-momentum $(E,P)$. A fundamental step in the proof is to establish that the scalar diffusion coefficient remains uniformly positive on every finite time interval. The key mechanism ensuring this property is the strict relativistic inequality $E^2-|P|^2>1$, combined with the uniform integrability of the energy tails on every finite time interval. We further prove that global weak solutions become instantaneously smooth and strictly positive for positive times. Finally, for even initial data, we prove that the diffusion coefficient remains uniformly positive for all time.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Probing Phase Fluctuations in Cuprates by Multidimensional Josephson Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21708)
*Albert Liu,Danica Pavicevic,Alex Gómez Salvador,Marios H. Michael,Michael Fechner,Pedro M. Lozano,Qiang Li,Genda D. Gu,Eugene Demler,Andrea Cavalleri*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用角分辨多维太赫兹光谱（angle-resolved multidimensional terahertz spectroscopy）探测 La1.83Sr0.17CuO4 中 Josephson 隧道共振的光学非线性，发现在 T ~ Tc 处存在从平均场型非线性到短程超导涨落介导非线性的交跨（crossover）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体（cuprate superconductors）正常态被认为存在 Tc 以上的预配对电子（precursor electron pairing），但现有谱学证据均为间接，且此前沿超导面测得的三次谐波产生（third-harmonic generation）响应与涨落态具体性质之间尚无明确联系。

Method: 采用角分辨多维太赫兹光谱，测量 La1.83Sr0.17CuO4 在超导—正常态转变过程中 Josephson 隧道共振（Josephson tunneling resonance）的光学非线性；并区分不同三阶非线性响应，指出非重相位（non-rephasing）多维信号对序参量（order parameter）的非平均场涨落尤为敏感。同时提出理论框架，将面外光学响应与面内涨落超导态相联系。

Result: 在所有三阶非线性响应中，非重相位多维信号对序参量的非平均场涨落最敏感；实验观测到 T ~ Tc 处的交跨，即低温下主导的平均场光学非线性转变为由短程超导涨落介导的非线性。理论框架成功将两种区域的面外光学响应与面内涨落超导态联系起来，并对比了不同类型太赫兹非线性对涨落序的敏感性。

Conclusion: 多维太赫兹非线性谱学可作为探测铜氧化物中超导涨落与预配对物理的灵敏手段；非重相位多维信号为识别非平均场涨落提供了特异性探针，并明确了其与平均场响应在 Tc 附近的区分。

Abstract: The normal state of cuprate superconductors is believed to host precursor electron pairing above the superconducting transition temperature. These pre-formed pairs, which extend over an unknown temperature range, are expected to lack long-range phase coherence. Because spectroscopic evidence for this hypothesis is only indirect, new methods to probe these physics are needed. Nonlinear terahertz spectroscopy allows one to probe high-order time correlation functions and is therefore sensitive to fluctuations. Measurements of third-harmonic generation along the superconducting planes have revealed responses persisting above Tc, but a clear relation between these optical responses and specific properties of the fluctuating state has not yet been established. Here, we apply angle-resolved multidimensional terahertz spectroscopy to probe the optical nonlinearity of the Josephson tunneling resonance in La1.83Sr0.17CuO4 across the superconducting to normal state transition. We find that among all third-order nonlinear responses, the non-rephasing multidimensional signal is especially sensitive to non-mean field fluctuations of the order parameter. We measure a crossover occurring at T ~ Tc between a mean-field optical nonlinearity that dominates at low temperatures and one that is mediated by short-range superconducting fluctuations. We present a theoretical framework that connects the out-of-plane optical response in both regimes to the fluctuating in-plane superconducting state, and contrast the sensitivity of different types of terahertz nonlinearities to fluctuating order.

</details>


### [203] [Hidden convexity of the Eliashberg condensation energy: existence, uniqueness and continuity of the superconducting state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21773)
*Davis Rens*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文确立了各向同性 Eliashberg 自旋链泛函（isotropic Eliashberg spin-chain functional）在库仑赝势为零时的热力学转变图像的非线性部分，证明凝聚能（condensation energy）具有隐藏凸性（hidden convexity），从而得到极小元在规范意义下的存在唯一性及转变连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kiessling、Altshuler 与 Yuzbashyan 曾猜想该泛函的热力学转变图像：临界温度以上正常态为唯一极小元、以下超导极小元在规范意义下唯一、且相变连续。本文旨在严格证明这一图像的非线性部分。

Method: 在变量 ρ_n=sin²θ_n 下证明限制于第一象限的凝聚能严格凸，且将容许链折叠进该象限不增加能量；结合隐藏凸性与紧性估计（compactness estimate），对正的非增幂律衰减相互作用建立极小元的存在唯一性；并将正常态的极小性与二次型（quadratic form）非负性等价刻画。

Result: 对正的非增幂律衰减相互作用，得到极小元在规范意义下的存在唯一性；正常态恰在二次型非负（含边缘稳定）时取极小；非线性 Eliashberg 方程的正解存在当且仅当正常态线性不稳定，且此时解唯一；对具逐点连续性与局部一致衰减界、依赖温度的族，极小密度在加权 ℓ¹ 范数下连续，从而对 γ-模型、Einstein 声子（Einstein phonons）及一般声子谱证明热力学猜想。

Conclusion: 论文完整建立了该热力学转变猜想所涉的非线性结论，其中有限维代数与凸分析（convex analysis）部分已在 Lean 中机器检验；证明独立于得出重叠结论的同期工作。

Abstract: Kiessling, Altshuler and Yuzbashyan conjectured the thermodynamic transition picture for the isotropic Eliashberg spin-chain functional with vanishing Coulomb pseudopotential: the normal state is the unique minimizer above and at a critical temperature, the superconducting minimizer below it is unique up to gauge, and the transition is continuous. We establish the nonlinear part of this picture. In the variables $ρ_n=\sin^2θ_n$, the condensation energy restricted to the first quadrant is strictly convex, and folding any admissible chain into that quadrant cannot increase its energy. For every positive, non-increasing interaction with power-law decay, hidden convexity and a compactness estimate give existence and uniqueness of the minimizer up to gauge. The normal state minimizes the energy exactly when its quadratic form is non-negative, including marginal stability. Among admissible chains, a positive solution of the nonlinear Eliashberg equations exists exactly when the normal state is linearly unstable and is then unique. For temperature-dependent families with pointwise continuity and locally uniform decay bounds, the minimizing density is continuous in a weighted $\ell^1$ norm. Together with uniqueness of the linear-stability temperature, these results prove the thermodynamic conjectures for the $γ$-model, Einstein phonons and general phonon spectra; the phonon applications use separately established spectral input. Finite-dimensional algebraic and convex-analytic components are machine-checked in Lean. The proofs were obtained independently of concurrent work that reaches overlapping conclusions.

</details>


### [204] [High-resolution top-down nanopatterning of NbN thin films for superconducting metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21848)
*Francesca Incalza,Davide Mondin,Dip Joti Paul,Matteo Castellani,Alejandro Simon,Rocco A. Vitalone,Dmitri N. Basov,Karl K. Berggren*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作建立了蓝宝石衬底上超薄外延NbN薄膜的高分辨率自上而下纳米图形化工艺，可实现70 nm周期超导纳米线网格与100 nm周期毫米级孔阵列，并观察到与孔阵列周期匹配的磁通涡旋钉扎增强及超流密度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性纳米图形化（periodic nanopatterning）是调控超导薄膜中涡旋运动（vortex motion）与电磁响应、构建超导超材料（superconducting metamaterials）的途径，因此需要面向超薄外延NbN薄膜的可靠高分辨率图形化工艺。

Method: 采用电子束光刻（electron-beam lithography），以HSQ与ZEP 530A为抗蚀剂，结合邻近效应校正（proximity-effect correction）与粘附层集成进行优化；引入薄Si覆盖层改善HSQ在外延NbN上的粘附性，并通过反应离子刻蚀（RIE）将图形转移到5 nm厚NbN薄膜；利用SEM、TEM、AFM进行结构与尺寸表征，并结合电输运测量与低温THz时域光谱（cryogenic THz time-domain spectroscopy）。

Result: 成功制备周期低至70 nm的致密超导纳米线网格，以及100 nm周期、毫米平方级、高保真度的孔阵列；电输运测量显示由孔阵列周期决定的周期性电阻极小值与开关电流振荡（switching-current oscillations），与涡旋构型匹配图形晶格时钉扎增强一致；纳米图形化区域超流密度（superfluid density）相对原始NbN降低，两区域光谱超导转变温度均接近14 K。

Conclusion: 结果确立了一个面向涡旋钉扎、超材料与量子器件应用的超导纳米图形化稳健制备平台。

Abstract: Periodic nanopatterning offers a route to tailoring vortex motion and the electromagnetic response of superconducting films, providing a basis for superconducting metamaterials. Here, we report a high-resolution top-down nanopatterning process for ultrathin epitaxial NbN films on sapphire substrates. Electron-beam lithography using hydrogen silsesquioxane (HSQ) and ZEP 530A resists was optimized through proximity-effect correction and adhesion-layer integration. Dense superconducting nanowire grids with pitches down to 70 nm were fabricated, alongside millimeter-square areas patterned with 100-nm-pitch hole arrays with high pattern fidelity. A thin Si capping layer was found to significantly improve HSQ adhesion on epitaxial NbN. The nanopatterns were successfully transferred into 5-nm-thick NbN films by reactive ion etching (RIE). Structural and dimensional characterization was performed using scanning electron microscopy (SEM), transmission electron microscopy (TEM), and atomic force microscopy (AFM), enabling cross-validation of nanoscale feature dimensions and etch quality. Electrical transport measurements reveal periodic resistance minima and switching-current oscillations at magnetic fields set by the hole-array pitch, consistent with enhanced vortex pinning when the vortex configuration matches the patterned lattice. Cryogenic THz time-domain spectroscopy further reveals a reduced superfluid density in the nanopatterned region relative to pristine NbN, while both regions have a spectroscopic superconducting transition near 14 K. These results establish a robust fabrication platform for superconducting nanopatterns targeting vortex pinning, metamaterials, and quantum-device applications.

</details>


### [205] [From the Full Fermionic Hilbert Space to the BCS Paired Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22493)
*Guo-Jian Qiao,Xin Yue,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文证明在吸引型约化BCS模型的全费米子希尔伯特空间中，零温下固定平均粒子数或固定偶数粒子数的基态总落在配对子空间内；并在该子空间内通过正广义P表示的赝自旋相干态变分最小化，统一导出BCS态与投影BCS态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BCS与粒子数投影BCS变分处理均预设于配对子空间（相反动量与自旋的模式同时为空或同时占据），但该子空间能否从完整费米子希尔伯特空间中依据能量被自然选出，尚缺乏直接论证。

Method: 利用区分单占据态与空、双占据态的守恒投影算符（conserved projectors），将完整希尔伯特空间分解为不变扇区，并在固定平均粒子数、固定偶/奇粒子数条件下直接比较各扇区能量；随后在配对子空间内对赝自旋相干态（pseudospin coherent states）的正广义P表示（generalized-P representation）作变分最小化。

Result: 零温下，固定平均粒子数的每个基态均位于配对子空间；固定偶数粒子数时结论相同且比较过程保持总粒子数不变；固定奇数粒子数时按宇称（parity）要求必有一个费米子保持未配对。配对子空间内的最小化给非零配对振幅选定共同相位并确定其模长，BCS态与投影BCS态分别为相应粒子数约束下的特定极小化态。

Conclusion: 配对子空间可由能量比较从完整希尔伯特空间中被选为基态所在空间，从而为BCS及粒子数投影BCS变分 ansatz 提供自洽性依据，并给出基于正广义P表示的统一变分刻画。

Abstract: Conventional Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer (BCS) and number-projected BCS variational treatments are formulated within a paired Hilbert subspace, where modes with opposite momenta and spins are either both empty or both occupied. Here we ask whether this paired subspace can itself be selected energetically from the full fermionic Hilbert space of the attractive reduced BCS model. Using conserved projectors that distinguish singly occupied states from empty and doubly occupied ones, we decompose the full Hilbert space into invariant sectors. A direct energy comparison between sectors shows that, at zero temperature, every ground state at fixed average particle number lies in the paired subspace. The same holds at fixed even particle number, with the comparison preserving the total particle number, while at fixed odd particle number one fermion remains unpaired, as required by parity. Within the resulting paired subspace, minimization over positive generalized-$P$ representations of pseudospin coherent states selects a common phase for the nonzero pair amplitudes and determines their magnitudes. BCS and number-projected BCS states arise as particular minimizers under the respective particle-number constraints.

</details>


### [206] [Ab initio Engineering of Topological Superconductivity in Substrate-Supported Magnetic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23016)
*András Lászlóffy,Balazs Ujfalussy*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文结合多种第一性原理方法，系统扫描置于Nb(110)表面不同覆盖层上的多类磁性原子链，以探索实现拓扑马约拉纳边缘态的工程化路径；发现Fe链在Ag/Nb(110)上展现出拓扑超导的显著特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立一条设计与调控拓扑马约拉纳边缘态（Majorana edge states）的可行路径，通过在Nb(110)表面覆盖层上构建磁性原子链来实现拓扑超导。

Method: 采用多种基于第一性原理（first-principles）的方法组合：先用原子尺度自旋模型（atomistic spin-model）计算确定磁构型，再以能带理论方法求解Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程以获得超导谱。

Result: 在二阶Heisenberg模型内即可稳定出铁磁、反铁磁、自旋螺旋及up-up-down-down等丰富磁构型；超导谱依磁结构与原子构型表现为拓扑平庸小能隙、宽重叠Shiba带或局域零能边缘态伴随可观拓扑小能隙；Fe链在Ag/Nb(110)上呈现强局域零能边缘态与Shiba带反转迹象。

Conclusion: 自旋结构的改变或少量空间弛豫（spatial relaxation）可显著改变超导能带结构及边缘态的局域性质，表明拓扑超导特征对磁构型与几何细节高度敏感。

Abstract: In this paper, we employ a combination of several first-principles-based approaches to systematically scan chains of several types of magnetic atoms in different chain geometries on top of various overlayers on the Nb(110) surface, to develop a pathway to engineer topological Majorana edge states. Using atomistic spin-model calculations, we first identify a rich variety of magnetic configurations, including ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic, spin-spiral, and exotic up-up-down-down states stabilized solely within a second-order Heisenberg model. In the next step, we solve the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations using a band-theoretical method. Depending on the magnetic structure and atomic configuration, the superconducting spectra may exhibit topologically trivial minigaps, broad overlapping Shiba bands, or localized zero-energy edge states accompanied by sizable topological minigaps. In particular, Fe chains on Ag/Nb(110) are found to display pronounced signatures associated with topological superconductivity, including strongly localized zero-energy edge states and indications of Shiba band inversion. We further demonstrate that changes in the spin structure or a small amount of spatial relaxation can substantially modify the superconducting band structure and the localization properties of the edge states.

</details>


### [207] [Atomic-scale Imaging of Time Reversal Breaking Supercurrents in $\mathrm{Bi_2Sr_2CaCu_2O_8}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23060)
*Bin Hu,Catherine Dawson,Shuqiu Wang,Siyuan Wan,Huiyu Zhao,Kuanysh Zhussubekov,S. Ishida,Hiroshi Eisaki,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Jonathan Ward,J. C. Séamus Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用双人工神经网络（dual-ANN, VMAP）技术，从 $\mathrm{Bi_2Sr_2CaCu_2O_8}$ 的电子态密度 $N(r,E)$ 中实现 $d$-波超电流（$d$-wave supercurrent）速度 $V(r)$ 的成像，在 $\mu$T 级磁场下观测到满足基尔霍夫定律（Kirchhoff's law）的超电流环网络，为铜氧化物中时间反演（$\mathcal{T}$）对称性破缺提供了直接探测视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CuO$_2$ 基高温超导（high temperature superconductivity）中时间反演（time reversal, $\mathcal{T}$）对称性破缺的机制与后果长期成谜，理论假设聚焦于持续电子电流（persistent electronic currents），但此类电流此前无法被直接实验探测。原子尺度超电流可视化技术的发展打破了这一僵局。

Method: 对 $\mathrm{Bi_2Sr_2CaCu_2O_8}$ 进行能量分辨电子态密度 $N(r,E)$ 成像，并采用人工神经网络（artificial neural networks, ANN）策略设计双神经网络（dual-ANN, VMAP），高效实现无序 $d$-波超导体中超电流速度 $V(r)$ 的可视化。借助量子“罗塞塔石碑”（quantum "Rosetta Stone"）将 $N(r,E)$ 映射为 $V(r)$，用以验证探测结果的有效性。

Result: 在 $|B| \leq \mu$T 磁场、空穴掺杂 0.17 $\leq p \leq$ 0.22 范围内，观测到统计上可重复的超电流流动网络，其各结点满足基尔霍夫定律。测得超电流速度 1250 m/s $< V <$ 4000 m/s，与无序 $d$-波超导体的理论预测及报道的 $|B| \approx$ 0.1 mT 磁场幅度一致。

Conclusion: 超电流环网络的探测与可视化为理解铜氧化物中的 $\mathcal{T}$ 对称性破缺提供了独特视角，同时凸显了 ANN 技术在高数据量量子物质研究中加速物理发现的效用。

Abstract: A persistent mystery of CuO_2-based high temperature superconductivity has been the mechanism and consequences of time reversal ($\mathcal{T}$)-symmetry breaking. Although theoretical hypotheses focus upon persistent electronic currents as a potential cause, these have been invisible to direct experimental probes. That impasse has recently been removed by development of techniques for atomic-scale supercurrent visualization. Here, by imaging the energy-resolved density of electronic states $N(r,E)$ in $\mathrm{Bi_2Sr_2CaCu_2O_8}$, we search for the specific signatures of flowing supercurrents in a disordered $d$-wave superconductor. Employing the strategy of artificial neural networks (ANN) for complex quantum matter visualization, we designed a dual-ANN (VMAP) to efficiently implement $d$-wave supercurrent visualization, achieving imaging of supercurrent speeds $V(r)$ at magnetic fields $|B| \leq μT$ in $\mathrm{Bi_2Sr_2CaCu_2O_8}$. Correspondence between specific characteristics of $N(r,E)$ around each supercurrent loop and a quantum "Rosetta Stone" which translates $N(r,E)$ to $V(r)$, emphasizes the validity of $V(r)$ detection and visualization. Then, for hole-densities 0.17 $\leq p \leq$ 0.22 we observe statistically repeatable networks of supercurrent flows. Crucially, these supercurrents satisfy Kirchhoff's law at junctions within each network. Further, theoretical predictions for supercurrent speeds $V$ in disordered $d$-wave superconductors are consistent with measured 1250 m/s $< V <$ 4000 m/s, and with reported magnetic field amplitudes $|B| \approx$ 0.1 mT Thus, detection and visualization of these supercurrent loop networks provides unique perspectives on $\mathcal{T}$-symmetry breaking in cuprates. It also emphasizes the accelerating utility of ANN techniques for physics discovery from high data volume quantum matter research.

</details>


### [208] [Quasi-long-range order in the quantum XY model on Sierpiński gaskets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23065)
*Robert Canyellas,Jannes van Poppelen,Annica M. Black-Schaffer,Mikhail I. Katsnelson,Andrey A. Bagrov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用视觉Transformer神经量子态（vision-transformer neural quantum states）结合变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo），在三角形与六边形Sierpiński垫片上研究自旋-1/2 XY铁磁体基态，发现横向两点关联函数呈幂律衰减，表明量子相位涨落不破坏准长程超导相位相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分形几何被证明可增强超导序参量幅度，但其上超导相的命运不清楚：有限分支分形（finitely ramified fractals）上的经典XY模型无有限温度相变，因涡旋能量随系统增长保持有界；但该结论针对无限垫片与热涡旋，而真实分形结构本质有限，且$T=0$下相位所对抗的是量子涨落而非温度，这一问题此前未被回答。

Method: 研究自旋-1/2 XY铁磁体（即每个岛至多一个库珀对的Josephson网络相位扇区），以最大化量子相位涨落效应；对象为三角形与六边形Sierpiński垫片，采用视觉Transformer神经量子态并由变分蒙特卡洛优化，达到$G=5$代（三角366格点、六边形609格点），远超精确对角化（exact diagonalization）能力。

Result: 横向两点关联函数呈代数衰减$C_{xy}(r)\sim r^{-\eta}$，$\eta\simeq 0.12\!-\!0.32$；该指数与距离沿图、沿分形边界或嵌入平面测量无关。量子相位涨落在基态中保留了准长程有序（quasi-long-range order）。

Conclusion: 量子相位涨落不破坏分形垫片基态的超导相位相干，据此进一步讨论了有限垫片在低但非零温度下超导响应的含义。

Abstract: Fractal geometry has recently been shown to enhance the amplitude of the superconducting order parameter, but the fate of the superconducting phase on such lattices is far less clear. Classical XY models on finitely ramified fractals have no finite-temperature transition, as the energy of a vortex stays bounded as the system grows. However, that statement concerns an infinite gasket and thermal vortices, while real fractal structures are finite by construction. It also leaves open what happens at $T = 0$, where the phase competes not with temperature but with its own quantum fluctuations. In this work we address whether superconducting phase coherence survives at $T=0$. %Whether coherence survives those is the prior question, and the one we address here. To that end, we study the spin-$1/2$ XY ferromagnet, the phase sector of a Josephson network with at most one Cooper pair per island, thus capturing the maximum effect of quantum phase fluctuations, on both triangular and honeycomb Sierpiński gaskets. By using vision-transformer neural quantum states optimized by variational Monte Carlo, we reach generations up to $G = 5$, that is $366$ sites for the triangular gasket and $609$ for the honeycomb one, well beyond the reach of exact diagonalization. We find that the transverse two-point correlator decays algebraically, $C_{xy}(r) \sim r^{-η}$ with $η\simeq 0.12\!-\!0.32$, independent of distance measured along the graph, along the fractal boundary, or in the embedding plane. Quantum phase fluctuations thus leave quasi-long-range order intact in the ground state. We further discuss the implication of these results for the superconducting response of a finite gasket at low but nonzero temperature.

</details>


### [209] [Emergent Attraction and Competing Orders near the Van Hove Singularity in Rhombohedral $n$-layer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23232)
*Jing-Yu Zhao,Yang-Zhi Chou,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用补丁重整化群方法，在含真实能带结构的三角（rhombohedral）n层石墨烯中研究内范霍夫奇点附近的电荷密度波与配对密度波不稳定性竞争，发现纯排斥相互作用经RG流可产生有效吸引，且二者的主导地位由对VHS的失谐与温度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角n层石墨烯中的非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）相图包含多个超导区域，但其微观起源尚不清楚，尤其是实验观测到的SC1与SC2之间的分离缺乏定性解释。

Method: 采用补丁重整化群（patch renormalization-group）方法，并纳入真实能带结构（realistic band structures），研究内范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities, VHSs）附近电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）与配对密度波（pair-density-wave, PDW）不稳定性之间的竞争。

Result: 在存在C3对称性破缺（C3 symmetry breaking）时，RG流可使纯排斥相互作用产生有效吸引相互作用，其耦合结构为两个排斥耦合与一个吸引耦合，从而驱动CDW与PDW的竞争。该竞争受对VHS的失谐（detuning）调控：CDW在VHS填充附近及较高温度下主导，而PDW在VHS两侧及较低温度下成为主导不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该机制自然产生被CDW主导区域分隔的两个超导区域，为实验观测到的SC1与SC2分离提供了可能的一种定性解释。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity in rhombohedral $n$-layer graphene exhibits a rich phase diagram with multiple distinct superconducting regions, whose microscopic origins remain unclear. Here, we investigate the competition between charge-density-wave (CDW) and pair-density-wave (PDW) instabilities near the inner van Hove singularities (VHSs), using a patch renormalization-group approach with the realistic band structures incorporated. We identify two key findings. First, we find an effective attractive interaction emerging from purely repulsive interactions through RG flow in the presence of $C_3$ symmetry breaking. The resulting interaction structure, with two repulsive couplings and one attractive coupling, gives rise to competing CDW and PDW instabilities. Second, the competition is controlled by the detuning from the VHS. While CDW dominates near the VHS filling and at higher temperatures, PDW becomes the leading instability on either side of the VHS and at lower temperatures. This mechanism naturally produces two superconducting regions separated by a CDW-dominated regime, providing a possible qualitative explanation for the experimentally observed separation between SC1 and SC2.

</details>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Riemannian geometry and quantum gravity on trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23103)
*Shahn Majid*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了允许正比例度量量子黎曼几何（QRG）的树图恰为有限型A、D、E与仿射型D̃、Ê的Dynkin图，并据此构造新的量子引力（quantum gravity）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于确定哪些图（树图）能够承载具有正比例度量的量子黎曼几何（quantum Riemannian geometry），从而为基于离散图的量子引力模型提供分类基础。

Method: 度量定义为对每条箭头赋予非零实数值，正比例要求任一边两个方向同号；单方向取值可自由选取、另一方向随之确定。有限A、D、E型借助q-整数（q-integers）构造，其中q=e^{iπ/h}、h为Coxeter数；并利用该几何构造自然的Einstein–Hilbert作用量（Einstein–Hilbert action），以泛函积分（functional integral）方法建立模型。

Result: 有限A、D、E型存在共轭的一对保*量子Levi-Civita联络（quantum Levi-Civita connections, QLCs），A型情形必落在已知的单参数联络空间中；仿射D̃、Ê型仅有唯一的实系数QLC。由此在A_3、D_4、D̃_4、D̃_5、D̃_6、Ê_6上得到新的量子引力模型，并重新审视了4-star D̃_4。

Conclusion: 结论给出允许正比例度量QRG的树图的完整分类（有限A、D、E与仿射D̃、Ê），并表明该类几何可自然导出Einstein–Hilbert作用量，为在Dynkin图上构建量子引力模型提供统一框架。

Abstract: We show that the tree graphs that admit a quantum Riemannian geometry (QRG) with a positive ratio metric are exactly the Dynkin graphs of finite type $A,D,E$ and affine type $\widetilde D,\widetilde E$. A metric in this context is an assignment of a nonzero real value to each arrow and positive ratio requires that the two directions on any edge have the same sign. It turns out that the metric value in one direction can be chosen freely and the other is then determined. For finite $A, D, E$ type, the construction involves $q$-integers where $q=e^{\imathπ/h}$ with $h$ the Coxeter number and there is a conjugate pair of $*$-preserving quantum Levi-Civita connections (QLCs). In the case of type $A$, these are necessarily contained in a previously known 1-parameter space of connections. For affine $\widetilde D,\widetilde E$ type, there is only one QLC, with real coefficients. Using the quantum Riemannian geometry, we construct a natural Einstein--Hilbert action and hence new quantum gravity models on $A_3,D_4,\widetilde D_4,\widetilde D_5,\widetilde D_6,\widetilde E_6$ in a functional integral approach, other than the 4-star $\widetilde D_4$ which we revisit.

</details>


### [211] [Holomorphic Vector Bundles on the Quantum Projective Line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23105)
*P. Aschieri,M. Khalkhali,G. Landi*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在Dolbeault模框架下研究量子射影直线上的全纯向量丛的Birkhoff–Grothendieck分裂，证明由标准线对象有限滤过的多项式全纯丛可唯一分解为标准线直和，并给出秩二联络族的分类与若干代数侧结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Birkhoff–Grothendieck定理在非交换（量子）几何中的类比尚不完善，需要建立量子射影直线与量子平面上全纯丛分裂的严格框架，并厘清其与无限制标准线分裂的区别。

Method: 采用Dolbeault微分分次范畴（differential graded category）与双箭头Kronecker箭图（two-arrow Kronecker quiver）；对三参数秩二扩张族用“量子连接映射”的行列式判别分裂类型与非零分裂层；对一般非对角秩二联络导出非交换Riccati方程并用全纯投影子表述整体分裂问题。

Result: 证明了可由标准线对象有限滤过的多项式全纯丛必为标准线直和，且分裂整数唯一确定；给出秩二扩张族的分裂型分类与非零分裂层的行列式判别，并解释量子射影直线的基础层范畴（bare sheaf category）与量子平面的Artin–Zhang范畴均继承经典分裂定理。

Conclusion: 分裂结果须与无限制标准线分裂区分：后者对多项式规范（polynomial gauges）不成立。文中另讨论扭曲正Hochschild上闭链、变分原理与共形稳定性猜想，并提出关注正则性与等变性的微分量子GAGA纲领。

Abstract: We study Birkhoff--Grothendieck splitting for holomorphic vector bundles on the quantum projective line in the Dolbeault-module framework. We prove that every polynomial holomorphic bundle admitting a finite filtration by standard line objects is a direct sum of standard lines, with uniquely determined splitting integers. The proof uses a Dolbeault differential graded category and the two-arrow Kronecker quiver. For a three-parameter rank-two extension family, we determine the splitting types and distinguish the nonzero splitting strata by the determinant of a quantum connecting map. For general non-diagonal rank-two connections, we derive noncommutative Riccati equations for invariant graph submodules and formulate the global splitting problem in terms of holomorphic projectors. We also discuss twisted positive Hochschild cocycles and formulate a variational principle and a conformal-stability conjecture. On the algebraic side, we explain why both the bare sheaf category of the quantum projective line and the Artin--Zhang category of the quantum plane inherit classical Birkhoff--Grothendieck splitting. We distinguish these results from unrestricted standard-line splitting, which fails for polynomial gauges, and outline a differential quantum GAGA programme with explicit attention to regularity and equivariance.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

### [212] [The Bessis Moussa Villani conjecture in finite von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23122)
*Liviu Paunescu,Florin Radulescu,Andreas Thom*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Bessis–Moussa–Villani 猜想在有限 von Neumann 代数（finite von Neumann algebra）中的有界算子情形成立：对带有忠实正规迹态（faithful normal tracial state）的代数，存在平面联合谱测度，其矩（moments）恰为对称化字（symmetrized words）的迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bessis–Moussa–Villani 猜想此前仅在矩阵代数（matrix algebras）中已知成立，将其推广到有限 von Neumann 代数中的有界算子可检验该猜想在更一般非交换框架下的普适性。

Method: 沿用 Heinavaara 的路线：对一对正算子构造穿孔平面（punctured plane）上的迹联合谱测度，其矩为对称化字的迹；再通过全纯谱选择（holomorphic spectral selection）与 Lyapunov 恒等式构造标量测度，并借助矩的积分恒等式得到两个坐标加权分布的正性。

Result: 由坐标加权分布的正性推导出平面测度，并给出迹指数（trace exponential）的 Laplace 表示（Laplace representation），从而完成猜想的证明。

Conclusion: 该猜想从矩阵代数推广至具有忠实正规迹态的有限 von Neumann 代数中的有界算子，所建立的整体构造（迹联合谱测度与迹指数的 Laplace 表示）为相关非交换分析问题提供了工具。

Abstract: We prove the Bessis--Moussa--Villani conjecture, which is known for matrix algebras, for bounded operators in a finite von Neumann algebra with a faithful normal tracial state. Following the approach of Heinavaara, for a pair of positive operators, we construct a tracial joint spectral measure on the punctured plane whose moments are the traces of symmetrized words. We construct a scalar measure by holomorphic spectral selection and a Lyapunov identity. An integral identity for its moments then gives positivity of two coordinate-weighted distributions. From these we obtain the planar measure and the Laplace representation of the trace exponential.

</details>

### [213] [Precision Muon Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23043)
*Tim Gorringe*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该综述概述了精确缪子物理（precision muon physics）的整体格局，涵盖从缪子衰变、储存缪子到束缚缪子的测量，及其对费米常数、弱相互作用手征结构、束缚态QED与标准模型的检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子已成为基础物理研究的通用工具，其精密测量既可确定基本常数与相互作用结构，也对束缚态量子电动力学（bound-state QED）和标准模型（Standard Model）构成深远检验，并对超出已知物理的新物理具有独特灵敏度。

Method: 综述梳理了多种实验技术，包括带电粒子计时（timing）、径迹测量（tracking）、极化测量（polarimetry）、储存环实验（storage-ring experiments）、微波共振方法（microwave resonance methods）以及无多普勒激光光谱（Doppler-free laser spectroscopy）。

Result: 缪子衰变测量可确定费米常数（Fermi constant）并确立弱相互作用的手征结构；储存缪子与束缚缪子测量实现缪子电荷、质量和磁矩的精密测定，兼具物理探索深度与实验技术广度。

Conclusion: 实验工作与量子电动力学（QED）、量子色动力学（QCD）及有效场论（effective field theory）的理论发展相互促进，共同推动精确缪子物理研究。

Abstract: The muon has become a versatile tool for fundamental physics. Measurements of muon decay determine the Fermi constant and establish the chiral structure of weak interactions. Measurements with stored muons and bound muons enable precision determinations of the muon charge, mass, and magnetic moment. These studies also provide far-reaching tests of bound-state QED and the Standard Model, as well as unique sensitivity to physics beyond our current understanding of the particles, forces, and laws of nature. We survey the landscape of precision muon physics, emphasizing both the physics reach and the experimental breadth. We highlight the range of techniques, from charged-particle timing, tracking, and polarimetry to storage-ring experiments, microwave resonance methods, and Doppler-free laser spectroscopy. We also describe the interplay between experimental work and theoretical developments in quantum electrodynamics, quantum chromodynamics, and effective field theory.

</details>
