# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [New traversable wormholes without invoking exotic matter in $f(Q)$ from the freedom of different connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21658)
*Ismael Ayuso,Mariam Bouhmadi-López,Che-Yu Chen,Konstantinos Dialektopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在$f(Q)$引力中，额外联络自由度可替代奇异物质，使Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞满足零能量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中的虫洞解需依赖违反零能量条件（null energy condition）的奇异物质（exotic matter），本文探究在$f(Q)$引力中借助额外联络自由度避免奇异物质的可能性。

Method: 以Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞为例，在对称远平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）框架下求解场方程，识别出两类与时空对称性相容的联络，并分别讨论$f_{QQ}$正则与在有限$Q$处发散两种情形。

Result: 两类联络下，当场方程中$f_{QQ}$正则时，常数非度规标量（non-metricity scalar）$Q$自然出现，所得精确解析解在全时空满足零能量条件；第二类联络在$f_{QQ}$于有限$Q$发散时可给出$Q$空间变化的解，同样处处满足零能量条件。

Conclusion: $f(Q)$引力中的非平凡联络自由度可自然支撑虫洞几何，无需引入任何奇异物质。

Abstract: Wormhole solutions in General Relativity can only exist in the presence of exotic matter that violates the null energy condition. In this paper, we investigate the possibility of constructing wormhole solutions in $f(Q)$ gravity in which the extra connection degrees of freedom contribute such that no exotic matter is needed. Considering the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole as an example, we find that the system admits two families of connections consistent with the spacetime symmetries and the requirements of symmetric teleparallel gravity. For both families, we show that constant non-metricity scalar $Q$ is not merely a simplifying assumption but arises naturally from the field equations when $f_{QQ}$ is regular, and that the resulting exact analytic solutions can satisfy the null energy condition throughout the entire spacetime. For the second family, which possesses richer connection degrees of freedom, we further demonstrate that solutions with a spatially varying $Q$ exist when $f_{QQ}$ is allowed to diverge at a finite value of $Q$, and that these too can satisfy the null energy condition everywhere. Our results show that the nontrivial connection degrees of freedom in $f(Q)$ gravity can naturally be responsible for supporting wormhole geometries, without resorting to any exotic matter.

</details>


### [215] [Hidden wormholes from an action principle in $D=4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21712)
*Francesco Del Porro,Alessia Platania,Samuele Silveravalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四维时空中仅保留引力子（graviton）为唯一引力自由度的引力有效场论（gravitational effective field theory）框架下，求解至曲率三次阶的场方程，数值获得具有"单向隐藏虫洞"结构的正则黑洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则黑洞（regular black hole）普遍存在内视界（inner horizon），从而遭受质量暴涨不稳定性（mass inflation instability）的困扰；本文旨在构造可规避该问题的黑洞几何。

Method: 在保留引力子为唯一引力自由度的引力有效场论扩展中，纳入至曲率三次（cubic order in curvature）的高阶项，求解相应场方程并进行数值构造。

Result: 得到"单向隐藏虫洞"（one-way hidden wormhole）数值解：其为正则黑洞，内部存在可单向穿越的虫洞喉（wormhole throat），通向内部宇宙学时空；解正则且无内视界。

Conclusion: 由于不存在内视界，该类解规避了困扰常规正则黑洞几何的质量暴涨不稳定性，表明高阶曲率有效场论可为构造自洽黑洞内部结构提供新途径。

Abstract: We present new black hole solutions for the class of gravitational effective field theories in four spacetime dimensions that extend General Relativity while retaining the graviton as the only degree of freedom of gravitational origin. We consider terms up to cubic order in curvature, solve the resulting field equations, and find one-way hidden wormholes as numerical solutions: these are regular black holes with an interior wormhole throat that is crossable in one direction and leads to a cosmological spacetime in its interior. They are regular and have no inner horizon, thus evading the mass inflation instability that afflicts conventional regular black hole geometries.

</details>


### [216] [Cyclic Boson Stars with Quantum Vortex Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21734)
*Xing-Yu Yang,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在复标量场与引力耦合的最小模型中，发现一类具有循环旋转对称性（cyclic rotational symmetry）的新型稳态玻色星（boson star），其每粒子角动量可为约化普朗克常数的非整数倍，星体内部呈自引力量子涡旋分子（quantum vortex molecules）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统旋转玻色星以轴对称（axisymmetric）方式携带角动量，每粒子角动量为整数倍，限制了玻色星角动量携带机制的认识，作者旨在寻找新的角动量携带方式。

Method: 在复标量场与引力耦合的最小模型（minimal model）框架下，通过求解场方程构造具有循环旋转对称性的稳态玻色星解，并分析其内部涡旋组态（vortex assemblies）。

Result: 所得构型可携带以约化普朗克常数为单位的非整数每粒子角动量；星内涡旋组合被识别为“自引力量子涡旋分子”，即尽管存在内部环流，引力仍维持稳态涡旋排列。

Conclusion: 该工作为玻色星提供了新的角动量携带途径，给出凝聚态中所研究量子涡旋分子的相对论实现，并启发了可能的实验室类比（laboratory analogues）。

Abstract: We find a new type of stationary boson star with cyclic rotational symmetry in the minimal model of complex scalar field coupled to gravity. Unlike conventional axisymmetric spinning boson stars, these configurations can carry noninteger angular momentum per particle in units of the reduced Planck constant, revealing a new way for boson stars to carry angular momentum. We identify the vortex assemblies within these stars as self-gravitating quantum vortex molecules, in which gravity supports a stationary vortex arrangement despite internal circulation. These structures provide a relativistic realization of quantum vortex molecules studied in condensates and motivate possible laboratory analogues.

</details>


### [217] [Gravitational Mode Instabilities in Kerr-like Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21735)
*Pedro Henrique Croti Siqueira,Rodrigo Dal Bosco Fontana,Maurício Richartz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在高自旋类 Kerr 时空（Konoplya–Rezzolla–Zhidenko 参数化）上，自旋权重 s=-2 的类 Teukolsky 算符在自旋与形变参数足够大时出现指数增长模式，而电磁模式始终衰减；所得准正规模谱还与 GW250114 铃宕数据相容参数区域进行了对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验超越 Kerr 的形变黑洞（含 a/M > 1 仍保有视界的构型）上微扰算符的稳定性（stability），此前对这类参数空间中准正规模（quasinormal mode, QNM）行为与不稳定性的认识有限。

Method: 在 Konoplya–Rezzolla–Zhidenko（KRZ）参数化时空上，采用连分数法（continued fraction method）计算电磁与引力准正规模，并施加准正规模边界条件（quasinormal mode boundary conditions）。

Result: 自旋权重 s=-2 算符在黑洞自旋和形变参数足够大时出现指数增长模式（exponentially growing modes），而电磁模式在整个参数空间保持衰减；与 GW250114 铃宕（ringdown）的无先验分析比较后，识别出与数据推断谱相容的参数区域。

Conclusion: 形变高自旋黑洞的引力微扰可出现不稳定性，构成对 a/M > 1 构型的约束；KRZ 参数空间中存在与 GW250114 铃宕观测相容的部分，可作为引力波检验的候选区域。

Abstract: We identify exponentially growing modes of a homogeneous gravitational Teukolsky-like operator on highly spinning Kerr-like black holes subject to quasinormal mode boundary conditions. Specifically, we compute electromagnetic and gravitational quasinormal modes of a spacetime proposed by Konoplya, Rezzolla, and Zhidenko that parametrizes deformations from the Kerr geometry, including configurations with black hole spin parameter $a/M > 1$ that retain an event horizon. Using the continued fraction method, we find that the spin-weight $s=-2$ operator develops exponentially growing modes when the black hole spin and deformation parameters are sufficiently large, while the electromagnetic modes remain damped throughout the parameter space. We further compare the quasinormal mode predictions with an agnostic analysis of the GW250114 ringdown, identifying regions of the parameter space that are compatible with the spectrum inferred from the data.

</details>


### [218] [Binary black-hole mergers with ultralight vector bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21774)
*Adrian Ka-Wai Chung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作数值模拟了等质量、无自旋双黑洞与有质量矢量场（Proca 场）耦合的旋近（inspiral）、并合（merger）与铃宕（ringdown）过程，发现 Proca 场虽迅速聚集于黑洞周围但仍保持非平凡动力学，轨道演化依赖于矢量场质量，而对齐后引力波形与真空情形接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有质量矢量场（massive vector field，Proca 场）与双黑洞系统耦合时，场对并合过程及引力波波形的影响程度，特别是检验其在并合—铃宕形态上是否留下可辨识印记。

Method: 采用数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟等质量、无自旋双黑洞与 Proca 场的耦合演化，并以满足约束（constraint-consistent）的电场初始数据起步；通过时间、相位与振幅对齐后与真空波形进行比较。

Result: Proca 场在演化初期迅速向黑洞周围聚集，但随后仍表现出非平凡的动力学行为；轨道演化对矢量场质量存在依赖；在时间、相位与振幅对齐后，主导引力波形与真空结果接近。

Conclusion: 上述对比表明，在当前分辨率下，Proca 场对对齐后的并合—铃宕形态（merger-ringdown morphology）留下的印记有限，其可观测影响可能主要体现在轨道演化等方面而非主导波形的形态特征。

Abstract: We simulate the inspiral, merger, and ringdown of equal-mass, nonspinning binary black holes coupled to a massive vector (Proca) field. Starting from constraint-consistent electric-field initial data, we find that the Proca field rapidly concentrates around the black holes. However, it continues to exhibit nontrivial dynamics. Orbital evolution depends on vector mass, while the dominant gravitational waveforms remain close to vacuum after time, phase, and amplitude alignment. This contrast highlights the limited imprint of the field on aligned merger-ringdown morphology at these resolutions.

</details>


### [219] [Self-gravitating Lee-Huang-Yang fluids as dark matter halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21911)
*Abdelaali Boudjemaa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自引力玻色流体（self-gravitating Bose fluid）的暗物质晕模型，其有效状态方程由Lee-Huang-Yang量子涨落主导，并通过拟合20个SPARC星系验证其优于NFW、标准BEC和量子液滴模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质晕的中心与外围结构难以被单一模型统一描述，需要引入能同时兼顾量子压力、量子涨落排斥与自引力的物理机制以提升对星系动力学的解释力。

Method: 采用耦合的广义Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson方程（generalized Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson equations），保留完整量子压力（quantum pressure），推导Jeans色散关系并解析给出平衡解的中心与大半径行为，再用SPARC数据库20个星系拟合并与NFW、标准BEC及量子液滴模型对比。

Result: 纯LHY模型能复现广泛的星系运动学观测，其约化χ²值（reduced χ²）及样本平均值均低于对比模型，表明量子压力、LHY量子涨落与自引力的联合效应增强了描述晕中心与外围区域的灵活性。

Conclusion: 纯LHY模型为研究量子涨落诱导相互作用（quantum-fluctuation-induced interaction）在暗物质晕形成与结构中的作用提供了理想平台，但推断的晕参数依赖于所设有效状态方程及其微观解释。

Abstract: We propose a model of dark matter halos based on a self-gravitating Bose fluid whose effective equation of state is dominated by Lee-Huang-Yang quantum fluctuations. The model is governed by coupled generalized Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson equations, which incorporate quantum pressure, Lee-Huang-Yang-induced repulsion, and gravitational attraction. By retaining the full quantum pressure, we examine the halo structure from its central region to its asymptotic regime. We first derive the Jeans dispersion relation and analyze how the Lee-Huang-Yang fluctuations alter the gravitational instability criterion and the associated characteristic length and mass scales. We then obtain analytical expressions for the central and large-radius behaviors of the equilibrium solutions. To verify the validity and the performance of the model, we fit the model-predicted curves with a sample of 20 galaxies from the SPARC database and compare the results with those obtained from Navarro-Frenk-White, standard Bose-Einstein condensate, and quantum-droplet dark matter models. We find that the pure-LHY model reproduces a broad range of observed galactic kinematics, yielding reduced $χ^2$ values and a lower sample average compared to the aforementioned models. These results indicate that the combined effects of quantum pressure, LHY quantum fluctuations and the self-gravity provide additional flexibility in describing both the central and extended outer regions of galactic halos. We also discuss the implications of the inferred halo parameters and emphasize their dependence on the assumed effective equation of state and its microscopic interpretation. The pure-LHY model constitutes an ideal platform for exploring the role of quantum-fluctuation-induced interaction in the formation and structure of dark matter halos.

</details>


### [220] [Finite-wavelength corrections to dynamical friction in fuzzy dark matter: a causal linear-response approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22134)
*Yu Wang,Meilin Liu,Haiguang Xu,Shoupan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用因果线性响应理论（causal linear-response theory）与统计Wigner表述，推导模糊暗物质（fuzzy dark matter, FDM）中有限波长效应对引力动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）的修正，指出半经典区的主导修正是ℏ/m_χ的二阶项，且其降低拖曳幅度并与源径向轮廓无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量天体在模糊暗物质中运动时产生的密度尾迹（density wake）通过引力反作用产生动力学摩擦，但波长有限性（finite-wavelength effects）如何修正经典Chandrasekhar结果尚需系统刻画。

Method: 采用因果线性响应理论与统计Wigner表述，针对均匀Maxwellian背景及各向同性有限尺寸扰动源（finite-size perturbers），给出引力拖曳的解析表示，并在匹配的正则化条件下回到经典Chandrasekhar结果；随后在受控半经典区（semiclassical regime）提取领头有限波长修正。

Result: 领头修正为ℏ/m_χ的二次项，可分解为速度依赖函数与刻画源结构的谱矩之比（ratio of spectral moments）两部分；在所采用的Maxwellian模型中，该修正降低拖曳幅度且不依赖于源的详细径向轮廓，高斯源与指数源作为示例验证了此点。此外，工作建立了受控的余项界（remainder bound），并澄清点源极限（point-source limit）的非一致行为。

Conclusion: 研究明确了波效应（wave effects）修改经典动力学摩擦的物理条件，为判断FDM波动性在何种情形下具有可观测影响提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A massive body moving through fuzzy dark matter (FDM) generates a density wake whose gravitational backreaction produces dynamical friction. We investigate how finite-wavelength effects modify this force using causal linear-response theory and the statistical Wigner formulation. For a homogeneous Maxwellian background and general isotropic finite-size perturbers, we derive an analytic representation of the gravitational drag and recover the classical Chandrasekhar result under matched regularization conditions. In the controlled semiclassical regime, the leading finite-wavelength correction is quadratic in $\hbar/m_χ$ and factorizes into a velocity-dependent function and a ratio of spectral moments characterizing the source structure. Remarkably, this correction reduces the drag magnitude independently of the detailed radial source profile within the adopted Maxwellian model. Gaussian and exponential sources illustrate this result. We further establish a controlled remainder bound and clarify the nonuniform behavior of the point-source limit. These findings identify the physical conditions under which wave effects modify classical dynamical friction.

</details>


### [221] [Gravitational waves from cosmological first-order phase transitions with fluctuation-induced friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22266)
*Jun-Ye Chen,Dongdong Wei,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 通过两个耦合标量场的三维格点模拟（three-dimensional lattice simulations），研究宇宙学一阶相变（cosmological first-order phase transition）中由涨落诱导摩擦（fluctuation-induced friction）影响下的引力波产生。附加场 $s$ 的能量在谱峰处主导，并在峰上方有限区间内呈现与体流模型（bulk flow model）相似的斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画宇宙学一阶相变中涨落诱导摩擦对气泡壁运动及引力波谱的影响，此前该效应在真实场动力学模拟中的谱特征尚不明确。

Method: 采用两个耦合标量场（$φ$ 与 $s$）的三维格点模拟（three-dimensional lattice simulations），考察附加场 $s$ 的动能与梯度能量在膨胀气泡壁附近的积累及其对壁运动的作用。

Result: 在谱峰处，$s$ 的贡献超过 $φ$ 的贡献，并在峰上方有限区间内具有与体流模型（bulk flow model）相近的斜率；在高波数（UV）方向由 $φ$ 主导，总谱呈现宽缓特征。较大耦合情形下，缓慢收缩的假真空残余（false vacuum remnants）在气泡并合后长期存在。

Conclusion: 涨落诱导摩擦使附加场在引力波谱峰区占主导并改变谱形，且强耦合下假真空残余的长期存续可能影响相变后期演化与引力波信号。

Abstract: We study gravitational-wave production in a cosmological first-order phase transition with fluctuation-induced friction using three-dimensional lattice simulations of two coupled scalar fields. Kinetic and gradient energy in the additional field $s$ accumulates near the expanding bubble walls of $φ$ and affects their motion. The contribution from $s$ exceeds that from $φ$ at the spectral peak and has a slope similar to that of the bulk flow model over a finite interval above the peak. In the high-wave-number (UV) direction, $φ$ dominates and the total spectrum develops a broad feature. For the larger coupling, slowly contracting false vacuum remnants persist long after bubble merger.

</details>


### [222] [What makes the gravito-thermodynamic arrow of time coincide with that of proper time?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22372)
*Samarjit Chakraborty,Rituparno Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该摘要探讨广义相对论中球对称理想流体演化的引力熵问题，指出体积畸变与非均匀性虽是引力熵单调增长的必需条件但并非充分条件，还需进一步限制畸变空间，仅偏好壳层穿越焦散、引发局部絮凝与结构形成的畸变才能保证熵单调增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为球对称理想流体（spherically symmetric perfect fluids）在广义相对论（general relativity）中的演化问题寻求一种几何学解决方案（geometrical resolution），特别是厘清引力熵（gravitational entropy）随时间单调增长的条件。

Method: 采用几何分析路径，考察体积畸变（volume distortions）与非均匀性（inhomogeneities）与总引力熵（total gravitational entropy）随固有时（proper time）变化之间的关系，并对畸变空间（space of distortions）作进一步细化分类。

Result: 发现体积畸变与非均匀性对引力熵单调增长是必要但不充分的；只有偏好壳层穿越焦散（shell crossing caustics）、引起局部絮凝（local flocculations）与结构形成（structure formations）的那类畸变，才能确保熵与固有时同步单调增长。

Conclusion: 该结果对研究各类引力坍缩（gravitational collapse）解与宇宙学解（cosmological solutions）的爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein field equations）的热力学可行性（thermodynamical viability）具有深远意义。

Abstract: In the pursuit of a geometrical resolution to this important question for the evolution of spherically symmetric perfect fluids in general relativity, we excavated a novel result: the presence of volume distortions and inhomogeneities are, though necessary, but not sufficient for the total gravitational entropy to monotonically increase with proper time. We need a further refinement of the space of distortions and only those that favour shell crossing caustics, causing local flocculations and structure formations, can ensure the alignment. This result will have far reaching consequences for the study of thermodynamical viability of various gravitational collapse and cosmological solutions of Einstein field equations.

</details>


### [223] [Gravitational Light Deflection via Fractional Calculus and Chebyshev Polynomial Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22459)
*Aniruddha Ghosh,Ujjal Debnath*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分数阶微积分（fractional calculus）的框架，用于计算静态球对称时空中的引力光线偏折，并将该方法应用于 Schwarzschild 黑洞与 Sgr A* 的强场透镜观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力偏折积分在光子球（photon sphere）附近存在奇异性，标准强偏折近似（strong-deflection approximation）与弱场展开各有适用范围限制，需要一个在宽广最近接近半径范围内都精确且灵活的评估方法。

Method: 通过径向坐标变换将偏折积分转化为 Abel 型积分方程（Abel-type integral equation），利用分数阶积分重构核函数；解析分离光子球附近的领头奇异行为，剩余正则部分用有限 Chebyshev 多项式展开近似。

Result: 以 Schwarzschild 时空为基准验证了方法精度，并与标准强偏折近似和两项弱偏折展开进行比较；进一步计算了 Sgr A* 的相对论像（relativistic images）的极限角位置、角分离与相对放大率等强场透镜观测量。

Conclusion: 该方法在广泛的最接近半径范围内提供了准确且灵活的偏折角评估手段，并可推广至其他静态球对称黑洞几何，包括修改引力理论（modified theories of gravity）中的解。

Abstract: We develop a fractional-calculus-based framework for evaluating gravitational light deflection in static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. By introducing a suitable transformation of the radial coordinate, the deflection integral is recast into an Abel-type integral equation, from which the associated kernel is reconstructed using fractional integration. The leading singular behaviour near the photon sphere is isolated analytically, and the remaining regular part is approximated by a finite Chebyshev polynomial expansion. This combination retains the correct strong-field behaviour while providing an efficient numerical representation of the deflection integral. We demonstrate the accuracy of the method using the Schwarzschild spacetime as a benchmark and compare the resulting deflection angles with the standard strong-deflection approximation and a two-term weak-deflection expansion. We further apply the method to calculate strong-field lensing observables, including the limiting angular position, angular separation, and relative magnification of relativistic images, for the supermassive black holes Sgr A*. The results show that the proposed approach provides an accurate and flexible means of evaluating gravitational deflection angles across a broad range of closest-approach radii. The framework can be extended to other static, spherically symmetric black-hole geometries, including solutions arising in modified theories of gravity.

</details>


### [224] [Geometric optics for gravitational waves in dynamical Chern-Simons gravity: amplitude birefringence and tensor-scalar mixing on general backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22469)
*Tsutomu Kobayashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究在动力学陈-西蒙斯引力（dynamical Chern-Simons gravity）中、几何光学近似下高频引力波的传播，允许标量场非零的一般背景，从而协变地处理振幅双折射（amplitude birefringence），并给出频率上界与传播本征模结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作通常假设背景标量场为零，难以协变地刻画振幅双折射；本研究放松该假设，以在更一般的背景下考察高频引力波的传播性质与自洽性条件。

Method: 采用几何光学近似（geometric optics approximation），在标量场非零的一般背景上对线性化场方程做分析，将背景标量梯度引入的三阶导数项按次领头阶处理，并结合沿射线的纽曼-彭罗斯标量（Newman-Penrose scalar）Ψ0 所导致的张量-标量混合，求解次领头阶的输运方程（transport equations）。

Result: 线性化系统无鬼（ghost-free）要求三阶导数项为次领头项，由此得到频率的协变上界；当张量-标量混合有效时，传播本征模为一个光速模、一个亚光速模和一个超光速模，且鬼-free 条件被破坏会导致经典不稳定性（classical instability）。当混合可忽略时，两种螺旋度（helicity）的相对振幅仅取决于发射与观测事件处沿射线的标量场梯度，而与中间路径无关。

Conclusion: 在含非零标量场的动力学陈-西蒙斯引力背景下，高频引力波的传播可用协变框架描述：存在频率上界与三模本征结构，振幅双折射在弱混合极限下由端点处的标量场梯度决定，具有路径无关性。

Abstract: We study the propagation of high-frequency gravitational waves in dynamical Chern-Simons gravity within the geometric optics approximation. In contrast to previous work, we allow for a general background with a nonvanishing scalar field, which enables a covariant treatment of amplitude birefringence. The background scalar gradient introduces third-derivative terms into the linearized field equations. These must be treated as subleading for the linearized system to be free of ghosts, which yields a covariant upper bound on the frequency. Tensor-scalar mixing is induced by the Weyl curvature through the Newman-Penrose scalar $Ψ_0$ along the ray. When the mixing is efficient, the propagation eigenmodes consist of one luminal, one subluminal, and one superluminal mode, and a violation of the ghost-free condition leads to a classical instability. From the transport equations at next-to-leading order, we show that, when the mixing is negligible, the relative amplitude of the two helicities depends only on the scalar-field gradients along the ray at the emission and observation events, independently of the path in between.

</details>


### [225] [Shadows and Images of Black Holes with Purely Nonlinear Scalarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22586)
*Hao Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 Einstein–scalar–Gauss–Bonnet 引力中，纯非线性标量化（purely nonlinear scalarization）的黑洞阴影半径可比同质量 Schwarzschild 黑洞小约 21.7%，且光子轨道指数与高阶像压缩提供阴影尺寸之外的补充信息；可见特征还依赖发射模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是考察不依赖 Schwarzschild 黑洞线性快子不稳定性（linear tachyonic instability）的纯非线性标量化机制，是否会以及如何改变黑洞阴影（shadow）与辐射图像（radiative images）。

Method: 采用静态球对称时空，在四次指数耦合（quartic exponential coupling）下构建标量化背景，比较质量与耦合依赖性，并用光学薄球面发射（optically thin spherical emission）与正面薄盘（face-on thin disk）在径向稳定背景上成像，同时引入光子轨道指数（photon-orbit index）刻画单位轨道角度的径向不稳定性。

Result: 所采样的径向稳定分支上阴影半径小于同质量 Schwarzschild 黑洞，减小幅度可达约 21.7%；径向不稳定分支的阴影尺寸偏离明显更小。光子轨道指数呈非单调变化，即使归一化阴影半径单调变化；该指数控制高阶像的渐近压缩。球面发射下径向内落（radial infall）使中心相对亮环更暗；圆盘情形下平滑度规变化可形成分隔两个稳定区的非稳定区间，使盘内边缘向外移动并在几何阴影边界内抑制发射，而归一化阴影半径仅轻微变化。

Conclusion: 纯非线性标量化可通过光子传播与发射物质轨道结构两条途径改变黑洞图像，且可见特征取决于所采用的发射模型。

Abstract: We investigate the shadows and radiative images of static, spherically symmetric black holes with purely nonlinear scalarization in Einstein--scalar--Gauss--Bonnet gravity. The quartic exponential coupling supports scalarized backgrounds without a linear tachyonic instability of Schwarzschild black holes. We compare mass and coupling dependences and construct images on radially stable backgrounds using optically thin spherical emission and face-on thin disks. On the sampled radially stable branches, we find smaller shadow radii than those of Schwarzschild black holes at the same mass, with reductions reaching approximately $21.7\%$. The sampled radially unstable branches show much smaller shadow-size deviations. The photon-orbit index, which measures radial instability per unit orbital angle, varies nonmonotonically even when the normalized shadow radius changes monotonically. The index also controls the asymptotic compression of higher-order images, providing information complementary to shadow size. For spherical emission, radial infall produces a darker center relative to the bright ring than static emission. For circular disks, we find that smooth metric variations can create an unstable interval separating two stable regions of timelike circular motion. When the disk occupies the stable region connected to infinity, its inner edge moves outward across this transition, suppressing emission inside the geometric shadow boundary while the normalized shadow radius changes only slightly. Our results show that purely nonlinear scalarization can alter images through both photon propagation and the orbital structure of emitting matter, with the visible features depending on the emission model.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond the point lens: radial structure and the horizon in wave-optics gravitational lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22618)
*Alice Garoffolo,Gianmassimo Tasinato*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出解析框架，研究波光学（wave optics）区域引力波透镜（gravitational-wave lensing）对透镜外部径向轮廓及中心边界条件的敏感性，并将结果表述为牛顿点质量透镜解的修正，进而统一了牛顿修饰（Newtonian dressing）、衍射与内部边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波光学区引力波透镜不仅依赖透镜总质量，还敏感于其外部径向轮廓与中心边界条件，而已有点质量图像不能刻画这些结构效应。

Method: 以标量波为对象，将修正按距透镜距离的逆幂展开，采用"修饰径向核（dressed radial kernels）"——包含全阶牛顿相互作用并重求和长程相位，以克服自由波附近失效的玻恩展开（Born expansion）；保留波前曲率，并通过对史瓦西黑洞视界（horizon）处入射解匹配来确定内部边界条件。

Result: 散射波并非全部响应：逆平方形变对直接波的改变量与散射波相当，即使散射波很弱；外部势的高阶项导致短距发散，表明仅外部几何不足以确定透镜波。对史瓦西黑洞，视界以吸收性接触项（absorptive contact term）进入放大因子（amplification factor），可重现视界截面；该效应对同质量非吸收透镜不存在且无法用薄透镜衍射积分描述，并在质量平方阶与有限距离光学修正同阶、在对齐源时大幅抵消。

Conclusion: 结果为点质量引力波透镜修正给出系统描述，将牛顿修饰、衍射与内部边界条件联系起来；内部边界条件的缺失信息必须由一组局域项补充。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave lensing in the wave-optics regime is sensitive to the structure of the lens beyond its total mass: to its exterior radial profile and, as we show, to the boundary condition at its centre. We develop an analytic framework to compute these effects for a scalar wave, as corrections to the wave lensed by a Newtonian point mass. The corrections are organized in inverse powers of the distance from the lens and computed with dressed radial kernels, which include the Newtonian interaction to all orders and resum the long-range phase that invalidates the Born expansion around the free wave. Retaining wavefront curvature, we find that the scattered wave is not the whole response: an inverse-square deformation changes the direct wave by an equal amount, even when the scattered wave is faint. Steeper terms of the exterior potential lead to short-distance divergences, which signal that the exterior geometry alone does not determine the lensed wave: the missing information is a set of local terms fixed by the inner boundary condition. For a Schwarzschild black hole we determine them by matching to the ingoing solution at the horizon. The horizon enters the amplification factor as an absorptive contact term, which reproduces the horizon-area cross section; it is absent for a non-absorbing lens of the same mass, and cannot be described by the thin-lens diffraction integral. It completes the correction quadratic in the mass, where it appears at the same order as finite-distance optical corrections, and largely cancels against them for an aligned source. Together, these results connect Newtonian dressing, diffraction and inner boundary conditions, in a systematic description of corrections to point-mass gravitational-wave lensing.

</details>


### [227] [Inflation with an Exponential Power-Law Potential:Analytical Slow-Roll Relations, Branch Dynamics, and Conditional Reheating Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22647)
*Feyzollah Younesizadeh,Younes Younesizadeh,Wen-Xiang Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在慢滚近似下解析研究一类指数型势（含修正因子）驱动的正则单场暴胀（canonical single-field inflation），给出标量倾斜、张量比与跑动的端点无关一致关系（consistency relation），并指出负场分支下裸单场模型无法自然结束暴胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始单场暴胀模型（尤其山丘型与指数型势）在负场分支常面临暴胀无法自行终止的问题，需要澄清裸模型内部能否提供退出机制，并在无法自然退出的前提下建立可条件性比较的解析框架。

Method: 采用正则爱因斯坦—标量作用量（canonical Einstein–scalar action），在慢滚近似（slow-roll approximation）下以对数斜率 A=kx/(1+kx)（x=φ/M_Pl）重写局域谱；利用相空间（phase-space）精确论证讨论负场分支；用朗伯W函数（Lambert-W）求解慢滚e折数积分；再热匹配（reheating matching）采用显式能标与热学约定。

Result: 得到标量倾斜（scalar tilt）、张量比（tensor ratio）与跑动（running）在给定近似阶数下的端点无关一致性关系；精确相空间分析表明负场分支上初始下坡的山丘型暴胀轨迹无法在裸单场模型内结束暴胀。以 x_e=-2 为唯象输入（endpoint），用 Lambert-W 解给出e折数，并以表格与图展示；再热关系同时施加端点能量约束与非负再热持续时间。

Conclusion: 研究确立了局域解析结构与条件性数值基准（conditional numerical benchmarks）。完整的退出机制（exit mechanism）、唯一再热状态方程（reheating equation of state）与精确谱预言仍未建立；数值结果须在能实现该端点并保持绝热谱的完备模型下解读，观测比较基于已发表的边缘化区间而非重建的联合后验。

Abstract: We study canonical single-field inflation driven by the potential $V(φ)=M^4\exp(φ/M_{\rm Pl})(1+kφ/M_{\rm Pl})^{-1/k}$ with $k<0$. We retain the canonical Einstein--scalar action and derive analytical relations within the slow-roll approximation. Expressing the local spectra through the logarithmic slope $A=kx/(1+kx)$, with $x=φ/M_{\rm Pl}$, yields an endpoint-independent consistency relation among the scalar tilt, tensor ratio, and running at their stated approximation orders. On the negative-field branch, an exact phase-space argument shows that an initially downhill, inflationary hilltop trajectory cannot end inflation within the bare single-field model. We specify the endpoint as a phenomenological input and use a Lambert-$W$ solution of the slow-roll e-fold integral. Tables and figures illustrate the fixed choice $x_e=-2$ and must be interpreted conditionally on a completion that implements this endpoint and preserves the adiabatic spectrum. The reheating-matching relation uses explicit scale and thermal conventions and imposes both endpoint energy bounds and nonnegative reheating duration. The observational comparison uses published marginalized intervals rather than reconstructed joint posteriors. These results establish local analytical structure and conditional numerical benchmarks. A complete exit mechanism, a unique reheating equation of state, and precision spectral predictions remain unestablished.

</details>


### [228] [Gravitational Waves Population Study for Testing Kerr Hypothesis: Are Non-Kerr Deformations Universal or Source-Dependent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22725)
*Debtroy Das,Swarnim Shashank,Cosimo Bambi,Yi-Ming Hu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 结合17个以旋进（inspiral）为主的引力波事件，对Johannsen度规的形变参数进行联合与层级贝叶斯分析；结果与Kerr假设一致，但尚无法区分统一形变与随源变化的形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞并合的引力波观测可在强场与动力学区域检验Kerr黑洞假设；前期单事件分析未发现偏离，需联合多事件以提升约束并考察形变是否普遍存在或随源不同。

Method: 先计算各事件的Savage–Dickey贝叶斯因子（Bayes factor）并检验其对先验范围（prior range）与剔除单一事件的依赖性；再在统一形变假设下联合事件似然（likelihood）；最后做层级分析（hierarchical analysis），假定事件级形变参数服从均值μ、本征弥散σ的总体分布。

Result: 统一形变约束为α13 = −0.01^{+0.14}_{−0.12}、ε3 = 0.06^{+0.59}_{−0.54}；总体均值对两参数均与零一致，弥散σ未被当前数据分辨。

Conclusion: 结果与Kerr假设相符，但现有数据尚不足以区分共享形变与随源依赖的形变。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations of binary black hole mergers can test the Kerr black hole hypothesis in the strong-field and dynamical regime. In our previous works, we constrained the deformation parameters $α_{13}$ and $ε_3$ of the Johannsen metric using individual inspiral-dominated events from GWTC-3 and GWTC-4 and found no evidence of deviations from the Kerr solution. Here we combine 17 of these events to test whether black holes are Kerr, share a common non-zero deformation, or have deformations that vary from source to source. We first estimate the Savage--Dickey Bayes factor of each event, and we test how it depends on the prior range and on the removal of single events. We then combine the event likelihoods assuming a universal deformation, yielding a tighter joint constraint. Next, we perform a simple hierarchical analysis by assuming that the event-level deformation parameters are drawn from a population with mean $μ$ and intrinsic scatter $σ$. A universal deformation is constrained to $α_{13}=-0.01^{+0.14}_{-0.12}$ and $ε_3=0.06^{+0.59}_{-0.54}$. The means from the population are consistent with zero for both parameters, and the scatter is not resolved by the current data. Our results are consistent with the Kerr hypothesis, but they cannot yet distinguish a shared deformation from a source-dependent one.

</details>


### [229] [The additive ambiguity of gravitational entropy fixed by the topology of the horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22731)
*Stylianos A. Tsilioukas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文论证在四维 Einstein–Gauss–Bonnet 引力中，广义熵（generalized entropy）迹的加性常数即 Gauss–Bonnet 对 Wald 熵的贡献，且由迹的归一化而非态所承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典展开下子区域可观测量代数为 III$_1$ 型，引入引力约束后变为 II 型，广义熵因此获得迹，但仅定义至一个加性常数；本文旨在确定该常数的物理身份与归属。

Method: 通过分析 Gauss–Bonnet 密度的锥形展开（在 deficit angle 上严格线性、在光滑 squashed cone 上化为切割的内禀曲率），讨论其 Noether 荷的形变不变性与中心性；并将边界按 Euler 作用完备化，考察预辛形式（presymplectic form）与交叉乘积（crossed product）；同时把因子性（factoriality）弱化为中心分解，并在 Jackiw–Teitelboim 引力与反 de Sitter 拓扑黑洞中示例。

Result: 该常数项在每一光滑截面为常数、不携带 boost 能量，其 Noether 荷在拓扑扇区内形变不变且为保持该扇区的代数的中心元，故只能重标度迹；边界完备化后 Gauss–Bonnet 项对预辛形式无贡献，交叉乘积继承自 Einstein 引力，常数即被挖去欧氏尖端的 Euler 数。

Conclusion: 广义熵的加性常数被解释为态无关的拓扑/归一化量；在拓扑黑洞中拓扑权重使 Hawking–Page 温度发生位移，黑洞并合使粗粒化边界熵移动一个态无关量。

Abstract: In quantum gravity the algebra of observables of a subregion is of type III$_1$ at leading order in the semiclassical expansion, and it becomes of type II once the gravitational constraint is imposed. Hence generalized entropy acquires a trace, however it is defined only up to an additive constant. In this work we argue that in four-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity this constant is the Gauss--Bonnet contribution to the Wald entropy, and that it is carried by the normalization of the trace and not by the state. In particular, the conical expansion of the Gauss--Bonnet density is exactly linear in the deficit angle, and on smooth squashed cones it reduces to the intrinsic curvature of the cut, so that the term is a constant on every smooth cross-section and carries no boost energy. Its Noether charge is invariant under all deformations within a topological sector and central in any algebra that preserves the sector, and thus a state-independent constant on such an algebra can only rescale the trace. Additionally, when the boundary of the subregion is completed as the Euler action requires, the Gauss--Bonnet term does not contribute to the presymplectic form, the crossed product is inherited from Einstein gravity, and the constant is the Euler number of the excised Euclidean tip. We state the hypotheses, we weaken factoriality to a central decomposition, and we illustrate the mechanism in Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity and in topological black holes in anti-de Sitter space, where the topological weight displaces the Hawking--Page temperature and a black-hole merger shifts the coarse-grained boundary entropy by a state-independent amount.

</details>


### [230] [Shell crossing and the dynamical formation of thin shells in the collapse of self-gravitating relativistic dust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22970)
*Matt Smith*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明球对称自引力相对论尘埃（relativistic dust）塌缩中的壳穿越（shell crossing）奇点可理解为尘埃薄壳（thin shell）的动力学形成，并在一般初值下构造分段光滑的极大整体双曲发展，使壳穿越奇点被“消解”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球对称爱因斯坦-尘埃系统（Einstein-dust system）中面半径有界远离零的首个奇点被称为壳穿越奇点，其物理意义与发展可延拓性此前缺乏清晰刻画，本文旨在给出其薄壳解释并处理相应初值问题。

Method: 采用双零坐标（double null coordinates）下的球对称爱因斯坦-尘埃系统（E-d），构造弱解（weak solution），其中形成尘埃薄壳；并引入“确定性条件”（determinism condition）与更强的“传播条件”（propagation condition）来约束分段光滑解（piecewise smooth solutions）。

Result: 证明存在唯一局部延拓，越过光滑塌缩解的非退化首个壳穿越奇点；对 R^3 型初始超曲面上含塌缩尘埃云的一般初值，存在满足传播条件的极大整体双曲分段光滑发展，可含任意有限个薄壳且薄壳可合并，所有壳穿越奇点被消解，面半径在首个奇点处连续延拓至零。

Conclusion: 壳穿越奇点可被重新解释并规则化为薄壳形成与合并的动力学过程；同时指出对光滑拓扑下开集的初值，按 Christodoulou 的结果，柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）由中心“壳聚焦”（shell-focussing）奇点发出。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that shell crossing in the collapse of self-gravitating relativistic dust in spherical symmetry can be understood as dynamical formation of a thin shell of dust. More precisely, we consider the spherically symmetric Einstein-dust system in double null coordinates (E-d), for which first singularities with area radius bounded away from zero are known as 'shell crossing' singularities. We show that there exists a weak solution of (E-d), in which a thin shell of dust forms, extending beyond any 'non-degenerate' first shell crossing singularity of a smooth 'collapsing' solution of (E-d). This local extension is unique in the class of piecewise smooth solutions of (E-d) satisfying a 'determinism condition'.
  We then consider the physical problem of smooth data for (E-d) on an initial hypersurface diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^3$, containing a collapsing dust cloud. We show that, for generic such initial data, there exists a maximal globally hyperbolic piecewise smooth development satisfying a 'propagation condition' (stronger than the determinism condition). The development may contain an arbitrary (finite) number of thin shells which can moreover merge. All shell crossing singularities are 'resolved' and the area radius extends continuously to zero at any first singularity of the maximal development. We note that, for an open set of initial data in the smooth topology, as shown by Christodoulou, a Cauchy horizon emanates from a central 'shell-focussing' singularity.

</details>


### [231] [\texttt{StochAXtic}: a JAX-Accelerated, End-to-End Differentiable Framework for Gravitational-Wave Backgrounds from Compact Binary Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22999)
*Haowen Zhong,Ethan Vislosky,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: StochAXtic 是一个基于 JAX 的软件包，用于计算致密双星并合产生的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background）的无量纲能量密度谱（dimensionless energy density spectrum）。它利用波形的尺度不变性（scale invariance）将昂贵波形计算缓存为查找表，并用快速傅里叶变换（fast Fourier transform）与截断奇异值分解（truncated singular value decomposition）加速与压缩计算，可在毫秒量级给出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）采样双星族群来计算随机引力波背景能量密度谱，计算成本高；且需要高效、可微分的方法以支持基于梯度的推断（gradient-based inference）。

Method: 利用波形尺度不变性：在固定质量比、自旋与潮汐形变（tidal deformability）时，波形仅通过无量纲组合 Mf 依赖总质量。由此用 LALSuite 一次性完成昂贵波形评估并存为与族群无关的查找表（lookup table），随后把质量与红移积分改写为卷积并用 FFT 高效求值；再用截断 SVD 压缩查找表。整个实现基于 JAX。

Result: 查找表计算并缓存后，StochAXtic 可在数毫秒内评估 Ω_GW(f)，在最高 1 kHz 频率下相对数值误差低于 10^-3；结果与 popstock 在其统计不确定性范围内一致。

Conclusion: StochAXtic 提供了快速、低误差的随机引力波背景谱计算，并给出 Ω_GW(f) 对族群超参数（population hyperparameters）的梯度，可直接与哈密顿蒙特卡罗（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo）等基于梯度的推断方法集成。

Abstract: We present \texttt{StochAXtic}, a package for computing the dimensionless energy density spectrum $Ω_{\mathrm{GW}}(f)$ of the stochastic gravitational-wave background generated by compact binary coalescences. Rather than sampling binary populations via Monte Carlo, \texttt{StochAXtic} exploits the \emph{scale invariance} of the waveforms: at fixed mass ratio, spins and tidal deformabilities, the waveform depends on the total mass only through the dimensionless combination $Mf$. This allows all computationally expensive waveform evaluations using \texttt{LALSuite} to be performed once and stored in a population-independent lookup table. The subsequent integrals over mass and redshift can then be recast as convolutions and evaluated efficiently using fast Fourier transforms. We further employ truncated singular value decomposition to compress the lookup table, reducing both computational cost and memory requirements. Once the table has been computed and cached, \texttt{StochAXtic} evaluates $Ω_{\mathrm{GW}}(f)$ in a few milliseconds, achieving a relative numerical error below $10^{-3}$ at frequencies up to $1$ kHz. Its results agree with those of \texttt{popstock} within the latter's statistical uncertainties. Implemented entirely in \texttt{JAX}, \texttt{StochAXtic} also provides gradients of $Ω_{\mathrm{GW}}(f)$ with respect to population hyperparameters, enabling its direct integration with gradient-based inference methods including Hamiltonian Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [232] [Convergence of the quasinormal-mode expansion of the Kerr Green function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23110)
*Davide Ciattaglia,Adrien Kuntz,Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出黑洞铃宕（ringdown）的准正规模（quasinormal mode）泛音求和不一定收敛，并证明在Kerr黑洞中收敛失效的时间具有清晰的几何含义：它由源与观测者到"反弹半径（bounce radius）"的光学距离决定，旋转进一步将其推广为"反弹曲面（bounce surfaces）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测拟合中越来越多地纳入高阶泛音（overtones），但泛音振幅在格林函数（Green function）中随时间增长，可能使模式描述在有限时间之前失效，因此需要理解泛音求和何时收敛。

Method: 对高度阻尼模式分析其振幅随源与观测者到黑洞附近特征"反弹半径"（模式振幅最小处）的光学距离呈指数增长的行为，并考虑旋转导致的方向依赖（偏离赤道面时某族模式振幅受抑制），从而给出绝对收敛（absolute convergence）的充分条件；对自旋 a/M = 0.7 的情形数值计算收敛时间随源与观测者位置的变化。

Result: 收敛起始时间对Kerr黑洞具有几何意义：由到反弹半径的光学距离控制，一类模式还随源与观测者偏离赤道面而振幅降低，故旋转使收敛起点既依赖方向也依赖距离；数值计算的振幅空间依赖与理论预测吻合优于百分之一。

Conclusion: 结果为准正规模求和何时收敛设定了几何限制，将模式描述的适用时间与源、观测者的几何位置联系起来。

Abstract: Black-hole ringdown is usually modeled as a linear superposition of quasinormal modes, and fits to gravitational-wave observations increasingly include high overtones. However, the overtone sum does not necessarily converge: overtones are more strongly damped and thus decay faster in time, but their amplitudes in the Green function (the signal at an observer produced by an impulse at a source point) grow, so the mode description may break down before a finite time. We show that for a rotating (Kerr) black hole this time has a clear geometric meaning. For highly damped modes, the amplitudes grow exponentially with the optical distance from the source and the observer to a characteristic "bounce" radius close to the black hole, defined as the location where the mode amplitude is smallest. For one family of modes, the amplitudes are also suppressed the farther the source and observer sit from the equatorial plane. Rotation therefore makes the onset of convergence depend on direction as well as distance, extending the geometric picture from bounce radii to bounce surfaces. Combining these effects gives a sufficient condition for absolute convergence. For spin $a/M = 0.7$, we map how this time varies with source and observer position, and amplitudes computed numerically reproduce the predicted spatial dependence to better than one percent. These results set geometric limits on when the quasinormal-mode sums converge.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Thermal and Acoustic Measurements of Hybrid Bonding Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22694)
*Zirou Chen,Xinyan Xu,Zifeng Huang,Xufei Guo,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时域热反射（TDTR）结合皮秒超声技术，测量Cu/介质混合键合（hybrid bonding）层的有效热导率，并通过浅角度抛光实现深度分辨测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维集成电路（3D-ICs）中的热输运理解依赖于埋入式混合键合结构的热学与结构表征，但此类埋藏层难以直接测量。

Method: 采用时域热反射（time-domain thermoreflectance, TDTR）结合皮秒超声（picosecond ultrasonic）技术；用浅角度抛光（shallow-angle polishing）暴露不同剩余厚度的键合层以实现深度分辨测量，并利用声学回波（acoustic echoes）识别埋入界面并确定厚度。

Result: 底层焊盘层热导率为1.14–1.75 Wm⁻¹K⁻¹，顶层焊盘层为1.76–4.34 Wm⁻¹K⁻¹；差异与两层Cu面积分数（Cu area fraction）不同一致。顶层热导率随剩余厚度显著增大，可能与键合界面热边界电阻（thermal boundary resistance）、SiCN中间层低热导率及薄Cu结构中增强的电子散射有关。键合界面回波波形与Cu/介质界面回波明显不同，提示其声学响应可能反映界面键合状况。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种研究埋入式混合键合层热学性质的实用方法，并为3D-ICs的热设计与建模提供实验数据。

Abstract: Thermal and structural characterizations of hybrid bonding structures are essential for understanding heat transport in three-dimensional integrated circuits (3D-ICs). In this work, we investigate the effective thermal conductivity of the Cu/dielectric HB layer using time-domain thermoreflectance (TDTR) combined with picosecond ultrasonic technique. Shallow-angle polishing enabled depth-resolved thermal measurements by exposing hybrid bonding layers with different remaining thicknesses, while acoustic echoes were used to identify buried interfaces and determine the thickness. The distinct waveform of the bonding interface echo compared with the Cu/dielectric interface echo suggests that its acoustic response may also reflect the interfacial bonding conditions. The extracted thermal conductivity ranges from 1.14 to 1.75 Wm-1K-1 for the bottom pad layer and from 1.76 to 4.34 Wm-1K-1 for the top pad layer. The difference in thermal conductivity is consistent with the different Cu area fractions of the two layers. Furthermore, the thermal conductivity of the top pad layer increases significantly with the remaining thickness, possibly due to the thermal boundary resistance at the bonding interface, low thermal conductivity of SiCN interlayer, and increased electron scattering in thin Cu structures. Our work demonstrates a practical approach to investigate the thermal properties of buried hybrid bonding layers, providing experimental data for thermal design and modeling of 3D-ICs.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Anomaly inflow for simplicial Kähler fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21860)
*Simon Catterall*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 研究三维流形有限三角剖分（triangulation）上带质量 Kähler Dirac 费米子（Kähler Dirac fermions）的反常结构，通过将 Pfaffian 分解为体与边界扇区并分析全局 $Z_4$ 变换下的相位，揭示拓扑反常与体-边反常流入（anomaly inflow）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探测此类格点费米子模型的反常结构（anomaly structure），并厘清边界 't Hooft 反常与体态反常流入（inflow）之间的对应关系，包括非可定向边界（non-orientable boundary）情形与拓扑超导体分类的联系。

Method: 在带一个或多个边界的三维流形有限三角剖分上，将 Kähler 场分解为两个约化 Kähler 场，并选取使边缘模式（edge modes）必然无质量的边界条件；随后把系统的 Pfaffian 分解为体扇区与边界扇区的乘积，考察其在同时改变质量符号的全局 $Z_4$ 变换下各自获得的相位。

Result: 这些相位是拓扑性的，仅依赖该扇区中格点 Kähler 算子（lattice Kähler operator）的指标（index）；边界扇区的非平凡相位标志 't Hooft 反常的存在，且存在体态流入抵消边界相位的情形，在无体态时对非可定向边界消去反常可重现拓扑超导体的已知分类。

Conclusion: 结果给出连续极限（continuum）解释：积掉带质量体 Kähler Dirac 费米子诱导出引力 Chern-Simons 理论（gravitational Chern-Simons theory），其流入在边界存在时产生 Wen-Zee 项，从而实现反常流入机制。

Abstract: We study massive Kähler Dirac fermions propagating on finite triangulations of various three dimensional manifolds with one or more boundaries. To probe the anomaly structure of these models we decompose the Kähler field into two reduced Kähler fields and choose boundary conditions that ensure that any edge modes are necessarily massless. We then show how to factorize the Pfaffian of this system into a product of bulk and boundary sectors each of which can separately pick up a phase under a global $Z_4$ transformation of the fermions that simultaneously changes the sign of the mass. These phases are topological, depending only on the index of the lattice Kähler operator in that sector. For the boundary sector a non-trivial phase signals the presence of a 't Hooft anomaly. We study cases where the boundary is anomalous but an inflow from the bulk cancels its phase. Canceling this anomaly for a non-orientable boundary in the absence of the bulk recovers the known classification of topological superconductors. We also give a continuum interpretation of these results in terms of inflow from a gravitational Chern Simons theory which is induced by integrating out massive bulk Kähler Dirac fermions. In the presence of a boundary the latter gives rise to Wen-Zee terms that mediate the anomaly inflow mechanism.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [A Brownian web invariance principle: Localized disturbance flows and Hastings-Levitov(0)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22175)
*Guanyi Chen,Xinyi Li,Eviatar B. Procaccia,Yuxuan Zong*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文为局部扰动流（localized disturbance flows）建立布朗网（Brownian web）不变原理，将弱流收敛强化为完整轨迹族的路径-Hausdorff收敛（path-Hausdorff convergence），并应用于 Hastings–Levitov(0) 聚集模型的径向、平稳与圆柱版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果（Norris–Turner 2015）仅给出弱流收敛，不足以刻画完整轨迹集合的收敛；同时 Norris–Turner 2012 的径向 HL(0) 小粒子极限只覆盖调和测度流（harmonic measure flow）与有限 gap–finger 集合，需要推广到整体轨迹层面。

Method: 建立布朗网不变原理判据，将弱流收敛提升为路径-Hausdorff收敛；核心技术工具为三点生存估计（three-point survival estimate），通过多尺度停时分解（multiscale stopping-time decomposition）证明。

Result: 证明径向、平稳与圆柱 HL(0) 模型的完整调和测度路径族路径-Hausdorff收敛到（圆柱）布朗网，且完整 gap–finger 族联合收敛到相应双布朗网（double Brownian webs）的几何嵌入；在径向与圆柱 HL(0) 中，重标度单臂支配时间（one-arm domination time）收敛到 T 上聚合布朗运动全局合并时间（global coalescence time）的常数倍，并伴随期望收敛。

Conclusion: 该不变原理统一并强化了 HL(0) 的既有小粒子极限结果，将收敛提升至完整轨迹集合层面，并揭示单臂支配时间与聚合布朗运动合并时间之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We establish a Brownian web invariance principle for localized disturbance flows, providing a criterion that strengthens the weak-flow convergence of [Norris--Turner 2015, Ann. Probab. 43, 935--970] to path-Hausdorff convergence of their full trajectory collections. We apply this approach to the radial, stationary, and cylindrical versions of the Hastings--Levitov$(0)$ aggregation model, proving path-Hausdorff convergence of the full harmonic measure path collections to the (Cylindrical) Brownian web, together with joint convergence of the full gap--finger collections to geometric embeddings of the corresponding double Brownian webs. This extends the radial HL$(0)$ small-particle limits of [Norris--Turner 2012, Commun. Math. Phys. 316, 809--841] for the harmonic measure flow and finite gap--finger collections. In the radial and cylindrical HL$(0)$, we also show that the rescaled one-arm domination time converges to a multiple of the global coalescence time of coalescing Brownian motion on $\mathbb T$, with convergence of expectations. The main new ingredient is a three-point survival estimate, proved via a multiscale stopping-time decomposition.

</details>


### [236] [Edge behavior of the KPZ fixed point under upper-tail conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22770)
*Yuanyuan Liu,Zhipeng Liu,Haoyang Ying*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究窄楔（narrow-wedge）初值下 KPZ 不动点（KPZ fixed point）在大高度条件 $\{\mathcal{H}^{\mathrm{KPZ}}(0,1)\ge L\}$、$L\to\infty$ 时的边界涨落，证明在空间窗口 $x=t\sqrt{L}+O(1)$ 内，中心化场在有限维分布意义下收敛到半布朗（half-Brownian）初值的 KPZ 不动点，且在任意正时间上联合成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已刻画 KPZ 不动点在不同初值下的涨落，但带大偏差型条件（如高处条件）所诱导的边界区域及其边缘涨落尚缺乏精确描述；本文旨在填补这一空白，厘清条件效应影响区域的边界行为。

Method: 方法基于条件窄楔多点公式（conditional narrow-wedge multipoint formulas）与半布朗 KPZ 不动点对应多点公式之间的渐近对应关系（asymptotic correspondence），由此导出有限维分布（finite-dimensional distributions）的收敛性。

Result: 在空间窗口 $x=t\sqrt{L}+O(1)$ 内，中心化场在有限维分布意义下收敛到半布朗初值的 KPZ 不动点；该收敛在任意正时间上联合成立，涵盖条件时刻之前与之后的时间点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，大高度条件在临界空间尺度上等价于半布朗型边界初值，即边界处的涨落由半布朗 KPZ 不动点普适刻画（universality），为条件 KPZ 场提供了边界极限理论。

Abstract: We consider the KPZ fixed point $\mathcal{H}^{\mathrm{KPZ}}(x,t)$ with the narrow-wedge initial profile, conditioned on $\{\mathcal{H}^{\mathrm{KPZ}}(0,1)\ge L\}$ as $L\to\infty$. Our main result describes its fluctuations near the boundary of the region affected by the conditioning. More precisely, in the spatial window $x=t\sqrt L+O(1)$, the centered field converges in finite-dimensional distributions to the KPZ fixed point with a half-Brownian initial profile. The convergence holds jointly at arbitrary positive times, including times before and after the conditioning time. The proof is based on an asymptotic correspondence between conditional narrow-wedge multipoint formulas and those for the half-Brownian KPZ fixed point.

</details>


### [237] [Second-class particle in ASEP. Rarefaction fan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23191)
*Alexey Bufetov,Adyan Dordzhiev*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究整数格点上非对称简单排它过程（ASEP）中第二类粒子（second class particle）的渐近行为，给出任意初始条件下其任意固定时刻位置分布的一般公式，并将稀疏扇（rarefaction fan）情形下的极限分布从TASEP推广至ASEP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二类粒子是刻画相互作用粒子系统（interacting particle systems）中激波与特征线结构的常用探针，已有结果多限于TASEP或特定初始条件；本文旨在对ASEP在任意初始条件下给出统一的分布刻画，并推广Cator–Pimentel的结论。

Method: 采用概率与精确可解性分析途径，推导第二类粒子在任意固定时刻位置分布的一般公式，并以该公式为基础分析稀疏扇情形下的极限行为。

Result: 获得了任意初始条件下第二类粒子位置分布在固定时刻的一般表达式；作为首个应用，得到稀疏扇情形下任意初始条件的极限分布，从而把Cator–Pimentel由TASEP到ASEP的结果加以推广。

Conclusion: 所给一般分布公式为ASEP中第二类粒子的渐近分析提供了统一工具，并表明稀疏扇下的极限分布结论可自然扩展至ASEP这一更一般的设定。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of a second class particle in ASEP on the integer lattice. We prove a general formula for the distribution of its position at any fixed time for arbitrary initial conditions. As a first application, we obtain its limiting distribution for arbitrary initial conditions in the rarefaction fan case, generalizing the result of Cator--Pimentel from TASEP to ASEP.

</details>
